Chapter 851: Truth Slate

Cang Xu was genuinely stunned.

He made no attempt to conceal his true feelings at that moment.

All along, he had been at the bottom tier of transcendents, utterly unfamiliar with the upper echelons like the saint level. After all, he had once been merely an iron level individual.

And today, he had finally witnessed the true might of a saint level powerhouse!

"It's like when the commander, back at silver level, took down a gold level Teng Dong Lang. Ordinary silver level fighters weren't even close to being his match back then."

"Teacher Si Ling must be something like that."

Many factors influenced combat.

Transcendent level was only one aspect; another crucial factor was bloodline.

Just like the dragonman youngster back then, his greatest asset was actually the legend level bloodline of the Flame Dragon King.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline granted him not only extraordinary defenses like dragon scales, but also substantial base strength, agility, vision, and more.

Beyond that, it bestowed the most direct combat abilities— like the racial magics dragon prestige, dragon breath, and the like.

Beyond transcendent levels and bloodlines, numerous other factors influence combat. Take initiative, for instance. This distinction was particularly pronounced for assassins and rogues. For them, an initiative to make the first move could often change the battle results.

Sensing Cang Xu's gaze of shock and admiration, Teacher Si Ling's smile became increasingly natural.

He taught without holding back information: "When a transcendent reaches the saint level, no matter which kind, they will have a domain."

“Magicians wield magic domains, fighters wield fighting domains, while clergymen are unique—their divine domain approximates the effects of the divine domain spell, yet carries personal characteristics.”

“Regardless of type, these are collectively termed saint domains.”

“The quality and size of a saint domain profoundly impact every saint level transcendent.”

“The saint domain also forms the foundation for their future advancement to legend level!”

Hearing this, Jian Gui couldn't help but feel a surge of envy and admiration toward Cang Xu once more.

He had never received such guidance, for the undead orthodoxy knew full well the limits of his bloodline.

“These words were meant for Xu Jia.”

“Because his bloodline mutated, it mutated into the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline.”

"The ultimate concentration of this bloodline remains uncertain. But at least for now, he genuinely holds hope of ascending to the saint level!"

That's right.

To infiltrate the undead orthodoxy, the fishman youngster had bestowed numerous deception spells upon Cang Xu. The classic spell combination of Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell was merely the foundation.

From Teacher Si Ling's investigative perspective, Cang Xu possessed a saint level bloodline yet remained in a state of mutation.

This was normal.

Typically, the lower the bloodline level, the shorter the mutation period. The higher the level, the longer the mutation period.

Moreover, even bloodlines of the same level exhibited varying mutation durations.

In fact, even identical bloodlines underwent differing mutation durations due to varying environmental factors. Consider this analogy: 2+1=3, and 1+1+1 was also 3. The former's process was shorter, the latter longer, yet the outcome remained identical.

Bloodline dynamics were profoundly mysterious and intricate. Detecting bloodlines was inherently challenging and often inaccurate; bloodline mutation was even more elusive.

From this perspective, one could truly appreciate how invaluable the Bloodcore truly was.

Back then, War Merchant placed the Bloodcore at the top, while relegating the two artifacts—the Deceptive Disguise spell and Mermaid Fairy Tale, to the lower levels of the mage tower. This was no accident!

Artifacts possessed unparalleled, unique power, yet they still had limitations in their application. Even the legend level War Merchant could only harness a fraction of the Mermaid Fairy Tale's true power. The Bloodcore was different. It could be wielded effortlessly, fundamentally reshaping one's foundation and unlocking boundless potential for its host!

Jian Gui was correct.

Teacher Si Ling's deliberate guidance at this moment was indeed granted for Cang Xu's sake.

Saint and gold were two distinct concepts, existing on different levels.

Cang Xu possessed the potential to ascend to the saint level, a qualification sufficient for Teacher Si Ling to offer guidance.

Should he confirm Cang Xu's capability to reach the saint level, the treatment he would receive would be even more exalted. It was entirely possible that You Shen himself would come to instruct him!

The Twelve Saints he had taken under his wing were all chosen for their assured potential to ascend to the saint level in the future.

You Shen's standards for accepting disciples were exceptionally stringent.

Of course, this was precisely the situation Cang Xu and the fishman youngster sought to avoid.

Because they were deceiving these people.

If You Shen were to see through them right before his eyes, it would be disastrous.

Facing a saint level opponent already put the survivors at risk. Facing a legend level opponent, the likelihood of being exposed inevitably skyrocketed.

This was not difficult to understand.

The Deceptive Disguise spell itself was merely a creation by the War Merchant, inspired by the artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale]. A legend level being like War Merchant still fell short of You Shen. Therefore, the likelihood of You Shen seeing through it was quite high.

Truthfully, there's no need to observe him.

Just look at the present.

In battles against peers, Zhou Zhang's deception divine spell now had minimal effect. Both Da Han and Qian Xing could swiftly discern the deception within a short span.

Cang Xu mused inwardly: “Charming Blue Goddess' strength isn't particularly high. If her deception divine spell were truly effective, she wouldn't have been gravely wounded and fallen into slumber...”

“Her Deceptive Disguise divine spell won't be reliable in the future.”

"They might work against weaker foes, like at the gold level, where they show clear effectiveness. But at the saint level, their practical utility in combat diminishes. Once targeted, their impact becomes even more negligible."

“Of course, when the Lord Commander is wielding the Charming Blue divine artifact, that's an entirely different matter.”

At the mention of divine artifacts, Cang Xu witnessed Teacher Si Ling slowly draw one from his robes!

It was a stone tablet.

Approximately one centimeter thick and the size of an A4 sheet, it formed a rectangular prism of deep blue-black stone.

Not a single word graced its surface. Far from rough, the stone felt remarkably fine and smooth.

Divine artifact - the Truth Slate!

This artifact was renowned throughout the Main Plane, no, the entire world.

It was a fragment of the core divine artifact, the Book of Truth, forged by the God of Truth. Legend held it recorded every law and principle governing the existence, operation, and demise of all things in the world.

With the fall of the God of Truth, the Book of Truth was shattered, its fragments scattered across the world. Each page became a Truth Slate.

Now, Teacher Si Ling held one such fragment in hand.

Teacher Si Ling channeled his magic power into the tablet, slowly activating the artifact.

The tablet began to emit a faint, grayish-white glow.

Undead magic power!

A faint aura of negative energy seeped out, though it was extremely tenuous.

Simultaneously, the pale gray glow slowly expanded, radiating outward from the tablet's center point in all directions. The surrounding space became tinged with a layer of grayish-white.

Cang Xu couldn't help but feel uneasy.

He realized the pale gray glow was drawing ever closer to the Deep Sea Monster Fish!

Within mere seconds, the glow's expansion halted.

Ultimately, it coalesced into a luminous sphere centered on the stone slab, spanning fifty meters in diameter.

The slab began to tremble slightly, initiating a process of self-carving.

As countless tiny stone fragments scattered and vanished mid-air, intricate characters etched themselves onto its surface.

Extremely complex runes.

Cang Xu glanced at them and was immediately struck by intense dizziness. He quickly shut off his vision, but the impression etched into his memory still sent shockwaves through him!

Fortunately, Cang Xu possessed the Memory Concealment spell.

He swiftly cast it, compressing and sealing away the memory from just a second prior.

Not seeing Cang Xu faint, Jian Gui, whose heart brimmed with envy, felt a pang of disappointment.

He offered guidance: "That is the truth divine script, transcendent characters created by the God of Truth to record all things in the world. Many believe this script is the divine question among divine scripts, beyond our comprehension."

Jian Gui didn't glance at the stone tablet once. As a member of the undead orthodoxy, he naturally knew more secrets.

Truth divine script!

Cang Xu's heart trembled with shock.

Teacher Si Ling, however, stared intently at the stone tablet, his gaze fixed on the ever-increasing number of truth divine script runes.

He spoke slowly, “Xu Jia, what do you think the divine domain is?”

Cang Xu had severed his vision, his field of sight plunged into darkness. Hearing Teacher Si Ling's sudden question, his heart jolted.

He keenly sensed this was a small test from Teacher Si Ling, gauging his insight or inspiration.

Cang Xu's mind raced: "Could it be that the divine domain is the ultimate form of a domain?"

“Hehehe, good insight, not bad!” Teacher Si Ling smiled with satisfaction.

“Whether it be battle qi, magic power, or divine power, at their core, they are all forms of energy.”

"The foundation of any domain must be built upon an energy cornerstone. Beyond that lies the rules."

“These rules are the very logic governing the existence, operation, and dissolution of all things.”

“For instance, the foundational energy composing the Charming Blue Divine Domain we currently inhabit is Charming Blue divine power. Its primary logic revolves around deception and disguise, while secondary logic encompasses the fishman race, water elements, and fishing...”

“Silence!” Teacher Si Ling was abruptly cut off before finishing.

Cang Xu instantly recognized the voice as Zhou Zhang's.

He hastily reconnected and activated his vision, seeing Zhou Zhang swim through the breach in the Mother Nest, her face twisted with fury.

The Snow Pear Octopus had also returned, but instead of entering the nest, he guarded the opening from outside.

“Teacher Si Ling! I never imagined the one I'd be waiting for would be you!” Zhou Zhang roared, her fury palpable. “Put away your tablet! How dare you so casually pry into my Lord's divine domain!”

Though Zhou Zhang's personal combat prowess paled beside Teacher Si Ling's, her unwavering devotion left her utterly unyielding.

This display stood in stark contrast to the earlier reactions of Da Han and Qian Xing.

Cang Xu deeply understood this fury.

For Teacher Si Ling's act of using the divine artifact tablet to pry into the secrets of the divine domain was a grave sacrilege.

This was one hundred percent certain, beyond any doubt!

Despite Zhou Zhang's furious rebuke, Teacher Si Ling remained unperturbed, leisurely stowing away the tablet. "Archbishop Zhou Zhang, you must understand this is part of the agreement between our alliance and yours. Your church must provide us with knowledge of the divine domain."

“Moreover, this isn't my first time entering this part of the Charming Blue Divine Domain.”

“Back then, I used the tablet to analyze and document the divine domain.”

“Even the avatar of Charming Blue Goddess was present.”

“Who do you think constructed the magic inscriptions of the formation outside?”

Chapter 852: Seeing the Hill

For a moment, Zhou Zhang fell silent.

Teacher Si Ling's appearance had startled her.

She knew her mission: the blood sacrifice was only one part, the other being to await the arrival of a necromancer. What she didn't know was that the one she was meant to wait for was Teacher Si Ling himself.

Zhou Zhang was a devout believer, not a fanatic. She felt profound revulsion and disgust toward Teacher Si Ling, yet her reason remained unshaken by her faith.

Cang Xu, however, experienced a moment of realization.

Indeed, the magic formation surrounding the Ocean Mother Nest contained not only the Charming Blue divine script but also magic runes.

In fact, the very foundation of this colossal formation was not the Charming Blue divine script at all, but the power of the magic.

This was highly unusual.

Consider the massive mobilization of the earth elemental army when they surrounded and hunted down the giant. The divine script formation they deployed was composed purely of earth elemental divine script, without a single magic rune involved.

Looking at the silent Zhou Zhang, Teacher Si Ling replied in a slightly proud tone: "I participated in the construction of this formation. But the ones who actually executed it, aside from the avatar of Charming Blue Goddess, were my teacher and myself."

Teacher Si Ling's teacher was none other than You Shen, the founding patriarch of the necromancers!

Teacher Si Ling: “This colossal formation isn't merely for coordinating with the Ocean Mother Nest for the blood sacrifice. It possesses another crucial function.”

“And that is precisely the subject of our collaboration.”

“This is why I have come.”

Zhou Zhang was aware of this point.

She struggled to speak: "I will cooperate with you going forward. But Qian Xing and Da Han have escaped. This means..."

Teacher Si Ling interrupted: “Rest assured, Fire Beard is not nearby. Da Han came to Ice Sculpture Island for his own reasons.”

“Even if they attack again, as long as Fire Beard isn't present, there's nothing to fear.”

"Next, we must join forces to restore the magic formation and repair the Ocean Mother Nest. The primary force for repairing the Mother Nest isn't you or me, but the octopus saint beast. This was foreseen long ago by the Charming Blue Goddess."

Zhou Zhang's face lit up with a hint of joy. She had been despairing over the Ocean Mother Nest's ruin, never expecting it could be repaired!

Gods were omnipotent.

Charming Blue Goddess had long foreseen such an occurrence and made corresponding arrangements.

The saint beast Snow Pear Octopus wasn't merely to assist in the blood sacrifice for the Mother Nest, it also served as the ultimate safeguard for its restoration!

Cang Xu, standing nearby, looked pensive.

Listening to the conversation between Zhou Zhang and Teacher Si Ling, he had discovered much.

“The undead orthodoxy and the Charming Blue Church may be allies, but their relationship is far from harmonious.”

This was evident.

Necromancers blasphemed against the gods, gravely infringing upon the most sacred benefit of the deities—souls. They were enemies of the gods. If allowed to proliferate, they posed an extreme threat to all gods.

By any reasonable logic, no deity would ally with them. Charming Blue Goddess was an exception. Targeted and gravely wounded, forced into slumber, her situation was precarious. She had no choice but to seek any external aid available!

The necromancers, too, were targeted, their own position equally dire. Having a deity as an ally would undoubtedly be advantageous. Even if that deity lay dormant, gravely wounded.

On the other hand, necromancers were magicians at heart, driven by an insatiable thirst for knowledge. Especially knowledge concerning deities, any insight that could aid them in confronting the gods. Thus, their need in this regard was immense and urgent.

“This alliance between the two factions is fundamentally about huddling together for warmth.”

"Though united, they're held together by immense external force and pressure. Thus, each remains wary of the other, constantly scheming against one another."

Reflecting on this, Cang Xu recalled Zhou Zhang's earlier actions, his expression growing subtly complex.

By all logic, the necromancers falling into an Archbishop's hands should be utterly doomed. Yet Zhou Zhang merely captured these individuals without immediately executing them.

This action alone was already highly suspicious.

Later, Zhou Zhang used blood sacrifices to coerce the necromancers into conversion through threats of life and death.

Xiao Wang Zi and others successively became sacrificial offerings. Now Cang Xu understood Zhou Zhang's true target—Jian Gui.

Jian Gui was different.

He was gold level, a member of the undead orthodoxy.

The Charming Blue Church and undead orthodoxy were allies, bound by cooperation. Zhou Zhang likely never intended to truly kill Jian Gui.

For doing so would likely have shattered their alliance.

Thus, Zhou Zhang resorted to intimidation.

Why would she do this?

Cang Xu guessed it easily: Zhou Zhang wanted to convert Jian Gui, allowing the Charming Blue Church to infiltrate their allies. This was a form of erosion towards the undead forces.

“I was an accident.”

"When I returned from the Mother Nest and mastered special divine spells, Zhou Zhang must have been overjoyed. Even if she didn't understand why I could return, she immediately recognized my value."

“Her shift in attitude toward me, and all her subsequent support, were all efforts to win me over.”

“She must have been overjoyed, for in me she saw the fruits of faith infiltration and even greater possibilities.”

“Jian Gui surely once wondered—why I passed the blood sacrifice.”

"I have now resolved that question. I revealed my true potential."

"Was Teacher Si Ling's guidance merely about recognizing my potential?"

"No."

"He told me about the divine domain and showed an attitude of nonchalance towards the Charming Blue Divine Domain. He even used the Truth Slate to peek into the secrets of the divine domain openly. This is blasphemy. He's trying to destroy my Charming Blue faith."

“He was correcting me, teaching me in a manner so gentle it was nearly imperceptible. He made me recognize the frailty of my object of faith, so I could become a purer necromancer!”

Ordinary people couldn't see through it, remaining in a state of blissful ignorance. But Cang Xu, seasoned by years of experience, immediately sensed the trickery and the hidden power struggle between the two factions.

“Who cares!”

"My Charming Blue faith was fake all along. If Teacher Si Ling knew this truth, he'd be relieved. In a way, we're far more blasphemous than he ever was."

“If he knew what the commander had done, he'd admire him even more, right?”

This thought left Cang Xu feeling strangely unsettled.

“The Lord Commander is devoted to the Justice God.”

"Wonder how he's doing now? Did he save the big guy?"

The fishman youngster had no energy left to pay attention to the outside world.

He poured all his focus into the big guy, using the divine artifact and divine magic with every ounce of strength.

He succeeded!

The Main Earth Elemental God thought He had killed the big guy, but in reality, He had killed Tu Ji.

The Main Earth Elemental God, satisfied, finally withdrew.

He had no interest whatsoever in the remaining figures like Da Dou and Hong Zhu.

But the earth elemental army did not stop.

Da Dou and the others had slaughtered so many of them—how could they possibly stop?

“What should I do?”

“Maintaining the Believer Perception and the Divine Power Channel is already pushing my limits. I can't cast a third divine spell!”

The fishman youngster watched as the big guy and his companions were surrounded on all sides, trapped by mortal enemies, his heart turning to ice.

With no other choice, he could only attempt to pray to the Charming Blue Goddess.

But just as he was about to do so, a squad of transcendents suddenly burst in.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

They used gold level alchemy artifacts to trigger a series of explosions.

"Follow me!!" The Imperial Spy Chief charged ahead, leading his subordinates to the rescue.

These secret agents were all elite operatives.

If they weren't, they wouldn't have been able to execute the plan, disrupting the order of the Magnet Archipelago and plunging all the earth elemental tribes into conflict and constant strife.

They successfully destroyed the divine script formation set up by the earth elemental army.

“Why are these people rescuing Xiao Guai?”

The fishman youngster was utterly perplexed.

Under his expectant gaze, the spies successfully linked up with Da Dou and the others.

Caught completely off guard, the earth elemental army was thrown into chaos by the potions hurled by the spies before they could pursue.

True to their reputation, the Imperial spies wielded top-tier equipment and weaponry.

But saving lives came at a cost.

By the time the secret agents emerged, they had lost at least half their members.

Fortunately, Da Dou and the other two were still alive.

“With these witnesses, my innocence is proven!”

"Tu Ji's death had nothing to do with me!"

“I did everything I could to assist. I am innocent.” The spy chief silently rallied his resolve.

Tu Ji was dead.

A member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution had fallen in battle within his jurisdiction.

Crucially, he had been killed by the Main Earth Elemental God!

This was utterly bizarre.

Who would believe it if he spoke?

The mighty Main Earth Elemental God personally intervening against a mere gold level?

“Xiao Guai was successfully rescued!”

Witnessing this, the fishman youngster relaxed instantly.

That release of tension was all he needed, he collapsed, unconscious on the spot.

As he drifted in and out of consciousness, his weary eyelids fluttered open.

“Where... am I?”

The roar of churning seawater filled his ears.

Surveying his surroundings, he realized he was sinking once more.

The deep sea extended around him.

“Here again...” The fishman youngster recognized this scene all too well.

Countless times, as he drifted into slumber, the Spiritual Navigation within him would activate, drawing him into prayer to Charming Blue Goddess.

Gazing downward, he spotted an immense, shadowy form.

The deeper he sank, the closer he drew to it.

“Is this the true form of Charming Blue Goddess?” the fishman youngster wondered inwardly.

"Wake up! Wake up!!" he screamed in his mind.

But this time, he was utterly exhausted. Though he served as a divine emissary, he remained only a gold level.

He had nearly drained himself dry, utterly devoid of energy.

So no matter how fiercely he screamed, he remained unconscious.

Closer and closer, he drew nearer to the colossal shadow in the ocean depths.

The shadow gradually revealed its true form.

The fishman youngster's eyes widened slightly. He realized this shadow wasn't a living creature at all, it was truly a hill.

“Why does this hill look familiar?”

“Where have I seen it before?”

Suddenly, a bolt of lightning struck the fishman youngster's mind.

He remembered.

He had seen it in the Dueling Divine Domain, through the memories of Lan Zao.

It was a wondrous place, a marvelous place.

A legendary place, a romantic place!

It was—Tranquil Hill.

Chapter 853: Flying Fish

“Why Tranquil Hill?”

“How could it possibly be Tranquil Hill?”

When the fishman youngster saw the truth behind the massive shadow, his heart filled with shock and doubt.

He had contacted Lan Zao and learned of Tranquil Hill.

Tranquil Hill wasn't confined to the legends of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, it was a real place, existing within the Dueling Divine Domain.

Tranquil Hill possessed extraordinary properties. Those buried within it might be resurrected by its power. This fact was explicitly recorded in the relevant legends.

Even if resurrection failed, consider the handling of Teng Dong Lang's corpse. As the coffin bearer, Spring Boxer reaped the greatest benefit. He fully absorbed Teng Dong Lang's memories from before death, causing his own cultivation to skyrocket!

The Spring Boxer of old was raw and brimming with youthful vigor. Yet through this period of absorption, he underwent profound transformation, maturing rapidly and developing the makings of the next leader of the Bayonet Gang.

Lan Zao witnessed all this firsthand, the Dueling Divine Domain, the Tranquil Hill, Teng Dong Lang, and Spring Boxer's metamorphosis.

The fishman youngster, who could access Lan Zao's memories, was well-versed in these matters.

Precisely because he understood, the fishman youngster now felt even greater astonishment and doubt.

He couldn't fathom how Tranquil Hill, residing far away in the Dueling Divine Domain on Ice Sculpture Island, could be connected to Charming Blue Goddess.

“In my slumber, I constantly prayed passively. The image I saw must have been the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“But now I see Tranquil Hill.”

“Could it be... that Charming Blue Goddess is Tranquil Hill?”

“No. Perhaps her true form is concealed within Tranquil Hill?”

A deity residing not in her own realm, but within the Dueling God's domain? This was truly beyond comprehension.

The fishman youngster continued his bold conjecture.

“Could it be... that Charming Blue Goddess is the Dueling God?”

“Or more accurately, is the Dueling God merely one of Charming Blue Goddess' identities?”

"After all, Charming Blue Goddess possesses many personas. Take the Net Goddess, born from her role as fishing authority; the Fishman God Mist, extending from her fishman authority; or the Calm Lake God, the Wave God, and the Splash God, all derived from her water authority..."

The fishman youngster sank deeper, drawing closer to Tranquil Hill.

In his downward gaze, Tranquil Hill grew larger and larger, its details becoming increasingly distinct.

The pressure in the fishman youngster's heart mounted with each passing moment!

“I wonder what will happen when I land on Tranquil Hill?”

An intuition deep within him whispered that something significant would occur then!

The fishman youngster tried to rouse himself but still failed.

He could only passively observe Tranquil Hill, watching it draw nearer. He discerned its central peak, it wasn’t steep, and its summit blanketed in snow. To the south lay a valley with rivers and green grass. To the west stretched a cliff, sheer as if hewn by blades, shrouded in thick mist...

Lan Zao scaled the western cliff face of Tranquil Hill.

During the surprise assault on Tranquil Hill, Lan Zao had distinguished himself, particularly through his successful prayers that saved countless lives.

Thus, he earned the trust of everyone, including Mei Lin, who granted him freedom of movement.

Lan Zao stood at the cliff's edge, looking down.

The precipice soared high, shrouded in thick mist, its depths invisible.

“A fall from this height would be certain death!” Lan Zao knew it at a glance.

Yet this time, he intended to leap.

He slowly closed his eyes, carefully recalling.

Soon, he immersed himself in another perspective.

In the dueling arena, a fishman darted through the air. Though only silver level, she executed maneuvers of short-range flight.

Her fishman bloodline wasn't Lan Zao's Fish Bladder bloodline, but the Flying Fish bloodline.

Yet the battle skill she wielded was one Lan Zao himself mastered—Air Cannon!

This fishman was locked in a brutal duel with her opponent. In her hands, the Air Cannon battle skill was nothing short of masterful.

When Lan Zao first accessed this memory, he was utterly astonished.

He discovered with amazement that Air Cannon could be imbued with various attack effects without using alchemy projectiles—simply by combining it with other battle skills.

Beyond that, this female fishman fighter could split an Air Cannon into three separate projectiles or fuse multiple Air Cannons mid-air into a single blast.

Most remarkably, she could harness the explosions of Air Cannon to generate propulsive force for flight.

This scene felt profoundly familiar to Lan Zao.

His thoughts immediately turned to the dragonman youngster.

By combining Bursting Explosion battle qi with the Surge battle skill, the dragonman youngster similarly generated force from successive micro-explosions to propel himself through forced displacement.

Lan Zao was now practicing this very technique.

He drew in a deep breath, causing his chest to swell high, his entire upper body expanding by at least a full size.

Maximum Air State!

The Fish Bladder bloodline had endowed Lan Zao with a new organ—a fish bladder. When he inhaled fully, the fish bladder inflated, granting him considerable buoyancy.

Simultaneously, the abundance of oxygen within his body significantly enhanced his physical prowess.

He leapt lightly from the cliff's edge.

Racial magic - Levitation.

He immediately activated the bloodline's innate racial magic, his entire body becoming remarkably light. Spreading his arms like a flying fish unfurling its fins, he began gliding through the air.

He had practiced this racial magic numerous times while on Flower Skirt Island.

Once, during practice, he had even been captured by Ba Qi.

With Levitation as his safety net, Lan Zao wouldn't die from a cliff jump.

He didn't take risks without certainty and wouldn't venture into danger lightly. After all, having escaped from Mystifying Monster Island, he cherished his life deeply. Of course, when cornered with no other choice, he possessed the courage to fight to the death, blazing with life's final brilliant radiance.

Battle skill - Air Cannon.

He began employing this battle skill, attempting to alter his trajectory mid-air.

After merely fifteen minutes of practice, his progress was strikingly evident.

“This is incredible!”

"Before, my use of Levitation was far too slow, rendering it practically useless in combat. I never dared employ it during sparring sessions with others. Because using it would leave me wide open."

“But now, with the Air Cannon complementing it, I can execute rapid mid-air turns. It's a complete transformation, a qualitative leap!”

"Still, compared to that female fishman, I'm not of Flying Fish bloodline, just Fish Bladder bloodline. My innate flight speed is far weaker. Even if I master every technique, I'll never match her flight capabilities."

Lan Zao knew his limits well.

As he practiced relentlessly, the memories he accessed grew increasingly vivid.

Countless insights emerged, fueling constant progress in his training.

Had he relied solely on solitary training, such terrifying progress would have been nearly impossible.

“I'm not learning, I'm reviewing.”

“Ever since the commander saved me and allowed me to disguise myself, the gifts I receive from the Dueling Divine Domain are no longer mere fragments of memory, but entire stretches of recollection.”

"The Dueling Divine Domain truly is an exceptional sanctuary for honing combat prowess! Too bad the commander isn't here."

“Speaking of which, the Lord Commander actually managed to contact me and remotely grant me the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, allowing me to deceive even the Dueling God within the divine domain.”

"Such methods are truly overwhelming! Beyond the limits of my wildest imagination!"

“How exactly did the Lord Commander accomplish this?”

"Could it be that he's mastered the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] even further? Has he constructed some new magic formation, or unearthed fresh power from War Merchant's legacy?"

The fishman youngster knew Lan Zao's situation well, but Lan Zao remained unaware of the fishman youngster's circumstances. He didn't even know the fishman youngster had suffered an accident while searching for him and hadn't returned to Ice Sculpture Island yet.

“I wonder how everyone is doing now?”

"The Lord Commander must be participating in the duels. This time of year is precisely when the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Grand Duel takes place."

Lan Zao himself knew about the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Grand Duel. While training in Tranquil Hill, he learned more about it from Bing Xiao and the human ringleader.

The youngster himself did not participate in the duel.

Zong Ge fought in his place.

Emerging victorious once more, Zong Ge personally pushed open the arena gates, striding out with head held high.

As a private duel, spectators were barred from the arena, so his numerous supporters gathered at the entrance.

When they saw Zong Ge emerge alone, the other combatant nowhere in sight, they erupted in a unified cheer, their joy surpassing even Zong Ge's own.

“Lord Long Fu has won again!”

“I told you so! His opponent looked fierce, but he was no match for Long Fu.”

"Long Fu! Long Fu!"

“Keep winning! I bet on you every single time.”

"Among the silver level fighters, Lord Long Fu is undoubtedly in the top tier. No, to be precise, he stands alone in his class."

With Zong Ge's continuous victories, the dragonman youngster's fame within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's capital continued to soar.

Zong Ge returned to his lodgings.

Zi Di finished her calculations and handed him a report.

The report detailed the latest results of the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony Grand Duel.

It revealed that the tournament was being held nationwide, with dueling arenas in towns and cities across the realm nearly at full capacity.

The Ice Sculpture citizens' enthusiasm had been fully ignited.

Topics concerning duels and duelists were the most talked-about subjects among the populace.

Compared to the native duelists of Ice Sculpture, he paid more attention to outsiders.

It was clear that increasing numbers of foreigners had landed on Ice Sculpture Island, with the number of foreign duelists surging dramatically. Some had won and gained fame, while others had fallen in battle, paying with their lives.

"Huh? The Little Flying Fish died in battle?”

Zong Ge's gaze sharpened slightly as he scanned a segment of the intelligence report.

He monitored every opponent capable of posing a threat to him.

Among them was the Little Flying Fish.

Little Flying Fish was merely a title. She was a female fishman with the Flying Fish bloodline. Though only silver level, she possessed the ability to fly during combat.

Zong Ge had seen footage of her, noting her exceptional flight prowess, agile turns and prolonged hovering. She would have been a troublesome foe to handle.

Little Flying Fish had withdrawn from the tournament gravely wounded; he hadn't expected her injuries to prove fatal.

She had died days ago, though the news had only now been officially confirmed.

Zong Ge swiftly shifted his focus to subsequent intelligence.

With Little Flying Fish dead, she no longer warranted his attention.

The National Ceremony Grand Duel had drawn a surge of foreign duelists, and their numbers continued to skyrocket!

Among the silver level duelists, numerous elite fighters had emerged.

Fortunately, Zong Ge had yet to detect any combatants matching the strength of the dragonman youngster.

Chapter 854: Registration

At the end of the intelligence report, Zong Ge saw information regarding prisoner duelists.

Under the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's system, during every National Ceremony Grand Duel, any prisoner in any cell had the right to apply for participation. If they performed well in combat, they could even earn sentence reductions.

Of course, prisoners first had to apply and obtain permission from prison authorities before engaging in duels.

Driven by the desire for early release, these uniquely motivated duelists fought with relentless determination, often displaying fierce combat resolve. Historically, such events frequently produced remarkable results, giving rise to numerous renowned duelists.

The intelligence estimated that over a hundred candidates from high-security prisons across the Ice Sculpture Kingdom would participate.

Zong Ge focused on the duelists from the Ice Prison and found three names listed, one of which was Shi Liu.

“Looks like what Yuan Ci told you might actually happen,” Zong Ge remarked to Zi Di upon seeing this.

Zi Di gave a slight nod.

Shi Liu was a crucial figure. He possessed the ability to craft the level zero secret order.

For the Wisteria Cache, both Yuan Ci and Jiu Jin sought to rescue him.

However, Shi Liu was imprisoned within the Ice Prison. This latter facility was one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's few super-maximum security prisons of a high level.

Breaking him out of prison would be an extremely difficult task.

Elder Jiu Jin understood the difficulty involved, so he backed out immediately, unwilling even to assist in gathering intelligence. He feared that if the rescue failed and the kingdom investigated, he would be implicated.

But he didn't consider that if Zi Di and Yuan Ci were captured, they might reveal the location of the Wisteria Cache to save their own lives. In that case, could he really escape?

This revealed Elder Jiu Jin's fundamental human weakness. It was precisely by exploiting this vulnerability that Elder Jiu Jin later visited the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, only to be successfully manipulated by Zi Di.

While Zi Di was at a loss regarding Shi Liu's rescue, Yuan Ci remained confident.

Two days prior, Yuan Ci approached Zi Di and shared his plan: wait for Shi Liu to leave the Ice Prison for a duel, then stage a fake death during the fight to facilitate his rescue.

This strategy completely bypassed the Ice Prison itself, after all, breaking out of this super-max facility would be prohibitively difficult.

After careful consideration, Zi Di concluded the plan held some merit.

Zong Ge also kept a close eye on developments, as he would likely need to coordinate during the operation.

“The key issue now is that Ice Prison, being a super-max facility, won't just send Shi Liu as its sole contender. Whether Shi Liu gets selected ultimately rests on the warden's decision,” Zong Ge analyzed.

Following standard protocol, each prison typically sends only one or two fighters.

Zi Di said, "Right now, Yuan Ci is actively maneuvering, shaping public opinion, contacting gambling houses, and attempting to bribe Ice Prison's management. Shi Liu's chances of being selected are high. According to Yuan Ci, at least 70%."

"For Yuan Ci, rescuing Shi Liu is imperative. So I have no doubt he'll spare no effort in this regard."

Zong Ge nodded.

He knew a great deal about this matter.

He also understood that Zi Di's mindset was entirely different from Yuan Ci's.

Zi Di's single level zero secret order held the weight of three. Combined with Fei She's and the recently subjugated Elder Jiu Jin, their five Wisteria secret orders were now assembled.

Therefore, the success or failure of Shi Liu's rescue held little consequence for Zi Di.

In fact, failure might even benefit her. It would give her more bargaining chips.

If Yuan Ci couldn't gather all five level zero secret orders, Zi Di could use that leverage in negotiations.

Zong Ge had no doubt that with Zi Di's exceptional negotiating skills, she would secure even greater advantages!

“How have you been lately?” Zi Di inquired.

She often asked such questions.

Zong Ge nodded. “Not bad.”

“I've become thoroughly familiar with Blood Vengeance and Hua Tang's battle skill.”

“My muscles have developed the reflex, even during deep slumber, I can jolt awake the instant Blood Vengeance activates and prepare my defenses.”

Zi Di's expression shifted slightly, catching the implication. “You're insane?”

“You dare sleep without any protective measures?”

Zong Ge smiled. “Rest assured, I had confidence before attempting this.”

“I'm not reckless!”

"This kind of training is precisely like that. You must place your body in peril to unlock its hidden potential."

“This is the essence of fighter training, it differs from your magician cultivation.”

Zi Di's expression darkened as she shook her head repeatedly. “I must warn you—don't take such risks.”

“You are extremely important right now.”

"Not just as an individual, you're the only gold level member of our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. And now you're also acting as the commander. If Long Fu falls, the reputation we've painstakingly built, along with the potion retail business that reputation sustains, will suffer a severe blow."

Zong Ge laughed heartily: “Don't forget, Miss Zi Di.”

“I've been afflicted by Blood Vengeance for quite some time now.”

"At first, its sudden outbreaks would injure me. But gradually, the severity of my injuries lessened, and the frequency decreased."

"Eventually, I stopped getting injured altogether. After a period of time, I only occasionally suffered injuries again."

“To this day, I've gone a very long time without being injured again.”

"My guard against the Blood Vengeance is now deeply ingrained in the deepest recesses of my mind. My body has memorized it, branded into my very nerves."

“Therefore, I have confidence in undertaking this experiment.”

Zi Di sighed: “So you've treated the Blood Vengeance as one of your training exercises.”

“No wonder you've been so indifferent to resolving it.”

“I'm starting to wonder if you're deliberately delaying it.”

Zong Ge shook his head slightly: “It's true, I did have that deliberate training intent.”

“But the Blood Vengeance... I genuinely have no way to resolve it.”

“The same goes for that mysterious battle skill used by Hua Tang.”

“But how should I put this?”

"As a magician, you don't understand a fighter's training mindset. This state I've reached is truly rare and hard to achieve."

"Confronting the Blood Vengeance has honed my ability to activate both internal and external defenses in an instant. The results of this training are truly exceptional."

"Meanwhile, countering Hua Tang's battle skill allows me to lock down my battle qi and vital blood. The few battle skills I've been practicing lately have seen rapid progress!"

“Finally, the poison within me is cultivating my resistance to toxins.”

“You needn't worry too much...”

“Enough.” Zi Di waved her hand, cutting Zong Ge off.

Through their time together, Zi Di had come to deeply understand Zong Ge's nature. He was absolutely not the type to take advice lightly!

“You must handle this matter yourself,” Zi Di sighed. “Today, I still have business to attend to at the Alchemy Guild.”

Zong Ge parted ways with Zi Di: “Then I shall continue my cultivation.”

He was a fighter, stubborn by nature. Negative states like Blood Vengeance were not torments to him, but trials and hardships, opportunities to break through his limits.

Since Hua Tang's attack, Zong Ge had lived a remarkably “peaceful” and fulfilling existence.

Though he frequently accepted challenges to duel, it was an undisputed fact that Long Fu was silver level. Thus, Zong Ge faced opponents of his own level. Being gold level himself, with formidable combat prowess, fighting opponents one level below him gave Zong Ge a significant advantage.

After about ten minutes, Zi Di stepped forward and formally entered the gates of the Alchemy Guild.

In truth, this wasn't her first time here.

This time, she navigated the place with ease. Upon entering the main hall, she turned right. She walked down a corridor and entered a small hall.

The hall was crowded.

Over a dozen staff members were handling the same task—processing registrations for the Warm Snow Cup competition.

Zi Di first retrieved the materials to review this year's Warm Snow Cup.

As the top domestic alchemy tournament of Ice Sculpture Kingdom at the highest level, the Warm Snow Cup's format had remained unchanged for many years.

The first stage consisted of alchemy examinations.

Contestants drew questions publicly and answered them on the spot to demonstrate their alchemy prowess. The examination covered diverse topics, beginning with a written test, followed by an oral interview, and culminating in a live alchemy demonstration.

This initial phase primarily served to reduce the number of participants, filtering out all but the most exceptional competitors.

The second stage comprised an alchemy exhibition.

Each contestant was assigned a display hall to showcase their alchemy achievements. At this stage, alchemy authorities from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, representatives from all social strata, and invited international guests would evaluate and score the exhibits.

The contestant with the highest average score would be crowned the champion.

Zi Di carefully reviewed the competition rules once more. Satisfied there were no issues, she filled in the name “Yao Ma” on the registration form.

Soon, her entry form reached Hua Ni's hands.

“Summon Elder Jiu Jin,” Hua Ni commanded.

Soon, Jiu Jin arrived at Hua Ni's mage tower.

In the study, Hua Ni handed him Zi Di's entry form: “Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has proposed this wager, they must have some confidence in their chances.”

“What do you intend to do?”

Elder Jiu Jin bowed his hands in respect and reported, “Magician Yao Ma is utterly ignorant of her place. This time, we shall show her the true depth of our Ice Sculpture Kingdom's alchemy heritage!”

Hua Ni immediately frowned, expressing her displeasure. “That won't do.”

“I want no accidents.”

“Arrange it so she's eliminated in the first round.”

Elder Jiu Jin's face twisted with bitterness as he looked up at Hua Ni. “Elder Hua Ni, this isn't wise.”

“If Magician Yao Ma has the confidence to compete, the first round shouldn't trouble her.”

"News of the wager contract has already begun circulating in small circles. I strongly suspect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps initiated this leak."

“It won't be long before everyone in the industry knows about it.”

“Under these circumstances, if we act too blatantly, it would be a blow to our reputation.”

Hua Ni chuckled softly. “Elder Jiu Jin, with your seasoned wisdom and caution, having such concerns proves I chose the right person.”

"But I trust you, Elder, to handle this matter. You can eliminate her while preserving our dignity, without drawing undue attention."

“This...” Elder Jiu Jin nearly wept.

He dared not attempt such a thing.

His weakness was firmly in Zi Di's grasp.

"Rest assured, Elder Jiu Jin. This year's Warm Snow Cup coincides with the national ceremony, making it vastly different from previous editions. Many will register, myself included. Eliminating an obscure silver level magician won't draw attention." With that final reassurance, Hua Ni dismissed Jiu Jin.

Elder Jiu Jin emerged from the mage tower with a troubled expression, his mind already made up: "I'll arrange the tournament schedule, nothing is too difficult! This way, Elder Hua Ni will have a satisfactory explanation."

“As for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, I'll tip them off in advance so they can prepare!”

Chapter 855: Mending

The White Hair Trench.

Charming Blue divine power formed a pale-blue light barrier, temporarily patching the gaping hole in the Ocean Mother Nest.

As time passed, new walls slowly grew where the divine light barrier met the Mother Nest's cavernous walls.

Zhou Zhang stood outside the Mother Nest, maintaining a half-kneeling posture of prayer.

Watching this scene unfold, a deep sorrow settled in her heart—the divine power expended to mend the walls was immense, a significant drain for the Charming Blue Goddess.

“Next, I must perform faster and more frequent blood sacrifices for my Lord!”

Zhou Zhang silently resolved.

Not far away, Cang Xu also observed.

“God is omnipotent!” the old magician murmured in awe.

The Ocean Mother Nest was a uniquely formed natural structure, yet under Charming Blue Goddess' power, even when shattered, it could be restored.

Meanwhile...

Cang Xu turned slightly, directing his gaze toward Teacher Si Ling nearby.

Teacher Si Ling was repairing the sacrificial formation.

Earlier, Qian Xing's battle skill attack had not only pierced through the magic formation and severed the golden altar but had also penetrated the Ocean Mother Nest.

What astonished Cang Xu was that Teacher Si Ling’s repairs extended beyond the magic runes to encompass parts inscribed with the Charming Blue divine script.

“He possesses profound mastery of the Charming Blue divine script!”

Cang Xu had initially assumed this section would require Zhou Zhang’s intervention.

“Xu Jia, come to me.” Teacher Si Ling, having completed a phase of the repairs, beckoned Cang Xu over.

Cang Xu approached as instructed.

Teacher Si Ling smiled. “Now, you can lend a hand.”

“Use the divine blessing you obtained, those unique divine spells, and cast them where I direct. This will form new divine scripts.”

Cang Xu was utterly astonished.

Jian Gui watched with an envious gaze. He understood this was yet another instance of Teacher Si Ling offering impromptu guidance, a clear sign of his genuine regard for Xu Jia.

After all, Xu Jia was only silver level, technically unqualified to repair such an formation.

Yet Teacher Si Ling preferred to expend extra effort, taking a minor detour, to mentor Xu Jia (Cang Xu).

Beyond Teacher Si Ling's personal inclination to mentor juniors, this also revealed his appreciation for Cang Xu.

Cang Xu proceeded as directed by Teacher Si Ling, casting divine spells at specific points within the formation.

Divine spell - Soul Link.

Divine spell - Spiritual Navigation.

Divine spell - Synchronized Conversion.

Each distinct divine spell, upon landing within the formation, condensed varying amounts of divine script.

Teacher Si Ling carefully selected these divine scripts, arranging them in specific positions.

Cang Xu had once studied this grand formation surrounding the altar. The more he observed, the more astonished he became, prompting him to blurt out, “Teacher Si Ling, are you modifying this formation?”

Teacher Si Ling smiled and nodded. “Correct. You've noticed.”

“It seems you've paid close attention to this formation in your daily work.”

“That's commendable. A thirst for knowledge is an excellent quality for a magician.”

“My involvement in constructing this formation was several years ago.”

“People will always grow.”

“Now, I am fully capable of refining this formation to perform even better. Efficiency could be increased by at least 7.8%.”

Cang Xu inquired again, "Teacher Si Ling, I have one more small question. Why do you seem so familiar with these unique divine spells of mine?"

Teacher Si Ling smiled faintly.

Cang Xu's question was precisely what he wished to hear.

Without delay, Teacher Si Ling replied, "Well then, what do you think? Xu Jia."

Cang Xu had long harbored suspicions. His expression now flickered with hesitation. “Could it be that these unique divine spells are actually yours...”

“Yes.” Teacher Si Ling nodded, a faint smile playing on his lips. “These unique divine spells were originally invented and created by me.”

“This is one aspect of our collaboration between the undead orthodoxy and the Charming Blue Church.”

"To pioneer these divine spells is actually extremely difficult. Charming Blue Goddess has slumbered for years, sustaining the sect solely through the Godhead to answer believers' prayers. Creating divine spells was impossible."

“My success owes little to personal skill, it is largely due to the artifact the Truth Slate.”

His suspicions were confirmed. Cang Xu still felt a jolt of awe. “Truly worthy of your stature, Teacher Si Ling. Not only did you pioneer necromancy, but you even created divine spells based on the Charming Blue divine script!"

Teacher Si Ling waved his hand, his smile growing more serene. “Is this not precisely what we magicians ought to do?”

“We study the heavens and earth, we study the gods, we pioneer spell after spell, expanding the boundaries of our capabilities.”

“This is the very essence of what defines us as magicians.”

His meaning was clear.

Cang Xu's eyes flickered as he instantly understood: Teacher Si Ling was once again using this opportunity to teach him, to demonstrate through words and deeds that magicians should not worship deities.

Everything was worth studying. Every substance could be better harnessed. Every force could be leveraged.

All it took was a fulcrum.

“I've found the pivotal lever to pry open the divine domain!” Within the Honey Snow Tower, Shuang Lian's eyes blazed with brilliance, her face radiant with excitement.

Though the strike force she dispatched to raid Tranquil Hill had been utterly annihilated, her tactical objective had been achieved.

Through on-site reconnaissance, she had obtained vast amounts of data on Tranquil Hill.

She had spent considerable time analyzing these data, gradually accumulating insights until today, when a qualitative breakthrough finally occurred!

Bu Quan, who had been assisting Shuang Lian all along, looked at the data with confusion and immediately asked, “Teacher, these data are too strange. They seem to indicate that this hill possesses consciousness.”

Shuang Lian smiled faintly, a hint of satisfaction in her expression. “You can discern this much, that's quite good.”

“You lack so much knowledge, though it's not your fault. Knowledge related to deities requires an extremely high cost.”

Shuang Lian pointed at the data: “These figures indicate a computational core within this hill, similar to a tower spirit.”

“Of course, its actual computing power far surpasses that of a tower spirit, by an immense margin.”

“Its true name is—Godhead.”

Bu Quan: “God... Godhead?”

While master and disciple discussed Tranquil Hill, outside the mage tower, duelists were discussing them too.

Xue Tuan Zi gazed at the rapidly shrinking fissure in the earth, marveling, “No wonder it's derived from the spell, Great Pyrolysis, Ice Breaker is truly formidable!”

“Even now, though Tranquil Hill Mountain has been restored, the divine domain's land still bears immense fissures.”

While others might struggle to gather intel on Ice Breaker, for Xue Tuan Zi it was effortless.

Not only was she an Alchemy Guild member, but her grandfather, the Snow Cake Tower Master, was a powerful elder within the guild.

“I wish I could have witnessed the Ice Breaker in action. It's a shame I wasn't there,” Xue Tuan Zi lamented.

“If our Lord were truly whole, these fissures wouldn't still linger,” remarked He Gai Tou from the other side.

Having endured so much alongside the Dueling God, these duelists had formed numerous plausible theories about him.

Xue Tuan Zi fell silent.

Such matters weren't convenient to discuss in depth.

Just then, a gold level duelist soared in.

It was Gu Dong.

He had come to take over from He Gai Tou.

Ever since the last raid on Tranquil Hill, the Dueling Divine Domain had maintained only two gold level members.

“You finally made it,” He Gai Tou murmured with a hint of reproach upon seeing Gu Dong.

Gu Dong apologized, “Something came up unexpectedly and delayed me.”

He Gai Tou sighed, “I told you, I have a meeting with the Ice Prison's higher-ups today.”

Xue Tuan Zi asked curiously, "Why are you contacting them? Did you place a hefty wager on this Grand Duel?"

He Gai Tou shook his head slightly. "Long Fu is still on his winning streak, showing no signs of slowing down. This won't do!"

“I've spotted someone in the Ice Prison.”

“I believe he can pose a genuine threat to Long Fu.”

“I agree with Lord Mei Lin's arrangements. This time, let me expedite the process a bit.”

Chapter 856: Ice Prison

He Gai Tou descended the ice ladder, gradually plunging deeper underground.

The surrounding walls were constructed from massive ice bricks. Along the way, alchemy torches burned with white ice flames, emitting wispy cold air.

This was Ice Prison.

The path He Gai Tou walked led only to the warden's chamber.

He knew this environment intimately. Yet this familiarity brought him no warmth.

Even with the Ice Heart Headband, a chill stirred within him.

He Gai Tou knew that though he had left the Ice Prison years ago, it had left a profound psychological trauma upon him. That trauma had never truly faded.

He had once been a prisoner within these very walls.

“Ah ha ha! Old friend, it's been a while!” The warden stood at the doorway; arms raised in an enthusiastic welcome.

Unusually, the warden of the Ice Prison was not a snow elf, but a human.

He was a gold level magician.

The Ice Prison was the top-level penitentiary in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Having a human serve as its warden was exceptionally rare within the kingdom's political landscape.

"Lord Prison Warden." He Gai Tou bowed slightly towards the warden.

The warden feigned displeasure: “Still so formal. Just call me by name.”

With that, he ushered He Gai Tou inside, his face alight with a warm smile.

He Gai Tou understood well. The warden's warmth and smile did not reflect his true disposition.

Perhaps this was the difference between humans and elves.

For He Gai Tou, he actually preferred dealing with the snow elves. Their businesslike manner and cool demeanor felt more genuine than the smiles of humans.

For humans, smiles were often masks concealing worldly desires.

Just like this time.

The Ice Prison Warden greeted He Gai Tou with precisely such desires.

The warden's chamber was not spacious.

He Gai Tou knew it hadn't always been this way.

After taking office, the current Ice Prison Warden had vigorously cut expenses, leading by example. The warden's chamber had consequently been reduced in size, its former space-expanding magic formation dismantled.

He Gai Tou understood, however, that this was merely a political performance by the Ice Prison Warden. In truth, he secretly pocketed more than any of his predecessors.

The largest sum the Ice Prison Warden truly embezzled came from He Gai Tou himself.

Though the warden's office was small, the desk was enormous, almost bed-sized, dominating the largest space within.

Separated by this massive desk, the two men took their seats.

The seating arrangement here was meticulously planned.

The Ice Prison Warden's seat was noticeably higher. While humans were generally shorter than elves, once He Gai Tou sat down, the difference in seat height made him appear a full head shorter than the warden across from him.

This naturally gave the Ice Prison Warden a condescending angle of gaze.

The warden gazed at He Gai Tou with a smile, his tone tinged with nostalgia. “It's been over twenty years since you left this place.”

“Do you recall that time? I was merely a deputy warden back then.”

“It was thanks to you that we secured the championship in that National Ceremony Grand Duel.” That achievement paved the way for my victory in the subsequent election, ultimately elevating me to warden of this prison."

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a policy: during the National Ceremony Grand Duel, prisons across the kingdom would nominate prisoners to compete.

Prisoners could receive sentence reductions based on their performance.

He Gai Tou was a beneficiary of this policy. After winning the championship, the kingdom granted him a full pardon.

Of course, this was also because his original crime wasn't that severe.

Despite the warden's polite tone and grateful demeanor, He Gai Tou knew this was a deliberate hint.

The warden's words carried at least two layers of meaning.

It wasn't merely an expression of kindness but also held hidden expectations.

So, He Gai Tou responded.

“Back then, it was all thanks to you, Warden, that I was pardoned and regained my freedom.”

"I have never forgotten this kindness. All these years, I have been seeking ways to repay it. Regrettably, your stature far surpasses mine, so I can only offer modest tokens of gratitude."

Each year, He Gai Tou delivered at least 3,000 gold coins to the Ice Prison Warden.

“This visit to discuss business with you, Lord, concerns a particularly lucrative venture.”

The warden's expression immediately sharpened with interest.

He leaned back in his chair, eyes narrowing as he studied He Gai Tou. “Oh? Tell me more.”

He Gai Tou: “I know there's currently a prisoner in the Ice Prison named Bing Yang.”

"This man was once a gold level, but he fell victim to the potion [Beautiful Future]. He's now dropped to the silver level."

“I want him to participate in this year's National Ceremony Grand Duel. Have him challenge someone.”

The warden immediately frowned.

“Hold on.”

“You know that despite being the nation's top prison, the Ice Prison only gets one recommendation slot.”

“Bing Yang is a sickly weakling. He can't fight.” “Even if he could, he's only at the silver level now.”

“I know he was once gold level. He might stand out among silver level fighters.”

“But it doesn't matter!”

“In every National Ceremony Grand Duel, the gold level fighters always dominate the finals.”

"Silver level fighters are relegated to the sidelines. No matter how spectacular their fights, they never make it to the later rounds."

“I'll be honest with you; I'm counting on this year's National Ceremony Grand Duel for my achievements.”

"Our current candidate is Shi Liu. He's a magnetic earth elemental who has consistently dominated our internal sparring sessions. I hold great promise for him."

“While his strength may not surpass Long Fu, he absolutely possesses the capability to secure a top five finish.”

He Gai Tou smiled. “Warden, please hear me out.”

“You still don't know who I want Bing Yang to challenge.”

The Ice Prison Warden: “Who?”

“Long Fu,” He Gai Tou replied.

“Him?” The Ice Prison Warden's eyebrows were raised.

Of course he'd heard of Long Fu.

More than once.

The dragonman youngster's fame spread far and wide. His feat of defeating and slaying a gold level opponent while only silver level was a rarity unseen in a century.

And since arriving in the capital, Long Fu impersonated by Zong Ge had accepted numerous duel challenges, winning every single one without a single loss.

While these victories weren't as spectacular as Teng Dong Lang's legendary battle, they solidified his reputation, earning him considerable attention, support, and admiration.

The Ice Prison Warden knew even more than that.

He also learned that the mount potions sold by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were incredibly popular. Everyone who used them raved about them!

He had even used them on his own mounts and was deeply impressed by the dragon rider potion.

Of course, that gold level dragon rider potion had been a gift from someone else.

When He Gai Tou mentioned Long Fu, a flash of understanding lit up the Ice Prison Warden's eyes.

He remarked, “Long Fu possesses formidable combat prowess, standing alone at the pinnacle of silver level fighters. The outside world widely considers him the ceiling of silver level strength. At least in this National Ceremony Grand Duel, no strong contender to challenge Long Fu has emerged yet.”

"If I recall correctly, his current winning streak has reached 17 matches, hasn't it?

At this rate, his streak will only continue to grow. Reaching a hundred victories wouldn't surprise anyone."

"This is mainly because no gold level duelist has challenged him yet. In fact, such a challenge might never occur. Unless Long Fu himself takes the initiative to challenge a gold level duelist."

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom has a deeply ingrained dueling culture, where courage and fairness are highly revered by its citizens.”

"Therefore, it's virtually unheard of for a gold level fighter to challenge someone below their level. It would be utterly disgraceful. Within the silver level, Long Fu faces almost no threat. From this perspective, his silver level status is a massive advantage."

“Unless he initiates the challenge, a gold level must accept before a fight can occur between them.”

Of course, other factors, like immense profit, personal vendettas, or national grievances, might prompt a gold level to challenge Long Fu. This was not a normal situation.

Such a gold level would naturally be looked down upon by others and not welcomed.

"If other gold level fighters genuinely issue him a duel challenge, Long Fu can outright refuse. He wouldn't be implicated, and the public wouldn't be overly disappointed. After all, a silver level rejecting a gold level's challenge is perfectly normal."

"In this scenario, Bing Yang becomes exceptionally valuable. Should Bing Yang challenge Long Fu, the situation would be entirely different."

“Bing Yang was once gold level, but has now fallen to silver level, this is an indisputable fact.”

“Precisely because he was once gold level, Bing Yang's combat prowess among silver level fighters should be in a league of its own.”

“He could absolutely pose a threat to Long Fu!”

"If Bing Yang issues a duel invitation, Long Fu will almost certainly accept. Simply because Bing Yang is now silver level. Once such a duel is arranged, it will immediately draw intense attention. Given Long Fu's reputation, spectators following this duel will span the entire nation!"

Having analyzed this far, the Ice Prison Warden looked at He Gai Tou: “Over the years, your gambling house's business has grown increasingly larger.”

"So, you intend to use this to make a major move. You plan to orchestrate and manipulate a nationwide gambling scheme?"

Though phrased as a question, the warden's tone and demeanor radiated certainty.

He believed he had seen through He Gai Tou's scheme.

“Correct.” He Gai Tou made no attempt to conceal it, nodding immediately in direct acknowledgment.

This was indeed one of He Gai Tou's objectives.

Much like the duelist Mi Fang, who targeted Zong Ge to undermine the Jing Xiang Clan's potion business and eliminate a commercial rival. He Gai Tou's motives were equally complex.

On one hand, as the Ice Prison Warden had discerned, he sought to orchestrate a gambling scheme for personal gain. On the other hand, as a duelist, eliminating a major enemy was inherently advantageous to him.

The duelists sought to reclaim the bodies of the Axe Gang leader, Jia Bing, and Lin.

According to their speculation, these gold level corpses wouldn't be easily abandoned by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps; they should still be in their possession.

Chapter 857: Bing Yang

Reclaiming corpses, strengthening Tranquil Hill, and contributing to the cause of the Dueling God they worshipped, this was the shared conviction among duelists.

There was another benefit that He Gai Tou valued greatly.

It was that through this matter, he could curry favor with Mei Lin.

He Gai Tou desired closeness with Mei Lin.

This wasn't born of romantic affection, but rather his inherent shrewdness and skill in maneuvering.

He Gai Tou understood that among duelists, those with military backgrounds formed the largest faction, and he sought to integrate into it.

But he wasn't from the military. On the contrary, he was a prisoner.

At least he had been.

Thus, he naturally lacked the trust of Mei Lin and others.

He Gai Tou's bloodline potential had been fully tapped. He could only reach gold level; advancement beyond that was impossible.

Unable to strengthen himself, he channeled his energy into cultivating connections.

Though gambling wasn't banned in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, his line of work still fell within the gray economy. Once operations grew too large, they risked crackdowns. They might even be targeted and absorbed by major nobles.

What He Gai Tou truly coveted was Mei Lin's military connections.

He sought to climb higher, finding a powerful protector.

This was why He Gai Tou annually delivered gold coins to the prison warden before him.

Leaning back in his leather chair at a slight angle, the warden opened a drawer and retrieved a metal box.

He placed the box on the desk.

The lid sprang open automatically, revealing neatly arranged cigars inside.

Each cigar was only as thick as an adult's finger, slightly slimmer than standard market cigars, a product unique to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

“Care for one?”

“I don't smoke,” He Gai Tou declined.

No lighter was needed. The warden placed the cigar directly in his mouth. After a few puffs, it ignited itself.

These specially crafted cigars were magic dishes in themselves.

Cigar production represented a unique branch within the culinary arts of magic.

The Ice Prison Warden drew in a breath, exhaling a puff of frost before continuing their earlier discussion: “If we form this partnership, and Long Fu accepts Bing Yang's challenge, who would you bet on?”

He Gai Tou answered without hesitation: “Long Fu, of course.”

“Even if Bing Yang was once a gold level, his combat strength still falls short compared to Long Fu.”

The Ice Prison Warden nodded slightly, acknowledging He Gai Tou's point.

He Gai Tou continued, “In fact, this collaboration doesn't conflict with your plan to earn merit, Lord Warden.”

“Bing Yang has consumed excessive amounts of the [Beautiful Future] magic potion. He's already nearing death.”

“Even if he's willing to downgrade his level to survive, the potion's effects remain overwhelmingly potent.”

“Once you approve his nomination, I'll aggressively promote him, shape public opinion, and rapidly inflate his odds.”

At this point, He Gai Tou adjusted his posture. “There's significant potential here.”

"When Bing Yang falls in battle, per national policy, you, the warden, can nominate Shi Liu as his replacement. As a gold level powerhouse, he could still vie for the ultimate crown."

The Ice Prison Warden did not respond, instead taking a deep puff of his cigar.

He parted his lips slightly, the mist from his mouth flowing into his nostrils and then into his lungs, circulating so swiftly it brought him a refreshingly clear sense of relief.

Only after a dozen seconds did he exhale the smoke.

“If I get involved this time, how much could I earn?”

"Hard to estimate precisely. But I'd say at least this much."

He Gai Tou held up his fingers, forming the number eight.

A sharp gleam flashed in the Ice Prison Warden's eyes. "Only eighty thousand gold coins? Hmph. This is a nationwide gamble. That's far too little for me."

He Gai Tou shook his head with a bitter smile. “This is already substantial.”

"Lord Warden, you may be overestimating me. Though I own a gambling house, it's not large. How many years have I truly been involved in this business?"

“My Lord, you know the truth.”

"Had you partnered with me years ago, the gambling house would certainly not be its current size. If we'd done this deal back then, our profits would've at least doubled!"

He Gai Tou shook his head. “But now, this current betting pool is already my limit.”

“Though it's nationwide, constrained by my gambling house, the scale of bets we receive won't be huge.”

The warden snorted coldly, narrowing his eyes. For a moment, he focused only on smoking, saying nothing.

He puffed on his cigarette while deep in thought.

In truth, he participated in the gambling events He Gai Tou organized every year.

Of course, he placed his bets.

Over the years, he's won more than he's lost. That was how He Gai Tou managed to funnel him 3,000 gold pieces annually on average.

As the house, He Gai Tou naturally had some leeway to manipulate the outcomes of the games.

To placate investigations from the royal family and others, he had staged numerous losing rounds. Moreover, since the Ice Prison Warden funded his own bets, any winnings were purely a testament to his skill. This had nothing to do with bribery.

He Gai Tou had long sought to curry favor with the Ice Prison Warden, but over the past two decades, his repeated overtures had been consistently rejected.

The warden understood clearly: participating in He Gai Tou's gambling operations and serving as his patron were entirely different matters.

The warden possessed self-awareness: “I am a human occupying a high position.”

“The role of Ice Prison Warden is a lucrative post.”

“Countless snow elves eye my seat.”

"Truthfully, accepting He Gai Tou's annual gold coins is already pushing the limit. Do you really think the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's higher-ups can't see through this?"

“Hahaha, as if!”

“It's just that all these actions stay within the game's rules.”

In short, the Ice Prison Warden would never engage in deep cooperation with He Gai Tou.

He smoked half his cigar before declaring, “Eighty thousand is too low. I want at least one hundred twenty thousand.”

“With a gold level powerhouse clearly residing in the Ice Prison, recommending a mere silver level candidate is untenable. It could even trigger an investigation.”

“Moreover, what if Bing Yang wins? My investment would go down the drain, and I'd bear all the risk personally."

He Gai Tou snorted coldly inside.

He could tell the Ice Prison Warden was tempted, but he was making excuses to drive up the price.

What investigation? With the network he'd built in the Kingdom, that was practically impossible.

Besides, it was entirely possible to explain himself.

In the Ice Prison's history, they had indeed fielded prisoners of the silver level before. Back then, the gold level prisoners were relatively weak and lacked competitiveness in the Grand Duel. They settled for the next best option, enlisting skilled silver level fighters, and achieved quite respectable results.

He Gai Tou knew the warden was trying to drive up the price. He immediately put on a smile and engaged in a round of bargaining with him.

Ultimately, He Gai Tou held firm at 100,000 gold coins, refusing to budge an inch.

“Very well then,” the warden exhaled smoke from his cigar. “For your sake, 100,000 gold it is.”

He knew when to stop.

After all, they were both gold levels. He Gai Tou even gave him gold coins yearly, so they had formed a relationship.

“I can recommend Bing Yang, but you'll have to persuade him personally.”

“I must warn you; he might not agree to fight.”

“He consumed massive quantities of the potion [Beautiful Future]. To survive, he'd even accept demotion to a lower level.”

“His imprisonment here stems from his own decision, after securing connections, he deliberately committed a crime and turned himself in. His primary purpose in entering the prison was to utilize its magic formation to suppress the potion's effects and extend his lifespan."

“And now you want him to walk out and participate in a duel?”

“Heh, it won't be that easy.”

On this point, He Gai Tou was brimming with confidence.

"Rest assured, Lord, I've made thorough preparations. Time is limited. Let's proceed now. You'll witness firsthand how I persuade him."

“Very well. Follow me.” The Ice Prison Warden rose to his feet.

He led He Gai Tou deep into the third underground level, stopping before a cell door.

A guard respectfully opened the cell door.

The Ice Prison Warden, He Gai Tou, and the others entered one after another.

The cell was compact yet lavishly furnished.

Bing Yang was dining.

Though the table was small, it was laden with exquisite magic delicacies, exquisitely presented and fragrant.

This felt less like imprisonment and more like a vacation.

After all, Bing Yang had once been a gold level, retaining his former connections and substantial wealth.

A significant portion of his assets came from compensation paid by the Alchemy Guild.

He was a victim of the potion known as the [Beautiful Future]. The incident had caused a massive uproar at the time, stirring nationwide controversy.

Upon seeing the warden and He Gai Tou, Bing Yang dared not act presumptuously. He promptly rose to his feet and bowed in respect.

After all, he was no longer a gold level individual.

"Lord Warden. Oh, and Lord He Gai Tou. I've been hearing a lot about you lately from fellow inmates and guards alike."

He Gai Tou was quite renowned, the most formidable fighter to emerge from the Ice Prison in recent years. Whenever the Grand Duel commenced, he became the talk of the entire prison, the subject of endless discussion.

He Gai Tou appraised the figure before him.

Bing Yang was a snow elf, handsome, slender, and undeniably frail. His face was deeply wrinkled, his eyes dim. Even standing felt like an effort, his movements tremulous, like an old man with little time left.

When he spoke, his teeth were visible.

He Gai Tou noticed immediately that many of Bing Yang's teeth were missing. Though his mouth appeared full, most were alchemy crafted false teeth.

The Ice Prison Warden nodded slightly. “It is he, He Gai Tou, who is looking for you.”

Bing Yang turned to He Gai Tou, curiosity evident in his expression.

Instead of stating his purpose directly, He Gai Tou turned to the small dining table. "The food is excellent, but the wine leaves something to be desired. As it happens, I brought some of my own. Have a taste."

With that, he drew a small metal flask from his robe.

He uncorked it and poured its entire contents into an empty glass stemmed cup.

The quantity was meager to begin with, leaving only a thin layer coating the bottom of the glass once emptied.

It was red wine, radiating a gold level aura.

He Gai Tou handed the glass to Bing Yang. “Drink up. It suits you perfectly.”

Chapter 858: Beautiful Future

Bing Yang glanced at the wine glass, then at He Gai Tou, before reaching out to take the glass.

He wasn't overly concerned that He Gai Tou might harm him. After all, they had no grudge against each other, and Bing Yang hadn't entered the Ice Prison to evade enemies.

He was here to prolong his life.

“The likelihood of He Gai Tou poisoning me is extremely low, since the Ice Prison warden is right here.”

After pondering, Bing Yang felt a sense of understanding.

He examined the wine in the glass, which held only a thin layer.

Bing Yang: "My current miserable state is entirely due to the magic potion [Beautiful Future]. To resolve this dilemma, a mere gold level potion is utterly insufficient. I've attempted it countless times. At the very least, a saint level potion is required."

“Potions won't do, and this magical dish is even less likely to work.”

At this point, Bing Yang couldn't help but sigh deeply.

To survive and solve the problems plaguing him, he had tried countless things. His final hope lay in a saint level potion.

But obtaining such a potion was exceedingly difficult. Even when Bing Yang was at the gold level, he had failed.

To concoct a saint level potion, one must be a saint level alchemist, and that alchemist must possess profound alchemy expertise.

Already rare, a saint level magician must also possess profound alchemy expertise, a challenge compounded by impossibility.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild held formidable power yet lacked a single saint level alchemist.

Among the kingdom's three saint level figures, the Royal Grand Magician existed, but he was not skilled in alchemy.

In truth, once one reached the saint level, market offerings became far scarcer. Unlike gold level items, which could still be somewhat widely supplied as high-end goods, saint level items circulating in the market were extremely rare, though at least still visible.

Legend level items, however, were nearly extinct.

When dealing with legend level transactions, due to their extreme rarity and high value, they often reverted to the primitive method of barter—exchanging goods for goods.

He Gai Tou remained silent, urging Bing Yang only with his gaze. For he knew: no amount of words could compare to the effect of Bing Yang taking a sip.

Bing Yang studied the red wine in his glass, then cautiously, hesitantly, took a sip.

Instantly, his brow furrowed deeply.

The taste was too sharp; it was like drinking blood!

The rich, bloody scent filled his entire mouth, even seeping into his bloodstream.

But in the next instant, Bing Yang's frown smoothed out, his eyes sparkling.
