As if the earlier taste had been an illusion, the mouthful of blood suddenly transformed into the aroma of wine. The rich wine fragrance enveloped him like a bath, drenching him from head to toe.

Every inch of his skin, every pore, relaxed and reveled in the sensation.

“What fine wine!” he exclaimed.

Then, the praise on his face turned to shock.

He instinctively raised his right hand, pressing his palm against his chest. Head bowed, he stared at his own torso in disbelief: "How is this possible?"

“The effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion are actually dissipating!?”

The next instant, he jerked his head up as if electrocuted, staring intently at He Gai Tou and shouting excitedly, “Quick! Quick! Tell me, what kind of wine is this?”

He Gai Tou replied, "Sorrow-dispelling red wine. It's a fine wine from the Sheng Ming Empire. Though only of gold level, its ingredients are exceptionally premium, one being divine blood."

“Divine blood?” Everyone present was shaken.

"Indeed. And it cannot be just any divine blood; it must be the blood of the Wine God. Only with this divine blood can the sorrow-dispelling red wine be crafted."

“It is precisely because of this that it possesses a unique efficacy: the dissolution of transcendent powers.”

“Both permanent spells and divine spells lose their effects under the influence of sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

“The potion you consumed, [Beautiful Future], would be no exception.”

Bing Yang stared blankly at the wine glass once more: “Divine blood... sorrow-dispelling red wine...”

Then, he sighed and asked, “So, Lord He Gai Tou, what do you want me to do?”

He Gai Tou stated bluntly, “Kill someone.”

“Who?”

“Long Fu.”

“Him?” Bing Yang frowned again.

Though confined within the Ice Prison, his senses remained unobstructed. On the contrary, he knew much, his intelligence network exceptionally sharp.

"Long Fu slew Teng Dong Lang, a silver level slaying a gold level. His combat prowess is extraordinary. Unless I regain my gold level status, I wouldn't dare challenge him." Bing Yang stated.

He hadn't refused outright; his words were merely bargaining points.

He Gai Tou shook his head. “If a gold level challenger approached him, he'd likely refuse.”

“It's precisely because you've fallen to silver level yet retain some gold level strength that I sought you out.”

Bing Yang's eyes flickered as hesitation took hold.

He Gai Tou smiled, understanding his thoughts. “The sorrow-dispelling red wine is extremely precious. Though only of gold level, its ingredients are exceptionally high-grade.”

“To be honest, this wine originates from the Intoxication Church.”

Truthfully, this wasn't something He Gai Tou could obtain on his own.

He acquired it through Mei Lin's connections.

The entire haul amounted to just this single sip of wine, an incredibly precious drop!

"The Intoxication Church? The Wine God of the Sheng Ming Empire? I hear Drunkard Harbor has become this deity's parish?"

“That's right. For this National Ceremony Grand Duel, the Intoxication Church is making a major push to spread their faith. They're heavily sponsoring the event, providing two thousand tons of wine free to the capital's residents. They're also involved in the national banquet. This sorrow-dispelling red wine is the wine served there, extremely rare. I only managed to get some samples by pure luck. You want to buy it? It's practically impossible!"

Bing Yang's expression faltered, his illusions shattered.

He Gai Tou chuckled softly and added, “Of course, you're welcome to try your luck later.”

Bing Yang nodded. “I will.”

Deep down, he already believed He Gai Tou's words.

This left him with no choice but to rely on He Gai Tou as his sole channel.

Even after the National Ceremony Grand Duel, approaching the Intoxication Church would prove extremely difficult. After all, the primary ingredient was the blood of the Wine God. That fact alone ensured the quantity of this sorrow-dispelling red wine would be exceedingly scarce.

It wasn't about excessive consumption of divine blood. It was obvious the Wine God's blood must be diluted many times over before being used in the wine's production.

The crucial issue was faith.

The Intoxication Church wasn't like the Alchemy Guild or a regular merchant alliance; it was a church.

They worshiped the Wine God, how could any believer drink the blood of their object of worship?

Such an act would be grave sacrilege!

This wine was destined to be a scarce commodity. Selling too much of it would undermine faith in the Wine God.

“Under these circumstances, I realize there's little left to negotiate,” Bing Yang quickly grasped the true situation in his heart.

He responded decisively, agreeing directly to He Gai Tou's terms.

He Gai Tou clapped his hands with a laugh: “Excellent!”

Then, turning to the Ice Prison Warden, he said: “I kindly request the warden to clear a space so I may personally assess Bing Yang's actual combat prowess.”

“After this personal evaluation, I shall also prepare equipment suited to him.”

With a hundred thousand gold coins at stake, the Ice Prison Warden naturally cooperated fully.

Within the Ice Prison lay a small duel arena, where daily disputes between prisoners and guards were settled.

This alone revealed how prevalent dueling was in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Soon, the group arrived at the arena.

Bing Yang, rubbing his hands together eagerly, strode to the center of the field.

He Gai Tou stood solemnly, ready for battle.

The Ice Prison Warden sat by the wall, observing.

The two commenced their battle.

In an instant, battle qi collided, figures flew through the air. Exchanges came thick and fast, the clashes thunderous.

The combat prowess Bing Yang displayed was indeed formidable, far surpassing silver level.

Moreover, his tactics were fully matured, battle qi technique and battle skills perfectly coordinated. His combat experience was profound, his battle skills and martial arts deeply ingrained, as if carved into his very bones.

Yet flaws remained, notably issues with his combat rhythm.

His attacks often lacked fluidity, abruptly halting mid-strike.

All three observers understood the reason.

Bing Yang had fallen from gold level to silver level, and many gold level battle skills became unusable at the silver level.

The crucial factor lies in the nature of battle qi.

Gold level battle qi could be condensed from nothingness, which was why many gold level battle skills were developed.

The most classic example were flight battle skills.

The principle behind the vast majority of flight battle skills relied on gold level battle skill to propel the fighter themselves through displacement.

Silver level battle qi lacks the properties of gold level, rendering certain battle skills ineffective.

Once He Gai Tou began applying pressure, the previous stalemate shattered, and Bing Yang found himself at a disadvantage.

The earlier back-and-forth exchanges had been possible mainly because He Gai Tou had deliberately held back, prioritizing defense to thoroughly assess Bing Yang's actual combat prowess in real combat.

After personally observing for a while, He Gai Tou had a clear understanding.

“Bing Yang possesses strength, but after his demotion, he hasn't focused on combat power.”

“He maintained a certain level of training, but he hasn't adjusted his battle skill or tactics.”

“However, all of this can be changed.”

"For the battle skill he's familiar with, we can try to find a silver level substitute. If none are available, equipment can compensate."

After several more exchanges, He Gai Tou gradually intensified his assault, completely overwhelming Bing Yang who was now thoroughly flustered.

“Any more moves? Use them all,” He Gai Tou urged. “Only then can I provide the most suitable equipment to offer maximum assistance.”

Bing Yang hesitated briefly before growling, “Then please be extremely cautious, Lord He Gai Tou. This move might very well injure you.”

This statement instantly piqued the interest of both He Gai Tou and the Ice Prison Warden.

The next instant, Bing Yang took a small step back, then slammed his chest, activating the alchemy brooch there while unleashing his battle skill—Frost Aura!

Though originally a gold level battle skill, Bing Yang could barely manage it with the alchemy brooch's aid.

Instantly, a halo radiated outward from him at breakneck speed.

"This is...!" The Ice Prison Warden's pupils contracted sharply.

“Oh no! Dodge!” He Gai Tou also sensed danger, showing no intention to block as he blasted backward.

Chapter 859: Accepting the Challenge

Bing Yang didn't pursue, instead remaining where he stood.

After creating some distance, He Gai Tou halted, a look of astonishment on his face as he stared at the three-meter radius circle of light. “Your Frost Aura actually intertwined with the effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion?”

“That's right. It was an accidental discovery,” Bing Yang replied.

Both He Gai Tou and the Ice Prison Warden fell silent. Their eyes flickered as they stared at the aura, their expressions as if avoiding venomous snakes!

“This time, Long Fu is in serious trouble!”

“It's entirely possible he could lose to Bing Yang.”

He Gai Tou felt a surge of excitement, while the Ice Prison Warden's face clouded with worry.

He Gai Tou voluntarily halted the battle, deeply wary of this mutated Frost Aura.

“Alright, I have a rough idea now. I know what kind of gear suits you.”

“What about your own gear?”

Bing Yang answered honestly: “Most of it's been sold off.”

Tormented by the effects of the magic potion, he sensed his days were numbered. After all attempts to prolong his life failed, he deliberately committed a crime. Using his connections, he arranged to be imprisoned within the Ice Prison.

Though the prison's environment temporarily suppressed the potion's effects, he had already sunk into despair and resignation.

To savor the last beautiful moments of his life, he not only sold off his gold level gear but also gradually liquidated some assets. With the money, he purchased luxury items, like magic delicacies, to indulge in.

He Gai Tou nodded, learned about Bing Yang's battle skills, and then took his leave.

The Ice Prison warden escorted him all the way out. Before parting, a deep look of worry crossed his face: “I'm truly worried.”

“The Frost Aura itself isn't terrifying. What's terrifying is that it mutated due to the [Beautiful Future] potion, gaining the power of time itself.”

“If Long Fu loses, won't our bets go down the drain?”

He Gai Tou thought: “Wouldn't it be best if Bing Yang could eliminate Long Fu outright?”

He desperately wished for that outcome.

But for now, he couldn't be candid with the warden.

So, he reassured him: "No, in my view, Long Fu still holds the greater strength. Even with Bing Yang's trump card, Long Fu has the better odds of winning."

The Ice Prison Warden dismissed this, shaking his head. "I don't believe it! I've seen the magic recordings myself. I know exactly how Long Fu defeated Teng Dong Lang."

He Gai Tou pondered briefly before offering a counterargument, “Then you must not have witnessed how Long Fu repelled Hua Tang.”

The Ice Prison Warden froze. "I've heard rumors about that. But whether a notorious thief like Hua Tang is real or fake, who can say for sure?"

He Gai Tou produced a magic crystal ball, small, no bigger than a baby's fist.

“This contains the footage of Hua Tang and Long Fu clashing.”

“Someone captured it by accident.”

“My Lord, after viewing this, you'll understand my confidence in Long Fu.”

This footage, of course, had been secretly recorded by agents deployed by Mei Lin.

It captured the battle from every angle.

Mei Lin was an exceptional commander who understood the critical importance of military intelligence. Thus, during Hua Tang's operation, she had deliberately begun gathering firsthand intelligence.

The one He Gai Tou presented was merely one of several copies.

The Ice Prison Warden immediately reviewed the magic footage, his expression quickly shifting.

Though the battle was brief, Hua Tang's combat prowess was unmistakably of gold level. Yet “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) performed exceptionally well, nearly matching him blow for blow.

“How is Long Fu so powerful?” the Ice Prison Warden wondered aloud.

He Gai Tou smiled faintly, deliberately lowering his voice as he voiced his conjecture: “You know the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has some deep connections, right?”

“Long Fu's strength isn't that straightforward either.”

“I'm inclined to believe he deliberately feigned weakness, only ‘barely managing’ to defeat Teng Dong Lang.”

“I even doubt whether he's truly silver level.”

“After all, our nation's dueling rules only prohibit outside assistance. If a duelist disguises their level so others can't tell, that's actually a skill in itself, not a violation at all.”

The Ice Prison Warden suddenly understood: “So that's how it is!”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is suspected of arms smuggling. To secure a market, they naturally need a reputation.”

“If a gold level fighter pretends to be silver level and duels, creating the stunning feat of a silver defeating a gold level, they'll become famous nationwide in no time!”

“What a brilliant plan... No wonder Teng Dong Lang's defeat in the final blow seemed so abrupt.”

Seeing the warden's reaction, He Gai Tou added, “Rest assured. As the bookmaker controlling the betting pool, do you think I'd want to make a loss?”

The Ice Prison Warden was finally completely convinced.

He Gai Tou also possessed exceptional initiative. Once Bing Yang's participation was confirmed, he immediately ordered the news to be spread while simultaneously leveraging channels like Mei Lin to procure equipment and battle skills for Bing Yang.

With duelists secretly aiding him, the relevant information quickly spread.

"Have you heard? Bing Yang has issued a duel challenge to Long Fu!”

"Who is Bing Yang? Sounds somewhat familiar."

“Lord Long Fu's combat prowess is top tier among silver level fighters. Can Bing Yang stop his winning streak?”

“Bing Yang isn't like other silver level fighters. He was once a gold level fighter who fell in level after overusing the magic potion [Beautiful Future].”

“So that's it!”

"He's also a victim of [Beautiful Future]? How is he still alive after all these years?"

“He was once a gold level, after all. And I heard he deliberately committed a crime, entered the Ice Prison, and discovered a way to counteract the potion's effects!”

"Bing Yang is not ordinary! He was once gold level, and now he can resist the [Beautiful Future] potion. He's even challenging Long Fu now, he must be confident. How much combat power does he have left?"

“Could it be that Bing Yang has recovered and returned to gold level?”

“No, multiple sources confirm he's still silver level.”

"But this isn't ordinary silver level. His combat power must be astonishing. I wonder who'd come out on top in a fight between him and Long Fu?"

"I'm dying to see it too! Their clash could easily be the strongest silver level showdown of this National Ceremony Grand Duel."

“Now it all hinges on Long Fu's response, will they accept or decline?”

"They absolutely have to accept! A challenge of the gold level would be one thing—after all, Long Fu is only silver level. We can understand a refusal in this case. But how can he possibly decline a duel with another silver level?"

"There's no need to speculate on that. I firmly believe Long Fu won't refuse. He dared challenge even the gold level Teng Dong Lang and took him down in public, why would he fear a mere Bing Yang?"

"This battle is definitely happening! Finally, the National Ceremony Grand Duel is starting to feel worthwhile."

“Too bad they're both in the capital. We won't get to see it live.”

"So what? The magic broadcast of this fight will be a huge hit."

"Hmm? Didn't you know Long Fu has been holding duels privately lately, without spectators?"

“Ah, I suppose that's true...”

"Then let's make our voices heard! We demand this duel be public, we want to watch. If they won't show us, we won't buy their dragon rider potions!"

“Hahaha, if the duel goes public, I'll buy ten sets of potions right away to support the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Public sentiment quickly surged like massive waves, swiftly engulfing Zong Ge and Zi Di.

Zong Ge keenly sensed the hostility: "Enemies are spreading and amplifying this public sentiment. Just as we discussed before, they aim to constantly harass me, hinder my recovery, assess my condition, and then strike when the opportunity arises."

Zi Di paced back and forth, her expression troubled. “Based on the intelligence we've gathered so far, Bing Yang was already an elite when he was still at the gold level!”

“Had he not been dragged down by the potion [Beautiful Future], his future would have been bright. He wouldn't have ended up voluntarily locking himself up.”

“Now that he's been chosen by our enemies, they'll definitely arm him properly.”

“This doesn't bode well...”

“Yet in our current predicament, we cannot refuse.”

Zong Ge's rejection of a gold level duel was understandable, but outright rejecting a silver level one would inevitably damage his reputation, making the Ice Sculpture citizens perceive him as weak and cowardly.

This deeply contradicted the values and ethos of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The best-case scenario would be sales of the dragon rider potion halving, and that was optimistic.

A sharp gleam flashed in Zong Ge's eyes, showing no sign of retreat: “Since we've leveraged our reputation, we'll naturally be bound by it.”

“Forget our business plans, Bing Yang must be eliminated.”

"Yuan Ci orchestrated this behind the scenes; originally, Shi Liu was to represent Ice Prison in battle. Now, Bing Yang has usurped that spot. Once I eliminate him and free that spot, Shi Liu can take his place."

Zi Di sighed and could only nod in agreement.

He Gai Tou remained unaware that his selection of Bing Yang for battle disrupted not only the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' commercial ambitions but also the secret plan to unlock the Wisteria Cache.

Bing Yang must die!

This was the resolve of Zong Ge and Zi Di.

After reaching their decision, Zong Ge prepared for battle.

Zi Di, meanwhile, contacted the Li Jian Clan.

She presented two potion recipes to the Li Jian Clan's alchemist: “One is the potion [Spirit Stimulation], the other is the potion [Smooth Stride]. Both are used to accelerate magic beast mounts.”

“The former accelerates mental processing, while the latter boosts physical movement speed.”

“Once these are brewed and released to the market, they're bound to spark a second wave of sales frenzy.”

Zi Di was confident.

The alchemist lacked her business acumen and couldn't grasp the vast commercial potential, but he could study the two potion formulas.

Soon, the Li Jian Clan member marveled: “Brilliant! The acceleration principle of the [Smooth Stride] potion doesn't just boost the mount's base speed, it also mimics a spell like [Grease Feet], drastically reducing resistance.”

When it reached the hands of the Li Jian Clan steward, who also possessed some business acumen, he saw even more: "The [Spirit Stimulation] potion is exceptional! Its alchemy cost is remarkably low. It accelerates mounts' thinking, making them more agile and preventing them from being overwhelmed by sudden high speeds. In our kingdom, mind-enhancing potions primarily focus on calming thoughts and resisting external distractions. When it comes to accelerating thought, none can compete."

“This potion is bound to be a huge hit!”

Chapter 860: Grand Tournament Reward

Zi Di had carefully considered her choice of potions— Smooth Stride and Spirit Stimulation.

In her original plan, her next alchemy product was to be the Song of Ice and Fire. This was not a potion and thus fell outside the cooperative agreement between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan.

Zi Di intended to establish her own alchemy laboratory, workshop, or factory, essentially relying on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' own strength for independent production.

The advantage of this approach was that the profits earned would not need to be shared with other factions but would be exclusively enjoyed by the survivors.

Simultaneously, it would grant genuine production capacity.

This production capability, combined with the already established sales channels, would form the cornerstone of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' alchemy business.

The immense fame of the dragonman youngster directly spared the survivors the effort of advertising.

The collaboration on dragon rider potions ensured the survivors continued to enjoy the protection of the Li Jian Clan.

Zi Di's business plan unfolded step by step, tightly paced and logically arranged. Each phase yielded tangible results. Upon overall completion, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would sell their alchemy goods nationwide across Ice Sculpture.

The plan was sound, but reality always brought complications.

The challenge from Bing Yang made Zi Di and Zong Ge realize this was another enemy move.

The survivors' own business plans, coupled with the stakes involving the Wisteria Cache, left Zong Ge and Zi Di no choice but to confront the threat head-on.

"Zong Ge is stubborn, he won't retreat. He's our mainstay now, the sole pillar holding this situation together. We absolutely cannot let him fall!"

“I should do something too.”

Before the discussion began, Zi Di had already anticipated Zong Ge's choice.

Spirit Stimulation and Smooth Stride were her ways of supporting him.

Originally, Zi Di had intended to collaborate with the Li Jian Clan on only a single potion. The profits from the dragon rider potion were sufficient to sustain their alliance.

Only after the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had grown strong enough to withstand risks would Zi Di consider re-entering the potion market with [Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation].

By partnering with the Li Jian Clan this way, the formulas for these two potions would inevitably be leaked.

“There was no choice. Circumstances forced this course.”

“Fortunately, War Merchant's database holds many such potion recipes.”

"The next priority is establishing production facilities. Setting up alchemy labs within the royal capital is relatively straightforward."

Over recent days, Zi Di observed numerous alchemy laboratories, many of which lay abandoned.

Several streets in the capital were once lined end-to-end with alchemy labs.

Not only on the surface, but beneath the ice lake, the number of alchemy labs was even greater!

“I can apply for some of these labs and secretly convert them into alchemy workshops for production.”

“Better yet, select a location and build a large-scale alchemy factory with assembly lines.”

“Just like what I once saw on Mystifying Monster Island.”

However, constructing an alchemy factory proved far more challenging than establishing workshops.

Site selection required careful consideration, and factory construction permits needed to be applied for.

“Funding for construction isn't a concern, profits from dragon rider potions are more than sufficient.”

“Regarding factory permits, we could explore avenues through the Li Jian Clan or Elder Jiu Jin.”

“The most critical issue is security!”

Currently, Zi Di remained unaware of the duelists' true identities. Neither she nor Zong Ge could uncover the depths of this mysterious faction.

Once the factory was built, it would inevitably become the prime target for attack.

Zi Di and the duelists had clashed over several rounds, and she deeply sensed the enemy's formidable strength. This power extended beyond combat prowess to encompass the intellect and strategic cunning exemplified by Mei Lin.

“The most thorough solution to the security issue is a mage tower!”

“Ah... a mage tower feels so far beyond my reach.”

The funds required to construct a mage tower were astronomical. Profits from dragon rider potions alone were utterly insufficient.

Qualification to build a mage tower within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was exceedingly rare. It was not something outsiders like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could obtain.

These two obstacles alone loomed as high as mountains before Zi Di, making even the mere thought of overcoming them feel overwhelming.

“Magician Yao Ma, according to our cooperation agreement, what does your side require this time?” The Li Jian Clan steward's expression was solemn.

The first collaboration, merely the dragon rider potion, yielded such enormous profits that the entire Li Jian Clan was thrilled.

Now, Zi Di was presenting two potion formulas, thoroughly proving the survivors' prowess in the field of potion-making. This action earned the Li Jian Clan a more proactive stance toward cooperation.

Zi Di stated directly, “Now, we've encountered a bit of trouble.”

“You've likely heard about the duel challenge from Bing Yang?”

The Li Jian Clan steward nodded, expressing confidence in Long Fu and doubting Bing Yang's victory.

Zi Di continued, "While we share that view, we must ensure absolute certainty. After all, should the commander suffer defeat, it would deal a devastating blow to our potion sales."

The Li Jian Clan steward nodded repeatedly: “You're absolutely right!”

Bound by mutual interests, they understood perfectly the consequences of Long Fu's defeat. The steward appeared even more concerned than Zi Di: “If there's anything we can do to help, just say the word.”

Zi Di shook her head: “We've gathered extensive intelligence on Bing Yang, but he's rarely fought publicly in recent years and has been secluded within his ice prison.”

“Honestly, I'm unsure how to prepare for this battle.”

“Understood, I understand,” the steward replied. “I'll depart immediately to relay this news back. We'll mobilize the clan's resources for an urgent investigation, then make targeted preparations!”

“Yes, hurry back. I await your report,” Zi Di instructed.

The Li Jian Clan steward hurriedly bowed and turned to leave.

Zi Di sighed inwardly. This was the benefit of having interests intertwined.

“Of course, our intelligence capabilities remain severely lacking.”

Zi Di once again felt the weakness of their position. The intelligence they purchased externally lacked depth.

For now, they could only rely on the Li Jian Clan, barely managing to address the issue.

It would be better to establish their own intelligence agency.

External entities like the Li Jian Clan and the Thieves' Guild had conflicting interests with the survivors. Many intelligence-gathering actions themselves leaked secrets.

For instance, when Zi Di previously investigated the Wisteria shop, she had to act alone.

Though she achieved results, she relied on the ice gargoyle, snake shadow ghost, and rat eye ghost. The former depended entirely on the Deceptive Disguise spell, while the latter leveraged the undead faction's advantages of being new.

But the Deceptive Disguise spell consumed the god level material pearl bubble, the cost was far too high!

The undead faction's advantage steadily diminished over time, drawing ever closer to its complete exhaustion.

“We need a stable, reliable intelligence gathering organization that truly belongs to us...”

When Zi Di returned to the inn, Elder Jiu Jin had been waiting for quite some time.

He looked at Zi Di, head held high, speaking with an air of superiority: "Magician Yao Ma, my time is precious. Do you have any idea how much business I've neglected waiting for you?"

Zi Di immediately smiled apologetically and offered monetary compensation for Elder Jiu Jin's losses.

“My time,” Elder Jiu Jin declared, though his expression softened, “cannot be measured by mere gold coins!”

The brief public display concluded, and Zi Di led Elder Jiu Jin into his study for a private conversation.

The moment the study door closed, Elder Jiu Jin lowered his previously haughty head. He dared not sit, instead standing as he reported the latest developments to Zi Di.

Zi Di frowned. “Hua Ni intends to target us?”

Elder Jiu Jin hastily protested that this was not his wish, but he had no choice, he could not defy Master Hua Ni's instructions.

“You must trust me!”

“Though I am an elder, I cannot compare to the likes of Hua Ni or Shuang Lian.”

“All I can do now is leak the exam questions to your side in advance.”

Elder Jiu Jin wore a deeply troubled expression.

With Zi Di holding his weakness, he could only obey her orders in secret, submitting without resistance.

Zi Di smiled, indicating understanding, and offered Elder Jiu Jin several words of comfort.

Then she asked what the exam questions were this time.

Elder Jiu Jin answered truthfully.

The hidden alchemy apparatus had already recorded this entire scene.

“Excellent. This gives me yet another leverage point over him.” This was precisely what Zi Di sought.

The previous leverage had proven rather ineffective.

Should it be exposed, Elder Jiu Jin would inevitably face a full-scale investigation due to the involvement of necromancers. Imprisonment, interrogation, and removal from office would follow and his personal value would be utterly destroyed.

Zi Di, intent on squeezing every last drop of value from Elder Jiu Jin, would never allow him to become a prisoner.

The exam questions were indeed challenging.

After reviewing them, Zi Di's expression grew grave.

Elder Jiu Jin, reading her mood, subtly offered his assistance, hinting he could secretly help Zi Di pass. However, the condition was that both parties sign a contract, never to mention the necromancer's scandal again.

Elder Jiu Jin refused to be controlled and had his own agenda. He went with the flow, using Hua Ni to create resistance and amplify Zi Di's need to advance in the Warm Snow Cup Tournament, thereby planning to achieve his own goals.

Zi Di shot Elder Jiu Jin a glance, her mockery unmasked. “Elder Jiu Jin, you seem to underestimate me.”

“Though I'm only a silver level with limited alchemy talent, passing the first trial poses no difficulty.”

Elder Jiu Jin's face twisted with awkwardness.

Zi Di shifted her tone: “Of course, your suggestion is sound.”

“Should I require assistance later, rest assured, you'll be my first consideration!”

Elder Jiu Jin offered a stiff smile.

After reviewing all the intelligence Elder Jiu Jin had provided, Zi Di continued: “So this year's Warm Snow Cup has drastically increased in difficulty, yet the rewards far exceed previous editions.”

"In the past, only the first-place winner earned the right to build a mage tower. But this year, the top three all qualify for that privilege."

“Moreover, the champion will receive substantial sponsorship from the Ice Sculpture Royal Family and the Alchemy Guild to aid in constructing their mage tower!”

Chapter 861: Time Ice Cube

Speaking of which, Bu Quan's mentor, Shuang Lian, once claimed the championship title at the Warm Snow Cup tournament.

The prize for the championship was a construction fund sponsorship specifically for the mage tower.

This substantial sum proved immensely helpful to Shuang Lian, accounting for nearly 40% of the total construction costs for the Honey Snow Tower.

Of course, the construction of the Honey Snow Tower had been a lengthy endeavor.

Even now, it remains a work in progress. This was because mage towers were constantly being remodeled and refined.

The initial championship sponsorship enabled Shuang Lian to lay the foundation and framework of the tower. Combined with the steady flow of funds from her demi-plane enterprises, this sustained the ongoing construction of the Honey Snow Tower.

Zi Di was well aware of this fact.

She also understood that the champion sponsorship was specifically tied to the tower's construction. Without the mage tower, the funding would cease.

Moreover, the relevant contract stipulated: While the mage tower built by the champion belonged to the alchemist, it also fell under the jurisdiction of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Zi Di interpreted this initiative thus: “This massive investment not only secures exceptional talent like the alchemist magician but also fully motivates the champion, adding a mage tower to the kingdom.”

“The beneficiaries of this policy are not only the champion but also the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

If she wished to effectively counter enemy threats, a mage tower was indeed the optimal choice.

With substantial sponsorship, Zi Di could fully construct the mage tower. The specifications for the Honey Snow Tower far exceeded ordinary standards. For the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, building a regular mage tower would have been entirely sufficient!

But when Elder Jiu Jin heard Zi Di inquire about the rewards for the top three, he shook his head immediately: “This year's Warm Snow Cup Tournament is indeed quite special.”

“Because it's being held during the National Ceremony period.”

"This time, the third-place prize is equivalent to the championship reward of previous years. The rewards for first and second place far exceed third place."

"The first-place prize isn't just funding; it includes vast resources for tower construction. Our Alchemy Guild has calculated that this reward alone could fund 80% of a standard mage tower."

“The second-place prize covers 60%.”

“But competition this year is exceptionally fierce. If your corps intends to rely solely on the Necromancer Aura Detector, don't even dream of securing a top-three spot.”

After delivering this warning, Elder Jiu Jin emphasized: “Don't even entertain the thought!”

Zi Di's expression remained composed as she pressed for details: “Tell me, just how intense is the competition?”

Elder Jiu Jin continued, "Take the first category alone, many elders from our guild have entered this competition. Elder Hua Ni is one such participant."

"Beyond that, there are rising talents like Xue Tuan Zi. And established alchemy powerhouses, such as the current ruler of Blood Kin City. For this tournament, most of them have spent over half a year preparing!"

“You can imagine how extraordinary their final alchemy creations will be.”

Zi Di nodded slightly. “I'm well aware of this situation. Who said I didn't know?”

Elder Jiu Jin hesitated briefly before continuing, “Let me share one of the latest alchemy breakthroughs.”

“Have you heard of time ice?”

Zi Di: “Of course I do. It's a specialty of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake. The power of a god level dragon's corpse has eroded and transformed the ice layers within the lake.”

“These ice layers contain the mysterious power of time. Through specific methods, the ice is mined and crafted into small ice cubes. Each cube holds a fragment of time's immense power, truly wondrous.”

“Right after they were first mined, time ice cubes became the hottest alchemy material in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, even sparking a global craze.”

Elder Jiu Jin took up the thread: “Sadly, this craze came quickly and faded just as fast.”

"The main reason was the extremely limited ways to utilize time ice cubes. The material itself is excellent, but finding suitable methods to harness this resource proved exceedingly difficult."

“Countless alchemists pursued research relentlessly, only to fail.”

“Until this year, when a collaborative project here achieved a breakthrough!”

"This alchemy endeavor is titled: Exploring the Consumption of Time Ice Cubes. It has now been successfully developed."

"By infusing time ice cubes into our sorrow-dispelling red wine, we achieve a controlled release of their temporal energy. Those who drink this wine experience accelerated time in a specific aspect of their bodies."

“After thousands of trials, the results are nothing short of perfect!”

Zi Di heard of the sorrow-dispelling red wine for the first time and immediately latched onto the key phrase, prompting, “What do you mean by ‘time in a certain aspect’?”

Elder Jiu Jin knew his explanation wasn't clear enough, so he simply gave an example: “While people savor time ice cubes and sorrow-dispelling red wine, they also complement them with other magic delicacies.”

"For instance, consuming certain magic beast meat allows the absorbed magic power to align with the fighter's own battle qi. After consuming the Ice Cube Red Wine, the time required for cultivating battle qi is dramatically accelerated, achieving the effect of massively saving the time needed to refine battle qi."

Zi Di realized something: "This means that eating magic cuisine together with the Ice Cube Red Wine can greatly shorten cultivation time. Can't transcendents then advance to higher levels within months or years then?"

Elder Jiu Jin nodded, recognizing that Zi Di had grasped the alchemy achievement's purpose.

Zi Di's expression remained composed, yet her heart filled with uncontrollable yearning.

This alchemy breakthrough held immense potential, not just for her, but for all survivors.

“With the Bloodline Purification Formation, we can refine our bloodlines.”

“What we truly lack is time.”

“Fighter and magician cultivation requires accumulated time; it's a step-by-step process.”

"But with the Ice Cube Red Wine, the growth cycle can be drastically shortened. What normally takes years can be reduced to months."

“Others aside, the Lord Commander needs it most.”

“His bloodline is immensely potent, yet far from reaching its peak. Using the Ice Cube Red Wine will enable him to rapidly break through to gold level, counteracting the negative effects of Zong Ge's own gold level advancement!”

Elder Jiu Jin inquired, “Magician Yao Ma, do you believe this alchemy achievement has the potential to compete for a top-three spot?”

Zi Di immediately grasped the elder's implication and couldn't help but express astonishment: “The Ice Cube Red Wine has such immense versatility. Could it truly not even compete for the top three?”

Elder Jiu Jin nodded solemnly: "If this achievement were solely the work of our Alchemy Guild, it could potentially secure a top three spot. But unfortunately, it is not."

"It is a collaborative project. Besides our Alchemy Guild, the other partner is the Intoxication Church."

“The sorrow-dispelling red wine is primarily crafted by the Intoxication Church, with the most crucial ingredient being the divine blood of the Wine God.”

Zi Di was stunned: “Divine blood?”

Elder Jiu Jin continued: “It is precisely because the Intoxication Church can provide the Wine God Blood that our nation permitted them to enter Ice Sculpture Island for missionary work.”

“Now, Drunkard Harbor serves as their parish.”

Zi Di's eyes widened in realization. "The Intoxication Church has been proselytizing for years. I know a bit about Drunkard Harbor. So, this alchemy project has been in development for quite some time, at least twenty years?"

Elder Jiu Jin nodded. “Correct.”

He fixed his gaze on Zi Di and continued, “This year's Warm Snow Cup competition was destined to be extraordinary from the very start!”

“A collaborative project like this has no chance of placing in the top three.”

“Our nation stands tall in the world of alchemy. We won't allow a collaborative project to secure a top three finish in this competition.”

“If your corps were to enter the Necromancer Aura Detector into this competition, the outcome would likely be unfavorable.”

"Based on my current understanding, a top three finish is absolutely impossible, and even the top five is unlikely. Perhaps there's a chance to break into the top ten."

Zi Di paused before asking, “Then what alchemy projects have a realistic shot at the top three?”

Elder Jiu Jin shook his head, a wry smile on his face. "Some I know of, but they're bound by confidentiality agreements. Others I've never even heard rumors about; they're private projects of the guild elders."

“In any case, to push the Ice Block Red Wine project into the top three, our guild will spare no effort.”

Zi Di couldn't help but sigh.

The snow elves were, after all, elves by nature, proud to the core.

On the other hand, this also touched upon national interests.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was secretly exerting every effort to uphold its prestige in the field of alchemy.

At this level, reputation was paramount, closely tied to profits.

Take the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' potion profits, for instance, weren't they largely driven by the fame of the dragonman youngster?

Zi Di had been reaping the benefits of that very reputation all along.

"Elder Jiu Jin, I'd like some time ice cubes and sorrow-dispelling red wine. Can you arrange this?" Zi Di asked.

Jiu Jin gave a bitter smile. “Impossible.”

“Even our Guildmaster only obtained half a bottle as a sample.”

“And that half bottle was quickly divided among the Guildmaster and other powerful elders.”

Jiu Jin wasn't considered a powerful elder, nowhere near the stature of Hua Ni, Shuang Lian, or the Snow Cake Tower Master.

“The elder collaborating with the Intoxication Church is Ban Tu.”

"You may have heard of him, he created the potion [Beautiful Future]. That potion cost him everything: his fortune and his future. For a time, he drowned his sorrows in drinking. That’s how he converted to the Intoxication Church."

“His connections with the cult’s leadership gave him the opportunity to collaborate, ultimately succeeding in creating the Ice Cube Red Wine alchemy project.”

"The Ban Tu of today is not the man he once was. That devastating blow left him reclusive and eccentric, averse to socializing, prone to paranoia and anger. He drifts on the fringes of the Alchemy Guild's mainstream, maintaining poor relations with everyone."

"Ban Tu might be a potential breakthrough, but even the Guildmaster likely has no leverage. What chance do I have?"

Zi Di sighed and gave up.

“It seems we'll have to wait until after the tournament for the official release of this alchemy product.”

“With the blood of the Wine God as its primary ingredient, sales of this concoction are unlikely to be high.”

At this thought, Zi Di's brows furrowed slightly, a quiet worry settling over her.

Chapter 862: Intoxication Fleet

Dark clouds blanketed the sky.

The sea wind grew increasingly fierce.

The bishop of the Intoxication Church stood personally at the bow, casting divine spells to scan the surrounding waters.

After a long moment, he exhaled a breath of stale air, thinking to himself, “A storm is approaching.”

“Detection shows no signs of human interference. This is a natural weather phenomenon.”

He then commanded his subordinates, “Act swiftly! Reef the sails, engage the thrusters, increase speed, and depart the storm's reach immediately.”

The fleet he commanded comprised 26 vessels: 10 standard ships, 8 bronze level magic ships, 4 iron level magic ships, 3 silver level ships, and 1 gold level ship.

This was a sizable fleet indeed.

Upon receiving the order, the fleet's speed gradually increased.

The sea wind howled, blocking the fleet's path ahead.

Even the standard vessels were equipped with alchemy components that could temporarily boost their speed. Yet they couldn't match the inherent power of the magic ships and soon fell behind.

“Lord Bishop, our fleet is splitting up,” a subordinate reported.

The Intoxication Bishop pondered, “Then separate immediately. Leave one silver level magic ship, two iron level, and four bronze level to escort the ordinary ships. The rest will follow my flagship!”

The subordinate showed a hint of confusion but acknowledged the order: “Yes, Lord!”

The Intoxication Bishop's brow remained furrowed, showing no sign of relaxation.

The subordinate, of low level, remained unaware of what lay aboard their flagship.

"There are 600 bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine, a staggering fortune. We won't be able to produce this wine again for at least five years."

The Intoxication Church had poured every last resource into this endeavor, even draining their future potential.

These 600 bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine were not merely wine, nor just wealth, they were a unique resource.

“If we use them wisely, we can leverage the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's national ceremony as a golden opportunity. Employing this wine could unlock even greater benefits from the kingdom.”

The Intoxication Church was an imported faith, maintaining an independent diocese within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. This diocese thrived as the kingdom's renowned coastal port city.

Yet the Intoxication Church harbored grand ambitions, not merely to proselytize along the coast, but to spread their faith deep into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's heartland.

This wine shipment was their most crucial tool, their fiercest weapon!

As the fleet's top leader, the Intoxication Bishop bore the secret mission of transporting the sorrow-dispelling red wine.

The pressure weighed heavily upon him.

“I'm merely gold level.”

“Yet around Ice Sculpture Island, saint level pirates are beginning to approach.”

“Da Han...”

The Intoxication Bishop harbored deep apprehension toward the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates. He knew the closer they drew to Ice Sculpture Island, the greater the peril.

Therefore, he didn't let the storm slow him down. Instead, he decisively split his fleet, intending to race against time and shorten the voyage as much as possible.

“Once we reach Drunkard Harbor, we'll be safe.”

“Even if Da Han leads his pirates to attack the port, we can still safely and securely transport this shipment of sorrow-dispelling red wine!”

As the Intoxication Bishop pondered this, an urgent military report arrived: "Report! Pirate ships spotted ahead!"

"What?!" Intoxication Bishop's eyes widened instantly.

Just what he feared most.

He immediately regained his composure and urgently inquired, "Which pirate faction is it? Scout them out immediately."

Within seconds, a subordinate returned with the latest intelligence: “Reconnaissance confirms it's the Divine Wind Pirates!”

“Them?” The Intoxication Bishop frowned deeply.

Though not as formidable as Da Han, the Divine Wind Pirates were still a force to be reckoned with. Their leader, Feng Lian, though only a gold level, possessed extraordinary combat prowess.

The Intoxication Bishop had no desire for battle and immediately ordered the fleet to turn, attempting to escape.

From his flagship, Feng Lian heard his pirate subordinates report: The target fleet had dramatically increased speed, likely utilizing some potent alchemy acceleration component.

Feng Lian remained unhurried, smiling faintly: “Just follow them.”

The Intoxication Church's fleet raced forward, their speed soaring, yet they could not shake off the Divine Wind Pirates.

Recalling the wolf pack tactic of exhausting the enemy, the Intoxication Bishop's face darkened. “The Divine Wind Pirates' ships are incredibly fast. Even if we push our engines to their absolute limit, we won't outrun them. Instead, we'll suffer severe magic power depletion, a loss far outweighing any gain.”

Though potent, the alchemy components proved poorly matched to the Intoxication Church's magic ships.

In contrast, the Divine Wind Pirates' ships hailed from Gear Island. Not only were each magic ship fully integrated, but they could trigger chain reactions between ships.

“Damn it!” The Intoxication Bishop could only send out distress signals, hoping for naval reinforcements from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Meanwhile, he ordered the ships to form battle lines, preparing to meet the enemy.

“We're nearing Ice Sculpture Island at this distance. I pray the Kingdom's Navy arrives in time!”

“We absolutely cannot lose those 600 bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine. Even if I die in battle, I'll protect this vital resource.”

“Damn it!”

“Why has Feng Lian targeted me? Could it be that word has leaked out?”

Seeing the Intoxication Church's fleet suddenly slow down, with their gold level flagship bringing up the rear and silver level vessels providing support, steadily securing the windward position, Feng Lian narrowed his eyes slightly.

“Oh, attempting a T-formation tactic?”

With his extensive naval combat experience, Feng Lian recognized it instantly.

“Boss, we intercepted enemy communications. Per the plan, we didn't intercept!” a pirate subordinate reported.

Feng Lian chuckled: “Then we wait a little longer.”

“The Intoxication Church and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom share a profound alliance.”

"They've sent a distress signal, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom must respond actively. And nearby lies part of the Second Naval Fleet's formation."

It turned out Feng Lian knew nothing about the sorrow-dispelling red wine incident. His targeting of the Intoxication Church's fleet was merely a diversionary tactic.

His true objective was the Kingdom's Second Naval Fleet.

This naval force was practically the private army of the Li Jian Clan.

Last time, Feng Lian suffered a major defeat at the hands of the Li Jian Clan, a loss he had never forgotten, vowing relentless revenge.

For this, he had traveled thousands of miles to find Hua Tang, borrowing the alchemy apparatus specifically designed to target the Li Jian Clan Leader.

“Hurry over. I can hardly wait.” A flame of vengeance burned fiercely within Feng Lian's heart, growing ever brighter.

The Intoxication Bishop was perplexed: “What's going on?”

They had slowed their pace, preparing for battle, only for Feng Lian's fleet to halt its pursuit and remain on the periphery.

“What exactly are they trying to do?” The bishop couldn't fathom Feng Lian's true intentions, "How much longer before they can rendezvous with us?”

The “they” the bishop referred to were part of the original fleet. They had temporarily departed because he needed to hurry, evading the storm.

Now, with battle imminent and the enemy stronger than them, the bishop naturally wanted to gather all available forces.

Amid the tense anticipation of the Intoxication Church's fleet, their ships finally converged at a single point.

“They let us regroup this easily?”

“What the hell is Feng Lian's crew playing at?”

The Intoxication Bishop had sent communications, but Feng Lian refused to respond, showing no willingness to talk.

"Why hasn't the Ice Sculpture Kingdom replied yet? Did they intercept our distress signals? Damn it!"

Crack.

Lightning split the gloomy sky.

Waves surged violently as torrential rain poured down.

The Intoxication Bishop had awaited his fleet and now awaited the storm.

The tempest spread, engulfing the battlefield.

“Hmph!” Feng Lian's expression darkened. "The Ice Sculpture Kingdom hasn't dispatched any reinforcements? Did the Li Jian Clan uncover my plan?"

Clearly, this diversionary tactic had failed.

In truth, since his return, Feng Lian had been actively seeking opportunities to strike.

But with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom celebrating its National Ceremony, the Second Naval Fleet adopted a defensive posture, launching few proactive offensives.

The immense pressure from Da Han had forced the entire kingdom's navy into a defensive posture.

Moreover, the Li Jian Clan's focus lay not in naval affairs, but in the domestic potion market.

After waiting a moment longer, Feng Lian lost his patience entirely: “Since the Ice Sculpture Kingdom offers no support, don't blame me for devouring this juicy morsel!”

Feng Lian waved his hand, his tone icy: “Begin.”

At the command, the pirates erupted into a howling cheer like ghostly wails.

The Divine Wind Pirates, like a pack of bloodthirsty wolves, had long been itching for battle. Now that Wolf King Feng Lian had finally ordered the attack, pirate morale soared like a rainbow. They rushed headlong toward the Intoxication Church's fleet.

First came the cannon fire.

Alchemy cannons spewed fire ceaselessly. Shells flew through the air, colliding mid-flight. Some exploded into towering columns of water upon hitting the sea, others tore holes in ships or blew unlucky souls into splattered blood and gore.

“We outnumber them in cannons!”

“We hold the advantage in this gunfight!”

“Damn it!”

“Feng Lian's here!”

The Intoxication Church's ships clearly outnumbered the Divine Wind Pirates in artillery, after all, this convoy carried vital supplies, so they were heavily armed.

The Divine Wind Pirates' naval tactics had always been renowned for their flexibility.

Against opponents with weaker artillery, they maintained distance for long-range combat. Against those with superior firepower, they would charge in for close combat.

Their greatest advantage was speed!

It was precisely this exceptional speed that enabled their agile tactics.

Seeing the Intoxication Church's formidable firepower, the pirates needed no orders from Feng Lian, they immediately opted for boarding and ramming tactics.

As the pirates' foremost leader, Feng Lian personally led the charge, skimming the sea surface at high speed as he bore down on the enemy flagship.

The Intoxication Church's flagship housed not only the bishop, a gold level fighter, but also two other gold level combatants.

Empowered by divine spells, these two fighters had also gained temporary flight capabilities.

“Don't let Feng Lian board!”

“Attack together and take him down.”

The two gold level fighters swiftly reached consensus and simultaneously leapt from the ship.

The three combatants engaged in aerial combat, skimming the sea surface.

After several exchanges, Feng Lian swung his scythe, unleashing the battle skill [Wind Blade: Continuous Slash].

The two gold level fighters of the Intoxication Church fended off the attacks, forced into retreat.

Feng Lian's attack speed surged again, each swing of the scythe faster than the last, blurring into a phantom.

He carved open their defenses.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Dragon Moon!

A dragon's roar reverberated, shaking their very souls.

Then, a massive moon blade shot forth, slicing one gold level warrior clean in half.

The other gold level warrior, terrified and utterly defeated, turned to flee. Feng Lian caught up effortlessly, his blade erupting in sparks with a single swing.

Bang. Bang. Bang.

The gold level's defensive gear shattered one after another.

With another strike, Feng Lian severed the gold level's head.

Using the sea's currents to evade divine spells and cannon fire, he charged onto the flagship of the Intoxication Church.

"How can he be this strong?!" The Intoxication Bishop turned ashen, pleading for mercy through divine telepathy.

Feng Lian remained utterly cold, offering no response. The scythe in his hand radiated a chilling intent to kill.

Several hours later.

Feng Lian's pirate crew slaughtered every crew member, sank all ordinary ships, and sailed away triumphantly with nearly ten magic ships salvaged from the naval battle.

Chapter 863: Drifting Barrels

A band of pirates fled in disarray.

The Second Fleet of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom pursued them at a measured pace.

"Lord Kong Pan, these pirates have been utterly crippled and pose no further threat. Our immediate priority must be to reinforce the Intoxication Church's fleet." [Sand Between Fingers] San Sha Man Tuo addressed the Second Fleet Captain.

Kong Pan first inquired whether the battle had been recorded.

Upon receiving the aide's confirmation that at least three magic recordings had been captured, Kong Pan nodded indifferently and told San Sha Man Tuo, “You're correct. We'll spare these defeated remnants and proceed to the rescue.”

“Hail the order!”

“Maximum speed. Proceed to the coordinates in the distress signal.”

The Second Naval Fleet swiftly altered course.

However, by the time they reached the battlefield of the Intoxication Church fleet, not only had the battle long ended, but even the storm had ceased.

“Level Three Alert. Conduct reconnaissance of the surrounding area and gather intelligence,” Kong Pan ordered.

The Second Naval Fleet was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's regular military force, possessing mature and comprehensive detection capabilities.

Soon, reconnaissance reports reached high-ranking officers like Kong Pan and San Sha Man Tuo.

“The Intoxication Church suffered heavy casualties.”

“Confirmed: Divine Wind Pirates!”

“Feng Lian personally led the assault, securing victory in one decisive strike.”

“The Intoxication Church fleet has been utterly annihilated.”

“Twenty-nine drifting barrels recovered.”

“At least ten sunken vessels detected.”

…

Upon reading this intelligence, San Sha Man Tuo's brow furrowed slightly, a surge of frustration rising within her.

Kong Pan sighed softly: “Who would have thought the Intoxication Church's fleet was so feeble? They didn't even hold out until we arrived.”

San Sha Man Tuo glanced at Kong Pan and murmured, “We should have abandoned those pirates immediately and come to support them directly.”

Kong Pan shook his head. “Those pirates invaded our defense zone. They had to be eliminated immediately to demonstrate our resolve in defending these waters, thereby deterring other pirates from harboring unnecessary ambitions here.”

San Sha Man Tuo continued softly, “But Lord...”

“The situation is complicated now.”

"The Intoxication Church and the Kingdom share a treaty alliance. Under military law, our rescue mission was top priority, it should have taken precedence."

“But we engaged the pirates first, delaying the rescue.”

“The Intoxication Church suffered heavy losses this time. They won't let this go. When they make a fuss, the Kingdom will launch an investigation. Should they uncover the details and timing of our actions today, we could face a court martial..."

Kong Pan sighed. “Given the circumstances, we have no choice but to alter the fleet's incident logs.”

“Eh...” San Sha Man Tuo was stunned.

The Second Naval Fleet was entirely controlled by the Li Jian Clan. It functioned less as the Kingdom's fleet and more as the Li Jian Clan's private army.

For Kong Pan to undertake this record tampering would be remarkably convenient.

Kong Pan continued, “Now it all hinges on the exact whereabouts of the Intoxication Church fleet's bishop.”

Her tone held a hint of significance.

But soon, the follow-up investigation results reached both of them.

“It has been confirmed that the Intoxication Bishop perished in battle, killed by Feng Lian.”

"Additionally... dozens of bottles of precious red wine were discovered in a drifting barrel. After database comparison, they've been preliminarily identified as sorrow-dispelling red wine."

This was the first time San Sha Man Tuo had ever heard the term “sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

Kong Pan, however, was different. As an insider of the Li Jian Clan, she was privy to many cutting-edge developments.

“Take a portion of this wine and send it to me,” Kong Pan ordered.

Soon, she saw the sorrow-dispelling red wine.

“It really is them...” Kong Pan frowned deeply.

San Sha Man Tuo quickly realized the significance of these wines. After inquiring, she received Kong Pan's response.

The look of worry on San Sha Man Tuo's face grew heavier: “This is bad. With the Intoxication Church suffering such heavy losses this time, the kingdom's investigation will surely be intense.”

"Over the years, the royal family has harbored resentment toward the clan's control of the Second Fleet. This could very well become the royal family's justification for taking action!"

Just then, another subordinate knocked on the door to report: “Captain, we discovered a secret message from the Intoxication Bishop on the inner wall of the drifting barrel containing the sorrow-dispelling red wine!”

The message from the Intoxication Bishop described the sorrow-dispelling red wine, advising whoever retrieved the drifting barrel to deliver its contents to the Intoxication Cathedral in Drunkard Harbor, promising a generous reward.

Kong Pan remarked, "It seems this bishop of the Intoxication Church wasn't entirely useless. At least he minimized the losses."

San Sha Man Tuo: “How much sorrow-dispelling red wine exists in total?”

“What we recovered certainly isn't all of it.”

“Numerous drifting barrels must have already left these waters.”

"Lord Kong Pan, this wine is the Intoxication Church's most vital resource, their absolute necessity. We should search the surrounding area as quickly as possible. The more wine we gather, the easier it will be to navigate the challenges ahead in our investigation."

Kong Pan pondered for a moment, then shook her head slightly. “No, we haven't found anything.”

“To be precise, we did discover some battlefield remnants, but not a single drifting barrel containing sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

San Sha Man Tuo froze, not quite understanding.

Kong Pan sighed and continued, “The sorrow-dispelling red wine is incredibly valuable, perfect for sparking conflict among pirate factions.”

“I know pirates' nature all too well.”

“They're greedy by nature. For sorrow-dispelling red wine, they'll attack each other.”

“Even Da Han, being a saint level power, can't control every pirate faction.”

“Let these pirates tear each other apart. It'll lighten our defensive burden.”

“Send the order: turn back!”

Magnet Archipelago.

The big guy, Da Dou, and Hong Zhu were having a meal.

"Father.”

“Xiao Guai misses you.”

The big guy stuffed his face while secretly contacting the fishman youngster through faith.

The fishman youngster had regained consciousness.

Using the divine spells [Believer Perception] and [Divine Power Channel], he reestablished contact with the big guy.

The big guy and his companions had been surrounded by an earth elemental army, only to be saved by Tu Ji's self-sacrifice. At the last moment, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency chief overseeing Magnet Archipelago intervened, rescuing the trio from enemy lines.

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency treated the big guy and his companions to lavish hospitality.

The fishman youngster comforted the big guy: “Da Dou has arranged the ship and bought the tickets. You'll board this afternoon, Xiao Guai.”

“You'll be heading to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“But I'll be able to meet up with you partway.”

Magnet Archipelago was no longer safe for the big guy.

Even now, the fishman youngster couldn't fathom why they'd been ambushed by an army of earth elementals—or why the Main Earth Elemental God had intervened.

But understanding those mysteries paled beside his concern for the big guy's safety.

So, the top priority was getting the big guy off the island.

The fishman youngster couldn't pilot the Deep Sea Monster Fish to retrieve him directly. He could only order Da Dou to purchase tickets and leave the island first, then intercept him partway.

Chapter 864: Saving Hun Tong

After offering a few more words of comfort to the big guy, the fishman youngster severed the connection.

His attention returned to the Ocean Mother Nest.

Teacher Si Ling was still constructing the grand ritual sacrifice formation.

Damaged by Qian Xing's assault, Teacher Si Ling had begun repairs immediately after the battle.

His repairs were completed by the second day. Yet he did not rest. Instead, he proceeded to add magic runes, constructing new formation frameworks.

Each day, thousands of magic inscriptions and vast new structures were designed by Teacher Si Ling, becoming integral parts of the sacrificial formation.

The intricate web of magic runes and lines no longer confined itself to a flat plane. It extended into the air above the formation and deep into the earth below.

Teacher Si Ling effortlessly neutralized the intense water element interference of the coastal environment, his mastery in designing and constructing formations proving astonishingly profound.

Such actions by Teacher Si Ling had put Zhou Zhang on high alert.

To prevent leaks about the secret alliance between the Charming Blue Church and the undead orthodoxy, Zhou Zhang had already relocated the vast majority of Charming Blue believers. The latter had resettled, maintaining a safe distance from the gold level altar.

Cang Xu had discerned that the Charming Blue Church and the undead orthodoxy had united out of external pressure, a relationship marked by both cooperation and mutual wariness.

These days, he remained close to Teacher Si Ling, eagerly absorbing knowledge. While constructing the formation, Teacher Si Ling generously imparted his teachings to Cang Xu and Jian Gui.

True to his reputation, Teacher Si Ling genuinely enjoyed teaching and proved exceptionally skilled at it.

Cang Xu felt both blessed and burdened.

The blessing lay in receiving guidance from a saint level teacher. Moreover, this Teacher Si Ling was no ordinary saint level being, evident from his previous battle where he effortlessly sent Qian Xing and Da Han fleeing.

“To receive such favor...”

Cang Xu felt honored yet remained vigilant: “The only reason I've gained his favor is the saint level bloodline of the Hanging Corpse Tree within me.”

“More precisely, I've demonstrated the process of bloodline mutation, with its concentration slowly increasing over time.”

“It's precisely this potential that makes Teacher Si Ling place greater faith in me. During our training, he favors and looks out for me more.”

“All of this is thanks to the Lord Commander...”

What troubled Cang Xu more was the constant surveillance. Teacher Si Ling kept a close watch on him, requiring his constant presence. He assisted with menial tasks, helping with simple work in the construction of magic formations.

Even when Cang Xu occasionally left Teacher Si Ling's side to be alone, he remained uneasy. He used alchemy devices to communicate with the fishman youngster.

“The undead faction's strength lies largely in the mystical bonds between souls.”

Wary of this, Cang Xu played it safe and refrained from attempting communication with the fishman youngster.

“The tower spirit records everything.”

"The Lord Commander can observe all changes in the outside world. He understands what's happening without needing to communicate with me."

Just as Cang Xu had surmised, the fishman youngster was stunned by Teacher Si Ling's sudden appearance upon awakening.

He summoned a magic projection via the tower spirit and, upon viewing it, instantly grasped the entire sequence of events.

The astonishment lingered on his face for a long while.

He never imagined it would draw such a formidable figure as Teacher Si Ling! Nor did he anticipate the true nature of the relationship between the Charming Blue Church and undead orthodoxy.

What surprised the fishman youngster even more was that the special divine spell Cang Xu had previously acquired originated from Teacher Si Ling's design.

Upon further reflection, the fishman youngster found this reasonable.

After all, Teacher Si Ling was no ordinary being; he also possessed a page of the artifact, the [Truth Slate].

Zhou Zhang paid far closer attention to Teacher Si Ling than the fishman youngster did.

She proactively inquired multiple times about the purpose behind Teacher Si Ling's construction of the magic formation.

Teacher Si Ling's addition of the formation surprised Zhou Zhang, yet it remained within the bounds of their pact, leaving her powerless to intervene.

What troubled Zhou Zhang deeply was the fragility of their alliance. What if Teacher Si Ling openly manipulated events to harm the divine domain or the Ocean Mother Nest, and she remained oblivious?

This was often the weakness of clergymen.

Among the three transcendent systems, magicians placed the highest value on knowledge accumulation. Fighters developed their physical bodies, while clergymen pursued their own deities, seeking higher energies to infuse their forms.

Whenever Teacher Si Ling constructed a formation, Zhou Zhang almost always came to observe.

Though she couldn't comprehend it, she maintained this posture of close supervision to exert pressure on Teacher Si Ling, urging them not to play tricks.

Zhou Zhang was watching Teacher Si Ling, while the fishman youngster was watching Zhou Zhang and Teacher Si Ling.

"Once the task at hand is completed, I'll leave this place."

"This place isn't safe to stay in any longer!"

The fishman youngster had already made up his mind.

Earlier, from the big guy, he'd deduced that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might be in dire straits. Even then, he'd considered withdrawing.

Later, Cang Xu persuaded him to stay. The fishman youngster pondered deeply and concluded that Cang Xu's perspective was sound. If he could rely on Charming Blue Goddess to ascend to the saint level, it would resolve many problems. Thus, he chose to take the risk and stay.

However, the situation changed too rapidly.

The big guy faced encirclement by the earth elemental army. Tu Ji sacrificed himself to help, but the fishman youngster's assistance was even more crucial, and extremely strained. In the end, the big guy narrowly escaped with his life.

Reflecting afterward, the fishman youngster realized: his rescue of the big guy had been sloppy! Numerous traces were left behind, loose ends that posed a massive threat.

Even more terrifying was that, just as he was about to act, he unexpectedly received a divine revelation from Charming Blue Goddess.

It was precisely by following the guidance of this revelation that he used the artifact, deceiving the Main Earth Elemental God. The price paid was also extremely heavy, the pearl bubbles within the artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] were nearly depleted!

Whenever he thought of that divine revelation, the fishman youngster couldn't help but feel a chill in his heart.

“Such timely and targeted divine revelation proves one undeniable truth.”

“That everything I've done has been under the goddess' watchful eye.”

Based on the fishman youngster's previous deductions, it wasn't Charming Blue Goddess herself who answered his prayers, but the Charming Blue Godhead.

In his understanding, the Godhead was akin to a tower spirit elevated many times over.

“My every action must have been recorded by the Godhead.”

“Just as I witnessed Teacher Si Ling's emergence through the tower spirit's magic projection.”

“In the future, should Charming Blue Goddess' main body awaken, how would She view me? Would She act against me?”

The fishman youngster analyzed that this was highly probable.

For he was not a true Surging Wave fishman; he had been transformed through the Bloodcore.

His prayers weren't born of genuine faith either, but rather a facade maintained through various means. With such blatant sacrilege, could he possibly expect Charming Blue Goddess to look upon him kindly?

“Regardless of Charming Blue Goddess' attitude, being within Her divine domain means placing my life in the hands of another's whim.”

Witnessing the Main Earth Elemental God's intervention had shifted the fishman youngster's perspective once more.

Another factor strengthened his resolve to depart:

Qian Xing and Da Han.

The safe retreat of these two saint level figures signaled that this Ocean Mother Nest had been fully exposed.

Should Da Han or Qian Xing return in the future, the fishman youngster would once again find himself in grave peril.

These two saint level pirates wouldn't even need to lift a finger. Merely presenting evidence of Teacher Si Ling's presence would draw the attention of powerful factions like the Blood Light Punishment Institution.

In short, this Ocean Mother Nest was no longer the hidden sanctuary it once was

The fishman youngster scanned his surroundings for a moment before refocusing his attention.

Divine spell - Perceive Believer.

Divine spell - Divine Power Channel.

The next instant, he connected with Dao Hen.

The moment the Divine Power Channel formed, Dao Hen sensed the gaze of the deity, his heart surging with emotion.

“Great Fish God Mist, my Lord, the current situation is as follows.” He immediately reported to the fishman youngster.

Truthfully, he didn't need to say anything. The fishman youngster simply scanned his memories and instantly grasped the previous events.

Dao Hen was concealed aboard the Treasure Chest, Tan Mo's ship.

This gold level naval vessel was now closing in on the pirate crew led by Cong Mang.

A clash between the two sides was imminent.

On the surface, it appeared Tan Mo had stumbled upon Cong Mang's crew by accident. In reality, however, this entire encounter was part of Tan Mo's plan.

Dao Hen had uncovered this intelligence. The classic combination of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell forcibly transformed a fishman swordsman into an exceptional intelligence operative.

Thus, the fishman youngster also learned of Tan Mo's battle plan, understanding that the latter intended to eliminate Hun Tong.

"To the human race, to the Empire, Hun Tong is a hero. He expanded territories and made significant contributions."

"His imprisonment stems largely from a frame-up. I know the truth about the Devil Gold Coins."

The fishman youngster initially held a poor opinion of Hun Tong, but after fighting alongside him, his perception gradually shifted.

Compared to Hun Tong, the fishman youngster held a far worse impression of Tan Mo.

Tan Mo was the primary focus of Dao Hen's surveillance, rarely leaving his side. Thus, through Tan Mo's daily actions and conversations, the fishman youngster discerned his greedy nature. Simultaneously, Tan Mo's repeated attempts to gather intelligence on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps revealed his sinister intentions.

“Tan Mo plans to use pirates, feign innocence, and eliminate Hun Tong.”

“Hun Tong can be saved.”

"He shouldn't die at the hands of a villain. Sparing his life might help me deal with Tan Mo's extortion in the future."

The fishman youngster had already devised a plan.

He did not intend to rescue Hun Tong directly.

He sought to ride the momentum, avoiding any slip-ups, allowing Tan Mo to mistakenly believe his treacherous scheme had succeeded. One crucial point remained: he needed Hun Tong to fully recognize Tan Mo's true colors!

“Tan Mo is an investigator within the Empire's spy agency. I must treat him with utmost caution.”

“Never imagined I'd be the one to save Hun Tong one day.”

The fishman youngster watched intently, waiting for the battle to begin. Just then, a new face approached the trio led by the big guy.

“Greetings, all three.”

“I'm an auxiliary agent from the Blood Light Punishment Institution. Due to Lord Tu Ji's unexpected demise, I've come to take over and investigate.”

The big guy kept his head down, stuffing his face.

Da Dou paused mid-sip, while Hong Zhu's pupils narrowed slightly. She immediately stopped eating and turned to face the newcomer.

Despite her efforts to conceal it, a flicker of tension escaped her.

Chapter 865: Captured

“I will now ask some questions. I ask all three of you to cooperate fully.” The visitor, who identified himself as an assistant from the Blood Light Punishment Institution, spoke with a smile.

Moments later, the smile vanished from his face.

He had gained no valuable intelligence.

The big guy was clueless and had been looked after by the fishman youngster, so he didn't utter a word.

Da Dou, the big guy's slave, had his own suspicions about the earth elemental army's ambush, but there was no way he'd speak up.

Hong Zhu, attached to both the big guy and Da Dou, naturally followed their lead.

“Gentlemen, your complete ignorance makes my job rather difficult.”

“How am I supposed to report this to the Blood Light Punishment Institution?”

Da Dou remained expressionless: “We've told the truth. Are you suggesting we fabricate intelligence and distort facts?”

“In my view, reporting the facts as they are is the most responsible course of action.”

The visitor sighed and continued, "What I mean is, I request the three of you remain on Magnet Archipelago for now. Until the follow-up investigators arrive, it will allow me to fulfill my obligations."

How could that be possible?

Da Dou didn't hesitate, shaking his head flatly in refusal.

The visitor's expression grew solemn. “Then at the very least, this lady cannot leave. She must stay.”

“We have reliable intelligence that this lady once fought alongside a necromancer on Two Eyes Island.”

“Are you two also accomplices of the necromancers, having traveled with her?”

“To be frank, the reason Lord Tu Ji came to the Magnet Archipelago was to capture Hong Zhu and uncover information about the necromancers.”

“As a representative of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, I must inform you: Hong Zhu is a major suspect who absolutely must remain here!”

The visitor's tone grew unyielding.

Da Dou's expression wavered.

Hong Zhu had indeed been helpful, especially when the big guy was snatched by the Earth Elemental Chieftain.

But Hong Zhu had also made a fortune.

Da Dou owed her nothing. On the contrary, it was Hong Zhu who had attached herself to Da Dou, to the big guy.

To risk offending the Blood Light Punishment Institution for someone as insignificant as her?

That was precisely Da Dou's hesitation.

Seeing his hesitation, Hong Zhu's pleading eyes filled with growing despair.

She knew all too well that once she became a prisoner of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, even if she truly had no connection to the necromancer, she would be finished.

One of the Blood Light Punishment Institution's principles was to err on the side of caution, better to kill the innocent than let the guilty go free!

Just as Da Dou remained silent and Hong Zhu's despair deepened, the big guy abruptly stopped eating and drinking, lifting his head.

“No.”

“Don't take her away.”

“She is mine.”

The big guy extended his greasy fingers, pointing at Hong Zhu.

In that instant, Hong Zhu's eyes flashed with the glimmer of hope born from the depths of despair.

Da Dou looked puzzled and hesitated.

He wondered silently: did the big guy's words reflect his own stance, or the will of the deity he served?

After a moment's silence, Da Dou said, “Hong Zhu must come with us. We cannot hand her over to you. Though I know little of her past with that necromancer, she did fight alongside us."

"You can investigate yourselves or ask us questions in the future."

Da Dou chose to protect Hong Zhu.

He could not discern the big guy's true situation. Was it the deity's decree?

Moreover, having signed a slave contract, Da Dou simply couldn't defy the big guy's orders. Therefore, it was better to state his position clearly and stand firmly with the big guy.

The visitor's brows immediately furrowed deeply, his gaze darkening. “In that case, don't blame me.”

“Oh? What do you want?” Da Dou instantly grew wary.

The next moment, Hong Zhu's face paled dramatically. She let out a deep, involuntary sigh before collapsing completely.

Da Dou sensed something was terribly wrong and immediately channeled his battle qi.

But the moment he activated it, the battle qi he'd just begun to accelerate abruptly dissipated, and instead, a sigh escaped his lips uncontrollably.

“What's going on? I've been poisoned! When did this happen?!" Da Dou's expression changed dramatically as he slumped into his seat, his face frozen in disbelief.

Ever since escaping the battlefield, he had never let his guard down against the imperial spy who had rescued them. Whether it was potions or magic delicacies, he had meticulously inspected everything, always on guard.

“But I clearly...”

A sudden realization struck Da Dou, his gaze locking onto a dish on the main table.

The visitor laughed heartily, clapping his hands with satisfaction. "Truly worthy of a gold level magic chef, you've immediately identified the culprit. Indeed, it is this very dish."

“On the surface, it appears to be merely a silver level dessert. Yet in reality, it bears a far more renowned name—Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea.”
