“Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea?!” Da Dou's heart jolted.

He was immobilized, yet his mind raced. Relevant intelligence flashed through his thoughts.

Long ago during the Age of Exploration, the renowned goblin explorer Ge Zi Bu led his fleet to the very edge of the Main Plane.

At that moment, a sea god intervened, halting their progress.

Disguised as a stranded magic chef, the sea god was rescued by Ge Zi Bu and offered to prepare a delicious magic dish as repayment.

That magic dish was Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea.

Ge Zi Bu adored fine cuisine, a weakness exploited by the deity.

Even as a legend level being, after consuming this delicacy, his fighting spirit vanished. He let out sigh after sigh, losing all desire to continue forward and ordered a return voyage.

Ultimately, he never reached the world's edge. Nearly a decade of exploration ended in failure at the very last moment.

“So this is the famed Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea?” “No, it doesn't taste anything like it,” Da Dou shook his head in denial.

The visitor nodded. "The genuine Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea is a legend level delicacy. What you consumed was certainly not that."

“However, this recipe does indeed originate from the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea, though it has been modified by a magic chef.”

Da Dou's face darkened.

His primary focus had been entirely on the potions they had consumed. Frankly, regarding these magic dishes, he’d trusted his own expertise. After inspecting them and confirming they were flawless, he hadn’t given them another thought.

“Still, the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea recipe must be incredibly intricate.”

"Back then, the sea god himself cooked it, using ingredients from Ge Zi Bu's ship. He even stir-fried it right before his eyes. Even Ge Zi Bu, a legend level figure, couldn't detect any tricks."

“There's a reason I couldn't see through it either.”

“What do we do now?”

Da Dou could barely move a finger, leaving all hope pinned on the big guy.

Hong Zhu also shot the big guy a pleading look.

Nom nom.

The big guy buried his head in his meal.

Though he'd consumed a fair amount of the modified Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea, he hadn't channeled battle qi. Without that crucial element, the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea's effects hadn't been triggered. Thus, he still possessed full mobility.

Hong Zhu and Da Dou: ...

The Blood Light Punishment Institution's support staff watched the big guy with smiles, waiting patiently for him to launch his attack.

Nom nom.

The big guy remained focused on eating, continuously shoveling food from the table into his throat.

After waiting a moment, seeing only the big guy stuffing his face, the smiles on the Blood Light Punishment Institution's support staff gradually faded.

Hong Zhu and Da Dou stared intently at the big guy.

He kept eating, completely oblivious to the tension.

The room grew uncomfortably awkward.

Hong Zhu couldn't hold back any longer, “Lord!” she shouted. “This man is our enemy!”

The big guy froze, “Huh?”

A huge ham hock stuffed his mouth full.

The big guy looked at the Blood Light Punishment Institution's attendant, who smiled back at him.

The big guy hesitated.

Da Dou sighed, “He wants to stop us from leaving the Magnet Archipelago.”

The big guy frowned immediately. He desperately wanted to reunite with the dragonman youngster, anyone trying to keep him from leaving the islands was a villain!

Thus, the big guy swung at the enemy.

Thus, the big guy suddenly lost all strength mid-swing, let out a sigh, his legs buckled, and he collapsed to the ground.

The visitor from the Blood Light Punishment Institution explained with a smile: “This is an improved Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea. Any intense activity triggers it.”

“Guards, take all three of them away.”

Da Dou sighed helplessly.

This time, it was a genuine sigh.

After the big guy and the other two were taken away, the head of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency responsible for the Magnet Archipelago finally entered: “Lord Su Xing, how should we handle these three?”

The so-called “auxiliary member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution” was, in truth, the head of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.

His level was high; originally stationed on Feng Awakening Island, he had immediately been granted the highest authority upon arriving at the Magnet Archipelago.

Su Xing said, “I never expected that Tu Ji actually died in battle.”

"The people of the Blood Light Punishment Institution are madmen. For a mere clue, they dared to defy the gods. Sigh..."

Su Xing sighed deeply as well.

This was a significant complication for him, forcing him to temporarily set aside affairs on Feng Awakening Island and rush to the Magnet Archipelago to personally oversee matters.

He pondered for a moment: "Treat them well. Keep a close watch on Hong Zhu. She is the answer we're giving to the Blood Light Punishment Institution."

“That big guy was specifically requested by headquarters, take good care of him.”

“As for Da Dou... keep him under close watch. After I report this, await further orders from above.”

They were unaware of the slave contract between Da Dou and the big guy.

Su Xing handled Da Dou with particular caution, for Da Dou hailed from Eruption Island. a powerhouse of legend level that demanded his utmost attention.

After issuing his orders, Su Xing added, “My purpose in rushing here isn't merely to capture these three. More importantly, I need to investigate the cause of this joint uprising by the earth elementals.”

“Have the specific rumors begun circulating?”

The Magnet Archipelago Chief nodded immediately. “They were officially released early this morning and have already sparked widespread discussion.”

"Naturally, the combined forces of the earth elemental tribes mobilizing on such a large scale created immense commotion. Compounded by the divine intervention at the time, nearly every transcendent on the island could sense that terrifying aura."

"Thus, after the rumors were released, they were swiftly accepted by the masses and received a very positive response. Some have already begun calling for treasure hunters to unite their efforts."

Su Xing nodded. “The various earth elemental tribes have been forcibly brought together by the Main God this time, creating a certain tendency toward cooperation.”

“Feng Awakening Island is quite close to the Magnet Archipelago, but the Main Earth Elemental God has already turned its attention to these seas.”

“Moving forward, we proceed according to plan.”

“Let our people fan the flames, uniting the transcendents to launch a preemptive strike and annihilate the earth elemental tribes here!”

As long as there were no believers, and the statues and altars were destroyed, no matter how powerful the deity, it would be difficult for them to project their power.

“With a group of earth elemental prisoners, I believe we can uncover the secrets behind the previous joint military operation.”

Chapter 866: Hun Tong Will Also be Used by Me

The fishman youngster remained unaware of the sudden turn of events involving the big guy. His attention was currently focused solely on one thing.

Paying attention to Dao Hen meant neglecting other areas.

Through Dao Hen, the fishman youngster closely monitored Tan Mo's movements.

Tan Mo had already engaged in combat with Cong Mang.

The two fought back and forth on the sea surface, locked in a stalemate.

“Take this! Ha ha ha!”

Cong Mang's pirate ship rammed into the Treasure Chest, its deck planks connecting. Instantly, a large number of elite pirates surged across the planks and stormed onto the Treasure Chest's deck.

Numbering over a hundred, these elite fighters were all bald, wielding long clubs, and clad in light green monk robes.

This was Cong Mang's ace unit—the Hoodlum battle squad.

Leading the charge, the Hoodlum battle squad swept through the Imperial Navy ranks with whirling clubs, forcing them into retreat.

“Fall back inside the ship and hold your ground! Wait for Lord Tan Mo to eliminate the enemy, and reinforcements will arrive!” The first mate bellowed deliberately, directing the naval forces to retreat.

The Treasure Chest's interior was layered, offering considerable depth.

“Charge in! Slaughter them all! Ahahahaha!” The pirates, now dominant, were brimming with morale.

The Hoodlum battle squad charged at the forefront, cutting through the defenses with ease.

“The Imperial Navy isn't all that, huh?”

“Slaughter them all! Loot everything!”

“Let's see who can kill the most imperial sailors!”

“Careful! This ship seems to have spatial alchemy devices. It's absurdly huge inside!”

The pirates were unaware this was a deliberate concession by Tan Mo's forces.

They charged in, swiftly securing the first three levels. Upon reaching the fourth spatial level, they smashed through the door and stormed into the prison cell holding Hun Tong.

Hun Tong lay motionless, eyes tightly shut, deep within unconsciousness.

Seeing his luxurious attire, the pirates assumed he was a high-ranking naval officer. Without a word, they hacked and chopped at him with swords and axes.

Soon, they had hacked him into a bloody pulp.

The surveillance system in the ship's hold recorded the entire scene.

After the pirates stormed out, shouting and brawling, Dao Hen, shrouded in Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, addressed Hun Tong: “Do you believe me now?”

The real Hun Tong was still alive.

It turned out that under the order of the fishman youngster, Dao Hen had secretly come here to warn Hun Tong: Tan Mo planned to use the pirates to kill him.

Dao Hen never revealed his true form. To Hun Tong, he appeared as a mysterious figure shrouded in black mist. Hun Tong couldn't even discern his specific race.

After Dao Hen used divine magic to save Hun Tong, the fishman youngster resorted to his old trick again, employing the Charming Blue divine spell to forge a lifelike “Hun Tong.”

The fake Hun Tong, empowered by a series of divine spells, easily deceived both Tan Mo's faction and the exploited pirates.

Hun Tong stared at his own corpse, then scanned the surveillance runes lining the cabin walls. He remained silent, his face expressionless.

As a ruling lord and noble of the Empire, his loyalty to the Sheng Ming Empire was unwavering. He had never imagined Tan Mo would seek his death.

Yet now, the evidence is undeniable. He could not help but believe it.

Seeing Hun Tong's silence, Dao Hen, guided by the fishman youngster, did not force the issue. Instead, he said, “Come, let's step outside and take a proper look.”

Hun Tong followed Da Han through the pirates' lines, stepping over the bodies of pirates and naval soldiers alike, exiting the hold onto the Treasure Chest's deck.

Standing on the deck, they witnessed the entire confrontation between Tan Mo and Cong Mang, culminating in Cong Mang's apparent “defeat.”

However, Cong Mang and his crew never got the chance to capitalize on their victory.

Once the first mate and his men truly exerted themselves, they “struggled” to repel the pirates, driving them all off the Treasure Chest.

After being “defeated,” Tan Mo, spitting blood, fled back into the Treasure Chest.

Cong Mang wanted to pursue, but was repeatedly hit by the Treasure Chest's cannons, forced to watch helplessly as the ship “escaped.”

Tan Mo ordered his naval forces to secure the battlefield and conduct post-battle cleanup.

Soon after, he arrived at the dungeon where Hun Tong was imprisoned. Staring at Hun Tong's decapitated corpse, he nodded with satisfaction: “Old man, you've been left behind by the times.”

In his eyes, the matter had been concluded perfectly.

"No, it's still some distance from being resolved." The fishman youngster thought this while observing Dao Hen.

This corpse of Hun Tong was fake, so it had to be disposed of.

Dao Hen and Hun Tong lay within the ship's hold.

Shrouded by divine magic, even at this close distance, Tan Mo remained utterly unaware.

Hun Tong's face turned ashen.

He had seen it with his own eyes and heard Tan Mo's words with his own ears. Ironclad evidence revealed a brutal truth—that treacherous bastard Tan Mo actually intended to kill him!

"How dare he treat a powerful ruling lord, an imperial noble, this way! What audacity!!" Raging fury blazed in Hun Tong's eyes.

Dao Hen watched Hun Tong's grim expression with keen interest.

His mood was delightfully light.

Seeing this formidable enemy of the fishman clan, the very man who had imprisoned him, now cornered and betrayed by his own people brought Dao Hen genuine delight and satisfaction.

“My Lord, serving you is the most righteous and honorable act of my life.”

In that instant, Dao Hen's devotion deepened once more.

He waited patiently for a moment before addressing Hun Tong: “Now, you should fully understand your situation, shouldn't you?”

Hun Tong looked at Dao Hen, his face betraying undisguised suspicion and apprehension. "Who exactly are you? What are your intentions in saving me?"

Following the fishman youngster's instructions, Dao Hen did not reveal his identity.

Seeing Hun Tong ask this question now, a sense of superiority and control over the situation surged within him.

Dao Hen said: "You'll know in the future regarding our identity."

“As for saving you, it was naturally to enter into a contract with you.”

With that, he handed over a scroll inscribed with divine script.

Hun Tong didn't recognize the Charming Blue divine script. Even if he did, the scroll was imbued with the fishman youngster's divine magic, making it impossible to discern the true form of the script.

Hun Tong's eye twitched. “You want me to work for you? At least let me know who you are!”

He was cautious, yet deep down, he hadn't given up hope.

In Hun Tong's view: he had merely been betrayed by Tan Mo's treachery. He remained the ruling lord of Two Eyes Island; his foundation still stood. Once he returned to the Empire, he himself would be the best witness to convict Tan Mo.

“The Empire is just.”

“By then, with the Empire's power, I'll eliminate Tan Mo and return triumphantly to Two Eyes Island!”

“But for now, I must play along with these people...”

Hun Tong felt a deep sense of apprehension toward the divine magic displayed by the fishman youngster.

He and Dao Hen strolled about the Treasure Chest with such brazen ease that no one could detect their presence, sending a chill through his heart.

Dao Hen snorted coldly: “Cut the nonsense!”

“What you need to know, you'll find out when the time comes.”

"Just sign it already. You're nothing but a sacrificial pawn. Did you really think we came here just to rescue you?"

Hun Tong fell silent for a moment.

The situation was crystal clear.

The moment Dao Hen removed the divine spell binding him, he would be exposed right under Tan Mo's nose. Then, death would be his only fate.

A wise man knew when to yield.

Hun Tong had simply failed to recognize the truth before, misunderstanding his true predicament.

Now, Tan Mo had perfectly helped him see it all clearly.

So, in the next moment, Hun Tong obediently took the divine scroll, signed it without even glancing at it.

He couldn't read it anyway.

Besides, he had no choice!

In his younger days, he might have fought to the death, resisting fiercely. But now, he was old.

He had been old for a long time.

He had toiled for most of his life to build a foundation like Two Eyes Island.

He still wanted to return there.

"Hun Tong is a man of exceptional business acumen. Such men are often the most adept at compromise and cooperation, they've grown accustomed to it," the fishman youngster thought to himself.

Seeing the contract successfully signed, he knew his assessment of Hun Tong's character was correct.

Dao Hen was right about one thing; this was indeed a strategic but not crucial move.

Yet the fishman youngster's reason for saving Hun Tong wasn’t quite what either Dao Hen or Hun Tong had imagined.

“Hun Tong expanded the Empire’s borders, repelled attacks from the fishman clans, and protected the territories, he did not betray his noble duty.”

Such a loyal man, yet because his bribes were insufficient, he fell prey to the nation's parasites! This is unjust to him."

“In the future, let him testify against Tan Mo, and let Tan Mo receive severe punishment, that is justice!”

“Moreover...”

Moreover, as a secret agent, Tan Mo still sought to trouble the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, planning to extort them.

'The enemy of my enemy is my friend.'

In this situation, Hun Tong was the sharp blade the fishman youngster had prepared for Tan Mo!

After all, Tan Mo wasn't just an imperial spy, he's also a naval commander. The dragonman youngster can't confront him directly.

Not a single survivor wished to antagonize the Sheng Ming Empire, not even Cang Xu. Everyone was still preoccupied with atoning for their sins.

Tan Mo stepped out of the cabin.

He immediately issued orders.

First, have the Navy properly bury Hun Tong's body.

Second, instruct the Navy to perform only minimal repairs on the Treasure Chest.

Third, dispatch subordinates to relay a request to Ice Sculpture Island for port repairs.

What Tan Mo didn't know was that Dao Hen, along with Hun Tong who had a hate-filled gaze, stood not far behind him. Through the cabin door, they watched him silently.

The fishman youngster withdrew his divine spell, pulling his focus back from Dao Hen.

"Xiao Guai is about to leave the Magnet Archipelago. Keep an eye on him during the voyage."

“Hun Tong is temporarily under my command. Since Tan Mo intends to head to Ice Sculpture Island, let Hun Tong and Dao Hen accompany him.”

“Next, we'll wait for an opportunity to leave this place.”

The fishman youngster shifted his focus to the interior and exterior of the Ocean Mother Nest.

Teacher Si Ling wasn't setting up additional magic formations but was conducting inspections.

Through the Deep Sea Monster Fish's logs, the fishman youngster realized this was already his third inspection.

Soon, the third inspection concluded, and Teacher Si Ling nodded slightly with satisfaction.

“Now, I'm about to begin. Watch closely,” he instructed Cang Xu and Jian Gui, yet this only drew Zhou Zhang's even more intense scrutiny.

This level of attention was practically indistinguishable from outright surveillance.

The next moment, Teacher Si Ling activated the magic formation.

Brilliant light erupted outward. This time, there was not a trace of divine power, it was pure, unadulterated magic power.

Within seconds, the magic aura swelled to staggering, overwhelming proportions.

Zhou Zhang was initially wary, but gradually, her expression grew peculiar.

She realized that while the activated magic formation projected an intimidating display of light and aura, it yielded no tangible effect whatsoever.

“What exactly is this Teacher Si Ling trying to do?” Zhou Zhang wondered inwardly.

Not only was she puzzled, but so were Cang Xu, Jian Gui, and the others.

They too sensed no practical outcome materializing.

Yet aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster's expression underwent a dramatic shift.

The instant the magic formation activated, he sensed it!

“Something... something significant is being birthed.”

“This aura...”

“It's connected to the gods!”

Chapter 867: Divinity

The fishman youngster was a divine emissary, and in recent times, his understanding of deities, sects, and faith had advanced by leaps and bounds.

Thus, when the magic formation hosted by Teacher Si Ling activated, the aura it generated was instantly sensed by the fishman youngster.

As time passed, this aura grew increasingly potent.

Staring at the magic projection, the fishman youngster's Adam's apple bobbed as a deep-seated yearning welled up within him.

Moments later, this craving grew more intense.

“Teacher Si Ling is manipulating this formation to condense something.”

“It's profoundly connected to the deities, far from mundane.”

“But something feels off...”

Through the Deep Sea Monster Fish's reconnaissance, the fishman youngster's vision was wide. He observed not only the changes in Teacher Si Ling and the magic formation but also the expressions and attitudes of others.

What puzzled the fishman youngster was that these people showed no strong reactions.

They were utterly indifferent!

Cang Xu was curious and puzzled, Zhou Zhang wary and suspicious, while Teacher Si Ling wore a grave expression, as if controlling this formation imposed considerable mental strain.

“They seem unable to sense it?”

“But why is my perception so intense?”

In an instant, a flash of insight struck the fishman youngster's mind.

He swiftly examined himself and made a new discovery.

“It's the passive divine spell [Perceive Divinity]!”

This was one of the gifts bestowed by the Charming Blue Goddess during his previous prayers.

This passive spell had remained active ever since the divine blessing was received. The fishman youngster had experimented with many divine spells, yet he'd never quite grasped how to use this one.

But now, he realized his intense sensations stemmed from this passive spell.

The answer was becoming clear.

“So...”

"Teacher Si Ling is using the magic formation to condense divinity?"

“The reason I felt it so intensely is because I possess the passive divine skill [Perceive Divinity].”

“They lack this perception ability, so they don't understand what exactly is being condensed within the formation.”

Yet as time passed, the radiance within the formation gradually shrank. Before Teacher Si Ling and his companions, a luminous sphere as vast as a mountain took shape.

The aura emanating from the sphere grew more surging and colossal.

At this point, the expressions of Teacher Si Ling, Zhou Zhang, and others shifted first. Soon after, Cang Xu, Jian Gui, and the rest followed suit.

Zhou Zhang and the rest felt an extreme oppression, as if a deadly blade were pressed against their throats, sending shivers down their spines and chilling their hearts.

Meanwhile, Teacher Si Ling, Cang Xu, and other undead beings sensed boundless vastness, profound stillness, and brutal cruelty.

“Teacher Si Ling, what... exactly is it?” Cang Xu could no longer contain his curiosity and attempted to ask.

Teacher Si Ling did not evade the question, stating plainly, “This is divinity.”

“Death... divinity?!” Cang Xu and the others stared wide-eyed.

The very name itself revealed its profound connection to the deity.

However, concerning such advanced knowledge, even Archbishop Zhou Zhang was not entirely clear.

“Do you know what divinity is?” Teacher Si Ling asked Cang Xu.

Cang Xu quickly shook his head.

Teacher Si Ling's expression grew solemn as he explained, “Divinity is a fragment of a deity's power.”

“It embodies the concepts of the world, the rules of nature, and simultaneously contains aspects like personality, beliefs, and behavioral tendencies.”

"For example, the fallen storm god Raging Storm possesses the storm divinity. This divinity records the natural laws governing wind within the world, while also embodying the storm god's impatience, violence, and fear-based proselytizing."

"If we were to acquire a fragment of storm divinity, we could gain knowledge of wind from it. Such as how wind forms, how it moves, how it dissipates, and its myriad effects on other things."

“Simultaneously, we become influenced, our temperaments grow volatile and quick to anger, favoring violent solutions to problems.”

Jian Gui's voice was dry: “So... are we currently condensing the Charming Blue divinity?”

“What do you think?” Teacher Si Ling turned to ask Cang Xu, his gaze carrying a hint of expectation.

Cang Xu pondered for a moment before shaking his head. “What I sense from this divinity is deathly stillness, murderous intent, and a silent, boundless emptiness...”

“It likely isn't Charming Blue’s divinity.”

Teacher Si Ling heard this answer, a hint of admiration for Cang Xu flashing in his eyes. He then inquired, “So, what do you think this divinity is?”

Cang Xu had already formed an answer in his mind while responding to the previous question. He immediately replied, “I believe it must be the death divinity.”

“Correct. You've guessed it right,” Teacher Si Ling nodded.

His gaze swept across the distant horizon, as if taking in the entire trench. His voice carried a weight of experience as he continued, "The White Hair Trench is immense, reaching depths of 15,000 meters and stretching approximately 3,100 kilometers in length. Its widest point spans 70 kilometers, while the narrowest section still measures over 30 kilometers."

"This place teems with life, sustaining a complex and tightly woven ecosystem. Feeding, hunting, reproduction, death, these cycles perpetually unfold."

“Yet each beast tide overwhelms the existing ecosystem, triggering massive hunts by countless civilized races.”

“Throughout countless ages, death has been one of this place's defining themes.”

“If left unchecked, a natural death divinity would eventually emerge here.”

“And now, I have merely accelerated that step.”

Jian Gui was profoundly shaken, stammering uncharacteristically: “D-death divinity... is something we can create?”

Teacher Si Ling corrected him: “We are not creating it. It already exists, merely dispersed and not yet condensed into a congealed entity.”

"This super-magic formation of divine script and magic has gathered the scattered concepts of death throughout the White Hair Trench. Now it has accelerated the process of condensation."

Teacher Si Ling looked at Cang Xu, his words laden with meaning: “To us magicians, everything follows logic; all things operate according to laws.”

“The greatness of deities must be underpinned by the logic and laws that support that greatness.”

"Divinity itself is one of the secrets behind their greatness."

Zhou Zhang snorted coldly, her expression displeased.

Cang Xu immediately understood: Teacher Si Ling was teaching him once more, attempting to win him over from the Charming Blue Church's faction.

Cang Xu's bloodline mutation was still progressing; he might well possess the potential for the saint level in the future. This alone was sufficient reason for Teacher Si Ling to act thus.

Cang Xu pondered: “What distinguishes naturally produced death divinity from ordinary divinity?”

Teacher Si Ling replied: “The overall differences are minor, yet some exist.”

"For instance, natural death divinity lacks pronounced individuality. Whereas the death divinity of deities often bears the distinct characteristics of the deity itself."

"Take the Ghost World's Death God, Yazi—He possesses a fierce, vengeful nature. Thus, His believers often sacrifice their enemies' corpses and blood to Him after successful vengeance. Such acts frequently earn the favor of the Death God, Yazi, greatly increasing the probability of divine blessings."

“I'll share another secret: Yazi maintains quite good relations with our undead orthodoxy.”

“I've gathered some of the death divinity, which will serve as crucial bargaining chips in our dealings with him.”

Death divinity.

Dealings with the Death God...

Teacher Si Ling's words sent shockwaves through Cang Xu and even Jian Gui.

The undead orthodoxy was undoubtedly a legendary superpower!

“It is precisely because the undead orthodoxy hold such status that the Charming Blue Church agreed to equal cooperation.”

“I see.” Cang Xu pieced together more hidden truths in his mind. "Back then, the undead orthodoxy and the Charming Blue Church joined forces to establish a super-scale magic formation here. The Charming Blue Church sought to perform a blood sacrifice, while the undead orthodoxy used that sacrifice to more readily condense death divinity!"

Through the magic projection, the fishman youngster also learned more from Teacher Si Ling.

“So, it was death divinity.”

The fishman youngster still felt perplexed, his hand pressed over his heart. “Why do I crave this divinity so intensely?”

At that moment, Jian Gui inquired, “Teacher Si Ling, how much death divinity can we harvest this time?”

Teacher Si Ling shook his head slightly. “This is the concept of death accumulated over countless ages in the White Hair Trench. I cannot say precisely how much.”

Cang Xu added, "Death divinity is crucial to the Death God. As necromancers, we should draw closer to divinity. Can we absorb it?"

Zhou Zhang snorted coldly. "Pure delusion! Even those at the saint level are scarcely different from mortals in the eyes of the great gods."

“Deities stand above legends, yet not all deities deign to acknowledge legendary beings.”

“Only You Shen is an exception!”

You Shen, the undying one who founded the undead faction. Even among legends, he stood in his own class.

Teacher Si Ling offered no rebuttal: “Indeed, that is true.”

"Divinity is extremely dangerous to us."

"Even the slightest proximity can cost a weak individual their life."

"Even someone like me cannot lay claim to divinity. To rashly devour it is suicide!"

“However,” Teacher Si Ling shifted his tone, “it's not that no transcendents can bear divinity.”

“Provided their bloodline reaches god level and possesses sufficient concentration, they can bear a measure of it.”

Cang Xu's heart stirred. His first thought was of his own commander!

“Divinity...” The fishman youngster's eyes gleamed.

“Divinity is here!” Da Han's body trembled violently.

His eyes widened in disbelief as he stared at the wine in his hand.

Qian Xing, beside him, frowned immediately, retorting, “How could there be divinity? If there were, we'd be dead already.”

“Neither of us possess god level bloodlines.”

Da Han shook his head; his gaze fixed on the wine in the glass. “I'm certain it's present.”

"Only, the divinity contained here is utterly fragmented and incredibly faint. But it is indeed divinity..."

Da Han turned to the first mate. “You mentioned earlier, what kind of wine is this?”

“Sorrow-dispelling red wine!” the first mate replied hastily. "Pirates salvaged a drifting barrel and opened it. A note inside the barrel revealed its name."

“All this wine was brewed by the Intoxication Church.”

“The Intoxication Church's fleet was recently annihilated by the Divine Wind Pirates.”

"Now, the church's branch in Drunkard Harbor has posted hefty bounties to recover their losses. Especially this sorrow-dispelling red wine."

“It's said the primary ingredient in this wine is the blood of the Wine God.”

Da Han nodded sharply: “That explains it.”

“The blood of the Wine God naturally contains the divinity of the deity.”

Qian Xing studied the red wine in his glass once more: "Even diluted ten thousand times over, divinity remains divinity. This batch of wine is priceless!"

“Divinity...” Da Han sighed deeply, then abruptly asked, “Where are the Divine Wind Pirates now?”

The first mate opened his mouth to answer, but Da Han cut him off with a command: “Contact Feng Lian. Tell him I'm very interested in the sorrow-dispelling red wine in his possession!”

Chapter 868: A Violent Close Quarters Battle

Pirates were greedy; plundering was in their blood.

As the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates, Da Han was a formidable saint level powerhouse. His so-called invitation to Feng Lian wasn't about negotiating a deal.

His meaning was clear—hand over this shipment of red wine willingly, if you know what's good for you. Because I, Da Han, want it. Refuse to hand it over, then prepare to pay with your life!

None of the pirates surrounding Da Han thought he was being unreasonable.

This was the pirate way.

The strong reigned supreme. The rules of the game were brutal, simple, and stark.

Qian Xing watched Da Han issue his commands, a deep envy stirring within him.

This shipment of sorrow-dispelling red wine was worth a fortune. If Da Han succeeded in this raid, the haul would surpass plundering an entire city.

After all, raiding cities didn't guarantee divinity.

If Da Han succeeded, his ranking on the sacred monument would surely rise!

"This is the advantage of having subordinates. You don't need to lift a finger yourself. Just issue a few commands, and a successful raid could be achieved."

“Not to mention the advantages in intelligence gathering and other matters.”

Qian Xing had once viewed his subordinates as burdens, but now he found them exceedingly convenient.

The key factor was the Pirate Throne!

Though Qian Xing possessed a saint level cultivation, his innate talent had reached its peak, leaving no room for further advancement. Lacking a god level bloodline, he had no conventional means to absorb divinity. Attaining godhood was utterly beyond his reach.

But the throne was no ordinary artifact, it was a shortcut!

Throughout history, those who ascended this throne were almost never of god level bloodline.

Yet every Pirate King possessed divine authority and power, their personal strength exploding exponentially!

It was precisely this phenomenon that ignited the madness of pirates across the entire Main Plane.

If the winner required the god level bloodline and also possessed sufficient concentration, there would be scarcely anyone on the Main Plane who qualified. Under normal circumstances, a confirmed saint level seed was already exceedingly rare, enough to spark all-out war between powers like the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and Eruption Island!

“But Da Han's true objective lies in the Ten Millenia Ice Lake.” Qian Xing understood this point all too well.

Da Han cultivated an ice-attribute battle qi technique and followed the Dusk Church. The Ten Millenia Ice Lake represented a resource perfectly suited to him.

Upon reaching the saint level, transcendents' cultivation delved into the realm of laws. The more laws one mastered, the stronger their saint domain became. And the saint domain was the factor that most profoundly influenced the personal strength of transcendent saints. This explained why Qian Xing had retreated immediately upon witnessing Teacher Si Ling wield his saint domain, showing absolute resolve.

The disparity between their saint domains was simply too vast.

Recalling Teacher Si Ling, a shadow of gloom inevitably crossed Qian Xing's expression.

“How do you plan to handle the White Hair Trench?” Qian Xing inquired telepathically through his battle qi.

After all, it had been several days since the two had returned from the White Hair Trench.

Da Han replied telepathically, “Both Teacher Si Ling and Zhou Zhang are saint level. There's no need to provoke them. You should know what my purpose is in this journey.”

Qian Xing nodded slightly but persisted, “Aren't you worried these two might interfere with your plans?”

Da Han chuckled. “If they choose to meddle in Ice Sculpture Island, that becomes the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's problem.”

“Truthfully, the more chaotic the situation, the better it is for me.”

Qian Xing held grudges well. Though he couldn't defeat Teacher Si Ling, that didn't mean he couldn't cause trouble for him.

He suggested, “What if we leak this information to the Blood Light Punishment Institution? What do you think?”

“With one of the Twelve Saints involved, the Blood Light Punishment Institution will certainly make a huge fuss.”

“No, let's not.” Da Han refused decisively.

“Doing this would have too great an impact.”

"Bringing in that bunch of madmen from the Blood Light Punishment Institution, they won't reason with us. Most likely, they'll use the Ice Sculpture Kingdom as a base, which would severely disrupt my future plans to reach the island.

“Furthermore, if Teacher Si Ling dies at the hands of the Blood Light Punishment Institution because of our report, we'll be in serious trouble.”

“Teacher Si Ling is one of the Twelve Saints. That old codger You Shen isn't just powerful, he's also fiercely protective of his own. He'll definitely seek revenge against us later."

"By then, even the boss might only be able to protect us for a short while. Once we're isolated, there's a high chance You Shen will eliminate us."

Da Han didn't think Qian Xing's suggestion was a good one.

Qian Xing fell silent for a moment: "Then what do you plan to do next? How much longer must I stay here?"

Ba Qi wasn't rescued by Qian Xing, but that wasn't Da Han's fault. In fact, it was Qian Xing who called for retreat back then.

The specific agreement they ultimately reached was this: Da Han would help Qian Xing find Ba Qi, while Qian Xing had to fight for Da Han with all his might once.

Da Han had fulfilled his part of the bargain. Qian Xing did indeed spot Ba Qi during the assault on the Ocean Mother Nest. It was simply beyond his power to execute the rescue.

Regardless, Qian Xing must honor his pledge to assist in battle. He must remain at Da Han's side.

Sensing Qian Xing's growing impatience, Da Han hastily offered reassurance: “I'll act the moment the opportunity arises.”

A sharp gleam flashed in Qian Xing's eyes. He knew Da Han undoubtedly had a plan, but it wasn't something he'd share just yet.

Though both members of the Fire Beard Pirates and both at the saint level, their bond was tenuous. This collaboration was merely a fortuitous convergence.

Until the moment arrived, Da Han would lead his pirates to prowl the waters near Ice Sculpture Island, circling like a pack of bloodthirsty sharks.

During this period, the sorrow-dispelling red wine became the temporary prey of Da Han and his pirate faction.

Kong Pan's strategy had succeeded.

This high-ranking member of the Li Jian Clan had truly seen through the pirates' nature.

She deliberately let things unfold, and the situation developed exactly as she had envisioned.

Even Da Han was tempted to join the fray, let alone the other pirates.

A storm of slaughter was rapidly brewing around the sorrow-dispelling red wine.

Meanwhile, in the Ice Sculpture Capital, another storm of slaughter had reached its peak—

Bing Yang challenged Long Fu!

One was a former gold level, the other a silver level capable of slaying gold level opponents.

This was the hottest topic not just in the capital, but across the entire nation.

“Who truly reigns supreme among the silver level?”

The citizens of Ice Sculpture debated this question with relish, never tiring of the discussion.

Many supported Long Fu, the dragonman youngster was undeniably the brightest star, unmatched in prominence. Yet some favored Bing Yang.

“Long Fu is a dragonman outsider. Bing Yang is a snow elf, one of us!”

"Long Fu is strong, but his power has limits. His killing of Teng Dong Lang was highly attributed to luck. Bing Yang, though now silver level, was once gold level. That alone gives him a significant edge!"

Reliable sources say: Though Bing Yang consumed many [Beautiful Future] potions, he's fully recovered these past years. Otherwise, he wouldn't have entered this grand duel."

"Bing Yang endured hardships and never abandoned hope. He withstood despair and has now been reborn! His return to the duel arena is a redemption of his life through relentless perseverance."

Praise for Bing Yang's character and the promotion of his inspirational story spread from unknown sources.

In short, it was hard for the citizens of Ice Sculpture not to warm to Bing Yang.

Consequently, public support for him grew steadily.

Behind the scenes, the betting syndicate under He Gai Tou was orchestrating this sentiment.

Betting markets for this event had already opened.

The largest market was operated by He Gai Tou.

Odds across all markets collectively indicated that the public generally favored Long Fu.

However, as time passed, the number of people favoring and supporting Bing Yang grew steadily. This shift was particularly evident in the odds.

The public voiced a strong demand—they wanted this duel to be open to spectators.

Previously, although Zong Ge had accepted challenges, they had always been private duels. This practice had long drawn criticism, as many eager spectators were denied the chance to witness the action, leaving them frustrated.

"This duel between the two is likely to be the strongest clash among silver level fighters in this year's National Ceremony Grand Duel. Keeping it private any longer seems unreasonable, doesn't it?"

“It depends on the participants' wishes.”

"Bing Yang has publicly declared his willingness to engage in an open duel, it's just that we fear Long Fu might not dare."

"If Long Fu insists on a private duel, Bing Yang will accept that too, but then none of us will be able to watch. Is Long Fu so afraid of being seen?!" Many were already dissatisfied with this.

The public's enthusiasm for spectating was high; they craved high-quality, high-level duels.

The dragonman youngster gained fame by defeating a gold level opponent as a silver level fighter. His popularity stems from fulfilling the nation's immense demand for this form of spiritual entertainment.

“Perhaps he possesses unique fighting methods, but revealing them early might not benefit his future duels?” Some defended Zong Ge.

"If you want excuses, you can find a thousand. But don't you want to watch?"

“Of course I do! I can't wait to see it!”

The fierce demands for the duel to be made public quickly erupted into a nationwide sensation.

Consequently, sales of dragon rider potions miraculously surged by about thirty percent.

This development left the forces led by the Jing Xiang Clan in a foul mood. The market share they had fought so hard to secure plummeted by nearly half due to this single duel.

Many supporters, armed with bottles of potions, gathered in droves around the encampments of Zong Ge, Zi Di, and others. They chanted incessantly, voicing their allegiance and their burning desire to witness the battle.

Watching the shouting crowd from the window, Zong Ge snorted coldly. "I smell a conspiracy. Bing Yang is our enemy. This is an attack meticulously planned specifically for me."

Zong Ge's battlefield intuition remained as sharp as ever.

Zi Di's expression grew grave. “If this is a carefully orchestrated enemy plot, it won't be easy to deal with.”

"According to intelligence from the Li Jian Clan, the mastermind behind this public sentiment is a gambling den owner. He's a gold level fighter named He Gai Tou. Perhaps he is one of our enemies."

Zong Ge remained expressionless. "There's no evidence to prove that. We can't be certain. It's perfectly normal for a gambling den owner to act this way when huge profits are at stake."

“We must guard against this being an enemy trick.”

“If He Gai Tou isn't involved, our overreaction will only earn us another enemy.”

Zi Di sighed, her face clouded with worry. “Are you confident about this battle?”

Zong Ge remained composed. “Don't forget, my true transcendent rank is gold level.”

Zi Di still felt uneasy. She knew Zong Ge wasn't in good condition, bearing numerous injuries. How much of his true strength he could unleash was uncertain.

Zong Ge reassured her. “Rest assured. With what the Li Jian Clan has prepared for me now, my confidence is much greater."

“For that, I owe you my thanks.”

Earlier, to aid Zong Ge, Zi Di had spared no expense in releasing two new potions.

To secure their cooperation, the Li Jian Clan had conducted exhaustive investigations. Not only did they bring back more detailed intelligence, but they also equipped Zong Ge with specialized gear highly effective against the Bing Yang.

Zi Di shook her head slightly. “We're on the same side. With the commander absent, you're our sole pillar. Of course we'll support you fully.”

“It's a pity our influence remains weak, forcing us to seek aid elsewhere.”

Zong Ge snorted lightly. “Though he isn't a gold level, he can indeed shoulder some of my burden.”

“But he still hasn't returned.”

Zi Di's expression grew solemn as she declared with unwavering certainty, “The commander would never abandon us. He will return.”

“Though he's only silver level, I've found a way for him to rapidly level up.”

“Sorrow-dispelling red wine?” Zong Ge shook his head slightly. “Obtaining that wine is far too difficult.”

Zi Di sighed softly: "There will always be a way. Right now, what we need to consider most is whether your battle with Bing Yang should be made public."

Zong Ge answered without hesitation: “It must be public.”

Zi Di sighed again: “I believe it should be so.”

Both were elites, both possessed talent.

Zong Ge analyzed from a military perspective: While past private duels largely preserved his secrecy, they also revealed a certain vulnerability. He needed a public duel, a resounding victory, to demonstrate his might and instill deep caution in his enemies.

Zi Di strategized from a commercial standpoint: Accepting the duel while keeping it private would inevitably spark intense public discontent. Even victory would attract negative backlash. Projected onto the potion business, it could severely dent sales of the dragon rider potions. Consider that they were about to launch two new potions...

Zi Di had secretly calculated: the choice between public or private would mean over 200,000 gold in profit difference!

Moreover, this matter also concerned the Wisteria Cache.

The two reached an agreement and immediately announced the news to the public.

“This is incredible!”

“Wow, Long Fu agreed to make the duel public.”

“Wonderful! This will be the most spectacular duel before the gold level clash!”

“Lord Long Fu truly is magnanimous; he hasn't disappointed us.”

Instantly, the crowd outside their residence erupted in thunderous cheers.

At the same time.

Deep within the White Hair Trench aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The fishman youngster reluctantly withdrew his gaze, “As Teacher Si Ling said, a god level bloodline is qualified to bear divinity.”

“Even if I possess that qualification, I can't snatch divinity from Teacher Si Ling's grasp.”

"The matter with Dao Hen has been settled. Judging by the timing, the big guy should be boarding soon, right?"

“I wonder how things are going with Zi Di and Zong Ge.”

“Time to get moving...”

The fishman youngster had already discovered a way for himself and the Deep Sea Monster Fish to teleport away instantly.

That method involved using the divine artifact!

The core ability of the divine spell was to vastly amplify all Charming Blue divine spells.

By using the divine artifact as a foundation and then casting Divine Domain Descent, he could transport both himself and the ship away.

The fishman youngster's plan was this: First, have the big guy and the others board the ship. Then, midway, have the big guy secretly enter the sea.

Once in the water, the big guy would cast Divine Domain Descent.

This would create a passageway between the father and son.

The fishman youngster would then escape with the Deep Sea Monster Fish and Cang Xu, successfully reuniting with the big guy.

Yes, this was the reunion plan the fishman youngster had reassured the big guy about.

What the fishman youngster didn't know was that a crisis had already struck the big guy's side.

Da Dou, the big guy, and Hong Zhu had all been captured by imperial spies!

Just as the fishman youngster prepared to contact the big guy again, god's will descended once more, delivering a second revelation—

Steal the death divinity from Teacher Si Ling's grasp!

Chapter 869: Rope Pirates

The fishman youngster's heart skipped a beat.

This wasn't the first time he'd received a divine revelation from Charming Blue Goddess.

The first time had been when he rescued the big guy, trying to save them from the encirclement of the earth elemental army. At the critical moment, it was precisely by following the Charming Blue Goddess' divine revelation that the fishman youngster successfully deceived the Main Earth Elemental God.

Truthfully, that first divine revelation still sent shivers down his spine.

The second time he received the revelation, he accepted it rather quickly, feeling calmer than he had anticipated.

“Come to think of it, it was Charming Blue Goddess’ and You Shen's collaboration that built this super-level magic formation.”

“Charming Blue Goddess must have known You Shen's plan, to collect and condense the death divinity here.”

“So, it's only natural that She coveted this divinity too.”

Their relationship had never been close; their collaboration stemmed from the harsh external pressures they both faced.

"As Teacher Si Ling mentioned earlier, divinity is a crucial commodity for deities. The undead orthodoxy intends to trade this death divinity with the Death God."

"Death divinity..."

The fishman youngster's Adam's apple involuntarily bobbed.

The craving within his heart grew ever more intense with each passing moment.

“I've deceived even the Main Earth Elemental God once, what's deceiving Teacher Si Ling?”

"I've arranged everything else. Dao Hen, Hun Tong, Xiao Guai, Da Dou... all fronts are secure."

“Let's steal the divinity first, then leave this place.”

A powerful intuition surged within the fishman youngster. It told him the death divinity held immense benefit for him!

What he didn't know was that while he focused on Dao Hen's side and worked to boost Hun Tong's morale, trouble had erupted with the big guy.

The big guy, Da Dou, and Hong Zhu had been rounded up.

Their bodies limp and weak, they were tightly bound by three or five types of alchemy restraints and secretly loaded onto a sea vessel.

The imperial spy codenamed Su Xing watched the ship preparing to set sail, his brow furrowed as he cautioned, “Be extremely cautious this time.”

The escort in charge of the big guy and his group was none other than the person-in-charge who had accompanied Tu Ji on their search for them in the Magnet Archipelago.

He nodded firmly. “This time, I won't make the same mistake.”

Su Xing patted his shoulder. “Tu Ji died in the Magnet Archipelago. Even with the Main Earth Elemental God's intervention, you're still in deep trouble.”

"I specifically assigned you this mission. Earning merit is secondary. My greater hope is that you can quickly establish contact with the Empire's investigators."

The Magnet Archipelago's person-in-charge nodded. “Fortunately, this time the investigator is Tan Mo.”

"He's greedy to the core, though skilled at demanding bribes, he's also reliable. Once paid, he gets things done!"

Su Xing nodded, “If you satisfy him, at least the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's investigation report will be favorable to you.”

“Ah, but this time, the Magnet Archipelago's overall situation requires my personal oversight. I can't break free just yet.”

The Magnet Archipelago's person-in-charge quickly responded with understanding: "Rest assured, Lord Su Xing. You've given me such a generous bounty this time. When I meet Tan Mo, I'll present these treasures to him. His regard for you will surely multiply!"

"Very well, very well. I trust your judgment. Otherwise, I wouldn't have appointed you to handle affairs here in the first place."

“You were defeated by your own misfortune.”

“You had the misfortune of encountering a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, and the even greater misfortune that he perished in battle.”

Su Xing offered a few words of consolation.

The Magnet Archipelago's person-in-charge snorted coldly: “Those from the Blood Light Punishment Institution are all madmen. Facing such reckless fools who disregard life itself, I can only accept my own misfortune!”

Su Xing patted his shoulder: "Enough said. It's time for you to set sail!"

“Yes, Lord.”

This time, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency fleet consisted of only three ships. On the surface, they were merely iron level vessels, but in reality, they were silver level ships equipped with gold level alchemy components.

Su Xing watched the small fleet depart into the distance. After a long moment, he turned and headed back.

These three captives, the big guy and the others, were crucial and needed to be delivered. For the Magnet Archipelago region was about to become very turbulent.

Imperial spies would stir the pot from the shadows, helping adventurers form groups to wage war against the earth elemental tribes of the Magnet Archipelago.

When the entire archipelago erupted in turmoil, guarding these three vital prisoners would become exponentially more difficult.

Though a fortress had been built on Feng Awakening Island, its proximity to the Magnet Archipelago meant it remained vulnerable should the Main Earth Elemental God turn its attention there again.

Besides the three, the ship was also loaded with large quantities of earth elemental magic crystals, earth elemental essences, magnetite, and other goods.

Shortly after the three imperial spy ships departed the Magnet Archipelago, a pirate crew set their sights on them.

A pirate flag fluttered in the wind.

The flag featured a pure white background, crisscrossed by numerous black lines woven into the pattern of crossed swords.

This was the Rope Pirates, a relatively newly formed crew.

The pirates had two leaders: Magician Huo Sheng and Archer Chuan Yun.

They had once been renowned bounty hunters, but with the emergence of the Pirate King's throne and the escalating piracy, they too had turned to a life of piracy.

“The Greed God Bone is reacting!” Archer Chuan Yun stared excitedly at the bone in his hand.

Through his magic crystal ball, Magician Huo Sheng observed the three imperial spy ships: “All three are magic ships. They look ordinary enough, yet they hide treasures within.”

A greedy gleam flashed in Archer Chuan Yun's eyes: The Magnet Archipelago is in turmoil. Adventurers are mobilizing to eradicate the earth elemental tribes there.

“Yet these men are leaving the islands at this critical moment. Something fishy is definitely afoot.”

“They're only three iron level ships. What are we waiting for?”

It turned out that these two, armed with the Greed God Bone that could pinpoint the most valuable items within range, had been constantly on the move.

And they'd made numerous successful hauls.

They'd unexpectedly overheard rumors of upheaval on the Magnet Archipelago, strongly suspecting a great treasure was about to surface, which is why they'd come to the vicinity.

The pair had previously landed on the Magnet Archipelago. Back then, they'd used the Greed God Bone but found nothing.

The rumors deliberately spread by the Empire's secret agents had ignited new hope within them. They reasoned that their previous failure to detect anything was likely due to the treasure being sealed or concealed, blocking the bone's sensing. Now, things were different.

They commanded their pirate crew not to rush ashore but to observe and wait.

After all, they were pirates. A rash landing carried a significant risk of provoking a joint assault from adventurers.

During this period of observation and waiting, they spotted three imperial spy ships.

Archer Chuan Yun was itching to act, but Magician Huo Sheng remained cautious: “Let me cast a spell first to probe their strength.”

After the probe, Magician Huo Sheng suppressed his excitement: “The defenses on these three magic ships are nothing special. Let's go all out and seize them!!"

The Rope Pirates charged aboard with fierce momentum.

On the imperial spy ship, their leader frowned slightly: “We've barely left the island, and pirates have already spotted us?”

“Is my luck really this bad lately?”

"Hmph, the Rope Pirates? If you want to die, then come on."

Magician Huo Sheng couldn't see through the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's disguise. When he cast his scouting spell, he triggered the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's alarm system, exposing himself first.

As secret agents, their specialty was intelligence gathering.

Therefore, when the Rope Pirates moved, they immediately confirmed their opponents' identities.

The three imperial spy ships accelerated their course.

“Stay slightly away from Magnet Archipelago. Engage near Feng Awakening Island.” The leader began laying out the tactics.

On one hand, they wanted to avoid fighting within Magnet Archipelago to prevent unnecessary attention.

On the other hand, they couldn't stray too far. If the battle turned unfavorable, they could still call upon the strength of nearby allies.

Thus, the vicinity of Feng Awakening Island was the optimal battlefield choice.

“They're running!”

“Hahahaha! Run! See where you can run to!”

The pirates grew increasingly excited, rubbing their hands together as morale soared.

Moments later, they caught up to the three imperial spy ships.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The battle erupted, and they were stunned by the imperial spies' explosive firepower.

Though outnumbered, the pirates were immediately suppressed.

This only fueled their savagery.

Archer Chuan Yun bellowed orders to charge forward for a boarding action.

He and Magician Huo Sheng launched a coordinated assault, one firing arrows, the other casting spells, attacking the imperial spies' ships from afar.

The imperial spies fought while retreating, maintaining their distance at all times.

After a brief charge, the pirates failed to board their ships. Worse, they were caught in concentrated fire during the assault, losing two more vessels.

Hordes of pirates leaped overboard in panic, throwing the scene into utter chaos.

Their morale plummeted.

“Bosses, these enemies are no joke. Let's get out of here!”

“Damn it, never expected them to be such tough nuts to crack.”

Numerous pirates had already volunteered to retreat.

Magician Huo Sheng and Archer Chuan Yun both wore grim expressions. Neither possessed leadership skills to rally their men, and their pirates were of low caliber. They could handle favorable battles, but when things turned sour, retreat was their first instinct.

“Then... retreat.” Magician Huo Sheng sighed and gave the order decisively.

Archer Chuan Yun's face turned ashen, but he offered no objection.

But just then, two gold level fighters suddenly launched a surprise attack from beneath the sea, scaling their ship and charging toward the two leaders.

This sudden turn of events sent both men into a panic, and they scrambled to retreat.

Soon, the naval battle concluded.

The entire pirate crew was annihilated, suffering heavy casualties.

Survivors were captured by imperial spies and enslaved to continue fortress construction on Feng Awakening Island.

Archer Chuan Yun perished in battle, while Magician Huo Sheng was taken alive.

As a spellcaster, he would be a high-quality slave!

Despite the victory, the fleet commander remained deeply troubled: “The pirates are growing increasingly brazen.”

“Lately, fueled by the sorrow-dispelling red wine, these sea hyenas have been roaming with wild abandon.”

“To be safe, we should still employ artificial ocean current boosters.”

Chapter 870: First Test

Artificial ocean current boosters were large-scale alchemy devices capable of artificially generating temporary currents between two points.

Ships navigating these currents sailed as if on a fast lane.

Though activating such devices consumed significant resources, they saved immense amounts of time.

After much deliberation, the spy chief resolutely chose this method to circumvent greater risks.

His concerns were not unfounded.

The Divine Wind Pirates had been blocked.

The Thorn Pirates' captain stood astride the foremast, hands on hips, bellowing, “Feng Lian, show yourself!”

Feng Lian stepped forward.

Tall and lean, he wore a bronze mask, wielded a scythe with a blade nearly two meters long, and was cloaked in white feathers.

Jing Wen puffed out his chest, pointing a thumbed finger at himself. “Feng Lian, your luck has turned!”

“Our leader, Da Han, has taken a liking to you and invites you to join us.”

"You wiped out the Intoxication Church's fleet; you must've seized plenty of sorrow-dispelling red wine. Our boss, Da Han, takes a keen interest in that wine. You know what to do."

Silence fell. No one spoke.

The atmosphere grew awkward.

Jing Wen felt belittled, his eye twitching. “Hey, Feng Lian, are you deaf?”

Feng Lian finally spoke, his voice hoarse: “Just now, what did you say your name was?”

Jing Wen flared with anger: “I'm Jing Wen! Just like you, I'm a gold level!”

Feng Lian pressed further: “You claim to represent Da Han. Are you part of the Fire Beard Pirates?”

Jing Wen's expression darkened, his voice dropping: "Not yet. But I'm following Da Han's orders closely. Joining the Fire Beard Pirates is only a matter of time..."

Feng Lian snorted coldly: “If you're not, how dare you brag in front of me?”

“Do you realize you've already put yourself in a deadly situation?”

The words had barely left his mouth when Jing Wen saw Feng Lian and his scythe blur into a haze.

The next instant, everything returned to normal.

Jing Wen thought he'd seen things and dismissed it.

He pointed at Feng Lian, cursing, “You brat! We're both gold level, what are you showing off for... Huh?”

The next moment, pain surged through Jing Wen.

He instinctively looked down at his chest, where excruciating agony radiated.

Pop.

Blood gushed violently, staining Jing Wen's vision crimson.

Staring fixedly at his chest, he saw a massive gash slashed diagonally across his torso.

The wound ran long, from left to right.

Then, Jing Wen watched his body slowly slide sideways and sink downward. His head and neck section crashed directly onto the deck.

Pfft... pfft...

His lower half remained standing, blood gushing relentlessly from the severed abdomen.

Blood spurted like a fountain.

Jing Wen had been cleaved clean in two!

When did this happen?

“When?”

“I... I actually...”

Jing Wen's face contorted with terror, his pupils shrinking to pinpricks before plunging into endless darkness.

“Boss!”

“Captain!”

“Oh no! The captain's been cut in two!”

The pirates behind Jing Wen erupted in a chorus of gasps and cries.

Some seethed with rage, ready to strike back. Others retreated in sheer terror, their flight evident.

The pirates, moments ago brimming with fury, now descended into chaos.

In stark contrast, the Divine Wind Pirates' morale soared. They roared in unison, drew their weapons, and charged forward at the first mate's command.

The two ships were already close together, allowing many transcendent pirates to leap directly across, slashing at anyone in sight.

Feng Lian stood motionless, clutching his long-handled sickle.

He remained as still as a mountain peak, letting his subordinates surge past him like a raging tide. The contrast between movement and stillness was stark.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Formless.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Instant.

Feng Lian unleashed both battle skills simultaneously.

But that wasn't the crucial point.

His gaze lingered on his wrist.

Once bare, it now bore an alchemy bracelet.

“This aura-concealing artifact is surprisingly effective,” Feng Lian silently praised.

It was spoils seized from the enemy after he had annihilated the fleet of the Intoxication Church's fleet.

Soon, the battle before Feng Lian concluded.

Jing Wen's pirate crew's morale had collapsed completely, offering no organized resistance.

In an instant, some died, others surrendered.

Of course, some chose death over submission.

Like the first mate and second mate.

Forced to their knees, the first deck was already soaked in their blood.

The first mate glared at Feng Lian with hatred. "Feng Lian, do you know what you're doing? "

“We served Da Han! You slaughtered so many of us, offending Da Han himself. You're doomed!”

The second mate added, "Hahahaha. We die first; you die later. But your end will be far more miserable than ours."

Among pirates, there were indeed many such stubborn, fearless brutes.

Feng Lian looked at them and spoke calmly: I've never heard of this Jing Wen fellow. But seeing the two of you, he does seem somewhat worthy."

“Compared to those spineless cowards who surrendered, I admire you more.”

“Take my next words back to Da Han.”

"Tell him that Feng Lian here does indeed possess a batch of sorrow-dispelling red wine. If he wants it, he'd better come negotiate with me personally."

“Just because he drops a line and sends a few men, he expects me to hand over the wine?”

“Does he think he's Fire Beard?”

Feng Lian stood firm, his resolve unshaken, showing no fear toward the saint level Da Han.

Soon after, he sailed away triumphantly with his spoils, leaving behind only a single ship and a handful of survivors, the first mate, second mate, and scattered stragglers.

"He really let us off." The second mate watched the Divine Wind Pirates depart, dazed.

He hadn't expected to survive.

Ironically, those who surrendered earlier had all been killed. Only the die-hard resistors remained.

“Hah—!” The first mate spat out a mouthful of blood-tinged phlegm. "That coward Feng Lian, killing our captain with a sneak attack! "

“Wait for death. Once we report this to Lord Da Han... Ugh.”

The next instant, a wind blade sliced through the air.

The first mate was cleaved in two, from head to toe.

He was dead.

The second mate jolted in terror and immediately ordered the ship to set sail.

The sea fell silent once more.

Both combatants had vanished, leaving only shattered hull fragments, sunken corpses, and an ocean stained crimson.

Blood swirled within the cup.

Zi Di scrutinized its composition, her focus razor-sharp.

This was the Ice Sculpture Capital, the Alchemy Guild's dedicated examination hall.

Zi Di wasn't the only alchemist taking this round of tests. Dozens of other alchemists surrounded her, each engaged in their own alchemy work.

Twelve examiners patrolled the hall, maintaining order throughout the testing grounds.

The subject of this assessment was one of the classic alchemy trials—Extreme Synthesis.

The Alchemy Guild distributed identical quantities and weights of alchemy materials to every examinee. The requirement seemed simple but was actually quite difficult: using alchemy synthesis methods, create as many different types of items as possible within the allotted time.

A key point was that it had to be synthesis alchemy, not decomposition or degradation methods.

Zi Di observed for a moment, confirming the blood water in the cup met the standards. She then poured a 4-gram piece of pure white crystal into the cup.

The crystal touched the blood, immediately triggering a minor explosion.

The blast was so small it produced only a faint crackling sound.

As the explosion occurred, a thick red mist rose.

Zi Di quickly stepped back to avoid it.

When the red mist dissipated, both the blood and the white crystal in the glass beaker had vanished completely. In their place lay a thin layer of finely ground brown sugar.

“The blood sugar is refined!” A look of joy spread across Zi Di's face.

This was the final step in her alchemy plan, the most complex and challenging part. Yet she succeeded in her very first attempt.

“This assessment is going exceptionally well!” Zi Di assessed her progress.

She then cataloged all the items she had refined.

“Sunlight petals and moon shadow grass stems synthesized into a thermoregulating stone, capable of adjusting temperature.”

“Moon shadow grass stems ground into juice, combined with purple jade, yielded purple crystal powder for inducing sleep.”

“Hallucinogenic mushrooms and purple crystal powder synthesized into a hallucinogenic floral scent.”

“Blending firebee tails and flame demon scales produced blazing powder.”

“Distilling shadow blossom and night dew yields a single drop of shadow essence.”...

…

Including the freshly concocted blood sugar, Zi Di had produced a total of 37 alchemy creations.

On the surface, the exam seemed to test only alchemy technique. But in reality, it encompassed many aspects.

Take alchemy recipes, for instance. Without knowing or accurately recalling all 37 recipes, producing this many creations would have been impossible.

Beyond that lay the hands-on alchemy execution.

How to decompose diverse materials, make precise adjustments, control reactions, handle deviations, these intricate details demanded profound skill.

Next came the design of alchemy trial procedures. With limited assessment time, maximizing output required meticulous time management and step sequencing. For instance, the most frequently used sunlight petals could be processed in bulk during the initial stage. Processing methods included drying, grinding, and puffing, each yielding specific quantities with clear requirements.

Only by mastering these could one save time from every tiny crack. These minute time savings, when accumulated, became significant.

After her calculations, Zi Di ceased alchemy.

Glancing at the hall clock, she knew the hour was nearly upon them. The remaining time was insufficient for any meaningful refinement.

Moreover, her materials were running low.

Soon, the clock chimed: ding-dong-ding. Present alchemists sighed in regret or jerked their hands away as if electrocuted.

The frosted glass panels around each alchemy table descended, revealing the others’ creations.

Zi Di was slightly taken aback.

“Even the least accomplished among them produced over ten varieties. The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy techniques truly are world-class.”

“Had I not received the assignment in advance and prepared beforehand, I doubt I could have managed even ten.”

Chapter 871: Ban Tu Discusses

At that moment, an examiner's voice commanded the alchemists to exit the hall in an orderly fashion.

As they filed out in a line, Zi Di overheard others conversing in the elven tongue.

“The first assessment task was unexpectedly difficult?”

“Indeed, far harder than previous years.”

"Sigh, I made a mistake. I used far too many raw materials trying to refine toad dung. Honestly, I could have used far fewer materials to refine the other three substances instead."

Zi Di listened patiently, saying nothing.

Combining this with what she had glimpsed earlier, she was certain her performance likely placed her among the top three in the hall.

As she stepped out of the Alchemy Guild, someone approached her.

It was the steward of the Li Jian Clan.

“Congratulations, congratulations, Magician Yao Ma! Your alchemy results this time are quite impressive. With such an achievement, you're certain to pass the first round,” the steward offered his congratulations.

The Li Jian Clan truly lived up to its reputation as one of the kingdom's top powers. Having placed spies within the Alchemy Guild, they could effortlessly gather intelligence on Zi Di's assessment.

Zi Di's expression remained composed. "The difficulty of the assessment exceeded my expectations. This is only the first round. The real challenges lie ahead."

Her results were achieved through cheating.

Though her performance was outstanding, it brought her even greater psychological pressure.

"My alchemy talent isn't exceptional, and my techniques remain unrefined. I once thought my skills were sufficient, but stepping onto this level of competition immediately revealed a vast gap."

“Sigh, my innate talent was simply too poor.”

Zi Di's original aptitude was limited. It wasn't that she lacked the will to strive, but her innate gifts restricted her, many things were beyond her reach no matter how hard she tried.

This world was so cruel and hopeless. Some people yearned to improve themselves but were denied the chance. Because for most, the very right to strive was stripped away at birth.

Without a good bloodline, what's the point of striving?

But now, Zi Di had hope.

“After bloodline purification, my body's bloodline reached gold level.”

“If I can deepen my utilization of the Bloodcore in the future, I might even replace my bloodline.”

"All this is thanks to you, but where are you now? Why haven't you returned yet?"

At this thought, Zi Di couldn't help but feel a pang of longing.

She quickly gathered her thoughts and turned to the Li Jian Clan's steward: "Did your invitation succeed? I hope you're bringing me good news."

The noble steward smiled and nodded: "We haven't disappointed you. Lord Ban Tu has been summoned. But he's only granted you three minutes for conversation."

Zi Di nodded. “Then let's go see him at once.”

Ever since learning of the alchemy breakthroughs, such as the sorrow-dispelling red wine and time ice cube from Elder Jiu Jin, Zi Di had been deeply intrigued.

These alchemy creations could drastically reduce leveling time, making them ideal for the dragonman youngster.

In Zi Di's heart, her beloved commander was still only of silver level.

Magician Ban Tu was the mastermind behind this alchemy project. Zi Di had once extended an invitation but Ban Tu hadn't even glanced at it.

Primarily because news of this alchemy breakthrough had leaked, causing Ban Tu's status and reputation to skyrocket. He was now meeting with countless high-ranking figures daily, how could he possibly spare time for a mere silver level magician from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

With no other option, Zi Di turned to the Li Jian Clan to arrange a meeting with Ban Tu.

True to their reputation as one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's most powerful factions, the Li Jian Clan successfully secured Ban Tu's presence.

Not long after.

Inside an upscale restaurant, Zi Di met Ban Tu.

Magician Ban Tu appeared decrepit; his face etched with deep wrinkles. His hair resembled a tuft of gray, dry straw—dull and brittle, seemingly untouched by a comb.

He merely glanced at Zi Di as she sat down, then ignored her entirely.

Ban Tu fixed his gaze on the blue liquid in his wine glass: “For the sake of this blue wine, get to the point.”

Zi Di stated plainly, “I wish to purchase the sorrow-dispelling red wine, along with the complementary method for using the time ice cube.”

Ban Tu lifted his eyes to Zi Di and shook his head slowly. "You? That seems rather unlikely."

Zi Di smiled. “Then please, Lord Ban Tu, take a good look at the bargaining chips I can offer.”

With that, she retrieved a set of documents, placed them on the table, and pushed them toward Ban Tu.

Ban Tu picked up the two thin sheets of paper and scanned them.

Almost instantly, the disdain vanished from his face. His eyes lit up with intense interest.

He examined the papers several times before asking, “You truly possess the recipes for these wines?”

Zi Di smiled: “Lord Ban Tu, even if you don't trust me, surely you trust the Li Jian Clan?”

Ban Tu instinctively glanced at the Li Jian Clan steward standing in the corner and gave a slight nod.

His reaction was exactly as Zi Di had anticipated.

These wine recipes naturally came from War Merchant's database.

Ban Tu himself was a wine enthusiast, possessing an innate passion for fine liquors. He was also a believer of the Intoxication Church, though his exact position within the cult remained unclear, it was undoubtedly high.

Presenting these recipes to the Intoxication Church would undoubtedly be a significant contribution.

After all, the church worshipped none other than the Wine God.

Zi Di was confident that presenting these recipes would secure her the sorrow-dispelling red wine.

Yet the next moment, she heard Ban Tu sigh.

He shook his head slowly, his gaze lingering on the recipes with a mix of longing and regret. “We cannot give you even a single bottle of sorrow-dispelling red wine right now.”

“Why?” Zi Di froze for a moment before asking.

Ban Tu's face showed hesitation.

But soon, he sighed again: "Never mind. The news has already leaked out; soon the whole capital will know. I'll just tell you directly."

“The sorrow-dispelling red wine isn't something ordinary alchemists like us can create, because its main ingredient is the blood of the wine god!”

"Our church poured every effort into crafting 600 bottles for the royal banquet. Unexpectedly, the fleet was raided by pirates!"

“What?!” Zi Di gasped.

These past days, she'd been fully immersed in exam preparations, oblivious to external events.

This news had actually been deliberately suppressed for some time by the Li Jian Clan and other factions. After all, it concerned the cooperation between the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and the Intoxication Church.

Therefore, this intelligence had been officially classified by the Ice Sculpture authorities as “unverified.”

Only now, with the Intoxication Church in Drunkard Harbor desperately seeking aid and reinforcements everywhere, had it become impossible to conceal, forcing them to acknowledge it.

Zi Di listened intently as Ban Tu revealed more detailed intelligence.

“Feng Lian?” Zi Di was surprised, not expecting the thief who stole this batch of wine to be someone they knew.

“In that case...” she lowered her voice, “we're also interested in purchasing the research records and specific alchemy formula for this alchemy achievement.”

Ban Tu looked surprised. “You want to brew it yourselves?”

He shook his head. “Do you have the blood of the wine god?”

Zi Di asked, “Couldn't we modify the formula and replace that god level ingredient?”

Ban Tu chuckled. “It's the most essential component. I shan't elaborate on the specifics.”

The elf's pride showed through in that moment.

Zi Di immediately replied, "I understand. All knowledge holds value. I can offer just one of the wine recipes contained within this document. I humbly request Lord Ban Tu enlighten me."

A sharp gleam flashed in Ban Tu's eyes: “Two recipes!”

Zi Di shook her head. Though she held many recipes, she refused to concede so readily. This was merely the opening phase of negotiations; yielding too readily would only fuel the opponent's greed.

Thus, Zi Di argued her case, employing every rhetorical tactic to persist.

Ban Tu, not one skilled in bargaining, soon waved his hand. “Enough. One recipe it is.”

“I'll tell you this much, it involves divinity.”

“Divinity?” Zi Di pressed for clarification.

After explaining, Ban Tu continued: “It's precisely because the blood of the wine god contains divinity that the sorrow-dispelling red wine achieves such astonishing effects.”

“You surely know that buried at the deepest point of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake lies the corpse of a god level dragon.”

“The reason the ice water within the lake possesses temporal power is due to the constant diffusion and erosion of the time divinity emanating from that dragon corpse.”

“Only the power of the wine god's divinity can neutralize the reflected power of the time divinity within the time ice cube.”

"This was the primary reason my earlier attempt to brew the [Beautiful Future] potion failed. I simply couldn't afford god level ingredients!"

Zi Di's eyes widened in astonishment.

Her previous understanding had been far too limited, as divinity was the secret of the gods themselves.

According to Ban Tu, he hadn't known about it either until he personally participated in the Ice Cube Red Wine alchemy project, which afforded him the opportunity to learn about it.

Upon gaining this knowledge, Zi Di immediately realized she had offered too low a price.

This knowledge wasn't even recorded in the War Merchant's database, perhaps it had been lost? After all, the records preserved by the tower spirit were incomplete.

Only to Ban Tu, in the eyes of this wine connoisseur, did the wine formula hold immense value and irresistible allure.

Having pinpointed this weakness in Ban Tu, Zi Di seized the opportunity immediately. During subsequent negotiations, she made it clear she intended to purchase the alchemy technique for creating the Ice Cube Red Wine. Even without the sorrow-dispelling red wine, she would buy it.

“You can't afford it,” Ban Tu shook his head.

Zi Di merely smiled, producing another document. “Lord Ban Tu, perhaps you should review this.”

Within seconds, Ban Tu's eyes widened in shock.
