He struggled, hesitated, his expression torn. “This project's success isn't solely my achievement; it involves members of the Intoxication Church.”

“Moreover, this formula holds immense importance to the Intoxication Church...”

Zi Di countered, "Compared to the blood of the wine god, is the formula truly that vital? Why guard it so fiercely?"

“Lord Ban Tu, doesn't your prestige and standing within the Intoxication Church hold enough sway to seal this deal?”

“Is this wine recipe truly non-negotiable? Are you genuinely unwilling to sell it?”

Ban Tu's brow furrowed deeply. “Come to think of it, no one in the church has ever explicitly prohibited it.”

His eyes remained fixed on the thin sheets of paper, motionless, his Adam's apple occasionally rolling as if he were already savoring the exquisite wine described in the documents.

Zi Di took in this scene, her confidence growing.

She pressed on, continuing her persuasion.

Ban Tu's expression shifted, his hesitation deepening, yet he still couldn't make up his mind.

Chapter 872: Stealing Divinity!

Zi Di seemed to see through Ban Tu, saying, “Perhaps this is a loophole.”

“This formula for the sorrow-dispelling red wine, along with the specific method for using time ice cubes, are major secrets of the church.”

“But Lord Ban Tu, you currently have no knowledge of this fact.”

"Even if you sell it, should the sect pursue you later, you can easily shift the blame onto this point, no one told me this discovery couldn't be sold! "

“In other words, future transactions would likely prove difficult.”

“For tomorrow, the Intoxication Church might patch this oversight and declare it non-negotiable.”

“Thus, your opportunity is now, and only now.”

“It may well be a once-in-a-lifetime chance.”

Ban Tu, whose mood had been shifting like the weather, finally hardened at these words that struck his core. Slamming his palm on the table, he made his decision: “I'll sell it!”

Zi Di smiled, countering unexpectedly: "Are you truly certain? This is actually a very risky endeavor."

Ban Tu paused, then shook his head. “Enough talk. My mind is made up!”

“I might regret it someday.”

“But if I don't make this deal, I'll regret it right now.”

“Hand over the complete recipe immediately!”

Zi Di: “No rush. Let's formalize the contract first.”

Though Zi Di was given only three minutes to negotiate, the final deal took well over half an hour to seal.

With the complete recipe in hand, Ban Tu hurried away.

Zi Di was about to leave when the steward of the Li Jian Clan followed to see her off.

The steward said, "Magician Yao Ma, congratulations. Your gains this time are truly immense!"

Having observed from the sidelines, the steward knew full well that Zi Di had made a huge profit on this deal.

Zi Di smiled faintly.

How many who had their weaknesses exposed during her negotiations had ended well?

The most recent example was Elder Jiu Jin.

Even now, he was still risking everything to secretly leak exam questions to Zi Di.

The steward continued, “Actually, Magician Yao Ma, today brings you double good fortune.”

"We've just received internal news from the Alchemy Guild, the results of today's assessment have been finalized. Your performance was head and shoulders above the rest. You passed the first stage with a commanding lead."

“I never imagined your alchemy talent, Magician Yao Ma, to be so exceptional.”

"This truly surpasses my expectations. If I were in charge, how could I dare assign such an outstanding alchemist to travel afar?"

Zi Di's expression remained calm.

She knew full well these compliments didn't match reality.

She had cheated.

Having known the questions in advance, she'd secretly prepared for a long time, practicing relentlessly. She had even had the designed alchemy steps verified by Elder Jiu Jin.

Zi Di said, "To be honest, the difficulty of the Warm Snow Cup Competition has surprised me. I've only passed the first question so far. Many more challenges await me. It's too early to offer congratulations now."

The steward then remarked meaningfully, “Truthfully, Magician Yao Ma, you needn't have gone to such lengths.”

“Participating in the Warm Snow Cup isn't crucial.”

“Truthfully, your objectives are already within reach, aren't they?”

The steward slowed his pace: “Because our two parties continue to deepen our collaboration...”

Zi Di's expression shifted slightly, her demeanor transforming from calm to serious. She chose her words carefully: “Your family wishes to deepen our collaboration?”

“Precisely!” The steward promptly produced a thick dossier.

Unlike Zi Di's documents, this file was substantial, densely packed with intricate cooperation clauses.

Zi Di swiftly located the pivotal section.

A 30-70 split.

The Li Jian Clan voluntarily relinquished profit, pledging to take only 30% of future earnings from deepened collaborations, directly ceding the remaining 70% to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

What immense sincerity!

Beyond that, Zi Di saw the Li Jian Clan was willing to provide numerous alchemy techniques.

These represented the cutting edge of Ice Sculpture Kingdom's technology, items previously deemed non-negotiable in past transactions. Yet now, the Li Jian Clan was proactively offering them all.

“Sole partnership?” Zi Di raised an eyebrow slightly.

This was the most critical stipulation in the Li Jian Clan's demands.

The Li Jian Clan demanded that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must collaborate with them, and only them in all future business ventures!

Zi Di carefully tucked away the documents, her face breaking into a bitter smile. "This is far too significant a matter for me to decide. I must report back immediately."

She gave an excuse first.

The steward nodded. “As expected.”

Then, he smiled and bowed to Zi Di. “We shall await your good news.”

Zi Di nodded to him and walked away alone.

Walking through the bustling street, Zi Di spotted a magic projection at the corner.

It was an advertisement for the two potions she had produced: [Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation].

The ad was cleverly designed.

A dozen people were racing sleds. It was a speed competition, with no potions allowed.

One competitor fell behind, then fed his reindeer sled driver consecutive doses of [Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation]. The reindeer's speed surged, propelling him into first place before crossing the finish line as the victor.

“[Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation], potions crafted exclusively for mounts.”

“Such potent effects, such silky acceleration!”

“Keep you accelerating, step by step ahead, far ahead, far ahead!”

The slogan “far ahead” echoed through the streets, its reverberating effect seeming to burrow into people's minds.

Zi Di sighed inwardly. She knew the Li Jian Clan's tactics all too well.

The first time the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps collaborated with the Li Jian Clan, they introduced the dragon rider potion. In a market where most players were still clueless, they carved out a vast blue ocean.

The profits from this niche mount potion market astonished and thrilled everyone.

The second time, for Zong Ge, Zi Di introduced [Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation]. Any discerning eye could see the value of these potions. Armed with the success of their first venture, the Li Jian Clan executed production and sales with greater maturity and on a much larger scale this time.

Leveraging Long Fu (Zong Ge)'s public duel with Bing Yang, sales of the new potions ignited like oil on a raging fire, far surpassing those of the dragon rider potion.

Zi Di understood perfectly: "On the one hand, we were the first to launch these on the market with no competitors. On the other hand, most people use mounts primarily for speed. [Dragon Rider] boosts mount strength, while [Spirit Stimulation] and [Smooth Stride] increase movement speed. That's the real selling point."

“Two major successes are enough to make the Li Jian Clan take us extremely seriously.”

"And my performance on the first question of this alchemy competition was nothing short of astonishing. Combined with Commander Long Fu's popularity from the duel, if this trend continues, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could completely break away from the Li Jian Clan and go solo."

Precisely because of this, the Li Jian Clan felt a sense of crisis.

They couldn't let go of the profits from their current partnership, yet they coveted even greater profits from future collaborations!

“The drawbacks of deep cooperation have become apparent.”

“What was once a helping hand is gradually turning into a hindrance.”

Zi Di sighed inwardly.

Delaying was merely a reluctant choice.

The reason the Li Jian Clan hadn't acted was simply because they were intimidated by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' constant display of strength.

Of course, there was another crucial factor.

That was Zong Ge's decisive use of the pearl bubbles earlier, achieving the undeniable feat of defeating three gold level opponents in one fell swoop. The Li Jian Clan had long been aware of this.

“How much longer can we possibly delay this time?”

“Ultimately, it boils down to our own lack of strength.”

Zi Di missed the dragonman youngster intensely.

“If the Lord Commander were here, he could use the Red Wine Ice Cube combination to rapidly advance to gold level.”

“That would greatly alleviate the current situation.”

The situation continued to evolve.

The pressure from the duelists had lessened considerably as Zi Di and Zong Ge smoothly entered the royal capital, and as Mei Lin and the others were drawn into the national ceremony, pirate affairs, and Shuang Lian.

Bing Yang was the offensive they had brewed up.

Yet the external force Zi Di had brought in, the Li Jian Clan, was gradually opening its mouth, bristling with sharp teeth, lured by the promise of rich profits.

Meanwhile, the young commander Zi Di held dear in her heart was busy stealing.

He was stealing divinity.

Divine spell - Divine Artifact Mastery.

Divine spell - Deceptive Scheming.

Divine spell - Opportunistic Deception.

Divine spell - Distortion Filter.

Divine spell - Divine Power Channel.

Guided by divine revelation, the fishman youngster maintained five divine spells.

Practice makes perfect. Armed with the experience of deceiving the Main Earth Elemental God last time, his actions this time were far more composed. The pressure he felt was also significantly less than before.

“Perhaps it's because the purpose is different.”

"Last time, Xiao Guai had to be saved at all costs. This time, it's merely the death divinity."

Though the divinity stirred intense craving and temptation within him, companionship remained paramount in the fishman youngster's heart.

Compared to his previous deception, the divine spells he employed this time differed slightly.

First, the divine spell [Believer Perception] was absent. With Teacher Si Ling within sight, there was no need to sense his presence through this art.

Divine spells like [Deceptive Scheming] and [Distortion Filter] remained active. Both divine spells proved exceptionally potent. Notably, this iteration of [Distortion Filter] targeted not Teacher Si Ling, but the artifact [Truth Slate] he carried!

This time, adding in [Opportunistic Deception] made the effect even greater.

As for [Divine Power Channel], it was present both before and after, but its purpose had shifted.

Previously, when saving Xiao Guai, the Divine Power Channel served to deepen their connection, facilitating the release of divine spells upon the big guy or those near him. This time, however, the Divine Power Channel was for transporting death divinity!

“It's coming,” the fishman youngster murmured silently.

He kept a close watch on the outside world.

Within the magic formation personally controlled by Teacher Si Ling, the light sphere shrank steadily until it stabilized at the size of an infant's fist.

The fist-sized orb blazed with an intensity blinding to ordinary eyes.

Yet within mere breaths, the light vanished entirely.

The fist-sized orb dissolved without trace, replaced by a single drop of liquid.

The liquid took the shape of a teardrop pendant, uniformly dark gray in color, emanating a gloomy aura devoid of any divinity.

It was the death divinity!

“Another drop,” Cang Xu sighed.

Jian Gui rejoiced: “This is already the second portion.”

At the same moment, aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, a similar drop of death divinity appeared within the artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale], held in the fishman youngster's hands.

Likewise, this was the second portion of death divinity the fishman youngster had stolen.

Teacher Si Ling could condense two drops at once, but the fishman youngster had secretly stolen one. After all, the other half of this magic formation was inscribed and constructed with Charming Blue divine script.

Combined with various divine spells, it was only natural that Teacher Si Ling had been deceived.

Teacher Si Ling carefully retrieved the Truth Slate, temporarily sealing the death divinity within it.

After completing these tasks, he paused, breathing heavily, to recuperate.

Meditation.

Consuming potions.

Casting spells to buff himself.

Only after completing these steps did Teacher Si Ling straighten up and resume controlling the massive formation.

Condensing divinity was extremely taxing, placing a heavy burden upon him.

Stealing divinity was far simpler.

It was like one person laboriously drawing water from a well, while another effortlessly scooped water from the bucket using a ladle.

The formation hummed once more, then erupted in brilliant radiance.

The light contracted, coalescing into a massive orb.

The orb slowly compressed again and again.

This step took the longest.

The fishman youngster had experience with this now.

After all, he'd witnessed it twice before.

When the orb shrank to the size of an infant's fist, its brilliance reached its peak. Then, the death divinity crystallized.

“Just how much death divinity can be condensed in the White Hair Trench?”

The fishman youngster felt a surge of impatience.

The time required to steal the death divinity exceeded his expectations.

He had initially thought this would be a one-time thing, but it turned out to be a process that needed repeating multiple times.

“At this very moment, Xiao Guai must be waiting impatiently on the ship, right?”

"Sigh..."

“I hope he's not too anxious. Just be patient.”

Chapter 873: Zong Ge vs Bing Yang – Publicly or Privately

Munch munch munch.

Inside the cabin, the big guy buried his head in his food, occasionally opening his wide mouth to stuff a whole ham hock in at once.

He ate with relish, his mouth glistening with grease.

“Some dessert, perhaps?” The Imperial Spy Chief smiled, presenting a tray of small cakes.

Beside the big guy sat Da Dou and Hong Zhu.

The sight of the cakes darkened both their expressions.

Da Dou snorted coldly, while Hong Zhu remarked, “Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea again? As the esteemed Imperial Secret Spy Agency, don't you have any new tricks up your sleeves?”

The leader smiled faintly. “We prioritize practicality.”

“Especially with you three, we have no intention of using force.”

“Such desserts are just right, wouldn't you agree?”

Da Dou and Hong Zhu could only resign themselves.

Earlier, they had not only witnessed but were invited to view the magic footage of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's battle against the Rope Pirates.

Thus, the trio knew these three ships appeared ordinary yet were lavishly equipped, with two gold level agents escorting them.

Even if their restraints were released, escape might prove impossible. Let alone now, when even walking was a struggle.

The leader narrowed his eyes. “Hurry up, all three of you. Don't keep me waiting too long.”

He began to urge them on.

“Right now, if escape is possible, only he stands a chance,” Da Dou thought to himself, casting an almost imperceptible glance at the big guy.

Hong Zhu shared his thoughts.

To conceal the big guy as their trump card, they had deliberately withheld much of what happened in the earth elemental tribe's underground lair.

Thus, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency remained unaware that the big guy had once undergone divine descent.

Had they known, his confinement would never have been the laxest among the three.

Yet the big guy's reaction was pure delight, his eyes lit up as he excitedly opened his huge hands, grabbed a handful of the small cakes, and shoved them straight into his mouth.

“Good.”

“Delicious!”

His cheeks puffed out as he mumbled his praise.

Admittedly, these little cakes were magic delicacies. Though their effects were negative, the taste was genuinely good.

Da Dou and Hong Zhu sighed inwardly, then resignedly picked up the cakes and ate them one after another.

The Imperial Spy Chief watched it all with a smile.

He was extremely cautious, personally overseeing this process every day. Though he had destroyed the Rope Pirates, the incident had only sharpened his vigilance, fearing he might make another mistake.

Even though Tu Ji's death wasn't his fault.

Da Dou and Hong Zhu finished their entire cakes, prolonging and solidifying the negative effects on their bodies.

Meanwhile, the big guy had already polished off all the leftovers.

He collapsed onto his back and fell asleep, his belly swelling like a small hill.

His body instinctively activated the battle qi technique, but the next moment, the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea effect kicked in. The big guy let out a deep sigh and collapsed completely limp.

This didn't affect his sleep, however. Instead, it made him sleep even more soundly and deeply.

“This big oaf has a pretty good attitude,” the Imperial Spy Chief marveled at the scene.

Da Dou and Hong Zhu were both speechless.

The big guy's days aboard the Hog's Kiss had been far worse than this. He could endure that, not to mention the treacherous Mystifying Monster Island. Now, though under escort, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency treated him to fine meals daily.

The big guy felt no panic.

He had long been assured by his father that they would meet halfway.

The big guy placed the deepest trust in the dragonman youngster, a trust that had deepened into something akin to faith.

Thus, feeling quite content, he cheerfully awaited his father’s arrival.

While Xiao Guai’s father was still stealing divinity, the day of Zong Ge and Bing Yang’s duel arrived first.

A large arena within the Ice Sculpture Capital.

Over 5,000 spectator seats were packed.

The crowd buzzed with chatter, some arguing over whether Bing Yang or Long Fu was stronger, others discussing how much they'd wagered on this match.

Among the audience, gamblers formed a substantial contingent.

He Gai Tou, concealing his identity under a hood, sat among the ordinary spectators.

He listened intently for a moment, a satisfied smile spreading across his face.

The sheer number of gamblers confirmed his efforts had paid off.

To build his betting pool, he had actively promoted the duel's prestige, touting it as the strongest silver level clash of the tournament. Simultaneously, he stoked ethnic sentiments. The fact that “Long Fu is a dragonman and Bing Yang is a snow elf” allowed him to exploit the situation to the fullest.

“The greatest regret is that although Long Fu publicly announced the duel, he didn't make it entirely public.”

“This duel prohibits the recording of magic footage. It won't be advertised externally, and the magic footage of this battle will never be sold to the public in the future.”

“Damn it, turning down money like that!”

Every time He Gai Tou thought about this, his mood soured.

Running the betting ring, he knew many gamblers had placed hefty wagers on both duelists. Would these gamblers be too poor to afford the ticket money?

Yet, Long Fu had only opened it up to this extent. The duel venue he'd chosen wasn't even one of those super-duel arenas. The number of spectator seats was relatively small even among large arenas.

The strict limit on in-person attendance significantly dampened gamblers' enthusiasm, stifling the growth of He Gai Tou's betting pool this time.

This dealt a severe blow to his profits.

Every time he thought about it, resentment flared within him.

As for Magician Yao Ma, Zi Di had taken her seat in the VIP section early.

Her seat was extremely close to the duel arena, offering an excellent view.

Zi Di scanned the packed crowd and sighed inwardly: "After all that picking and choosing, we ended up with this arena. Controlling the spectator numbers will also drastically reduce the consumption of pearl bubbles this time."

The reason they only opened a portion of the arena wasn't by choice, but sheer necessity.

Their pearl bubbles was nearly depleted!

Fewer spectators meant less deception required, preserving more pearl bubbles.

The ban on capturing magic images stemmed from the same necessity.

“Therefore, the stakes are even higher for Zong Ge in this battle.”

“Not only must he win decisively, but he must win swiftly!”

“The longer it drags on, the more pearl bubbles are consumed.”

As agreed between the two, after this public duel, Zong Ge, in his Long Fu impersonation, would temporarily announce a suspension of duels.

Publicly, they would claim: silver level opponents offered no challenge, and they had lost interest in continuing duels.

In reality, they were desperately short on pearl bubbles and needed to conserve it.

Zong Ge would avoid public appearances as much as possible afterward, reserving the remaining pearl bubbles for Zi Di's use.

Participation in the Warm Snow Cup was non-negotiable.

As long as Zi Di achieved strong results in the Warm Snow Cup and demonstrated her alchemy prowess, she would gain widespread recognition within the alchemy industry.

She would eventually become independent, running her own alchemy business, and thus be less likely to face exclusion or suppression from other alchemists.

She also had to break free from the Li Jian Clan to allow the survivors to truly develop and grow.

Of course, future collaboration with the Li Jian Clan could not be ruled out. After all, both sides had a foundation for cooperation, having achieved mutual benefit at least twice, and the collaboration had been pleasant.

However, the contract currently proposed by the Li Jian Clan absolutely cannot be signed. Such an arrangement would effectively make the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a dependent of the Li Jian Clan, subject to their strict oversight.

This would create excessive dependence on the Li Jian Clan, making the survivors unstable and without a secure future.

Should the Li Jian Clan face trouble, the survivors would be dragged into it. If no trouble arose, they would be held hostage by the Li Jian Clan.

The scarcity of pearl bubbles ironically delivered a precise strike against one of their enemies, He Gai Tou—an outcome neither Zi Di nor Zong Ge had anticipated, a fortunate mistake.

Besides He Gai Tou, the duelist Mi Fang had also arrived.

Ever since he successfully poisoned Zong Ge last time, his focus on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had intensified.

With the recent launch of the new mount potions [Smooth Stride] and [Spirit Stimulation], which were selling like hotcakes, Mi Fang's desire to eliminate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps grew even stronger and more urgent.

Yi Jiu did not come. Last time, he suffered significant losses in his battle against Shuang Lian, losing much of his equipment. After all, he had physically traversed the spatial vortex. Currently, he remained at the Thieves' Guild, relentlessly accepting quests to earn money.

He personally held no interest in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, even looking down on most of its members.

After all, he had personally led a surprise attack on their Snow Bird Harbor branch, effortlessly subduing their “Zong Ge” (an alchemy puppet). Though he later learned the deputy commander had survived such severe injuries, which surprised him somewhat. But overall, it was nothing special.

After subduing Bu Quan, Yi Jiu suffered a setback at the hands of Shuang Lian.

Apart from Long Fu, the others didn't warrant his serious attention.

Bao Li Gen hadn't shown up either. He mainly looked down on the ex-criminal He Gai Tou, believing Bing Yang's chances of victory were slim. After all, Long Fu had once taken on three opponents single-handedly, effortlessly eliminating Jia Bing, Lin, and the Axe Gang leader from Blood Kin City.

Qing Hong Yan certainly wouldn't come. Ever since his alchemy transformation, he'd grown eccentric, preferring solitude and loathing public appearances.

Hua Tang did show up though.

Disguised as an ordinary person, he bought a ticket and entered to watch the battle. Most duelists remained unaware.

He secretly recorded magic footage to hand over to Mei Lin for analysis.

Mei Lin wanted to attend, but she couldn't.

As a naval commander responsible for coastal defenses, her duties demanded her full attention.

Though the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had banned magic imaging, such regulations were no match for Hua Tang.

The arrangements of the arena couldn't contain someone like Hua Tang.

Similarly, the Li Jian Clan, though figures like Kong Pan weren't present in person, were secretly monitoring events.

After all, Long Fu's win or loss would significantly impact the sales of their mount potions.

For the Li Jian Clan, covert filming wasn't even necessary. They had already secured connections. After the battle, the arena would discreetly provide them with magic footage captured from an exceptionally advantageous angle.

The Li Jian Clan were local powerhouses. At this level of influence, rules were merely tools they designed to regulate others. These regulations were their instruments, not their shackles.

Whether the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps made things public or not was utterly irrelevant to them.

Chapter 874: Zong Ge vs Bing Yang – Contest of Equipment and Tactics

Another figure lurked unseen within the crowd.

It was Elder Yuan Ci!

He too watched today's battle with keen interest.

“I hope Long Fu proves formidable enough to eliminate Bing Yang outright.”

"Otherwise, how could Shi Liu ever rise to prominence?"

The Ice Prison Warden's decision to field Bing Yang in this grand duel had genuinely caught Yuan Ci off guard. He had secretly bribed numerous individuals, yet things had ultimately gone awry.

From his perspective, Bing Yang had once been gold level. Though now demoted to silver level, his combat prowess undoubtedly surpassed that of most silver level fighters.

A figure like Long Fu truly represented the greatest hope for eliminating Bing Yang.

Yet Elder Yuan Ci had simultaneously placed a wager on Bing Yang's victory.

If Bing Yang won, he'd rake in a fortune. If Bing Yang fell in battle, he'd lose his bet, yet feel even more delighted.

Yuan Ci displayed his true colors as a merchant: regardless of Bing Yang's outcome, he stood to gain.

“Just a silver level duel, yet it's this heated?”

“The betting frenzy around this match has amplified the crowd's enthusiasm.”

“Notice how few gold level spectators are present? Seems Long Fu isn't quite enough to draw gold level attendees."

“True. He defeated Teng Dong Lang, but that magic projection showed a very close victory. The final outcome had a lot to do with luck.”

Such murmurs circulated among the crowd.

Most could only see the surface.

In reality, numerous gold level figures watched from the shadows. Many others were deeply invested, their attention intense.

The masses wouldn't dream that this duel held immense significance, touching upon the vital interests of various factions, as well as deep-seated grudges and vendettas.

Suddenly, the crowd erupted into cheers.

Both combatants entered the arena simultaneously.

Stepping onto the right side was “Long Fu” (Zong Ge).

A massive dragon head, crimson vertical pupils, sharp fangs and teeth, its entire body covered in red dragon scales, dense and intricate. His hands and feet were noticeably larger, each ending in sharp, clawed dragon talons. His crimson tail, also covered in dense scales, radiated a sense of solidity and resilience.

Entering from the left was Bing Yang. A snow elf, his complexion appeared much healthier than during his imprisonment in the ice cell. Tall and slender, his features bore the natural, refined beauty characteristic of the elven race. Yet he bore the severe ravages of the potion [Beautiful Future], now aged and bearing a deeply troubled expression.

“Seeing Bing Yang again, I never imagined he'd aged so much.”

“That [Beautiful Future] potion was truly disastrous. It caused quite a stir back then.”

“What's infuriating is that old fool Ban Tu still holds a position as an elder in the Alchemy Guild. The higher-ups are blind!”

Among the audience were many victims from that time, their resentment palpable.

Little did they know that Ban Tu would soon make a remarkable comeback. The alchemy project “Ice Cube Red Wine” had fundamentally altered Ban Tu's life.

The two gradually approached each other, halting simultaneously when they reached a hundred paces apart.

Compared to Long Fu, Bing Yang looked even more aged. But the audience paid little attention to age; their focus was entirely on the two men's equipment.

Zong Ge was clad in three layers of armor, carrying a pair of gold level short spears on his back, a Gold Flower Hacksaw, and a Small Thunder God Flying Hammer at his waist. Metal gauntlets covered both his hands. Those in the know recognized these gauntlets as the Striking Shifter.

Zong Ge also carried a Battle Qi Boosting Syringe. Pearl bubbles were concealed within his armor lining, with an additional layer of the Striking Shifter wrapped around the foam. To puncture the pearl bubbles, he could simply use the Striking Shifter, convenient and swift. The pearl bubbles served as a trump card, to be used only when absolutely necessary.

After all, their supply of pearl bubbles was already pitifully scarce, barely enough to get by.

As for the dragonman youngster's Golden Silk Chainmail, Grand Arena medal, and ski goggles, he had taken them with him, leaving none behind. After all, the youngster had intended to pursue Lan Zao, necessitating his weapons. Crucially, he hadn't anticipated the subsequent chain of unforeseen events that would prolong his stay.

Thus, the pair of gold level short spears strapped to Zong Ge's back weren't the dragonman youngster's either, they belonged to Zong Ge himself. He had carried it with him since his time on Mystifying Monster Island.

Looking at Bing Yang.

He wore an Ice Heart Headband, a one-handed sword at his waist, and a pair of anti-magic bracelets on each wrist.

His black leather armor, leather pants, and leather boots formed what appeared to be a complete defensive set, all designed in nearly identical styles.

The audience scrutinized both combatants' gear, then began whispering and murmuring among themselves.

Both “Long Fu” and Bing Yang possessed numerous gold level items. For a silver level player to have such depth of resources was nothing short of extravagant.

“Judging by the equipment, Bing Yang has the upper hand.”

“No wonder he was once gold level. His financial resources still far surpass those of a silver level.”

“Long Fu is ultimately a silver level, not a gold level.”

After careful observation and comparison, the audience generally agreed that Bing Yang's gear was superior.

What they didn't know was that Bing Yang had sold off most of his own equipment. These gold level pieces were instead procured through the efforts of He Gai Tou and Mei Lin, among others, behind the scenes.

They had been meticulously selected, each piece tailored for specific purposes.

The Ice Heart Headband countered Long Fu's Dragon Prestige skill.

Bing Yang's main weapon was a gold level one-handed sword, superior to his previous one-handed sword. Its piercing capability was formidable, specifically designed to counter dragon scales.

The anti-magic bracelets were intended to neutralize any magic scrolls Long Fu might pull out.

Most crucially, his black leather armor, leather pants, and leather boots. Confirmed through external channels, this was indeed a full set of gold level defensive armor. Crucially, this armor set was officially registered with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The kingdom enforced an armor ban law, strictly limiting the existence, sale, and use of gold level armor.

This prohibition remains in effect during duels.

Regardless of origin, any transcendent participating in a duel must abide by the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's laws.

If not officially registered, outsiders could not use armor of gold level or higher within the dueling arena.

Zi Di's gaze fixed on Bing Yang's leather armor.

This defensive set was the most crucial element. In terms of pure defense, Bing Yang completely outclassed Zong Ge.

“Though Zong Ge is of gold level and surpasses Bing Yang in fighter level, he carries significant hidden dangers.”

Blood Vengeance, Flower Willow, and a deadly poison—Zong Ge harbored three such threats, causing Zi Di considerable worry. Yet, they had no means to resolve them.

"This is merely the equipment Bing Yang has displayed. He must have other items concealed on his body. Much like Zong Ge's Qi Boosting Syringe."

Yuan Ci also assessed the situation in silence: “Judging by both sides' equipment, things look rather grim.”

“The Gold Flower Hacksaw used by Long Fu is spoils of war he captured. Will he be accustomed to using it?”

“Even if he could, the Gold Flower Hacksaw is designed for heavy weapon clashes. Bing Yang wields a one-handed sword, he's an agile fighter who clearly avoids direct confrontations with Long Fu.”

“That makes the Gold Flower Hacksaw cumbersome and a liability.”

Hope surged within He Gai Tou's heart.

He knew Bing Yang inside out. To understand him thoroughly, he'd even fought him personally.

“Bing Yang excels at attrition tactics.”

“Therefore, the key to this battle lies in speed!”

As long as he maintained a speed advantage, Bing Yang could keep his distance from Zong Ge, allowing his attrition tactics to succeed.

For this reason, He Gai Tou had specially brought Bing Yang a pair of spider silk stockings.

Though designed for women, they would suffice.

Bing Yang not only secretly wore the spider silk stockings but also adorned all ten toes with rings.

Beyond boosting speed and agility, these rings served to fortify bodily balance and movement coordination.

His speed-enhancing gear was maxed out.

After all, using such equipment demanded focus. Too many unfamiliar items during intense combat could create openings, making it counterproductive.

But this alone wasn't enough.

He Gai Tou also arranged for Bing Yang to have an alchemy pistol.

It was a ladies' model, pink, named Chirp.

Whatever it fired produced no gunshot sound, only a chirping bird call. This was an assassination weapon, having claimed the lives of many important figures.

It held considerable renown within assassin circles.

He Gai Tou had borrowed it through channels connected to Yi Jiu.

The alchemy bullets loaded into the ladies' pistol [Chirp] were mostly frost rounds, hits drastically slowed victims. Others were mind-disruption rounds, causing cognitive chaos. Some were lethal poison rounds, the toxins supplied by Mi Fang.

“Once the battle commences, accelerate immediately and create distance.”

“Use pistols and other means to continuously fire, reducing Long Fu's speed.”

“Achieving this step will allow us to calmly execute an attrition tactic.”

"Your Frost Aura will be the trump card. I'm quite looking forward to seeing Long Fu face the aura."

These were the words He Gai Tou spoke to Bing Yang.

His tactical plan deeply resonated with Bing Yang.

When Bing Yang unleashed the Frost Aura, it carried the potion effect of [Beautiful Future]. Once Long Fu was enveloped by it, it would be equivalent to ingesting the [Beautiful Future] potion. and the effect would take immediate effect!

This was terrifying.

He Gai Tou's anticipation grew even more.

He anticipated “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) perishing under this tactic.

Though duelists analyzed that “Long Fu” was likely gold level, falling victim to [Beautiful Future] and dying remained entirely possible.

It all hinged on Bing Yang, whether his luck had carried him through the early stages, or if he'd paid the price of downgrading to survive this long.

In truth, [Beautiful Future] had claimed the lives of numerous transcendents, including the fall of three gold level fighters!

He Gai Tou had bet on Long Fu's victory.

Yet he preferred the outcome of Long Fu's defeat.

He could use this to earn merit and actively align himself with Mei Lin and others. The hidden benefits behind this far outweighed the mere tens of thousands lost in wagers!

After the arena officials verified the participants, the duel commenced.

Following their prearranged tactics, Bing Yang swiftly retreated, widening the distance between himself and Zong Ge.

Zong Ge, meanwhile, drew a folding crossbow.

This alchemy weapon automatically loaded bolts into its grooves.

Chapter 875: Zong Ge vs Bing Yang – Pouring Rain of Blood

Zong Ge pursued while firing arrows.

Witnessing this, He Gai Tou couldn't help but chuckle softly.

He thought to himself: “Long Fu has one weakness that isn't really a weakness—compared to his close-range combat prowess, his long-range capabilities are weaker.”

“In standard duels, he relies on his alchemy crossbow to compensate for this deficiency.”

"Against Teng Dong Lang, he wielded a dragon's reverse scale—Sword Return Dragon Scale. That equipment proved invaluable!"

“But that badge isn't his own. Unless he's borrowed it, his ranged combat remains flawed.”

This was the conclusion reached by He Gai Tou and Bing Yang through their joint analysis.

Their research focused on the magic footage of duels broadcast by Long Fu, including the battle against Hua Tang.

After all, Long Fu publicly declared his level to be merely silver. At this level, fighters were far weaker in long-ranged attacks than melee.

Although Zong Ge had transformed the Striking Shifter into a ranged weapon during his battle with Hua Tang and combined it with ranged battle skills, it didn't yield significant results against Hua Tang.

Faced with the agile and swift Hua Tang, Zong Ge appeared clumsy and cumbersome.

While pursuing, Zong Ge drew a folding crossbow.

Holding it with both hands, he unleashed a barrage of arrows.

Bing Yang dodged with all his might, his movements agile and graceful.

“Impressive evasion skills. Is that your only weapon? You dare challenge me with this?” Zong Ge taunted.

Bing Yang snorted coldly, executing a graceful somersault to evade most of the arrows. Regaining his footing, he flicked his single-handed sword lightly, deflecting the subsequent crossbow bolts and revealing his solid swordsmanship.

Battle skill - Rapid Advance.

Battle skill - Charge.

Zong Ge's battle skills were all military or commonplace battle skills, simple, practical, yet ordinary in effect.

However, the simultaneous use of two battle skills now dramatically increased his speed, rapidly closing the gap between them.

Zong Ge closed in.

Bing Yang remained fully alert, showing no panic.

He activated the ring on his toe, his speed instantly surging as he effortlessly pulled away again.

Zong Ge's gaze darkened slightly. He deliberately slowed his pace, focusing on positioning.

Though slower, his battle experience was vast. Using the walls surrounding the arena and relying on ranged firepower, he forced the faster Bing Yang into a corner.

Bing Yang's speed vastly outstripped Zong Ge's, yet the relatively confined combat environment held him back.

He took a step back, nimbly leaping onto the vertical wall. With a powerful push from his legs, he shot upward like an arrow released from its string, his entire body propelling him forward.

His speed surged once more, eliciting gasps from the spectators.

Such speed was rare, momentarily making people doubt whether Bing Yang had truly recovered to his gold level form.

Battle skill - Violent Shot.

Battle skill - Rapid Shot.

Battle skill - Firepower Release!

Zong Ge's eyes flashed with intensity, without hesitation, he unleashed every crossbow bolt on his body.

In an instant, his ranged firepower reached its peak.

Bing Yang dodged frantically in midair, evading most of the bolts. Yet their sheer volume meant he still took numerous hits.

The bolts' lethality was significantly reduced by his leather armor set.

Zong Ge tossed aside both folding crossbows and closed in again using the battle skill [Rapid Advance] and [Charge].

As Bing Yang saw Zong Ge charging, he prepared to activate all acceleration devices when Zong Ge suddenly drew twin pistols from his back.

Battle skill - Power Throw.

Battle skill - Single Reflection.

Zong Ge's biceps twitched as battle qi erupted, propelling the gold level pistols to trace two golden phantoms through the air.

Bing Yang accelerated, retreating diagonally backward.

The pistols struck the spot he had just vacated, refracting like beams of light to pursue him once more.

As Bing Yang dodged again, Zong Ge hurled the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

Three weapons converged from two directions, front and rear, closing in on Bing Yang.

Bing Yang took a deep breath, unleashing his battle skill for the first time while simultaneously flinging his dagger.

The dagger struck the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer. In an instant, both turned to stone and crashed to the ground.

Empowered by his battle skill, Bing Yang moved with heightened agility, successfully evading the reflected dual pistols.

During his evasion, Zong Ge finally closed the distance between them to about 30 meters.

Seeing Bing Yang about to retreat again, Zong Ge reached behind his back. In the next instant, he drew the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Infused with battle qi, the saw spun wildly, emitting a violent whirring hum.

Zong Ge's movements grew more violent.

Instead of wielding the Gold Flower Hacksaw, he hurled it like a giant dart with savage force.

Battle skill - Auto-Target Seek.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw spun vertically as it sliced toward Bing Yang.

Startled, Bing Yang jerked his wrist. A spell scroll fell from his sleeve. He flicked it away with a swift motion, sending it flying.

In the next instant, the scroll activated, forming an ice wall.

The ice wall rose instantly.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw traced a parabolic arc through the air, plummeting down to strike the ice wall.

Crack, crack.

The saw relentlessly cut through, swiftly slicing the ice wall apart, erupting in a shower of golden sparks.

The golden sparks danced through the air, forming blossoms of golden flowers drifting toward Bing Yang.

Bing Yang paid them no mind, sweeping his single-handed sword to summon a cold wind that swept the golden flowers away.

His own attention remained fixed on the Gold Flower Hacksaw and Zong Ge himself.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw had lost all momentum, stuck fast in the ice wall.

Zong Ge still held some distance from Bing Yang.

Just as Bing Yang prepared to retreat, an unexpected turn occurred!

The drifting golden flowers pierced through the sword wind, accelerating wildly. Before Bing Yang could react, they shot into every part of his body.

The golden flowers rapidly dissolved, transforming into streams of liquid metal.

Then, within a split second, the liquid metal fused together, forming chains that bound Bing Yang's limbs. The restraints tightened with increasing force.

“This... is the Striking Shifter?!” Bing Yang was utterly shocked.

“What's going on? The Striking Shifter is still on his hands, clearly.” He Gai Tou was equally bewildered.

His guess was correct; the Striking Shifter was indeed on Zong Ge's fists.

But disguised as golden flowers, it was also present.

The answer was simple - two Striking Shifters!

Gold level equipment itself could be mass-produced.

The duelists secretly funded He Gai Tou, equipping Bing Yang to the fullest. Through the two new potions, Zi Di deepened cooperation with the Li Jian Clan, securing more detailed intelligence and additional gear.

The second Shifting Striker was one such piece of equipment.

Battle skill - Iron Shackles.

Zong Ge's battle skill, infused into the Striking Shifter, activated with perfect timing.

Bing Yang instinctively strained to break free, the chains clanging as if about to snap.

But the Striking Shifter disguised as golden flowers were too few, their transformed metal chains too thin.

With a soft snap, Bing Yang severed the chains, regaining his freedom of movement.

But Zong Ge seized this opening, closing in to strike.

In that instant, He Gai Tou frowned: “Bing Yang hasn't fought in too long. Despite countless assault drills, his combat reflexes lag a beat, his tactical execution remains incomplete!”

Had it been He Gai Tou, wounded as he was, he would have retreated to create distance.

But Bing Yang chose differently.

He swung his one-handed sword, parrying Zong Ge's twin iron fists.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

The Striking Shifter transformed into gauntlets, clashing relentlessly with the one-handed sword. Each collision produced a shrill, crisp ring, sparks flying.

The metal on Zong Ge's right fist suddenly warped into a shield.

His left hand clawed forward, transforming his fist into a short spear.

He parried the one-handed sword's horizontal slash with his shield while thrusting the short spear forward in rapid succession.

Battle skill - Spear Barrage.

The short spear thrust dozens of times in an instant, its speed so rapid it left streaks of black shadows on the retina.

Bing Yang had no time to use a battle skill, forced to rely solely on his defensive leather armor to withstand the assault.

Fortunately, it held firm.

The defensive armor proved formidable, absorbing the spear thrusts and leaving only scattered white marks on its surface.

Bing Yang swung his one-handed sword, unleashing his battle skill.

The blade of the one-handed sword instantly softened, curving into a sharp angle as it lunged toward Zong Ge's right ear.

The shield in Zong Ge's right hand warped again, transforming into a long whip.

With a flick, the whip coiled around the slender sword, deflecting the attack.

The short spear in his left hand also shifted form, becoming a straight-bladed cutlass.

Zong Ge flipped his left wrist, aiming directly at Bing Yang's right hand holding the one-handed sword.

Bing Yang refused to let go, unable to execute a defensive battle skill in time, and had to brace with his defensive armor.

The blade struck Bing Yang's arm guard.

The defensive leather armor held firm, absorbing the blow.

But in the next instant, the straight-bladed cutlass transformed into a shackle, locking Bing Yang's wrist. Simultaneously, Zong Ge delivered a kick with his right foot.

A stream of liquid metal erupted, coalescing into a spherical hammer.

Bing Yang's eyes widened in fury. He tried to defend, but it was too late.

He could only brace against the impact with his defensive leather armor.

Bam!

A dull thud echoed as Bing Yang's back arched. Though he'd blocked it, the sheer force of that blunt impact was difficult to absorb.

Thus, Zong Ge wielded two Striking Shifters simultaneously, unleashing relentless assaults.

The blades danced in his hands, shifting patterns endlessly.

One moment it was a slender sword thrust, the next a broad sword chop, then a sweeping staff strike, followed by a shield block...

The attacks were rapid, the frequency relentless. More astonishingly, each strike was executed with remarkable skill, not a single one was half-hearted.

Zong Ge the Weapon Master's true depth was laid bare at that moment!

If it were merely weapon mastery, that would be one thing. But crucially, Zong Ge also seamlessly integrated corresponding battle skills.

Whip techniques, blade techniques, spear techniques... Though all were the most widely circulated styles, their frequent deployment created a dazzling formation.

Bing Yang retreated step by step.

He struggled to parry, feeling as if a metallic storm had erupted before his eyes! Blades, spears, axes, halberds, hooks, forks, whips, all relentlessly descended upon him.

His reactions were perpetually too slow. Unable to defend in time, he could only rely on his defensive armor to absorb the blows.

Once or twice was manageable. Five or six times was still bearable. But after a dozen, twenty strikes, the armor began to weaken.

The marks left by the weapons covered the surface of his leather armor, arm guards, and leather pants.

"You fool, retreat now! Fall back!!" He Gai Tou roared at the top of his lungs.

But his voice was drowned out by the shouts of the other spectators.

Those betting on Long Fu cheered wildly, while those backing Bing Yang roared in frustration.

“Go on, don't back down!”

“Take him down, don't retreat!”

“Fight back! Fight back, you coward!”

Watching Bing Yang take a beating since the close combat began, these gamblers were truly frantic, absolutely beside themselves.

Battle skill - Chain Stab.

Battle skill - Armor-Piercing Thrust!

Zong Ge's dagger in his right hand stabbed repeatedly, piercing the leather armor and dragging out streaks of bright red blood.

Blood!

His defense was broken!

Zong Ge's relentless assault finally paid off.

The searing pain jolted Bing Yang back to his senses.

“I stand no chance in close combat.”

“If this continues, I'll lose.”

“No! I must force distance and wear him down!”

But escape was no easy feat now.

Zong Ge would never let him pull away easily.

His attacks were relentless, and now that the defense was breached, Bing Yang would pay a heavy price if he tried to flee recklessly.

“Use the Frost Aura now!” He Gai Tou nearly shouted into Bing Yang's ear.

As if hearing his warning, Bing Yang recalled his ultimate trump card: “Only that move will do!”

“I must activate the Frost Aura! Force Long Fu to retreat!”

"Exactly! Frost Aura is imbued with the potion [Beautiful Future]. Even He Gai Tou and the Ice Prison Warden wouldn't dare touch it. How could a silver level Long Fu withstand it?"

Battle skill - Frost Aura!

Bing Yang's body trembled as he channeled all his power into unleashing the aura.

A warning bell rang sharply in Zong Ge's heart. He sensed an intense threat emanating from the Frost Aura.

A savage expression surged across his face.

No retreat. Not even a flicker of hesitation.

Rapid attacks, continuously attacking.

Fury, relentless fury!

“You're insane! My Frost Aura retains the potency of [Beautiful Future]!”

“You're essentially consuming the magic potion [Beautiful Future] over and over. You're finished.”

“Stop fighting! Stop fighting!”

“Cease this at once! Cease it, you madman!”

Bing Yang roared.

But Zong Ge, bathed in the Frost Aura, remained as unyielding as iron, showing no sign of retreat.

"I demand a decisive victory! I demand a swift victory!"

"So, Bing Yang... I need you to die!"

Zong Ge roared this in his heart.

The Striking Shifter in his hands transformed into two short blades, whirling into dazzling streaks of light.

Thud, thud, thud...

Slicing through leather armor, each strike pierced flesh.

Bing Yang strained to summon his battle qi, only to discover with horror that it had completely drained!

How could this be?

The reason was actually quite simple.

Bing Yang had once been of gold level, but now he was merely silver level. The quality and scale of silver level battle qi and scale were vastly inferior to gold level.

Though he had often been unable to use battle skill in time, he had used equipment far too frequently.

Especially since this equipment was exceptionally good, he possessed more gold level gear than Zong Ge.

How could a silver level fighter not deplete his battle qi when using gold level equipment beyond his level?

The reason Bing Yang only realized this now was entirely because Zong Ge had controlled the pace. The pressure Zong Ge exerted was overwhelming; Bing Yang was forced to defend with all his might, his focus entirely on blocking attacks, leaving him no room to spare.

In contrast, Zong Ge, though outwardly silver level, was actually gold level! His battle qi was abundant!

Bing Yang's battle qi drained completely, marking the beginning of his end.

Without its support, his defenses plummeted to rock bottom.

Zong Ge transformed his hands into gauntlets, their fingertips razor-sharp as they pierced straight through Bing Yang's chest.

In the next instant, Zong Ge channeled his battle qi directly into Bing Yang's body, using his flesh as a weapon.

The infusion reached its peak.

Bam!

Bing Yang's entire body exploded violently, flesh and bone fragments scattering in all directions, transforming into a shower of blood.

Zong Ge was covered in blood, a pool forming rapidly beneath his feet.

He slowly lowered his arms, his posture standing tall and unyielding like a mountain. He lifted his head slightly, his gaze sweeping slowly across the spectator stands.

Those caught in his stare instinctively fell silent.

Savage!

Fierce!

At that moment, Zong Ge utterly intimidated everyone present.

Chapter 876: Zong Ge- I am Very Good

After the deathly silence, deafening cheers erupted within the arena.

“Long Fu! Long Fu!”

“So brutal, so savage, I absolutely love it!”

“Long Fu is undoubtedly the top silver duelist!”

“Damn you, Bing Yang! You sickly bastard! I misjudged you!”

“You're the shame of the snow elves! Shame! Why didn't you die sooner, Bing Yang? You scammed your own people's money! You deserve to die! Damn it! You really should've died sooner!”

Some spectators cheered wildly, others cursed loudly.

With a high proportion of gamblers in the crowd, emotions fluctuated wildly.

Yuan Ci gave a thumbs-up, sitting back in his seat and laughing heartily: “Excellent! Absolutely brilliant!”

Though disguised as an ordinary spectator, his joy was genuine.

Watching Long Fu stand motionless in the arena, Yuan Ci marveled inwardly: “Never imagined Long Fu was this powerful. Now it seems, even if a silver level defeating a gold level is extremely difficult, it's still incredibly impressive.”

“Bing Yang was once gold level, yet he was completely overwhelmed by Long Fu throughout the fight, unable to mount any counterattack.”

Yuan Ci's perspective mirrored the crowd's.

To them, the duel unfolded like this.

In the opening phase, Bing Yang retreated without even testing the waters, seeking distance. Long Fu pursued, unleashing a hail of crossbow bolts.

Mid-battle, “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) hurled melee weapons relentlessly, even flinging away the Gold Flower Hacksaw, successfully immobilizing Bing Yang. Now it’s clear Long Fu's tactics were meticulously planned in advance. His subtle tricks were incredibly clever, successfully dragging the fight into close quarters.

In the later phase, “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) rapidly switched weapon forms, launching frenzied attacks. For the first two or three rounds, Bing Yang still managed counterattacks. But as the fight progressed, he grew increasingly desperate, scrambling to block left and right while taking relentless beatings from Zong Ge.

In the final moments, Bing Yang unleashed his Frost Aura, his last defiant act. Yet Zong Ge stood his ground, neither dodging nor flinching, as if unaffected, and finished Bing Yang off.

The final slaughter, though bloody and brutal, truly pushed this duel to its climax!

Yuan Ci, a silver level magician rather than a fighter, couldn't discern more intricate details. His perception mirrored that of the vast majority.

They had expected an evenly matched contest, where at least Bing Yang and Long Fu could pose mutual threats. Instead, it turned into a complete rout.

This explained the bitter resentment among those who had bet against him.

Bing Yang had utterly disappointed his backers!

Zong Ge, however, had achieved his pre-battle objective. Not only did he secure a swift victory, but it was a resounding triumph!

“With Bing Yang gone, Shi Liu should step up now.”

“Youth is truly wonderful, so full of vitality!”

“I'm starting to like this young man.” For a moment, Yuan Ci's regard for “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) soared.

Mi Fang, however, felt the opposite. He wished “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) would die right now. This duel's outcome was absolutely not what he wanted.

“Has the poison in Long Fu been neutralized?”

“Can't tell. Not a bit.”

“Mainly, that trash Bing Yang is utterly useless. He hasn't applied any pressure at all, he just become a punching bag.”

Yes, useless.

That was Mi Fang's overall assessment of Bing Yang as a gold level duelist.

To elaborate, there were many aspects.

For instance, Bing Yang's execution of the agreed-upon tactics was severely subpar.

When the real clash began, Bing Yang couldn't withstand the immense pressure Long Fu applied. He was caught off guard countless times during exchanges, his weak core exposed for all to see.

Only at the very end did he finally snap out of it and deploy his trump card. Contrast that with Long Fu: he struck decisively when the moment called for it, without hesitation or dithering. Take one example: when Long Fu fired arrows from his crossbow, he emptied his quiver entirely. This bold move created an opening, laying solid groundwork for the ensuing close combat. He didn't hesitate, nor did he worry that his ranged capabilities would diminish after exhausting his arrows.

“Still, this battle wasn't entirely without gains.”

“At least Long Fu absorbed the Frost Aura, gaining the effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion.”

Mi Fang watched Zong Ge intently as he stood proudly in the center of the arena.

Dueling arena staff approached him cautiously, exchanged a few words, then led him off the field.

Zong Ge walked into the duelist entrance with an unruffled expression, as if nothing had happened.

He Gai Tou watched Zong Ge leave the arena, his brow deeply furrowed.

“I've been targeted!”

“I've been severely targeted!”

After reviewing the duel, he realized: it was obvious his chosen tactics had been specifically countered.

Bing Yang had chosen an attrition tactic, while Zong Ge employed an aggressive assault strategy, hitting hard and fast, leaving Bing Yang no breathing room.

Even their equipment had been countered.

Zong Ge wielded two Striking Shifters, one disguised as golden flowers. A minor trick, yet it proved effective.

“They must have studied Bing Yang's past combat habits and style to prepare countermeasures.”

“Damn it, Bing Yang was too weak.”

He Gai Tou had considered altering Bing Yang's tactics.

But in such a short timeframe, it was utterly impossible.

Habits were incredibly difficult to change.

Even if change were possible, the cost would be too great. Most crucially, Bing Yang's foundation was weak. All this time, he'd hidden away in his ice prison merely to prolong his life and indulge himself. With little regular training, his foundation had rotted and decayed. While not entirely hollow, the contrast with Long Fu was stark.

For the short term, dragging him onto the dueling grounds and employing an attrition tactic was essential.

He Gai Tou had no other choice. He could only focus on rehabilitative training for Bing Yang, with no time for anything else.

“Ah, it was the Frost Aura imbued with the [Beautiful Future] potion that gave me so many surprises.”

He Gai Tou devised the attrition tactic partly because Bing Yang excelled at it, and it was the only viable strategy in the short term. The other reason was his Frost Aura.

Attrition battles often become protracted wars. Over time, the cumulative effects of potions undergo a qualitative shift, paving the way for Bing Yang's victory.

He Gai Tou's expectations had been inflated far too high, inevitably leading to severe disappointment. The outcome was truly unbearable to witness, utterly disheartening! Although Bing Yang successfully cast the Frost Aura, its actual duration proved surprisingly brief.

Zong Ge's decision to strike swiftly without flinching, swiftly dispatching Bing Yang, proved a shrewd choice.

This meant his time spent under the Frost Aura was minimal.

For a moment, He Gai Tou could only console himself: “It's fine.”

“Though I failed, didn't Mi Fang face the same fate before me?”

“I'm not the first to fall.”

"He didn't kill Long Fu directly, but Long Fu still got hit. He must be severely weakened. The effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion aren't so easily dispelled!"

He Gai Tou had gained something from this attempt.

Though Bing Yang had fallen in battle, through this action, he had demonstrated his proactive stance to Mei Lin, significantly narrowing the distance between them.

Zong Ge had already vanished into the passage leading to the duel chamber.

Other staff began organizing the audience for an orderly exit.

The crowd dispersed much earlier than usual, primarily because the arena owner, sensing the audience's heightened emotions, feared potential altercations and chose to evacuate them early.

Hua Tang also followed the crowd, gradually leaving the arena.

His disguise skills were formidable, and at this moment, his face was dark and sullen. He muttered under his breath, seemingly cursing Bing Yang and Long Fu, clearly someone who had lost a bet.

In truth, he had successfully captured the magic footage, completing his primary mission and ready to report back to Mei Lin.

Yet a layer of dark clouds seemed to gather within his heart.

Unlike others' focus, what played in Hua Tang's mind was the scene of Zong Ge wielding two Striking Shifters, unleashing relentless assaults.

“Who knew Long Fu possessed such formidable weaponry skills?”

This battle had laid bare the mastery of a true weapons specialist, fully showcased through the Striking Shifter's performance.

Though the battle skills he displayed were commonplace, they integrated flawlessly with his weapons.

Each time Zong Ge transformed his weapon, he selected the most perfectly suited tool for the task.

Against Teng Dong Lang, he showcased his fist and foot techniques.

In this battle, his command over weapons was breathtaking.

Even if he weren't a [Weapon Master], he was undoubtedly close to that level.

Though Hua Tang had clashed with “Long Fu” (Zong Ge) before, that encounter was too brief for Hua Tang to truly grasp what a [Weapon Master] was.

“Long Fu is exceptionally powerful.”

“He didn't unleash his full strength in that fight.”

“At the very least, he didn't display his martial arts skills or his divine spells.”

Zi Di's face lit up with delight as she followed Zong Ge into the duelist's passageway.

"Lord Commander, are you alright? Your injuries require treatment." Zi Di's thoughts stood in stark contrast to her expression.

The potion's effect was to accelerate time.

Its original advertising slogan proclaimed: “A brighter future, now within reach.”

Initially after ingestion, the potion indeed delivered results. It accelerated time for transcendents, hastening cultivation progress and shortening growth cycles.

However, the divine power within the time ice cubes became uncontrollable during the mid-to-late stages of potion consumption.

Time could accelerate in all aspects for the user.

For instance, accelerated lifespan equated to shortened life.

Take the circulation of battle qi. When executing battle skill, accelerated circulation in any segment could easily cause battle skill failure and trigger backlash.

Consider eating. Accelerated eating motions, rapid chewing and swallowing, outpaced the stomach's capacity. This led to massive food accumulation and indigestion.

Crucially, transcendents have poor perception of their own accelerated time, they barely notice it. Among the unlucky souls back then, some ate too fast. Their time perception failed to sound the alarm in time, causing their stomachs to rupture from overeating.

Zong Ge glanced at Zi Di and laughed heartily: “Don't worry, I'm fine. This injury barely affects me at all.”

The staff member leading the way ahead spoke with reverence: "Lord Long Fu, you truly live up to your reputation. Your performance in this battle was simply outstanding. It was a spectacular fight."

“Hahaha!” Zong Ge laughed heartily, head thrown back.

But Zi Di's heart sank to the depths.

Chapter 877: Code Name [Emancipation]

Starlight spilled across the sea as three ships slowly docked at the pier.

Crews disembarked in orderly lines, beginning to load and unload cargo.

Hidden within massive wooden crates lay the three captives: the big guy, Da Dou, and Hong Zhu.

All three lay limp, utterly powerless to resist.

“We're here?” Hong Zhu frowned, desperately scheming an escape.

“Where exactly is this?” Da Dou grew tense.

“Snore... snore...” big guy slept soundly, his belly puffed high.

The Imperial Spy Chief oversaw the handover, while two concealed gold level fighters escorted the prisoners.

When the wooden crate opened, Da Dou and the others found themselves in a dungeon.

Each was thrown into separate cells; the sleeping big guy was carried inside.

In a secret chamber, the Imperial Spy Chief frowned. “Where is [Emancipator]?”

“Lord [Emancipator] is entertaining important guests and cannot leave at this moment,” the receptionist replied.

“Important person...” The Imperial Spy Chief's brow relaxed. “Has Tan Mo arrived?”

The attendant hesitated before nodding.

The Imperial Spy Chief drew a breath, sat down, and began waiting patiently.

Directly above his head loomed the city government palace.

Tan Mo was ushered into the city lord's study.

He strode unapologetically to the cabinet, selected a bottle of red wine, and poured himself a glass first.

Taking a deep, satisfying swig, he narrowed his eyes and smiled slyly like a weasel. “Your red wine here is quite good,” he remarked to the other person in the room.

“Any sorrow-dispelling red wine left?”

"I heard the 600 bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine from the Intoxication Church were all snatched up. Now the Intoxication Church, pirates, and coastal factions are all scrambling for these treasures."

The other man shook his head, seated himself at the head of the table, and gestured toward the guest seat opposite the desk.

But Tan Mo deliberately refused to sit, instead leaning against the window. “Should I address you by your Imperial Secret Spy Agency codename, [Emancipator], or is it better to just call you Snow Bird Harbor City Lord?”

The Imperial Spy codenamed [Emancipator] was none other than Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!

Leveraging the pretext fabricated by “fake defeating Cong Mang,” Tan Mo had smoothly entered Snow Bird Harbor with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's deliberate cooperation.

As the investigator dispatched by Imperial Secret Spy Agency, Tan Mo's targets extended beyond the dim-eyed inhabitants of Two Eyes Island. His scrutiny also fell upon figures like [Emancipator] (Snow Bird Harbor City Lord) and [Usurper] on Ice Sculpture Island.

Naturally, having achieved a resounding victory on Two Eyes Island, Tan Mo's appetite had grown considerably. In his eyes, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a vast gold mine waiting to be mined!

From their private meeting onwards, Tan Mo began applying pressure.

Entering the study, he took the liberty of pouring his own wine and leaning against the window, all strong hints designed to exert psychological pressure on the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Extorting bribes was something Tan Mo had done countless times; he was highly skilled and cunning in his methods.

Sensing the hostility, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fell silent for a moment before retrieving a small wooden box from a drawer.

The box was compact yet adorned with gold inlays, its design embodying the intricate, fairy-like artistry characteristic of the region.

He slid the box slowly across the desk toward the empty guest seat.

Tan Mo remained expressionless, holding his wine glass in one hand as he strolled slowly to the table. With the fingers of his other hand, he gently flicked the edge of the box.

The lid lifted, revealing the treasures within.

Dozens of gems lay scattered inside, varying in size.

Their hues were diverse, but the aura emanating from them was at least gold level. At least ten were saint level gems.

The multicolored brilliance of the gems reflected off Tan Mo's plump face.

Only then did a faint smile appear.

It was his first smile since meeting the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Honorable Investigator, you've traveled far and endured much hardship. Please accept this token of welcome,” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said.

Hearing it was merely a greeting gift; Tan Mo chuckled softly before slowly sitting down.

A battle of words, a test, a clash, a tug-of-war, unfolded immediately.

Much of their conversation revolved around idle pleasantries, yet beneath lay the true bargaining. Magic lights illuminated pretense; red wine rippled with greed.

Amidst this, Tan Mo asked about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps directly: “I hear this group passed through Snow Bird Harbor to reach Ice Sculpture Island? They've also worked with you, City Lord?”

The city lord looked surprised, his guard rising slightly. “Why would Lord Tan Mo take an interest in a mere mercenary corps?”

Tan Mo's lips curled into a grin, revealing stark white teeth. “These fellows aren't ordinary.”

“I've heard their name mentioned repeatedly on Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island.”

“Their leader, Long Fu, even slew a gold level opponent while only silver level himself. He's now a household name in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“I hear you used them to successfully eliminate the local Bayonet Gang in Snow Bird Harbor.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed: “It was merely mutual exploitation.”

“I shall detail this matter thoroughly during tomorrow's formal investigation.”

"I used them as a legitimate pretext to swiftly eliminate the Bayonet Gang. They, in turn, exploited me and the conflict with the Bayonet Gang to gain nationwide fame."

Tan Mo inquired, “How formidable is their leader, Long Fu?”

He intended to extort the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and Long Fu's strength was a key factor in his calculations.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord frowned slightly: "I happen to possess the latest magic footage of Long Fu in combat. It shows his duel with Bing Yang, where he held absolute dominance. His opponent, once a gold level fighter, couldn't land a single blow."

“Do not underestimate this group!”

“Long Fu once single-handedly eliminated three gold level fighters at once: the Axe Gang leader, Jia Bing, and Lin.”

“Truthfully, my reason for collaborating with them was to lure out the arms dealer behind them.”

Tan Mo smiled: "With the Empire's full-scale invasion of the Wilderness Continent, the beastmen's equipment generally lags behind ours. Yet numerous arms dealers smuggle in and sell cutting-edge weaponry to those beastmen at exorbitant prices."

“Eradicating such arms dealers would indeed be a major achievement.”

“It seems Lord Tan Mo is quite interested in this merit as well?” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled, “We could collaborate.”

Tan Mo shook his head slightly, casually deflecting, “Traces of necromancers have appeared on Two Eyes Island. I suspect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps may be involved in this matter.”

This casual deflection was merely to conceal his true purpose.

Compared to merit, he was far more interested in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' money!

From Yan Yi on Two Eyes Island, he'd obtained intelligence suggesting the island's entire wealth likely ended up in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' hands.

This detail, however, remained unknown to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Tan Mo had no intention of revealing his true motives to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes flickered slightly. "Speaking of Two Eyes Island's Lord Hun Tong, he was quite a talent in business matters. What a pity he fell to pirates."

Tan Mo took a sip of red wine, his expression turning heavy with sorrow, “It was my fault.”

“I was ambushed by pirates and wounded by Cong Mang.”

"During that time, the pirates brutally murdered Hun Tong!"

“I have already submitted my report, apologizing to the Empire for my failure.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes flickered again, his words offering Tan Mo solace, the real culprit was the pirates, not him.

"That damned dog! I'd cut off his head right now if I could!!" Beside him, Hun Tong clenched his fists in rage, grinding his teeth.

Beside him stood Dao Hen.

Both had cast the deception divine spell, following Tan Mo all the way and witnessing everything.

After about ten minutes, Tan Mo left the study, escorted by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The steward led him to his lodgings.

"Tan Mo... he really is a tough nut to crack. His appetite is far too big..." Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed inwardly.

He pushed aside his distracting thoughts and turned to his duties.

"Oh? Sleeping Man, Da Dou, and Hong Zhu have all been brought in?" Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression brightened slightly.

After a moment's consideration, he made his decision: "Da Dou hails from Eruption Island. Though his master is dead, he was a founding elder of the island. Eruption Island is a legend level power; he must not be harmed."

"Hong Zhu once fought alongside the necromancer. This time, when Tu Ji died on the Magnet Archipelago, she emerged unscathed. We must keep her alive to handle future investigations and interrogations from the Blood Light Punishment Institution."

“As for the Sleeping Man...”

Observing the big guy's exemplary conduct during captivity, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord let out a peculiar chuckle.

"What a clumsy oaf... yet he might possess saint level potential. Just keep feeding him well!"

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's speculation was quite natural.

Primarily, the big guy had already shown astonishing feats in the eating contest, and Da Dou's act of taking away the big guy had exposed many secrets.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: “If he truly has saint level potential, my contribution will be immense!”

“Did Tan Mo just inquire about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps because he's interested in this big oaf too?”

“This matter must be kept from him.”

Dao Hen and Hun Tong split their forces: one followed Tan Mo until he fell asleep, while the other shadowed Snow Bird Harbor City Lord until he concluded his affairs. They then followed him through a secret passage into the dungeon.

There, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord met with the Imperial Spy Chief and conversed at length.

After the surveillance concluded, Dao Hen knelt on the ground, silently praying to the Fish God Mist to report his recent findings.

There was no response.

The fishman youngster was still absorbing divinity, utterly unable to spare any attention.

Dao Hen had no choice but to decide, following previous orders, to continue monitoring Tan Mo and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord alongside Hun Tong.

The night grew ever deeper.

Zong Ge and Zi Di had returned to the inn.

Zong Ge was now bedridden.

Zi Di stood by his side, casting spells with all her might, but the effect was negligible.

Zong Ge's eyes remained tightly shut, his brow furrowed. His condition was dire!

Exposed to the Frost Aura, he had been affected by the potion effect of [Beautiful Future], causing his physical and mental processes to occasionally surge at accelerated speeds.

Zong Ge bore three latent threats: Blood Vengeance, Flower Willow, and a deadly poison, all now amplified within the accelerated timeframe.

During their return journey, these latent threats had erupted multiple times.

He had fought them off with great difficulty and was now severely wounded!

Chapter 878: A Painful Night

The problem Zong Ge now faced extended beyond the latent dangers he carried.

Zi Di was deeply troubled. She discovered that the effects of the Deceptive Disguise on Zong Ge were also being accelerated by the potency of the [Beautiful Future] potion, rapidly depleting.

"Pearl bubbles are god level materials. God level materials possess fragmented divinity. That's precisely why the Deceptive Disguise spell is so potent."

“This potency stems from its supremacy over laws and concepts.”

“But the effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion are equally derived from an incredibly faint time divinity.”

“The two can influence each other.”

Up until now, survivors had used the Deceptive Disguise spell to deceive others and disguise themselves with unparalleled success.

Now, under Zi Di's witness, the Deceptive Disguise spell finally faced a severe challenge.

"Fortunately, Zong Ge maintained his disguise well until the very end. He didn't collapse before returning to his quarters."

Thinking of this, Zi Di couldn't help but feel admiration for the tough man before her.

Before returning, the turmoil had already begun erupting within Zong Ge's body, but he had stubbornly endured it.

Such endurance was extraordinary.

One must understand he was risking his life. Only now, lying in bed, could he fully focus on controlling his body and battle qi for defense, thereby reducing the mortal danger.

Alongside her relief and admiration, Zi Di felt intense anger.

She was furious with herself.

Why hadn't she done everything in her power earlier to help Zong Ge resolve the hidden threats within him? Why had she allowed things to reach this dire state?

In truth, Zi Di was being overly hard on herself.

Before this, she and Zong Ge had not been idle.

But the Blood Vengeance and other effects were truly difficult to resolve.

Zong Ge's eyes were tightly shut; he couldn't even speak. He had to focus all his energy, every single moment, every second, on monitoring his body.

The Blood Vengeance could erupt anywhere in his body at any time. It had already erupted on the way back from the arena. Fortunately, it hadn't struck a vital spot like his heart.

The Willow Flower battle skill was still draining Zong Ge's battle qi, forcing him to intensify the battle skill's suppression of his life force. Maintaining the battle qi itself had become an immense challenge due to the lingering power of the time divinity.

Moreover, the strange poison Mi Fang had inflicted was now taking effect, its toxins spreading anew throughout Zong Ge's body.

Though Zong Ge achieved a swift and decisive victory, he had collapsed.

Now, all hope rested solely on Zi Di.

“Stay calm.”

“It hasn't reached the worst-case scenario yet.”

“There's still hope!”

“Theoretically, Zong Ge can overcome this crisis on his own.”

The lingering power of the time divinity continuously affected the Blood Vengeance, Flower Willow, the strange poison, his battle skills, physical body, and deception spell.

Since it was producing effects, it naturally meant this temporal power was constantly being depleted.

As long as Zong Ge persevered, the day would come when this residual time divinity diminished to nothing.

“But how long will it take for this residual time divinity to be exhausted?”

“And now, of all times, Zong Ge must step forward publicly.”

Zi Di stared at the wall, her gaze seemingly piercing through it to the deep night beyond.

"Zong Ge endured the Frost Aura head-on during the battle. Since the enemies targeted Bing Yang, they must have coveted the [Beautiful Future] effect carried by the aura."

“They will certainly verify Zong Ge's true condition as soon as possible.”

"During the duel, Bing Yang's loud shouts also shattered the Frost Aura's concealment. So by tomorrow, this news will spread far and wide."
