“As time passes, the outside world's concern for Zong Ge's true condition will only grow.”

Zi Di felt heavy-hearted.

She decided that besides waiting for Zong Ge to handle this dilemma himself, she must lend a hand and help him!

Zong Ge must recover swiftly.

His collapse severely weakened their position. Never mind others, if Hua Tang launched another ambush, neither Zi Di nor Zong Ge would have the strength to resist!

“Fortunately, I recently acquired the alchemy technique for the Ice Cube Red Wine from Ban Tu.”

“Like the [Beautiful Future] potion, time ice cubes harness the power reflected from the time divinity.”

“In fact, one of the primary ingredients in the [Beautiful Future] potion is time ice cubes.”

Since the sorrow-dispelling red wine could perfectly utilize time ice cubes, it should naturally also help Zong Ge neutralize the potion's effects on his body.

"I acquired this alchemy technique for the Lord Commander's sake. Unexpectedly, it was used on Zong Ge first."

Now, only one dilemma remained before Zi Di.

How could she obtain the sorrow-dispelling red wine?

"Elder Jiu Jin? The Alchemy Guild?" Zi Di shook her head slightly.

She had learned about the sorrow-dispelling red wine and the time ice cubes from Elder Jiu Jin. At the same time, she had also learned of the wine's rarity.

"The Intoxication Church? They're the ones who produce the sorrow-dispelling red wine."

"Not good. Their fleet was raided by Feng Lian. Right now, the entire cult is frantically recruiting, raising bounties, desperately trying to recover every drop of that wine."

“Wait, maybe the Li Jian Clan?” A thought flashed through Zi Di's mind.

From gathered intelligence, she knew: The Kingdom's Second Fleet had been the first to arrive at the battlefield in response to the distress call. They were also the ones who confirmed the demise of the Intoxication Church's fleet.

“They were the first on the scene. Could they have salvaged some of the sorrow-dispelling red wine?”

“The Second Fleet is practically the Li Jian Clan's private army.”

“So by that logic, the Li Jian Clan might have some of the sorrow-dispelling red wine?”

Zi Di's conjecture was correct, matching the facts.

Of course, she couldn't be completely certain yet.

“We need to contact someone from the Li Jian Clan as soon as possible.”

Just as she was thinking this, a report came in: the Li Jian Clan's steward had arrived.

Zi Di glanced at Zong Ge, then double-checked the room's defenses. Satisfied, she gritted her teeth and left.

In a small reception hall, she met the Li Jian Clan steward.

The steward smiled. “I wonder if Magician Yao Ma has considered our family's previous cooperation proposal?”

Zi Di remained expressionless. “I've already submitted it for review. Regarding this matter, we can only await notification.”

The steward nodded. Mentioning Zong Ge, he voiced concern on behalf of the Li Jian Clan: "Bing Yang's Frost Aura actually carries the potion effect of [Beautiful Future]! This is utterly vicious!"

"We witnessed Lord Long Fu being completely enveloped by the Frost Aura during the duel. Is he all right?"

“The power of time is no trivial matter.”

Zi Di smiled. "He's fine. So far, no issues have surfaced. I believe this was Bing Yang's desperate lie. Even if it's true, it's rather alarmist."

The steward said earnestly, "If Lord Long Fu feels unwell, please inform us immediately. Our clan can find a solution."

"Our interests align. In this grand duel, your mercenary corps’ commander stands as a beacon. Should he fall, sales of our two newly launched potions would suffer a devastating blow."

Zi Di nodded. "I'm well aware of that. But how exactly do you propose to resolve it?"

The steward smiled. “To be perfectly honest, our clan possesses a batch of sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

“And didn't you, Magician Yao Ma, recently secure the alchemy formula for this wine through your deal with Ban Tu?”

It was too timely. Zi Di had been scheming to acquire the sorrow-dispelling red wine, never expecting the Li Jian Clan to offer it up voluntarily.

For a split second, Zi Di opened her mouth to agree.

But the next moment, she shook her head. “I have no need for the sorrow-dispelling red wine at present. The commander is in excellent condition.”

“Are you seeking cooperation with us in this matter?”

“Now that you possess the sorrow-dispelling red wine, do you also desire this alchemy technique?”

Zi Di suppressed the urgency surging within her.

Her negotiation skills were formidable, and she understood clearly that this was not the time for haste.

If she revealed her true emotions, the Li Jian Clan would inevitably open their greedy maw to devour the survivors' interests.

Both sides explored this topic, attempting to negotiate.

Even when the Li Jian Clan's steward took his leave, no agreement had been reached.

After seeing the steward off, Zi Di exhaled a heavy breath.

“We've temporarily stabilized the situation.”

“But this predicament will only worsen with time.”

“Zong Ge's condition will eventually become impossible to conceal.”

The dire circumstances left Zi Di unable to sleep.

She assisted Zong Ge's treatment until dawn, monitoring his condition.

Hours later, the first light touched the horizon.

Zong Ge remained with eyes tightly shut, his physical condition far from promising.

Zi Di's brow furrowed deeply, her expression worn and weary. She found Zong Ge's condition had deteriorated further. His wounds were multiplying, even with Zi Di's assistance from outside.

That night, Zi Di stayed awake. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord managed a brief nap but slept poorly.

Tan Mo weighed heavily on his mind, especially after learning of Hun Tong's death at the hands of pirates.

"What truly lies behind Hun Tong's demise? Was it genuinely a pirate ambush, with Tan Mo fighting valiantly but ultimately defeated?" The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sensed a thick layer of conspiracy in the air.

“Today marks the start of the investigation. May everything proceed smoothly.”

After a hurried wash, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord began his breakfast.

During breakfast, however, he received some good news.

"Oh? That big oaf nicknamed Sleeping Man, his bloodline is at least saint level?"

“Yes.” His subordinate confirmed.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a cup of milk in one hand while deep in thought.

“A saint level seed placed within a mercenary group, was that intentional or accidental?”

“Heh, how could it be accidental?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes flashed with sharp insight as he silently marveled: “Impressive. The arms dealer backing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has astonishing ambitions. He actually wants to become the fourth pillar of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom already had three saint level figures. If the big oaf ascended to that tier in the future, wouldn't he become part of the kingdom's high command?

By then, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would also become a pivotal force within the kingdom.

At that point, no matter how vast the arms trade grew, it would be shielded by saint level power.

Chapter 879: Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s Statement

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was an imperial spy by nature, and his mindset was inherently prone to overthinking and overanalyzing everything.

In his imagination, the arms dealer backing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps harbored immense ambition. This arms dealer had a voracious appetite, seeking to make massive investments in Ice Sculpture Island.

“To advance into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, this arms dealer has gone to extraordinary lengths.”

“Initially, he deliberately dispatched a special silver level mercenary like Long Fu to challenge Teng Dong Lang, using the duel to make a name for himself.”

“From this alone, it's clear the arms dealer conducted thorough preliminary research into the Ice Sculpture King's populace.”

“Later, leveraging my connections, he directed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to form an alliance with the Li Jian Clan.”

"The arms dealer proved quite astute, spotting the hidden market for mount potions within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. They secured the Li Jian Clan's cooperation through massive profit-sharing agreements."

“Long Fu and Yao Ma's journey to the capital signaled the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' formal entry into the kingdom's upper echelons. They established tacit agreements with native factions, securing an initial foothold.”

"Following this, the arms dealer's next scheme was to expand and strengthen the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' capabilities. After all, this mercenary group served as his overt proxy and protective umbrella."

"As the arms trade expanded, profits surged dramatically. By then, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed formidable strength to stabilize the business, intimidate rivals, and safeguard the arms dealer's interests."

“Thus, from the very beginning, the arms dealer secretly planted a pawn.”

“This was the entity known as ‘Sleeping Man’, a seemingly dim-witted giant.”

"This giant had long been a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, its sole giant. Who could have imagined he harbored potential to reach the saint level?"

"As time passes and the moment ripens, the giant will ascend step by step to the saint level. The kingdom will have long since accepted him in their hearts, making the emergence of a fourth saint level figure far less jarring."

"Who exactly is this arms dealer? Their tactics are truly masterful!"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn't help but speculate about the mysterious arms dealer, secretly harboring admiration.

"The reason the big oaf is so dim-witted is likely the arms dealer's doing as well. Only a dim-witted saint level being poses less threat, making it easier for the populace to accept."

“People naturally feel sympathy for fools.”

“On the other hand, this might very well be the price the arms dealer paid to control this giant!”

"A mentally sound transcendent, upon ascending to saint level, would inevitably demand much. But a fool is different."

A sharp gleam flashed in the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes: “So it seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was meticulously handpicked and painstakingly assembled by the arms dealers as their proxy.”

"Long Fu, those peculiar silver levels, perhaps even hidden gold levels, were handpicked."

"The organization previously recruited their boatswain (Lan Zao), who shows extraordinary loyalty to the entire mercenary group. This indicates that even these low level transcendents have undergone cultivation."

"Finally, the big oaf. This hidden saint level is the true game-changer. Once the saint level emerges, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will leap to become a top-level power across the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom!"

As Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pondered, the figure of the arms dealer behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gradually came into sharp focus.

His interest in this arms dealer, which didn't actually exist, grew ever stronger.

“To wield a saint level pawn suggests this arms dealer is at least saint level themselves.”

“Legend level or even backed by deities or churches... it's entirely possible.”

“After all, international arms dealing is beyond the reach of anyone too weak.”

Uncovering such an entity would be a monumental achievement for the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

At the same time, he held no fear of confronting this arms dealer. For he was a secret agent, not a combat operative.

All along, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been steadily deepening his connections with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, striving to earn their trust to the greatest extent possible. This would enhance his future prospects of uncovering the shadowy arms dealer behind the scenes.

This explained why, when duelists attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' merchant convoys and the alchemy workshops in Snow Bird Harbor, the city lord repeatedly intervened. He even deployed merchant ships under his covert control to assist the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with their cargo.

“Additionally, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' boatswain has become ‘Hen Fa’.”

"If he can return and pledge renewed loyalty, then ‘Hen Fa’ can rejoin the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to conduct espionage."

“But... the secret agent at Tranquil Hill hasn't relayed any intelligence for quite some time.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord harbored some hope, though not much.

In truth, over a dozen espionage operations targeting Tranquil Hill under his command had all ended in failure.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was fully prepared for this outcome.

"Treat that big oaf well but keep him under house arrest. Investigate his bloodline origins immediately and uncover how the arms dealer controls him!" the city lord planned inwardly.

Generally speaking, methods for controlling sentient beings were often tied to bloodlines.

For instance, human enchantment spells and goblin enchantment spells were developed based on specific races.

As far as Snow Bird Harbor City Lord knew, the method Bing Xiao possessed to control his gold level giant slave was also rooted in giant bloodlines.

“If we're lucky, we might even remove the control from the big oaf and take direct command of him!”

“Achieving that would be absolutely brilliant.”

“A saint level subordinate...”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn't help but feel his heart race at the thought of such a possibility.

Even if it meant antagonizing the arms dealer, he would likely detain the big guy and make him serve his own purposes.

A saint was truly different, holding extraordinary significance, a whole different level from gold.

"As for Da Dou, he must be placed under house arrest. We must verify if he carries any monitoring devices. If not, eliminating him becomes a viable option."

“After all, should the big oaf become my pawn in the future, Da Dou would be the one in the know.”

"As for Hong Zhu... Hmph. A mere treasure hunter... Interrogate her directly. Use torture if necessary. If she knows the reason behind the earth elemental tribe's uprising on Magnet Archipelago, that would be ideal."

He dined while his thoughts raced.

Soon, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord finished his last sip of milk, wiped his mouth, and rose from his seat.

The investigation phase was about to begin.

“In any case, I must make Tan Mo leave as soon as possible.”

“His continued presence here poses a serious obstacle to my plans for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Instead of leaving the city government palace, Snow Bird Harbor's ruler took a secret passageway down into the underground.

One cell in the dungeon beneath the residence had been hastily converted into an interrogation room.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord entered to find Tan Mo already waiting.

Only the two of them occupied the room.

Tan Mo smiled at Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, maintaining a cordial demeanor. After adjusting the alchemy apparatus, he preemptively reassured the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, there was no need for tension, he could speak freely. However, caution was still advised, as this recorded magic image would ultimately be submitted for higher review.

“Seems my ‘welcome gift’ did the trick,” the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord thought to himself.

The interrogation commenced.

Facing the alchemy recording device, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord began his introduction with a composed expression: "My codename is [Emancipator]. I am a subordinate recruited by Lord [Usurper]. My primary mission is to cooperate with and assist Lord [Usurper], cultivating high-level connections within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to serve the Empire's future objectives..."

"Several years ago, through the efforts of Lord Usurper and others, I was appointed as Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. The specific orders I received from Lord Usurper were to actively develop Snow Bird Harbor, ensuring complete control over it. If necessary, I was authorized to overthrow the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's rule within a single day, transforming it into an imperial naval landing site."

"Upon taking office, I vigorously restructured the city, purging loyalists to the kingdom and gradually replacing them with our own people. Simultaneously, I began eliminating other factions within Snow Bird Harbor."

"Snow Bird Harbor was dominated by three main factions. These were the city government palace, the Sea Bird Church, and the Bayonet Gang."

"Thanks to Lord Usurper, the Sea Bird Church became our close and loyal ally. Thus, only the Bayonet Gang remained as our target."

“Eradicating this local gang was no easy task.”

"The Bayonet Gang boasted a large membership and extensive grassroots support among Snow Bird Harbor's populace. Their leader, a former military officer, possessed unyielding resolve and formidable individual strength. I had to account for their military background and connections."

“Thus, without justifiable cause, mobilizing the city guards against the Bayonet Gang was impossible.”

“While actively awaiting the right moment, I'd attempted to infiltrate spies into the Bayonet Gang.”

"The spies codenamed [Snowflake] and [Flute] were the fruits of these efforts. They infiltrated the Bayonet Gang's upper echelons, becoming two of the six ringleaders: the human ringleader and demon hybrid ringleader."

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, as outsiders, clashed with Bing Xiao. Consequently, Long Fu, the silver level commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, publicly challenged Teng Dong Lang, the leader of the Bayonet Gang, to a duel."

"This sudden incident quickly spread throughout the port. I immediately realized this could be an opportunity."

“Based on my assessment, as a gold level fighter, Teng Dong Lang could not refuse a silver level challenger. As the prominent leader of the Bayonet Gang, he would never avoid battle and risk being branded a coward.”

“Events unfolded exactly as I predicted. Teng Dong Lang accepted the challenge but simultaneously dispatched six ringleaders, ostensibly to test Long Fu's combat prowess, but in reality to ambush him.”

"If there was no opportunity, I would create one. My first move was to order the demon hybrid ringleader to intervene outside the duel arena, commanding him to use his Evil Eye to aid Xiong Ju in defeating Long Fu. This way, I could expose the truth after the battle, ruining Xiong Ju and the Bayonet Gang's reputation, stripping them of their public support!"

“The operation wasn't entirely successful.”

“Though [Flute] used the Evil Eye, Xiong Ju refused to accept it. He held strong principles about duels and despised such underhanded tactics, so he crushed the Evil Eye on the spot.”

Chapter 880: [Gold Hand]’s Death

In the underground dungeon, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued giving his statement to Tan Mo.

"Although Xiong Ju crushed the Evil Eye, [Flute] had indeed intervened in the duel between Xiong Ju and Long Fu. I immediately notified the Snow Bird Arena's owner, and together we dispatched an investigation team to [Flute]'s location."

"However, Teng Dong Lang was exceptionally sharp. He arrived at [Flute]'s location ahead of time and cleared away the evidence. Simultaneously, he persuaded Snow Bird Arena's owner, causing my attempt to verify the facts to fail."

"I did not give up. After the duel, I proactively sought out Long Fu to communicate with him. I informed him about the matter concerning the Evil Eye, intending to incite Long Fu to expose it."

“Even without concrete evidence, if he publicly voiced suspicion, I would have grounds for a formal investigation.”

"To persuade Long Fu and stir his distaste for the Bayonet Gang, I specifically prepared numerous documented crimes committed by the gang. But though Long Fu worships the Justice God, his faith clearly lacks devotion. He chose not to publicly question them, fearing it would enrage the Bayonet Gang and make them enemies. He opted to endure in silence."

"Though this attempt also failed, I remained undeterred. For I knew that even if I couldn't sway Long Fu, the Bayonet Gang would make their own moves."

“Sure enough, as I anticipated, the Bayonet Gang resorted to underhanded tactics. They manipulated the investigation team to probe the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, pressured port officials to demand and impose additional docking fees, and so on.”

"I promptly dispatched a team of alchemists to step forward and assist them in repairing their ships. Most crucially, I proactively lent the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps several pieces of gold level equipment."

"This deepened the rift between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang, while earning me greater trust and goodwill from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"But the real focus of my plan wasn't any of that."

"What came next was the critical move!"

“I deployed [Gold Hand] to impersonate Hua Tang, secretly infiltrating the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship. His mission was twofold: to steal the treasure equipment I had previously provided, and to assassinate the magician members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

"Gold Hand executed this operation flawlessly. He assassinated the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' only two openly acknowledged magicians and recovered the equipment I had lent them."

"This created a deep, irreparable enmity between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang, greatly increasing the likelihood of them seeking vengeance with full force. Simultaneously, the loss of these treasures gave me greater leverage to dominate our future dealings with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"Enraged by his men's deaths, Long Fu abandoned his previous stance and actively sought my assistance. I feigned cooperation, leading him into the port's mage tower. After investigation, I deliberately revealed certain ‘evidence’. This, of course, had been deliberately leaked by [Gold Hand]. Thus, I successfully misled Long Fu into believing that Hua Tang was responsible and closely linked to the Bayonet Gang."

Tan Mo nodded. "Now, please elaborate, City Lord. I'll be direct: [Gold Hand]'s sacrifice is precisely the focus of my investigation."

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression grew even more solemn: "The earlier shifts in the situation unfolded exactly as I had planned. But Long Fu's subsequent actions greatly exceeded my expectations."

“He actually ordered his ships to voluntarily leave the harbor, appearing to prepare for evacuation.”

"Naturally, I was unwilling to let such a valuable pawn leave the stage. Upon rushing over, however, Long Fu was already prepared. He proposed a collaboration, and what shocked me even more was that he knew the precise location of [Gold Hand]!"

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed at this point: "In that moment, I suddenly realized the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wasn't as straightforward as it appeared on the surface. Rumors suggested arms dealers backed them. From that point on, I became more inclined to believe those rumors."

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps knowing the location of Gold Hand was a significant problem. Because if they captured Gold Hand or uncovered more clues, they might deduce the truth, that the Gold Hand wasn't Hua Tang, and the Bayonet Gang was framed."

"So, when Long Fu proactively suggested I take action against the Gold Hand, I chose to agree. As the city lord, I could not tolerate such a gold level criminal. My agreement was the most natural response."

"At the same time, I also hoped that with my deep involvement, the situation would never slip beyond my control."

"So next, I deployed three magic squads and brought in the Sea Bird Bishop to launch a feigned encirclement against [Gold Hand]."

Because too many people were involved, I didn't notify [Gold Hand] beforehand to avoid arousing suspicion or revealing any flaws.

“Once the battle commenced, since I was in command, everything unfolded precisely as I intended.”

"Gold Hand, caught off guard by the assault, recognized the magic squads and immediately understood the situation. When I entered the fray, he secretly used battle qi transmission to communicate with me."

“I explained the entire plan to him. After discussing it, we decided he would use his alchemy puppet to escape.”

“Gold Hand activated the alchemy puppet. I feigned deception, allowing him to retreat to the battlefield's edge.”

“But just as he was about to escape, the port's mage tower unleashed an offensive spell.”

"Gold Hand absolutely had a chance to flee. If he'd dodged that spell, he could have vanished without a trace. But what I never anticipated was that at the most critical moment, he attempted a battle skill and failed!"

He was struck by the spell and disintegrated on the spot.

“Huff...” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord exhaled a heavy breath, his expression grave. “I made a mistake.”

“I bear grave responsibility for [Gold Hand]'s death!”

“I should have devised a more reliable plan. I should have communicated clearly with [Gold Hand] beforehand before initiating the operation.”

“I was too reckless.”

"Perhaps it was because since taking office, I've been relentlessly ambitious, achieving constant successes, which made me arrogant. Or maybe it was because all my energy was focused on the Bayonet Gang at the time, and I didn't pay enough attention."

"I thought everything would go smoothly... Never did I expect [Gold Hand] to falter at the critical moment, perishing under the mage tower's assault!"

“So that's how it was.” Tan Mo sighed as well, offering a few words of consolation to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

After several minutes spent composing himself, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord resumed his account.

Meanwhile, upon the sea.

The allied forces of three pirate crews had encircled the Divine Wind Pirates.

The three pirate captains stood at the bows of their flagship vessels, bellowing threats and promises at Feng Lian. They demanded he surrender every drop of the sorrow-dispelling red wine.

Feng Lian snorted coldly. “How naive you are!”

“You think mere words will secure the treasure?”

“Come at me. Let me see if you have what it takes to make Feng Lian bow his head.”

Chapter 881: Feng Lian vs Da Han

Upon hearing Feng Lian's refusal, the three pirate captains flew into a rage.

“Hmph! Ungrateful fool!”

“Blind to the situation, you idiot!”

“Then we'll show you our might!”

Though furious, the captains maintained restraint. Instead of ordering a full assault, they decided to fight personally.

Two took to the skies, while the third manned a broadside cannon.

“Too slow!” Feng Lian sneered coldly as he watched the two gold level magicians ascend.

In the next instant, he shot forward like a bolt of lightning, closing in on one of the magicians.

The gold level magician paled slightly, utterly unprepared for Feng Lian's astonishing speed. He hastily activated the defensive spell within his magic pendant.

A protective shield abruptly materialized, enveloping his entire body.

Feng Lian passed him in a flash. For an instant, the sky seemed to dim, illuminated only by the faint blade light trailing behind Feng Lian, radiating a pale gray glow.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Darkening Azure.

The gold level magician's eyes widened as he froze mid-air.

The next instant, he was cleaved in two at the waist. His defensive artifacts sparked and crackled, all triggered simultaneously only to be shattered by Feng Lian's blade!

The remaining two gold levels paled in terror.

The gold level magician who had ascended with them turned pale, immediately pulling out several spell scrolls. She cast them in rapid succession, frantically layering defensive spells upon herself.

Feng Lian didn't spare her a glance. Instead, his gaze slowly settled on the gold level fighter below.

The pirate captain, witnessing his comrade's brutal death, turned ashen faced. He was now frantically manipulating the cannon.

This was a gold level cannon.

Battle skill - Heavy Cannon·Iron Fist!

The pirate captain poured all his battle qi into the cannon. Empowered by the flagship's gold level components, he completed the charge in just a second or two.

Boom!

Empowered by gold level battle qi, the cannonball coalesced into a black fist, smashing viciously toward Feng Lian.

Feng Lian's expression remained unchanged. He didn't dodge, instead unleashing his battle skill directly.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Gigantic Slash.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Swift Slash.

The scythe in his hand swung forward, slicing out a wind blade.

The blade moved swiftly, rapidly expanding as it struck the iron fist.

“Useless! This is my specialty... What?” The gold level fighter's smugness vanished instantly, replaced by horror.

He watched in shock as the iron fist was cleaved in two by the wind blade.

Its momentum carried on, striking the pirate ship that had fired the projectile.

The pirate ship was merely silver level, sliced open as smoothly as butter meeting a hot knife.

Fortunately, the gold level fighter immediately retreated, dodging the blow.

But his face was grim, cold sweat beading on his forehead: “This can't be! My heavy cannon uses gold level shells, reinforced by my own battle qi. How could Feng Lian's wind blade sever it cleanly?"

Meanwhile, the gold level pirate captain, frantically channeling spells, screamed: “Something's off about that Feng Lian!”

“His battle skills are far too powerful, it's unreasonable!”

Feng Lian sneered coldly: “The words of the weak... Still, it shows progress.”

“Because you've finally accepted your place.”

Before he could finish, the gold level magician growled low, casting spells and launching an assault.

Surrounded by azure winds, Feng Lian moved with blinding speed and agility, effortlessly evading the attacking spells.

The battle continued.

Facing two opponents alone, Feng Lian held a decisive advantage. His wind blades were razor-sharp, their destructive force defying logic.

The defenses of the gold level magician were shattered by eighty percent in no time.

The gold level fighter, lacking aerial battle skill, could only maneuver across the decks of the ships, relying on cannons or his own gold level battle qi to attack Feng Lian from afar.

This posed far less threat than close combat.

Enraged, the gold level fighter roared, commanding his subordinates to launch a full-scale assault.

The gold level magician also sensed trouble and immediately followed suit. The pirates who had lost their own captain were equally determined to seek revenge and joined the assault.

Three pirate coalitions vs. the Divine Wind Pirates.

The Divine Wind Pirates' first mate sneered coldly. They had long been prepared for battle. As the full-scale conflict erupted, the Divine Wind pirate ships collectively retreated, swiftly creating distance.

Facing overwhelming numbers, the Divine Wind Pirates avoided direct confrontation. Instead, they leveraged their ships' superior speed to wage guerrilla warfare.

The pirate coalition's coordination was abysmal. After being repeatedly drawn out by the Divine Wind Pirates, their formation shattered completely, rendering their assault unsustainable.

The Divine Wind Pirates seized the initiative. Soon after, Feng Lian exploited a gap in their defense and slew the gold level fighter.

The sole remaining gold level magician, utterly demoralized, abandoned the subordinates and fled.

Magicians had the advantage of long-range flight with minimal energy expenditure.

Feng Lian did not pursue her. Instead, he remarked, “You seem to have forgotten I'm a magician too.”

Wind spell - Wind Blade: Reaper.

Wind spell - Wind Blade: Invisibility.

The invisible wind blades now possessed lock-on capabilities. No matter how frantically the gold level magician shifted her position, she couldn't evade them.

The gold level magician screamed wildly, “Don't... don't kill me, Feng Lian!”

“Spare me! Pardon my life! Or you'll regret it!”

As she shouted, she cast offensive spells, attempting to intercept the invisible wind blades.

Her desperate gamble succeeded, intercepting three blades.

But the remaining blades still struck her one after another.

In her dying moments, she glared fiercely at Feng Lian: “Even if I die, you won't live well!”

With that, she crushed a rune stone in her hand.

The floating stone shattered, releasing a strange light.

Feng Lian sensed the strange light approaching and attempted to dodge, but failed.

Struck by the light, his mask concealed his expression, yet his voice betrayed astonishment: “This is... a time divine spell?”

The gold level magician wielded a divine script talisman. The talisman, given by Da Han, sealed a saint level time divine spell!

Feng Lian, struck by the divine spell, quickly realized his time had slowed considerably.

His movement speed, attack speed, even his thinking speed, every aspect plummeted. This caused his combat effectiveness to crash.

Feng Lian thought for a moment, then began dealing with the pirates.

The leaders of all three pirate crews fell by Feng Lian's hand. Even under the time spell's effects, he swiftly slaughtered the pirates, leaving them scattered and defeated.

The pirates' morale collapsed, and they fled in panic.

The Divine Wind Pirates pursued relentlessly, inflicting heavy casualties.

In the end, only three ships managed to escape the battlefield.

As the Divine Wind Pirates cleaned up the battlefield, Feng Lian landed on the flagship's deck.

He attempted to dispel the divine magic binding him, but failed.

“Da Han's handiwork this time is substantial. He must have sent quite a few divine script talismans.”

"His target is me. This strategy is undoubtedly correct."

“It seems he will arrive soon.”

Hearing Feng Lian's analysis, the first mate grew frantic: “Captain! Quickly activate the alchemy components! Bind yourself to every ship in the Divine Wind Pirates! Share the burden of this divine magic!”

Feng Lian nodded slightly: “Exactly. That was my intention.”

The ships of the Divine Wind Pirates all originated from Gear Island. Their similar models allowed for coordination far surpassing ordinary pirate groups.

After activating several different alchemy components, Feng Lian's battle qi and magic power became interconnected with the entire fleet.

The ships thus shared the burden of the saint level divine magic upon him.

With this step accomplished, Feng Lian's combat strength was finally restored to more than half. He swiftly ordered the entire pirate crew to retreat from the battlefield at maximum speed, paying no heed to the fact that only one-third of the loot had been gathered.

The Divine Wind Pirates' loyalty to Feng Lian was unwavering; not a single voice raised in protest against the retreat order.

However, the divine magic also affected the Divine Wind's ships. While their speed remained impressive, the journey stretched out considerably.

Soon enough, the fleet led by Da Han caught up.

Da Han soared high into the sky, heading straight for Feng Lian.

His murderous intent radiated unchecked: “Feng Lian, you've killed so many of my men. You think you can escape?”

“Today, you will die by my hand.”

“To save time, why not just kill yourself? How about it?”

“I'll spare your subordinates. I swear it on my name, Da Han, fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates.”

Feng Lian sneered coldly, “Da Han, even if you're a saint level cultivator, you won't make me surrender without a fight, let alone commit suicide.”

“Come! Let me witness the might of a saint level cultivator!”

Da Han snorted coldly, wasting no more words.

With a sweeping gesture of his massive hand, his battle qi surged forth, causing the heavens and earth to shift.

The surrounding temperature plummeted, frigid air surged, frost blossoms crystallized wildly, freezing the sea surface around them.

Feng Lian bore the brunt of this “attention.” His white cloak froze rigid, and a layer of frosty white coated his bronze mask.

“Saint domain expansion...” Feng Lian keenly sensed the transformation in the surrounding world—temporarily imbued with other laws!

Without a doubt, these temporary laws originated from Da Han.

To save time, Da Han had directly deployed his most potent means—his saint domain!

The domain's original radius, centered on him, was only a dozen meters in actual size. But upon active expansion, it spread like diluted liquid, its scope rapidly increasing.

Now, Da Han directly enveloped the entire battlefield with his expanded saint domain.

Within this domain, the power of his battle skills received immense amplification, while Feng Lian's abilities suffered severe suppression.

This increase and decrease instantly created a colossal disparity.

Feng Lian's gaze hardened with unprecedented severity.

He drew a deep breath and immediately closed his eyes.

Magic power and battle qi surged forth, blending into one under the influence of his bloodline talent.

The next instant, he snapped his eyes open and exhaled sharply, aiming at the long-handled scythe in his grasp.

In that split second, a shadowy human form materialized from his body. This phantom dragged a spectral scythe, making a light slash directly ahead.

A small, emerald-green wind swirled forth, no larger than 30 centimeters, delicate and intricate.

The emerald wind lunged toward Da Han, trailing a long, pale-green vapor in midair.

Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom!

This time, it was Da Han’s turn to show astonishment, hastily unleashing his battle skill.

In an instant, ice crystallized, forming towering walls that stretched between heaven and earth.

The emerald wind penetrated directly through the ice walls, passing through one after another. It traversed seven layers before slowly dissipating.

Da Han's expression turned grave. His gaze locked intently on Feng Lian in the distance, a trickle of blood slowly flowing from his nostrils.

Chapter 882: Breeze Bloodline

“So that's it, you possess the Breeze bloodline.”

As he spoke, the blood in Da Han's nose receded, the traces vanishing completely in moments.

Feng Lian noticed this subtle detail and instantly understood: Da Han was under the effect of some constant time divine spell, enabling rapid healing.

He cultivated both magic and battle qi, while Da Han was a dual practitioner of sorcery and divine power. Both men had mastered dual disciplines.

Feng Lian felt a chill in his heart: Da Han... truly worthy of being the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates. He had exposed Feng Lian's true nature in just the first round of combat.

The Breeze bloodline had a racial magic known as Wind Combination. Any wind-element energy source could be fused into a single entity.

Under normal circumstances, the energies of fighters, magicians, and clerics, battle qi, magic power, and divine power, could not merge. Transcendents could only wield one.

Simultaneously using battle skill, spells, and divine spells wasn't difficult. What's rare was the true fusion of battle qi, magic power, and divine power.

Feng Lian achieved this through his bloodline.

It was precisely because of this that his offensive and defensive capabilities, among all aspects, were vastly superior.

"Your bloodline allows energy fusion, but it doesn't grant you an overwhelming combat advantage. The reason your battle skill and spells are so powerful is because of the sacred artifact in your hands—the Sea Wind's Howl," Da Han continued.

Feng Lian's eyes flickered, betraying his inner turmoil.

True, he remained a believer of the Sea Wind God, though his faith fell short of the level required for a clergy position.

Nevertheless, through his contributions to the church, he had secured a saint level sacred artifact: the long-handled scythe in his grasp, the Sea Wind's Howl. It could be wielded in close combat or used as a staff, but most crucially, the scythe was imbued with divine magic.

Thus, Feng Lian's strength was forged by the convergence of three elements.

His bloodline allowed the fusion of battle qi and magic power. Amplified by the sacred artifact, his might was multiplied several times over.

It must be said that the Sea Wind Church suited Feng Lian exceptionally well.

His choice of faith proved remarkably wise, significantly boosting his combat prowess.

However, precisely because he had chosen his faith based on self-interest rather than sincere devotion, his belief in the Sea Wind God remained shallow. Consequently, his faith level never truly deepened. Ironically, many of his subordinates became priests of the Sea Wind Church instead.

Having priests within a pirate crew was an exceptionally rare occurrence.

Typically, only formal naval forces maintained such personnel.

“Let me see what secrets you hold,” Da Han declared, initiating the time divine spell.

One of his pupils remained unchanged, while the other began to shift, reflecting a series of magic images within its depths.

Divine spell—Time Rewind.

The necklace and ring on Feng Lian's body flashed repeatedly, yet they couldn't withstand the opponent's scrutiny. He certainly wouldn't sit idly by. Wielding his scythe, he charged forward.

“Lord Captain!” Every move Feng Lian made filled the Divine Wind Pirates' crew with worry and tension.

Among the pirates surrounding Da Han, some admired Feng Lian's courage, while others sneered at his recklessness.

By conventional logic, Da Han, a sorcerer by primary vocation, should be weak in close combat.

Feng Lian, specializing in battle qi, excelled in melee.

Yet he avoided direct combat, pulling back to mid-to-long range and refusing to close in on Da Han.

His weakness in close combat only applied against peers of equal level. Feng Lian was one level below Da Han, so his melee advantage couldn't be fully exploited.

Moreover, Da Han also cultivated divine magic. Clergymen possessed remarkably versatile abilities, as the gods themselves were omnipotent. Naturally, most clergymen filled the healer role within teams of transcendents.

This was because, compared to magicians and fighters, their healing capabilities were far more pronounced.

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Great Slash.

Spell - Wind Blade: Phantom.

Battle skill - Wind Binding Armor.

Spell - Tornado.

…

A cascade of transcendent powers unfolded in succession through Feng Lian's hands.

Whirling wind blades and raging tornadoes coalesced into a colossal offensive surge.

But when this onslaught reached ten meters of Da Han, its power diminished drastically. It was like a ferocious warrior suddenly having his backbone ripped out, or a roaring hero having his vocal cords severed in an instant.

Saint domain!

Centered on Da Han, a layer of white frost blanketed the area within a radius of over ten meters.

It was precisely this frost that severely curtailed Feng Lian's attacks.

This was a true saint domain!

Earlier, Da Han had proactively expanded it, effectively diluting the domain's power and granting Feng Lian an opening. Now, restored to its normal form, the saint domain immediately shifted the battle's momentum upon activation.

All of Feng Lian's assaults entering the saint domain suffered a catastrophic drop in potency. Not even 20% remained, only barely 10% of the original force.

"Oh? I see." Da Han's reconnaissance efforts yielded fresh intelligence.

“So you're a remnant of the Tai He Clan.”

“No wonder you've been lurking in the shadows...”

Transcendent clans primarily descended from the Breeze bloodline were few and far between. The most renowned, and once the most powerful, was the Tai He Clan.

This human clan had originally possessed immense influence, with branches spread across multiple continents and profound foundations.

Regrettably, during the demonic invasion of the Main Plane, the Tai He Clan, for a confluence of reasons, ultimately surrendered to the demons, becoming guides for the demonic hordes assaulting the Main Plane.

After the demons were repelled, the Tai He Clan faced reckoning. Not only were their ancestral holdings confiscated, but the entire clan faced a united manhunt by the major powers of the Main Plane.

“Slanderous fabrication!” Feng Lian retorted coldly.

·

Battle skill - Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom!

He resorted to his old trick, expelling a small swirl of breeze.

It was the very move that had just pierced the saint domain.

Without a doubt, this was one of his trump cards.

The breeze invaded Da Han's vicinity, penetrating about a meter into the saint domain before dissipating harmlessly.

Like an eraser, it wiped away a meter-long stretch of frost, revealing the underlying surface.

Da Han snorted coldly. Without even chanting, he swept his hand, causing a surge of frost to rise from the saint domain. The frost blazed like light, a streak of brilliant rainbow hurtling toward Feng Lian.

Feng Lian attempted to dodge, but at the critical moment, his time slowed.

The frost grazed Feng Lian's shoulder, instantly crystallizing into a thick layer. With a series of cracks, a solid block of ice formed.

Feng Lian swiftly switched hands to grip his scythe, the affected shoulder already numb.

He retreated again, widening the distance. His entire body resembled a soaring bird, circling around Da Han while unleashing a relentless barrage of wind blades.

The diverse formation of wind blades formed a razor-sharp offensive, leaving the watching pirates watching with dry throats.

The gold level pirates imagined themselves in Da Han's position, it would be difficult to withstand Feng Lian's assault.

But Da Han was saint level.

He stood immovable, relying on his saint domain's defensive effect that also amplified his racial magic's offensive might.

Da Han took the initiative, closing the distance.

Feng Lian dared not engage head-on, choosing instead to retreat, desperately maintaining his distance.

He excelled at acceleration, but Da Han wielded the time divine spell, accelerating his own time while slowing Feng Lian's.

With the doubled effect, Da Han steadily closed in while Feng Lian retreated, countering as he fell back.

Feng Lian's attacks proved ineffective.

Earlier, Da Han had been careless. Now fully focused, he fought methodically. His mastery of the saint level granted him complete control over the battlefield.

“Quick, aid the Lord Captain!”

“Lord Captain, catch!”

The Divine Wind Pirates' first mate and others, sensing the dire situation, swiftly activated the alchemy components.

Nearly every pirate ship participated.

A section of the deck flipped open, revealing a massive fan.

The fan whirred, spinning rapidly and generating a spiraling green wind.

Strands of green wind, serpentine in form, shot toward the battlefield, growing thinner and thinner as they flew.

By the time they reached Feng Lian, they were as thin as belts.

The green winds bound Feng Lian and his pirate ship together.

In an instant, Feng Lian's aura surged, and his attacks multiplied nearly tenfold!

“Interesting. The Breeze bloodline has advanced this far.” Da Han remained unmoved, still able to comment with leisure.

Even a tenfold increase in attack couldn't breach Da Han's saint domain!

The gap between saint level and gold level was that vast.

Yet Feng Lian's state differed from the ordinary. Using the winds, he channeled every magic pool from the pirate ships into himself, accumulating so much power it overflowed!

While he freely squandered it to form an offensive against Da Han's advance, he simultaneously drew magic through the wind currents, surging it into his mask.

The next moment, the bronze mask erupted with an aura, revealing itself as saint level equipment.

Only, this artifact was no longer a sacred artifact, but a magic item.

"Huh? Part of a magic construct?” Da Han caught a glimpse of the truth.

Feng Lian's entire body began to swell.

From his normal height, he transformed into a figure over three meters tall.

The bronze mask, the long-handled scythe, and everything else grew in proportion.

In this state, even a simple Wind Blade from Feng Lian rivaled the power of Wind Blade: Gigantic Slash.

With the mask fully activated, Feng Lian's combat power surged again, leaving Da Han visibly impressed. "In this state, Feng Lian is far beyond the reach of my gold level subordinates. He's practically a quasi-saint!"

“But unfortunately, he still can't break through my saint domain.”

Under Feng Lian's relentless assault, Da Han's saint domain had shrunk from its original radius of over ten meters to roughly eight meters.

Yet this represented Feng Lian's absolute limit.

Witnessing this, Feng Lian exhaled a breath of stale air. Without further hesitation, he swung his spear and cast a spell upon his own pirate crew.

Spell—Wind Booster.

A powerful wind surged forth, propelling the pirate ships forward.

The Divine Wind Pirates began their retreat.

Da Han snorted coldly and immediately launched an attack at Feng Lian.

Relying on his two saint level artifacts, Feng Lian struggled to resist.

Watching the Divine Wind Pirates flee into the distance, Da Han ruthlessly suppressed Feng Lian before turning to assault the latter's pirate ship.

Feng Lian swiftly followed. Dragged by the wind, his speed was astonishing, and he soon entangled Da Han once more.

Time and again, Da Han failed to gain the upper hand.

A murderous glint flashed in Da Han's eyes. He threw everything he had at Feng Lian, quickly overwhelming him.

But the wind current intervened again, pulling Feng Lian away.

Da Han pursued relentlessly, chasing for several minutes before finally catching Feng Lian again.

Yet while he could handle Feng Lian, he couldn't handle the pirate ship. Soon, the pirate ship pulled away, dragging Feng Lian off once more.

At that moment, Qian Xing transmitted his voice: “Shall I intervene?”

He still owed Da Han a promise of full-fledged assistance.

Chapter 883: Zong Ge is Saved

Qian Xing had previously sought help from Da Han to rescue Ba Qi.

Da Han fulfilled their agreement and helped him locate Ba Qi. Then, both were startled away by Teacher Si Ling.

Though Qian Xing hadn't achieved his goal, Da Han had fulfilled his part of the bargain. So after returning, Qian Xing remained aboard Da Han's fleet without leaving.

He also stayed out of the public eye, becoming Da Han's hidden ace in the hole under the latter's arrangements.

Seeing Da Han's “ineffectiveness” in pursuing Feng Lian, Qian Xing volunteered.

Da Han rejected him telepathically: “It's unnecessary.”

Qian Xing's pledge to “fight with full force” was hard-earned, he wouldn't squander it on Feng Lian. Deploying a saint level combatant to take Feng Lian's life was simply not worth it.

Da Han decided against personally engaging in battle. Enveloped within the saint domain, he flew back to his flagship.

With a sweeping gesture, he ordered the entire fleet to pursue the Divine Wind Pirates.

Qian Xing immediately fell deep in thought.

He could see that Da Han hadn't deployed his full strength against Feng Lian.

"Though Feng Lian is only gold level, his combat prowess shouldn't be underestimated. He also possesses two saint level pieces of equipment...”

“Is Da Han deliberately conserving his strength for the advance into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom?”

Indeed.

Qian Xing understood clearly: Da Han's sorcerer bloodline was frost-based, and he had also cultivated time divine spells. Da Han's situation differed from Feng Lian's. His faith in the Dusk God was heartfelt, and his adherence to the Dusk Church's doctrines ran deep in his bones.

Da Han's power stemmed from two sources: the might of frost and the grandeur of time. Thus, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Ten Millennia Ice Lake was an ideal environment for him.

Qian Xing considered the situation from Da Han's perspective. If he were in Da Han's shoes, he too would undoubtedly head for the Ten Millennia Ice Lake. There, the opportunity to improve his saint level was undeniable!

Beyond preserving combat strength, Qian Xing contemplated another possibility.

“Could it be... that Da Han foresaw something during his recent reconnaissance?”

Da Han's time divine spell not only allowed him to revisit the past but also to peer into the future. When Qian Xing had once requested Da Han's divination, the latter had been deceived while revisiting the past. Yet, when predicting the future, he had seen Teacher Si Ling.

In Qian Xing's view, Da Han's foresight surpassed his own ability to revisit the past.

"Da Han uncovered many secrets about Feng Lian by revisiting the past. How could he not have predicted the future?"

“If I were in his place, I would have done exactly that.”

“If this possibility holds true, it means that Feng Lian possesses a means capable of threatening a saint level existence!”

The more Qian Xing pondered it, the more plausible this possibility seemed, and not by a small margin.

This also explained Feng Lian's unusually assertive stance toward Da Han.

Under normal circumstances, a gold level would go out of their way to avoid a saint level opponent.

Coupled with what Da Han had revealed during their battle—that Feng Lian was a remnant of the Tai He Clan.

Qian Xing mused inwardly: "The Tai He Clan's legacy likely contains methods to counter saint level threats. Much like the Ice Sculpture Kingdom must harbor trump cards against legend level foes."

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had once been a legend level power. Though it now possessed only three saint level cultivators, as an independent maritime nation with world-leading alchemy technology, it undoubtedly possessed legend level capabilities.

This was a widely accepted fact.

Though Qian Xing cultivated only battle qi as a pure fighter, he was no reckless fool. His mind remained exceptionally sharp, possessing ample combat wisdom.

As Qian Xing pondered, Feng Lian landed on the deck of his flagship.

With Da Han no longer personally engaged in battle, Feng Lian also withdrew the wind currents, severing his connection to his pirate crew.

Consequently, Feng Lian decisively ceased using the bronze mask. After all, relying solely on his personal magic pool and internal battle qi reserves to wield a saint level artifact was simply too taxing.

The first mate urgently asked, “Captain, the Icebound Pirates are hot on our trail. What should we do?”

Feng Lian remained composed. “Da Han possesses the time divine spell. I can sense he's employing different divine spells to track me.”

“Perhaps he's discovered something.”

“That's why he's voluntarily stepped back, using his subordinates to wear us down.”

“Heh heh heh.” At this point, Feng Lian laughed to himself. “Then let them chase.”

The first mate's face darkened with worry.

On the surface, the Divine Wind Pirates held the advantage in speed. But did the Icebound Pirates lack the means to counter such a swift opponent?

Certainly not.

And they possessed more than one method.

As the fourth-in-command of the Fire Beard Pirates, a saint level powerhouse, the Icebound Pirates have dominated the seas for so long. How could they possibly fail to handle a high-speed target while maintaining such a massive fleet?

Not to mention, just Da Han's time divine spell alone poses a tremendous threat to the Divine Wind Pirates!

The first mate said, “We're already under the effect of the time divine spell ourselves. We can't shake off the Icebound Pirates.” Who knows what tactics they'll deploy next against us?"

“Even if this stalemate persists, this attrition pattern is extremely disadvantageous for us.”

The first mate's words held undeniable logic.

The Divine Wind Pirates were currently sailing at maximum speed.

This was abnormal.

Ships typically traveled at normal cruising speed. Maximum speed consumed enormous amounts of magic power.

Over time, even the Divine Wind Pirates couldn't sustain such intensity.

Beneath his bronze mask, Feng Lian's brow furrowed slightly.

Under these circumstances, he could voluntarily abandon the sorrow-dispelling red wine and hand it over to Da Han. The latter would have gained something and found an excuse to leave, likely choosing to back down.

But that didn't serve Feng Lian's interests.

After a moment's thought, Feng Lian gave a cold laugh. “Change course. Head for Ice Sculpture Island.”

“All of this is for revenge against the Li Jian Clan.”

"Last time we attacked the Intoxication Church's fleet, we failed to draw them out. This time, leading Da Han's Icebound Pirates, I'll deliberately assault their defenses. I'll see how these bastards respond!"

“Cough cough cough.”

Feng Lian coughed several times, spitting out mouthfuls of blood.

The blood splattered onto the deck, instantly crystallizing into frost.

The first mate obeyed without question.

Though Feng Lian had been defeated in battle against Da Han, his prestige remained utterly undiminished.

He had remained resolute, bearing the external pressure brought by the Icebound Pirates while skillfully executing a diversionary tactic, killing with a borrowed knife!

The Li Jian Clan’s Kong Pan had deliberately abandoned the sorrow-dispelling red wine, luring the pirates into fighting amongst themselves.

Though Feng Lian had fallen for the trap for days, he had long since discerned the scheme.

This time, he would use himself as bait to exact vengeance upon the Li Jian Clan once more.

Because of Feng Lian's move, the storm of naval combat rapidly closed in upon the coastline of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Meanwhile, within the Ice Sculpture capital, turbulent undercurrents surged as the national ceremony drew near.

Zi Di gazed at the pearl bubbles in her hand and sighed inwardly: “Only five left.”

These were the final reserves.

Not just hers alone, but the pearl bubbles for Zong Ge and everyone else!

Only five remained.

And now, she had no choice but to take one more for herself.

Deceptive Disguise spell!

Zi Di had long constructed a portable magic formation capable of casting this spell.

When she stepped out of the formation, her appearance had drastically changed—she was now Long Fu.

Zong Ge, who had been impersonating Long Fu, had collapsed and remained unconscious. Zi Di had no choice but to step forward herself, assuming the role of her own commander to face the outside world.

In the reception room, Zi Di received the steward from the Li Jian Clan.

The steward saw “Long Fu” and wore a perfectly measured look of delight: “Lord Long Fu's prowess is astonishing. To be struck by the Frost Aura yet remain unscathed, as if you'd consumed the [Beautiful Future] potion without getting affected—truly remarkable.”

"To be honest, the clan leader was quite concerned about this very point. For this mission, he not only sent me with specialized alchemy testing tools but also three bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine."

Long Fu (Zi Di) felt a stir within and smiled faintly. “The effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion are indeed troublesome. However, after the battle, I offered prayers and was fortunate enough to receive the favor of my lord, the Justice God. His grace completely dispelled the potion's effects from my body.”

“Of course, I appreciate your clan's concern.”

“Why not let me test it now? That way, Lord Mian Li Zang can be completely at ease.”

Seeing “Long Fu” volunteer for the test, the Li Jian Clan steward visibly relaxed.

One of his missions on this diplomatic journey was to facilitate this very test.

When “Long Fu” mentioned the Justice God and prayer earlier, the steward had hesitated to broach the subject. After all, it touched upon matters of faith. To raise it would be to question the Justice God. For the Justice God's believers, this would be an immense insult.

However, now that “Long Fu” (Zi Di) had volunteered, it was the best possible outcome.

Without delay, the steward retrieved the alchemy testing device and handed it to Zi Di.

Zi Di first inspected it, confirmed it was in order, and then used it.

The results of the alchemy test, of course, showed no issues whatsoever. First, Zi Di certainly possessed none of the potion's effects. Second, even if she did, the Deceptive Disguise spell far surpassed the capabilities of the testing device.

The steward breathed a sigh of relief at this outcome, offering a few words of flattery toward “Long Fu” and praising the divine might of the Justice God.

He then inquired about the earlier proposal for cooperation.

Zi Di's heart sank slightly as she informed him, “I have already reported this matter upward, but I cannot say when a specific response will arrive.”

The steward urged her a few times before rising to take his leave.

Zi Di called him back, proposing a trade for sorrow-dispelling red wine. Her justification was straightforward, she had recently acquired the alchemy technique for the Ice Cube Red Wine, and while time ice cubes was readily available, sorrow-dispelling red wine was rare. Thus, she wouldn't pass up any opportunity to obtain it.

The steward smiled, pausing to negotiate with Zi Di.

Zi Di showed no trace of emotion on her face, patiently haggling over the price. Ultimately, to avoid suspicion, she purchased only two bottles.

“Alright, Zong Ge is saved now!” Zi Di suppressed her inner excitement. As they parted, she indicated to the steward that if the price was right, she would be very interested in acquiring all the sorrow-dispelling red wine the Li Jian Clan currently possessed.

Chapter 884: Urgent Military Intelligence

Igloo City.

In the city government palace.

Mian Li Zang studied the intelligence in his hand: “Still no clear response from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps...”

Under the magic light, his expression darkened.

Members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, including Long Fu (Zong Ge) and Yao Ma (Zi Di), were temporarily lodged in accommodations provided by the Li Jian Clan.

“So far, the royal capital has detected no activity matching the reports from Magician Yao Ma.”

“They've mastered some form of communication, one so covert even our clan can't detect it?”

“Also... Magician Yao Ma purchased two bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

"Heh, such resourcefulness is truly remarkable. Entrusted by her arms dealer to open markets in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, she clearly possesses considerable skill."

“Unfortunately... we've already detected that Long Fu is bedridden.”

Despite Zong Ge and Zi Di's extreme caution and repeated checks, they remained under the secret surveillance of the Li Jian Clan. Neither Zong Ge nor Zi Di had noticed.

Clearly, the Li Jian Clan wielded formidable surveillance capabilities.

Thus, when Zong Ge returned to his quarters bedridden after his victorious duel, the Li Jian Clan learned of his condition.

The steward they had dispatched knew the truth—that “Long Fu” was actually the Magician Yao Ma in disguise.

“From this, it's clear the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps possesses exceptional disguise techniques.”

“Had we not detected two ‘Long Fus’, Magician Yao Ma's disguise would never have been exposed.”

Mian Li Zang murmured to himself, though he found it unsurprising.

In his view, as an arms dealer's agent, smuggling weapons demanded formidable camouflage skills.

“The two bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine you purchased, may they offer you a taste unlike any other... Heh heh heh.”

A glint of anticipation flickered in Mian Li Zang's eyes.

“Urgent military intelligence, Clan Leader!”

At that moment, a voice suddenly sounded outside the study door.

Mian Li Zang's expression shifted slightly as he immediately commanded, “Enter.”

Intelligence delivered by specialized agents was invariably critical and urgent.

“What?”

“Feng Lian engaged Da Han in combat and fled in defeat.”

“The Icebound Pirates are pursuing the Divine Wind Pirates and have approached our coastal defense zone?”

Mian Li Zang's expression instantly hardened.

“Feng Lian did this deliberately!”

“He deliberately lured his formidable enemy toward our clan to escape Da Han's pursuit.”

“His earlier raid on the Intoxication Church's fleet may have been intended to draw out the Second Naval Fleet.”

Analyzing the intelligence, Mian Li Zang immediately sensed Feng Lian's deep animosity toward the Li Jian Clan.

Beyond the intelligence, there was also Kong Pan's request for instructions.

The enemy threat was grave, and Kong Pan, weighed down by concerns, needed to reach consensus with the clan leader.

Mian Li Zang grew restless. Rising abruptly, he paced the study with furrowed brows.

His mind raced, thoughts flashing relentlessly through his mind.

"The bulk of our clan's strength lies within the Second Naval Fleet. It absolutely must not be compromised; it is our clan's greatest foundation!"

“Feng Lian and his Divine Wind Pirates alone are troublesome enough, let alone with Da Han and the Icebound Pirates backing him.”

“Military logic dictates we should avoid his sharp edge.”

“But that's impossible now.”

“Feng Lian is attacking the Second Naval Fleet's defense zone; retreating isn't an option.”

"The military tribunal's accountability is secondary. The greatest loss lies in the political realm. Should we retreat without a fight, our clan's reputation will plummet to rock bottom. After that, the royal family will have a legitimate reason to intervene in our affairs and dismantle our power."

Just moments ago, Mian Li Zang had been smugly plotting his scheme against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Now, he had to rack his brains to devise a course of action that would align with the Li Jian Clan's interests.

“A direct retreat is out of the question.”

“But engaging in direct combat offers no chance of victory either...”

With the military situation urgent, Mian Li Zang raced against time. Once he reached a conclusion, he immediately dispatched a message.

He ordered Kong Pan and the others to contact the First Naval Fleet of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom without delay and join forces with them to initiate hostilities!

“We absolutely must bring in the First Fleet.”

“This way, all sides will suffer losses, greatly mitigating the negative impact of our navy's weakened strength.”

“Once the battle reaches a certain stage, we can plan our retreat.”

By then, withdrawal would be justified.

After all, Feng Lian's forces were formidable opponents. Da Han was a saint level sorcerer, and his Icebound Pirates even possessed an Ice Crystal Jellyfish.

The Ice Crystal Jellyfish was a saint level magic beast. Counting them up, that made two saint level enemies!

This was the world of the strong.

In terms of sheer numbers, the Second Naval Fleet naturally had the advantage.

But the mere thought of facing two saint level opponents sapped the clan leader's will to fight.

He didn't know that the Icebound Pirates also harbored Qian Xing, a third saint level fighter.

Military intelligence traveled through the fastest communication channels. Kong Pan swiftly received the order from Mian Li Zang.

She fully endorsed it and acted immediately.

Shortly thereafter, at the First Naval Fleet.

“Rear Admiral Mei Lin, the Vice Admiral summons you for urgent military matters!” a naval officer announced.

Receiving the notice, Mei Lin felt puzzled: “Strange. If it's urgent military intelligence, why didn't the Vice Admiral just use the communication device to notify me?”

“Sending someone to report like this wastes time.”

With lingering doubts, Mei Lin arrived at the flagship of the First Naval Fleet.

Entering the conference chamber, the First Fleet's primary commander was already present, along with another Rear Admiral level official.

The main commander was a snow elf of royal lineage, holding the rank of Vice Admiral.

Below him stood three Rear Admirals. Mei Lin was one of them.

Seconds after Mei Lin took her seat, the third Rear Admiral arrived.

The Vice Admiral pointed at the alchemy sea chart and addressed the three Rear Admirals: “Feng Lian is being pursued by Da Han and has charged headlong into this sea sector.”

“The Second Naval Fleet was tasked with defense. Now, Kong Pan has delivered an urgent distress signal.”

“Share your assessments.”

“Da Han?!” Mei Lin instantly grasped the gravity of the situation and immediately stated, "Our forces are not far from the Second Fleet's defense zone. If we deploy now, leveraging the speed of our new warships, we can definitely reach them in time."

“We can preemptively set up the battlefield, arrange our formations, and maximize our advantage of terrain.”

The Vice Admiral remained noncommittal, turning his gaze to the other two Rear Admirals.

One of them cleared his throat. “The enemy force is overwhelming. Even if we give our all, we absolutely cannot match two saint level opponents.”

“Quite right,” the other Rear Admiral chimed in. “Even if we form the strongest possible battle formation, it won't withstand Da Han's assault.”

Mei Lin's frown deepened. "Gentlemen, it's true the enemy is stronger than us. But as soldiers of the Kingdom, how can we shrink from battle? At worst, we sacrifice our lives for our country!"

This statement immediately drew a rebuttal from the other Rear Admiral: “Mei Lin, you are mistaken. We should preserve our useful bodies to serve the kingdom.”

“Precisely,” the other Rear Admiral added. “This battle cannot be won by our navy alone. Our chance of victory lies with our nation's three saint level combatants.”

“Our navy's role is to buy time, gather intelligence, and wear down the saint level forces like Da Han as much as possible.”

“And that task can be accomplished by the Second Naval Fleet.”

“Indeed.”

“There's no need to sacrifice all of us.”

"Once the Second Naval Fleet has depleted the enemy's strength, our saint level forces will surely prevail. At that point, our First Naval Fleet will still need to shoulder the defense of the Kingdom's maritime zones."

“And when that time comes, surely we can't expect the three saints to personally patrol the waters around Ice Sculpture Island?”

Mei Lin fell silent, but a sudden realization dawned within her.

Her earlier thoughts had focused solely on military strategy, yet there were political factors to consider as well.

“The Li Jian Clan maintains ironclad control over the Second Naval Fleet, which has practically become their private military force.”

"Not long ago, when the Intoxication Church's fleet was plundered, the Li Jian Clan reported that even the accompanying recording devices were destroyed. Such brazen audacity lays bare the Li Jian Clan's complete control over the Second Naval Fleet!"

What did the Vice Admiral mean by this?

Mei Lin immediately recalled that when she received the notification earlier, it hadn't come through the alchemy communication device, but via a naval soldier.

Though it was an urgent military matter, they'd deliberately chosen this time-consuming method of delivery.

Not only that, but the Vice Admiral had also summoned his subordinates for a meeting.

If the military situation were so straightforward, why would discussion be necessary?

Viewed this way, the Vice Admiral's intentions became crystal clear!

Mei Lin promptly nodded in agreement, acknowledging the validity of the other two Rear Admirals' arguments.

Seeing the three Rear Admirals reach consensus, the Vice Admiral's heart swelled with satisfaction. Seizing the momentum, he decided to provisionally agree to the rescue mission, but insisted on time to reorganize and deploy forces. He demanded the Second Fleet remain stationed at the battlefield, awaiting the full reinforcements of the First Fleet. To safeguard the homeland, he urged the Second Fleet to uphold military discipline and honor the nobility's legacy!

After the meeting, Mei Lin returned to her flagship, letting out a soft sigh that betrayed a hint of melancholy.

She wasn't a snow elf, but a mermaid. Though a star within the Naval First Fleet, she remained an outsider in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom dominated by snow elves.

Truth be told, she felt more at ease among the duelists than within the navy.

The main reason was the duelists' diverse backgrounds, like rabbitman, wood elves, humans, and more.

Thinking of the duelists, Mei Lin naturally turned her thoughts to Long Fu.

“Bing Yang suffered a crushing defeat at the hands of Long Fu. This battle clearly shows he is in peak condition and possesses even greater combat strength.”

“We must continue to weaken him.”

“Next, we can spread the rumor that he was struck by the Frost Aura, fell victim to the effects of the [Beautiful Future] potion, worsened his injuries, and cannot be healed.”

"On one hand, this urges Long Fu to keep accepting challenges and prove himself. On the other hand, it lures other silver level experts to clash with him."

At this thought, Mei Lin sighed softly to herself.

“Long Fu is likely gold level, but his silver level disguise serves as his best cover.”

“Truly, very few silver level fighters pose a real threat to him.”

Mei Lin resolved to continue her previous strategy against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Her primary focus now could not be on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Da Han and his Icebound Pirates were closing in.

Chapter 885: Divinity Inspiration, Trial of the Gods

The White Hair Trench.

Beyond the walls of the Ocean Mother Nest.

The hum of the colossal magic and divine script composite formation gradually faded.

The blinding radiance had long since vanished.

Teacher Si Ling knelt half-bent on the ground, feeling extreme exhaustion.

Even as a powerhouse within the saint level, one of the Twelve Saints, and aided by such a super-formation, extracting death divinity remained an arduous task.

“Thankfully, it's finally over.”

“We've gathered four portions of death divinity in total...”

“Sigh, I never imagined condensing and extracting divinity would be so taxing.”

Teacher Si Ling's magic power and mental energy were severely depleted, blurring his vision.

What he didn't know was that he had actually condensed and extracted eight portions of divinity, half of which had been secretly stolen by the fishman youngster.

Throughout this process, Cang Xu and Jian Gui were present, witnessing everything.

Zhou Zhang was no exception.

She had watched anxiously from start to finish.

Under everyone's gaze, Teacher Si Ling slowly rose to his feet, his fingers trembling slightly as he drew the artifact [Truth Slate] from his robe.

Half of the Truth Slate was now corroded with deathly black. This anomaly was strikingly obvious.

Noticing Cang Xu's puzzled gaze, Teacher Si Ling smiled faintly and offered an explanation: “Divinity is difficult to bear. This time, I relied primarily on the [Truth Slate] to contain it.”

“However, though the [Truth Slate] is a divine artifact, it embodies knowledge, logic, and order, qualities incompatible with the death divinity.”

“Therefore, after containing four portions of death divinity, the tablet's power was suppressed by 50%.”

Cang Xu inquired curiously, “Does this mean we transcendents cannot bear divinity?”

“I've heard of some instances.”

“We are necromancers, isn't the death divinity most suited to us?”

Teacher Si Ling nodded. “You are correct.”

“Transcendents can indeed bear divinity.”

“However, this varies from person to person.”

“Generally speaking, the higher the transcendent level and the nobler the bloodline, the greater the potential to bear, contain, or even absorb divinity.”

Jian Gui interjected, “Teacher Si Ling, you are a saint level powerhouse. Could you not bear the death divinity?”

Teacher Si Ling shook his head. “Of course not.”

“Given my strength and bloodline, I could bear up to three portions of divinity.”

“But first, divinity serves other vital purposes.”

"Second, should I bear divinity, I'd immediately and passively absorb it. I'd constantly fall into profound contemplation, unable to extricate myself, jeopardizing my own safety. This is called the divinity inspiration."

“Third, I must guard against the trial of the gods.”

Cang Xu understood the divinity inspiration well. His understanding largely stemmed from the fishman youngster's rapid advancement as a clergyman.

Cang Xu knew: Divinity inspiration occurs when crystallized divinity exerts a powerful influence on compatible transcendents, actively sharing its power. Once a transcendent comprehends it and possesses the capacity to contain it, prolonged exposure allows them to absorb the divinity, ascending into a divine lifeform.

A significant portion of deities had come into being this way.

But Cang Xu remained ignorant about the trial of the gods.

Fortunately, Teacher Si Ling volunteered an explanation: “Deities wield the divine spell [Perceive Divinity], enabling them to conduct searches across vast expanses.”

“Our undead orthodoxy maintains a deep cooperative relationship with the Death God, bound by a contract.”

“The moment I take in death divinity, the Death God will immediately sense it, triggering the Death God's Trial.”

"If I pass the trial, I will develop faith in the Death God. The highest achievement on this path of advancement is becoming a subordinate deity of the Death God."

“If I fail the trial, I will still gain some benefits, but the death divinity within me will be taken by the Death God.”

“Deities always strive to possess divinity that aligns with their nature.”

"Divine trials are one method of acquiring such divinity. Naturally, many deities also employ this method as a crucial means of spreading their faith."

“They actively disseminate their divinity outward, typically diluting it to cast a wide net.”

“Upon encountering a gifted seed, even this diluted, fragmented divinity allows the deity to sense its absorption, thereby triggering the divine trial.”

Jian Gui, who had listened intently throughout, gained profound insight.

“So that's how it is,” Cang Xu murmured. “Even deities have their needs and desires.”

Simultaneously, he understood: this matter touched upon the secrets of the gods and had immense value. Even his own commander didn't know. This showed that, despite the commander being so favored, the gifts bestowed upon him by the Charming Blue Goddess were still withheld.

“Teacher Si Ling revealing these secrets to me directly is still meant to undermine my faith in Charming Blue Goddess, isn't it?”

“Given Teacher Si Ling's attitude, no wonder necromancers are enemies of all gods.”

As Cang Xu pondered this, Teacher Si Ling had already recovered considerably by downing potions in rapid succession.

He stared intently at the super-level magic formation before him, then turned his head to cast a profound gaze at the Ocean Mother Nest.

“Let's go.”

“It's time to leave this place.” He declared to Cang Xu and Jian Gui.

Cang Xu froze in surprise, Teacher Si Ling's departure felt utterly abrupt. He hadn't anticipated this at all.

Teacher Si Ling stated, “My mission here is complete.”

“You two will accompany me.”

Jian Gui's face lit up with excitement.

Though he belonged to the undead orthodoxy, such an opportunity to follow the Twelve Saints was rare indeed.

Jian Gui glanced at Cang Xu, understanding clearly: “I'm riding his coattails.”

“His bloodline mutation continues to progress. He possesses the potential to reach the saint level.”

“No faction would ever willingly abandon a saint level seed.”

“Teacher Si Ling naturally intends to keep him close, personally observing and witnessing the outcome of his bloodline mutation.”

Even if Cang Xu ultimately only reached gold level potential, Teacher Si Ling would still gain a gold level necromancer.

Thoughts raced through Cang Xu's mind. The next moment, he wore an expression of profound honor, declaring his willingness to actively follow Teacher Si Ling and expressing gratitude for this opportunity to learn.

Cang Xu knew Jian Gui wouldn't let him go. After all, there was the possibility of reaching the saint level!
