Truthfully, Cang Xu also desired to follow Teacher Si Ling.

Don't forget, his original goal was to get close to Jian Gui, using him as a bridge to connect with the undead orthodoxy.

Now, serving Teacher Si Ling would prove far more efficient than courting Jian Gui. Teacher Si Ling was one of the Twelve Saints, a veritable decision-maker within the undead orthodoxy!

Yet Cang Xu hadn't anticipated Teacher Si Ling departing so abruptly.

“No time to bid farewell to the Lord Commander.”

“But he'll learn of what transpired here.”

“As for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps... sigh.”

Cang Xu naturally harbored concerns, yet he was powerless. Now under Teacher Si Ling's watch, even contacting the fishman youngster carried significant risk.

Teacher Si Ling far surpassed Zhou Zhang in strength.

Aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster watched helplessly as Teacher Si Ling led Cang Xu and Jian Gui out of sight.

He had hoped to bring Cang Xu back with him, but reality was unforgiving.

“From now on, I must keep a close watch on Cang Xu.”

“Though he's temporarily gained Teacher Si Ling's favor, the risks of exposure and the consequences have multiplied many times over.”

“Fortunately, he possesses the Charming Blue faith. I can use divine spells to monitor his situation while also casting spells to aid him remotely.”

The fishman youngster examined the divine artifact in his hand: [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

Similar to Teacher Si Ling's True Slate, his Charming Blue divine artifact had also been corroded by death divinity, consuming one-quarter of its power due to bearing the divinity.

Divine artifact - Artifact Mastery.

The fishman youngster expended divine power to deepen his grasp over [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

In an instant, he realized that his Charming Blue divine artifact, burdened by death divinity, could not unleash a quarter of its true power.

“Teacher Si Ling's artifact [Truth Slate] is half-corroded, while [Mermaid Fairy Tale] is only a quarter.”

“This alone reveals which is stronger.”

“But considering the [Truth Slate] is merely a fragment of the [Book of Truth], the [Mermaid Fairy Tale] likely ranks low among all divine artifacts.”

Supporting the fishman youngster's conjecture was another theory.

Namely, the power of a divine artifact often correlates directly with the strength of the deity who forged it.

“A total of four portions of death divinity...”

Without a doubt, this was an immense gain.

The fishman youngster was certain: the value of these four portions of death divinity far exceeded War Merchant's inheritance from Mystifying Monster Island!

“But how to utilize them?”

Having heard the words of Teacher Si Ling earlier, the fishman youngster understood what divinity inspiration and the divine trials were.

“Though I'm only a gold level, I possess a god level bloodline—my divine blood concentration now approaches 100%!”

“How much death divinity can I withstand?”

"Logically, the divinity suited for me should lean toward water elements or fishman-related powers. Death divinity doesn't suit me; it's like trying to contain it with the [Truth Slate] or [Mermaid Fairy Tale]."

“So, to be safe, I must ascend to the saint level first!”

“Before leaving, I'll pray to Charming Blue Goddess one last time and attempt to break through to the saint level.”

The fishman youngster made this decision swiftly.

But this had been his original plan all along.

He planned to leave the Ocean Mother Nest and meet up with the big guy partway. It was only because stealing the death divinity had delayed him a bit.

“Let's see where Xiao Guai is first.”

“After delaying for a few days, he must be worried, right?”

The fishman youngster decided to reassure the big guy first, then make his final prayer.

Moments later, the fishman youngster's eyes widened in shock.

He contacted the big guy and discovered, to his astonishment, that he had been captured and taken to Snow Bird Harbor.

He also learned that Dao Hen had arrived at Snow Bird Harbor.

This much, the fishman youngster already knew. After all, Dao Hen had long since uncovered Tan Mo's schemes and even seized the opportunity to rescue Hun Tong.

What shocked the fishman youngster wasn't this revelation, but something else—Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was the ultimate mastermind behind it all? The first gold level assassin who attacked them? He had sent that person.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was actually one of the Empire's secret agents?

Chapter 886: Disappearance

Ice Sculpture Capital.

Zi Di stared intently at the glass beaker in her hand.

“60ml of sorrow-dispelling red wine, paired with 15g of time ice cubes.”

“Requirement 1: The energy level of the time ice cubes must be between 400-560 magic power.”

"Requirement 2: The time ice cube must undergo pre-cooling, reducing its temperature to at least -20°C."

"Requirement 3: Monitor temperature after placing the time ice cube into the sorrow-dispelling red wine. If the temperature rises by 10℃ or more within 30 seconds, promptly add golden coconut oil. If it does not reach this level, do not add."

Following this, two alchemy paths diverged, corresponding to the temperature rising to the standard and failing to reach it.

Zi Di meticulously recalled every step while carefully inspecting the sorrow-dispelling red wine, time ice cubes, and other components.

After confirming all alchemy materials met specifications, Zi Di commenced the formal preparation.

After placing the time ice cubes into the beaker containing the wine, the temperature failed to rise to the required level.

She immediately switched to the second route.

“Lucky.”

The second route proved significantly less challenging than the first.

“Temperature detection is merely an observational dimension. The true standard lies in a specific critical data point.”

“Yet the content Ban Tu traded to me didn't include this core element.”

“It's likely tied to the divine power of the Intoxication God...”

Zi Di could only speculate silently.

It wasn't Ban Tu's fault for holding back during the transaction.

In fact, alchemy technology trades were generally conducted this way.

Ban Tu had already detailed every alchemy step, and the contract they signed largely guaranteed he hadn't deceived her.

Zi Di followed the instructions step by step and could achieve the corresponding alchemy results.

It was like buying a potion formula.

Zi Di wouldn't tell the buyer the reasons behind her design steps, the conceptual process, or the sources of inspiration for developing this potion.

That knowledge transcended mere alchemy, it resided on a higher plane.

Of course, such knowledge wasn't inherently unsellable. magic academies, alchemy schools, and similar institutions thrived on precisely this trade. Students paid tuition for the opportunity to learn such knowledge.

As for Zi Di, she hadn't yet grasped the underlying principles of this purchased alchemy technique, let alone reverse-engineered its design philosophy or conceptual framework.

Her original plan was to thoroughly master it, perhaps even attempt improvements (primarily relying on War Merchant's database and the tower spirit, as Zi Di's alchemy expertise alone was utterly insufficient), before applying it to the dragonman youngster.

But now, the situation was urgent. There was definitely no time for Zi Di to properly study and understand this technology.

After considering her options, Zi Di decided to use it on Zong Ge first.

“Long Fu” needed to make an appearance, especially after enduring Bing Yang's Frost Aura.

Zi Di alone was absolutely insufficient to handle the disguise and impersonation. She herself had crucial matters (the Wisteria Cache) to attend to.

Three hours later, Zi Di gazed weakly at the beaker, her eyes reflecting a flicker of delight.

Her first attempt at alchemy had failed midway.

This was her second attempt.

The ice cube heated significantly this time, exceeding 10℃. Following the protocol, Zi Di had no choice but to proceed with the first route.

Though the difficulty had increased considerably, she managed to complete the alchemy.

The finished product, the sorrow-dispelling red wine, revealed a vivid crimson hue, like blood. When the liquid swayed, it emitted a faint orange-yellow glow.

The time ice cube had transformed dramatically, no longer a solid block but a phantom-like presence.

It floated within the wine.

When the wine was perfectly still, the hidden ice remained invisible.

Only after swirling the wine could the pure white ice barely be discerned by the naked eye. These ice cubes resembled magic images, drifting up and down within the wine.

Zi Di glanced at the clock: “I only have 18 minutes.”

The Ice Cube Red Wine in this state couldn't last long.

Zi Di gave it her all, inspecting the finished product as quickly as possible. She found no issues.

Only 2 minutes remained.

Zi Di immediately began stirring.

During the stirring process, Zi Di did not draw upon her own spirit power.

This was quite unusual.

Typically, the brewing of potions required heavy involvement of the alchemist's spirit power.

Zi Di merely used an ordinary glass rod for stirring.

The stirring pace was not fast.

As the sorrow-dispelling red wine gradually formed a vortex, the magic phantom-like time ice cubes were also pulled into strands of white filaments, swirling continuously within the liquid.

“The aura has unified!”

Zi Di's eyes lit up.

Originally, the transcendent aura of the Ice Cube Red Wine was intermingled. But as the stirring reached a certain point, the multiple transcendent auras miraculously fused together.

“This is the moment.” Holding the beaker, Zi Di approached the bed.

She pried open Zong Ge's mouth and inserted a small glass funnel inside. Then, towering above him, she slowly poured the Ice Cube Red Wine into the funnel.

After being stirred, the wine became remarkably viscous.

Like honey, it dripped slowly, falling into the funnel and then flowing into Zong Ge's mouth.

The Ice Cube Red Wine emitted a faint, cold glow, occasionally revealing the outline of ice cubes.

The wine's aroma was intensely rich, filling the air. Even as an undead being, Zi Di sensed the wine's beauty, a deep-seated desire to drink it welling up within her.

An entire glass of wine was poured directly into Zong Ge's mouth.

He didn't need to swallow. The wine dissolved instantly upon touching his mouth, permeating his skin, muscles, blood, and bones.

About a minute later, Zong Ge slowly opened his eyes.

“It worked!” Zi Di exclaimed joyfully.

Zong Ge's blurred vision gradually sharpened. Recognizing Zi Di, he rasped out a thank you.

Zi Di exhaled a cloud of stale breath, relaxing. “That was perilous.”

“Fortunately, I struck a deal with Ban Tu earlier, acquiring the alchemy formula for Ice Cube Red Wine.”

"Afterward, I purchased two bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine from the Li Jian Clan. That's what gave me the means to counteract the effects of the ‘Beautiful Future’ potion."

Zong Ge struggled to sit up. “The potion's effects overwhelmed me. I nearly couldn't maintain my defenses.”

"On its own, it wouldn't have been a threat. The problem is it acted like a fuse, igniting all the latent issues building up inside me."

Zong Ge raised his upper body, attempting to circulate his battle qi. A flicker of joy crossed his face.

“A blessing in disguise.”

“This time, I was pushed to my absolute limit, trapped in mortal peril, struggling on the brink of life and death.”

"My body underwent tremendous training. My battle qi now circulates at least twice as fast. The protective mechanisms for my internal organs have now stabilized, becoming one of my instincts."

“Hahahaha...”

Seeing Zong Ge laugh, Zi Di rolled her eyes in exasperation. “Glad you're awake. Get up quickly, it's time to make your public appearance.”

Zong Ge nodded, about to rise, when suddenly he too rolled his eyes.

But this time, his eyes rolled upward and stayed there.

Thud.

Zong Ge fell backward, the back of his head striking the bed frame with a soft thump.

Zi Di: ?!

She froze for a moment, then lunged forward like lightning. Her palm pressed against Zong Ge's chest as she probed with her spirit power.

Then, as if electrocuted, she jerked her hand back.

Zong Ge's body was enveloped in the Intoxication divine power that nearly injured her.

Zi Di's face paled dramatically as she gasped, "What's happening? His soul is gone!"

Chapter 887: Whistleblowing

On the bed, Zong Ge's eyes were wide open, pure white from rolling back.

His entire body was unresponsive, as if dead.

Yet his body still held the breath of life; he was still breathing, and a layer of divine power shielded his entire form.

Zi Di stared in shock, frozen for several seconds before snapping back to reality.

“What's going on?”

“What in the world is going on?! Why is Zong Ge like this?”

“Did I make a mistake in the formula?”

"No, impossible. I was fully focused throughout the entire process, and the alchemy apparatus recorded every step."

“Yes, I have magic footage to review.”

This was standard practice for alchemists.

Because even the slightest factor during alchemy could cause the final result to fail. Therefore, alchemists employed various methods to document their experiments, making it easier to analyze later.

Zi Di grew tense and hurriedly reviewed the magic footage.

Soon, she reached a conclusion: “I made no mistakes. I followed every step precisely.”

“Could it be that Ban Tu acted maliciously, trading me defective alchemy techniques?”

"Ban Tu possesses the ability to counter magic contracts, enabling him to carry out a covert attack. Or perhaps he himself was unaware of this flaw. After all, he was merely one of the researchers, with a larger partner—the Intoxication Church."

“The most crucial thing is Zong Ge's strange state.”

“His entire body is enveloped in divine power, and his soul has vanished without a trace...”

If Zong Ge had vomited blood after drinking the potion, or if time had accelerated again, or if he'd fallen into an intoxicated stupor, Zi Di could understand.

But this situation Zong Ge was in, she truly didn't know. It was beyond her knowledge.

“What should we do?” After much deliberation, Zi Di felt utterly at a loss.

The divine power barrier seemed to be protecting Zong Ge, sustaining his life. Thus, while his soul vanished, the divine power suppressed all latent threats within his body, rendering them completely dormant.

Snow Bird Harbor.

In the city government palace.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hosted a banquet, lavishly entertaining Tan Mo.

"Lord Tan Mo, your journey has truly been arduous. Not only did you endure a long trek, confronting rampant pirates, but you also devoted your utmost energy to the investigation. Exemplary agents like you are the very foundation of our Empire's strength." Snow Bird Harbor City Lord raised his wine cup, his words brimming with praise.

Tan Mo laughed heartily, raised his cup, and tilted his head back to drain it in one gulp. Then, staring at the empty vessel, he remarked pointedly, “The wine is excellent, but it's not what I truly crave.”

Truthfully, Tan Mo held little interest in banquets or fine cuisine. What truly intrigued him was how much wealth he could extract from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

But extortion couldn't be overt, what if it left a trace?

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord chuckled heartily and clapped his hands.

The next moment, the dining room doors swung open. Two attendants entered respectfully, carrying two bottles of red wine.

“Lord Tan Mo, I have prepared what you desire.”

“Behold, this is the sorrow-dispelling red wine!”

Each attendant carried a bottle to Tan Mo's side.

Tan Mo reached out, taking one. He peered through the glass, nodding. “This is the most talked-about wine these days.”

“Only you, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, could procure two bottles so swiftly.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord narrowed his eyes and chuckled, “Ever since the incident, the Intoxication Church has been desperately seeking aid from major factions across the kingdom, to no avail.”

"They had no choice but to offer hefty rewards."

"So, treasure hunters, mercenaries, and explorers swarmed in, eagerly setting sail to search for the scattered bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine."

“Beyond that, pirates dispatched secret teams to land on the island to liquidate their stock.”

“The Intoxication Church's high-profile maneuvers sparked intense interest among buyers, driving prices ever higher.”

“This resulted in vast quantities of the wine flooding underground markets throughout the region instead of falling into the Intoxication Church's hands.”

Tan Mo nodded in understanding. “After all, with Da Han approaching and pirates rampant, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's navy can only retreat into defensive positions.”

“Under these circumstances, venturing out to sea to search for sorrow-dispelling red wine is nothing short of risking one's life for fortune.”

“Given the mortal danger involved, it's only natural to sell to the highest bidder.”

As Tan Mo spoke, he had already appraised the second bottle of sorrow-dispelling red wine.

Satisfied, he stored both bottles directly into his spatial storage equipment. “These two bottles are of fine quality. They shall become part of my collection.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was slightly surprised. “Lord Tan Mo, you won't sample them?”

Tan Mo shook his head. “Of course not.”

“The sorrow-dispelling red wine contains fragments of the Wine God's divinity. What if drinking it triggers the Wine God's trial?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sneered inwardly: He knew Tan Mo's bloodline limit was capped at gold level. With such potential, even downing both bottles at once wouldn't trigger the Wine God's trial. Did he think divine trials were so cheap?

Only when a certain trait (usually bloodline), caught a god's attention would a trial be triggered.

Essentially, it was a god's method of proselytizing.

Ordinary proselytizing relied on church members spreading the word, while divine trials involved the gods themselves (the divinity) intervening.

Thus, after a divine trial, the trialist's faith often shifted.

“To be perfectly honest, I'm rather concerned,” Tan Mo continued. “If a divine trial were to be triggered here, and my soul were drawn into the Intoxication Divine Domain, wouldn't I be at your mercy?”

"At that point, City Lord, you could simply claim the divine trial failed, resulting in my immediate demise. Would my family dare question a deity over someone like me?"

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression darkened instantly. “Lord Tan Mo, have I failed you in some way to cause such profound misunderstanding?”

Tan Mo propped his elbows on the table; fingers interlocked into a fist pressed against his nose and mouth. "I merely fear suffering the same fate as Gold Hand, falling victim to your treachery. After all, this is your territory."

Gold Hand, also a gold level secret agent, had perished in Snow Bird Harbor.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression darkened further, his gaze turning icy. "Lord Tan Mo, you are slandering me. As an investigator, is it appropriate to spout such reckless, unfounded accusations?"

“I already explained the entire matter concerning Gold Hand to you yesterday!”

Tan Mo chuckled: “If it weren't for Gold Hand's preemptive report to the organization via a whistleblowing letter, I would have believed you, really.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord frowned deeply: “A whistleblowing letter?”

Tan Mo replied: “In that letter, Gold Hand accused you of embezzling organization funds, exploiting your dual identities as a secret agent and city lord to falsify military intelligence, deceiving subordinates and superiors alike, and amassing wealth through corruption.”

“One purpose of my investigation here is to expose you, Emancipator.”

Chapter 888: The Truth of Tan Mo’s Investigation

The atmosphere in the restaurant grew tense.

Tan Mo and Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sat at opposite ends of the long table, facing each other across the space.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head slowly, his eyes fixed intently on Tan Mo. "Perhaps there were indeed some differences in perspective and actions between the Gold Hand and me. What I never anticipated was that he would prove such a petty villain, secretly slandering me behind my back."

"Embezzlement and corruption? Hah! Everything I've done has been for the Empire!"

After professing his loyalty, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's lips curved slightly, revealing a trace of composure. “Lord Tan Mo, surely you wouldn't base your judgment on the accusatory letter from the Golden Hand alone and conclude I murdered him?”

Tan Mo also smiled faintly: “You mean to say this is merely conjecture, lacking evidence and witnesses?”

“In fact, I have them.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression turned solemn: "Oh? I'd like to hear the details."

Tan Mo stated directly: “That witness is Mei Lan Du!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's pupils contracted imperceptibly, his face becoming expressionless.

Mei Lan Du was a key figure under the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, a human magic chef of silver level.

His background mirrored the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's: both had been secretly recruited by [Usurper] to join the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's covert intelligence network.

Mei Lan Du had always followed Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Not long ago, Ci Hai Shen arrived at Snow Bird Harbor and invited Mei Lan Du to serve as his deputy, assisting in preparing the Sea Feast.

Ci Hai Shen promised that upon completion, he would impart a gold level recipe to Mei Lan Du. This offer was exceptionally generous, yet Mei Lan Du's refusal at the time was resolute.

Tan Mo sipped his red wine leisurely: "Though Mei Lan Du is only silver level, he possesses unique talents."

"His specialty is crafting an enhanced version of the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea."

"After consuming this magic dish, anyone who merely taps into their battle qi, or even engages in vigorous movement, will trigger its effect, causing their battle qi to dissipate instantly, leaving them paralyzed and incapacitated."

“Of course, the legendary Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea is a legend level magic delicacy. Mei Lan Du can only refine a silver level modified version. But with other cooking tools and gold level ingredients, he could create a gold level modified version.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fell silent for a moment before saying, “So you believe I used this magic dish to secretly assassinate Golden Hand?”

Tan Mo nodded slightly, a faint smile playing on his lips. “Precisely!”

“I investigated the circumstances at the hidden location before Gold Hand's death. It appears he was recuperating and consuming delicacies before the ambush.”

“And those were magic dishes of exceptionally high level.”

"Magic dishes differ from potions. Their shelf life is far shorter. They often spoil within days, or even hours."

“Who prepared the magic dishes Gold Hand consumed?”

“Clearly, it was Mei Lan Du!”

"Though Gold Hand secretly reported you, he didn't believe his cover was blown. Or rather, in his mind, even if you knew about the report, it wouldn't make you want to kill him!"

"Moreover, unlike magic potions, magic dishes are difficult to identify. To be precise, dishes like [Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea] are extremely hard to distinguish!"

“And its modified versions share this same advantage.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord remained silent.

His expressionless face concealed immense pressure!

For Tan Mo's words held absolute truth.

The culinary masterpiece [Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea] was originally created and personally cooked by Ge Zi Bu.

Ge Zi Bu, a legend level explorer, was a connoisseur of fine cuisine with an exceptional ability to discern magic dishes.

Yet he detected no deception. After consuming [Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea], his fighting spirit vanished. He ordered the fleet to turn back, causing nearly a decade of exploration to collapse at the final hurdle.

Mei Lan Du refined the recipe according to his own understanding. The dish he created recently succeeded in ensnaring the trio of Da Dou, Hong Zhu, and the big guy.

At the time, even Da Dou, a formidable gold level magic chef, fell victim before belatedly realizing what had happened.

Mei Lan Du's innovations extended beyond magic cuisine into potions as well.

His modified version of Sighing Water proved remarkably effective too.

Long ago, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had allocated it as supplies for Lady Sea Snake, enabling her to successfully subdue the Clapping Pirates.

Seeing the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fall silent, Tan Mo continued, “Teng Dong Lang actually died by your hand, didn't he?”

“Don't be so quick to deny it.”

“Heh, I've watched the magic footage of Long Fu and Teng Dong Lang's battle at least fifty times!”

“Why did Long Fu's final throw of the short spear prove fatal to Teng Dong Lang?”

"The common belief is that Teng Dong Lang was weakened after prolonged combat, allowing Long Fu to seize the decisive moment. But in reality, he consumed the modified [Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea], and when its duration ended, the effect triggered, didn't it?"

“The more I watch that final strike, the more suspicious it seems.”

“It bears a striking resemblance to Teng Dong Lang's death. On the surface, Golden Hand's demise appeared to be a battle skill mishap, failing to dodge the mage tower's beam.”

“But in truth, it was you who deliberately triggered the effect of the magic dish, causing his battle qi to spiral out of control in an instant!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn't help but chuckle softly, interrupting, “Enough, enough, esteemed Investigator. Your analysis is thorough and convincing, quite the performance.”

“I'm almost about to clap for your entertaining story.”

“Unfortunately, after listening this long, I haven't heard a shred of concrete evidence or a single witness.”

“All of this remains mere conjecture.”

Tan Mo chuckled again. “Don't worry, I was just about to get to the evidence.”

“I've discovered that after Long Fu slew Teng Dong Lang, you secretly mobilized personnel and actively stirred up public opinion. You extolled Long Fu's unyielding fighting spirit, proclaiming his ‘excellence in never giving up, which allowed him to seize victory in a desperate battle.’”

"You had absolutely no need to do this. Unless your goal was to use this to conceal Teng Dong Lang’s true cause of death!"

“And the best evidence, of course, would be the bodies of Teng Dong Lang and Gold Hand.”

“In this regard, Lord City, your execution was remarkably clean and efficient.”

“Teng Dong Lang’s body remained in your possession for some time, ensuring no incriminating evidence was left behind.”

"Even more ingeniously, you orchestrated Tranquil Hill's reconnaissance mission, providing a plausible reason for the Bayonet Gang remnants to ‘seize’ Teng Dong Lang's body. This way, you had a perfectly acceptable explanation to the organization."

"As for Gold Hand's body, you undoubtedly planned this long ago. Thus, Gold Hand perished under the mage tower's assault, disintegrating instantly without leaving a single trace."

“These two pieces of crucial evidence are gone.”

“But I still have a way.”

"For instance, I could continue investigating matters related to Mei Lan Du or the Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea. I believe there must be other leads in these areas."

“In fact, within my authority, I could directly detain Mei Lan Du for personal interrogation.”

“The question is, will his loyalty to you, City Lord, withstand the test?”

After speaking, Tan Mo paused and resumed sipping his wine.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stared intently at Tan Mo, his brow furrowed. Then, a realization dawned on him, and his expression softened considerably.

He understood.

If Tan Mo truly intended to investigate him or target him, wouldn't it be simpler to just arrest Mei Lan Du outright? Why bring up the plan deliberately at the dinner table?

Wasn't that practically inviting the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to sabotage it?

Tan Mo wouldn't be that foolish.

Therefore, his motive for voicing this plan clearly went beyond mere investigative duties.

What else could it be?

A word flashed through Snow Bird Harbor's Lord's mind: extortion!

Yes, Tan Mo was extorting him.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord weighed his words carefully before replying slowly, “Mei Lan Du is loyal to me, and even more loyal to the Empire.”

“There's no need to imprison and interrogate him. It simply isn't worth it.”

Tan Mo set down his wine cup. "Oh? Why do you say that, City Lord?"

"Upon further reflection, perhaps you are innocent. You were merely deceived by Mei Lan Du. In truth, the modified [Deep Sigh of the Blue Sea] is the crucial element. Therefore, it's highly likely Mei Lan Du acted alone, and you knew nothing of his actions."

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head immediately: “I can guarantee his innocence.”

Tan Mo sneered coldly: “What guarantee do you offer?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held up five fingers: “A fifty-thousand gold coin bond. How about that?”

Tan Mo threw his head back and laughed heartily several times before lowering his gaze, his eyes burning as he fixed them on the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He said nothing, only his face twisted with profound contempt!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord instantly grasped Tan Mo's unspoken message: You think 50,000 gold coins will buy me off? Do you take me for a beggar?

“This greedy pig!” cursed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord inwardly, yet he had no choice but to raise his bid.

Fifty thousand gold coins became one hundred fifty thousand gold coins.

Tan Mo shook his head.

Then came the time ice cube, earth elemental essence, and various other materials.

Tan Mo shook his head again.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's offers grew ever higher, so steep they made his heart bleed.

Yet Tan Mo remained unyielding.

This filled the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with fury and resentment, yet also profound helplessness. What could he do? Tan Mo held his weakness in his hands.

“I underestimated this fellow!”

"Though greedy to the extreme, he truly possesses skill. In just a few days, he uncovered the truth."

“No wonder the Empire assigned him as the investigator for this case!”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord found himself in a highly passive position. He longed to strangle the fat man before him on the spot.

But he couldn't.

Tan Mo was an investigator from the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could plot Golden Hand's death, but he absolutely couldn't lay a finger on Tan Mo. Was the Imperial Secret Spy Agency so easy to fool?

Only when Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had raised the price to its absolute limit, his face contorted with frustration, did Tan Mo finally speak, calmly stating his demand: “I want the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

Chapter 889: Plotting and Scheming Against Each Other

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord wore a look of astonishment.

His first thought was: “Could it be that Sleeping Man's saint domain aptitude has been exposed?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's greatest gain from hosting Tan Mo this time was a full appreciation of the latter's investigative capabilities.

Naturally, as an investigator, Tan Mo himself possessed considerable authority, able to leverage the power of the Secret Spy Agency for his inquiries. This undoubtedly amplified his own capabilities.

As for why Tan Mo could investigate the secret agents here in Snow Bird Harbor, that involved the factional divisions within the Secret Spy Agency.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, codenamed [Emancipator], was not a native-born citizen but an aristocrat from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, defected by [Usurper].

He, along with [Usurper] and Mei Lan Du, belonged to the outsider faction.

Tan Mo did not.

He was a native-born citizen of the Sheng Ming Empire, having undergone extensive covert training, representing the native faction.

Lan Zao and his pseudonym Hen Fa were also outsiders. Though trained, their time spent there was shorter, definitely outsiders.

Gold Hand, framed by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, was a secret agent of the native faction.

The Empire's Secret Spy Agency deliberately placed Gold Hand alongside outsiders like the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, a move with deep significance.

But Gold Hand died, shortly after submitting his denunciation letter against the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

This triggered the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's follow-up action: “dispatching an investigator.”

Tan Mo himself possessed exceptional investigative talent. He could see through disguises and find the correct investigative direction amidst tangled surface clues.

Within Snow Bird Harbor, he mobilized the secret spy forces of the native faction.

This dual approach allowed him to uncover numerous secrets in a short time.

Secret agents from the native faction weren't confined to Snow Bird Harbor; there were even more on Feng Awakening Island.

The secret agent codenamed [Su Xing] belonged to the native faction. The castle he built on Feng Awakening Island functioned like a hidden wedge, silently watching over Ice Sculpture Island from afar.

“Can Tan Mo uncover the truth about the Sleeping Man?” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had pondered this question long ago.

The answer was unequivocally yes.

The arrests of Da Dou, the big guy, and others weren't carried out by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord himself, they involved Su Xing and his network. The capture occurred on Magnet Archipelago.

As native faction members, Su Xing and Tan Mo were true allies.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a higher position than [Su Xing]. Yet he still relied on [Su Xing] and others to bring the big guy back.

Where did Su Xing's loyalty lie?

Forget loyalty, in terms of closeness alone, Su Xing would undoubtedly be far closer to Tan Mo!

Thus, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had long anticipated this outcome.

He pinned his hopes on delaying tactics, covering up this critical secret, swiftly handling the investigation, and dispatching Tan Mo before he could catch on.

Now, upon suddenly hearing Tan Mo mention the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord instantly felt exposed.

Yet he gave nothing away, showing not a trace of anxiety.

The city lord stared intently at his wine glass. Within mere seconds, his mind churned violently, processing countless thoughts.

Finally, he lifted his gaze to Tan Mo seated across from him.

His tone remained calm: “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is of critical importance. I've invested substantial resources and effort into them, all to uncover the arms dealer behind them.”

“This is my most crucial source of merit. You want to share...”

At this point, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head slightly.

Yet he didn't display outright refusal. Instead, he weighed the stakes pragmatically: “If you decide to meddle, then our previous agreement is null. We'll need to renegotiate.”

Tan Mo fell silent.

In truth, he hadn't uncovered anything about the big guy yet. The speculation about the big guy's lineage had already surfaced on [Su Xing]'s side, but Tan Mo hadn't been able to access it yet.

The reason was simple, and somewhat ironic.

Tan Mo's reputation for greed and extortion has spread far and wide, becoming deeply ingrained in people's minds!

Even within the native factions, Su Xing needed to establish solid relations with Tan Mo first, ensuring he could survive this investigation, before proceeding to deeper connections.

Only then would Tan Mo have a chance to uncover the big guy's secrets.

Tan Mo's investigation had never focused on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In his view, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was merely a representative of arms dealers who had lucked out by reaping the windfall from the battle of Two Eyes Island.

From the very beginning, Tan Mo's sole objective had been to seize this fortune from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Of course, had he uncovered the big guy's secret, his intentions would have been entirely different.

Between a saint level seed and the riches of Two Eyes Island, any fool would know which to choose!

Tan Mo paused to deliberate, deliberately slowing his speech. His previously leisurely demeanor shifted to one of earnest solemnity. He was striving to convey sincerity, signaling that what followed came from his heart: “There is a piece of information, City Lord, that you may not be aware of.”

Tan Mo voiced Yan Yi's conjecture—that the wealth of Two Eyes Island had ultimately fallen into the hands of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Is that so?” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed in surprise. “It seems Yan Yi has kept quite a few things from me.”

By rank, Yan Yi was naturally far below Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

But he wasn't directly subordinated. In this regard, his constraints were far less than those of [Su Xing]. Though Su Xing belonged to the native faction, his authority was ultimately subordinate to Usurper and thus constrained by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Another crucial point was that Yan Yi was an agent of the Empire.

Two Eyes Island was originally imperial territory, conquered by Hun Tong during his expansion. Therefore, Yan Yi was a local operative within the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.

From this perspective, it was unsurprising that Yan Yi concealed information from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Tan Mo chuckled. "Upon arriving here, I dispatched investigators. It has now been verified that after the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps landed and entered the harbor, they indeed trafficked large quantities of antique calligraphy and paintings."

This was indeed the truth.

After arriving at Snow Bird Harbor, the survivors resold many items to replenish their funds. Especially with antiques and paintings, offloading them often requires time.

Later, when the survivors ventured into the potion trade, part of their startup capital came from this source.

“Therefore, I'm quite interested in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps,” Tan Mo concluded.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fell silent once more.

His probing had yielded favorable results.

He felt a surge of quiet excitement: "Tan Mo doesn't know the secret of the Sleeping Man's saint bloodline!"

This undoubtedly left him with immense room for maneuver.

“I must seize this opportunity.”

Outwardly, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord remained composed: “I see.”

“You seek to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for the sake of the Two Eyes Island's wealth.”

“I wish to draw closer to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to lure out the figures behind them.”

“Perhaps our objectives are not in conflict.”

Tan Mo couldn't help but raise an eyebrow.

He felt his intentions had been made abundantly clear.

He was targeting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

Having successfully extorted the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and forced him into a corner, he now held the upper hand.

He intended to make the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord act. As the local lord, dealing with and controlling the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be child's play for him!

Tan Mo himself would not lift a finger.

Yet, though the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord seemed to grasp Tan Mo's intent, he had a different plan.

“Do you have a better idea?” Tan Mo inquired.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, “You want money, I want people. Our goals aren't conflicting.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord certainly wouldn't directly target the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

On one hand, he genuinely wanted to continue fishing for the arms dealer behind them. On the other hand, he had no desire for a major confrontation. Because the bigger the operation, the more it would inevitably expose. If Tan Mo uncovered the big guy's secret, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would suffer the greatest loss!

Seeing the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord emphasize this point twice, Tan Mo understood the latter seemed to have a new proposal.

Tan Mo expressed his willingness to hear the details.

But Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shifted his focus: "Do you know? Da Han, leading a large pirate force, has already advanced to our nation's coastal defenses."

Tan Mo immediately replied, “Of course. That's the most critical intelligence currently.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, "The pirates are attacking the defenses belonging to the Kingdom's Second Naval Fleet. Based on my speculation, Feng Lian, harboring resentment over his conflict with the Li Jian Clan, deliberately lured Da Han into this."

“Furthermore, I believe the Second Naval Fleet will likely be breached in this battle.”

Tan Mo immediately voiced his skepticism: “The pirates only have one saint level expert—Da Han. Though he commands a saint beast, we all know beasts have limited combat tactics and low intelligence. At best, they're only considered half a saint level.”

“Meanwhile, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom possesses three saint level experts. They needn't deploy all three, just two would secure battlefield dominance.”

Compared to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Tan Mo favored the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's side.

But Snow Bird Harbor City Lord chuckled: “Lord Tan Mo, you're unaware of the inner workings.”

“The political atmosphere here in the kingdom is rather delicate.”

"The Royal Grand Magician and the king hold divergent views. As you know, the current king is only at the saint level, while the Royal Grand Magician is also at the saint level. Moreover, the grand magician commands the Han Yu Clan, which stands as the foremost noble house in our nation."

Tan Mo frowned slightly. He understood—intense political maneuvering existed within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The king wanted the Royal Grand Magician to take action, ideally defeating Da Han only to return wounded and in need of recuperation.

This would allow the king to exert his influence, implement his political agenda, and break the current stalemate.

Conversely, the Royal Grand Magician harbored similar ambitions.

The king represented not just himself, but the interests of the royal family and the kingdom. The royal grand magician, meanwhile, sought to defend his political ideals and the rights of his bloodline kin, the Han Yu Clan.

“And what of the White Dragon King?” Tan Mo inquired.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's third saint level figure was the supreme leader of the White Dragon Clan—a saint level White Dragon King.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stated, “The current White Dragon King has always lived in seclusion, disdaining mundane affairs. She typically resides in a mountain cave in deep slumber, rarely making public appearances. She holds no interest in such matters.”

“According to the royal alliance treaty, the kingdom does indeed possess the opportunity to summon the White Dragon King to intervene. But absolutely not at this moment.”

The peril in the waters surrounding Ice Sculpture Island and the attacks on the navy do not warrant deploying a saint level trump card like the White Dragon King.

After all, they were merely allies, essentially guests.

How could a host stand idly by, letting their guests take the first move?

Tan Mo's eyes flashed with sharpness: “So, you believe none of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's three saint level beings will intervene?”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord voiced his conjecture: “They will certainly act, but the likelihood of it happening at sea is very low.”

“In naval combat, the pirates not only have the freedom to advance and retreat at will, but they also make their living on the sea, it's the battlefield environment they're most adapted to.”

“We speculate that Da Han, craving the resources of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, will definitely land on the island!”

"In that case, why not simply set the battlefield in the royal capital? The three saint levels would hold an overwhelming home-field advantage. After defeating Da Han, his escape would become exceedingly difficult. Perhaps this battle could even secure Da Han's demise, greatly enhancing our renown and bolstering national prestige. "

"Of course, there are drawbacks. Should saint level combat erupt, the capital as a battlefield would suffer catastrophic losses.

Tan Mo nodded. “Your analysis is sound.”

He fully agreed with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's assessment.

The reason was simple and straightforward—this was a world of the strong!

No matter how many soldiers perished or how much wealth was lost, it would not shake the foundations of their power.

Because the true foundation lay in the powerhouses themselves.

For the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, that meant its three saint level figures: the king, the grand magician, and the White Dragon King.

Conversely, if any of them were injured, their respective factions would immediately falter, retreating on all fronts and contracting step by step.

The greater the severity of their injuries, the heavier the losses for their factions!

This explained why none of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's three saint level figures had responded after Da Han's provocation.

As the very foundation of their factions, they must be extremely cautious, indeed, they have no choice but to be.

At this point, Tan Mo had already vaguely guessed Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's intentions.

He stated directly, “You intend to disguise yourself as pirates and attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

Speaking with a clever man was refreshingly straightforward.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord realized once more: Though Tan Mo was greedy beyond measure, he possessed genuine talent!

That was precisely the plan.

Having deduced that the kingdom's three saint level figures would be extremely cautious and highly unlikely to appear at sea, the defeat of the Second Naval Fleet was all but certain.

At that point, the maritime defenses would inevitably collapse!

The coastal regions of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom would likely face pirate raids.

At this stage, directly posing as pirates and launching a direct assault on Snow Bird Harbor offered numerous advantages!

The first benefit: Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not loyal to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom; he was a member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. Under the guise of pirates, he could eliminate numerous mid-to-lower-ranking officials and seize the opportunity to infiltrate a large number of secret agents.

The second advantage: a swift pirate-led assault on the island would drastically diminish Snow Bird Harbor's value in the eyes of figures like Da Han. What riches could remain in a harbor already plundered once? And Snow Bird Harbor was merely one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's eight major trading ports.

The third advantage: posing as pirates to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would satisfy Tan Mo while allowing Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's plan to proceed. When Snow Bird Harbor City Lord then offered aid and “repelled” the “pirates,” the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, robbed of their wealth, would be profoundly grateful to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!

Upon receiving Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's affirmative response, Tan Mo clapped his hands and laughed heartily. “This plan is indeed excellent.”

"Then let's sign the contract. You want money; I want people," declared the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

A look of hesitation flashed across Tan Mo's face.

Seeing this, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord firmly stated that this condition was non-negotiable!

If Tan Mo refused to sign, he could never trust him.

Only after the contract was signed would he release a portion of the funds to Tan Mo. Once the investigation storm subsided, he would hand over the remainder, at which point the contract would automatically be nullified.

Tan Mo fell deep in thought.

This was undoubtedly risky.

Should Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fall from grace in the future, this contract could surface as incriminating evidence.

Yet Tan Mo understood: Refusing this condition meant forfeiting Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's bribe.

And it was a substantial sum!

“And then there's the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' share...”

“It's not just the wealth from Two Eyes Island. I hear their magic potions are selling like hotcakes, bringing in a fortune.”

Greed gradually overpowered reason.

Finally, Tan Mo nodded slowly. "Where is the magic contract? I need to examine it thoroughly."

Chapter 890: Ice Ocean Battle

At sea.

The Divine Wind Pirates' fleet plowed through the waves.

A pirate jogged to the captain's cabin and knocked on the door: “Captain! Warships from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom are ahead, along with numerous icebergs!”

The cabin door opened the next moment.

A gust of wind swept past, and Feng Lian materialized at the bow of the flagship.

His condition was far from optimal.

Earlier, during his clash with Da Han, he had suffered a divine spell attack. Though he'd fled while desperately healing himself, he hadn't managed to dispel the spell's effects.

A pale blue wind aura now perpetually swirled around him.

This wind aura bound him to the pirate ship, sharing the burden of Da Han's divine magic.

“Icebergs?” Feng Lian's pupils contracted slightly.

He had indeed spotted icebergs, and in vast numbers.

Snow-white hills drifted swiftly across the sea, stretching endlessly like a gathering of furious peaks, radiating a chilling, murderous intent.

Amidst these icebergs, the ships of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Second Naval Fleet flickered in and out of view, their numbers too vast to count at a glance.

Soon, the flagship's detection systems yielded results—the count of warships between the icebergs.

This allowed Feng Lian to confirm that the force before him was indeed the main body of the Second Naval Fleet!

“Hmph, you finally deigned to show yourselves,” Feng Lian sneered coldly.

He bore a grudge against the Li Jian Clan. His earlier attack on the Intoxication Church's fleet had been a deliberate attempt to lure out a portion of the Second Naval Fleet.

Feng Lian knew full well: the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Second Naval Fleet served as the Li Jian Clan's private military force. Destroying them would inflict devastating losses upon the Li Jian Clan, an ideal choice for vengeance.

Yet despite Feng Lian's formidable strength, fate dealt him a cruel hand. He never imagined this assault would involve the sorrow-dispelling red wine. Then, Kong Pan's stratagem turned Feng Lian into the target of all hostility.

It had to be said, Kong Pan's scheme was brilliant. Without the Ice Sculpture Kingdom lifting a finger, other pirates had been lured into repeatedly targeting Feng Lian.

Ultimately, Feng Lian was forced into a confrontation with Da Han, and now found himself relentlessly pursued by Da Han's Icebound Pirates.

Feng Lian's predicament was dire. His attempt at revenge backfired, instead falling into Kong Pan's trap and plunging both himself and his crew into peril.

His hatred for the Li Jian Clan naturally deepened.

Thus, upon spotting the Second Fleet, even while still being hunted by Da Han, a surge of vengeful satisfaction welled up within him.

“Open fire!” Feng Lian commanded.

The Second Fleet's bombardment arrived even faster than the Divine Wind Pirates'.

Shells crisscrossed the sky, landing among the icebergs and around the pirate ships.

“Feng Lian, you damned bastard!” Kong Pan gritted her teeth, watching the Divine Wind Pirates draw ever closer while their pursuers—the Icebound Pirates—appeared on the horizon.

The Divine Wind Pirates charged straight into the drifting icebergs, braving the hail of bullets.

Their intent was clear: they were determined to break through the defenses and escape this battlefield.

Feng Lian moved again, wielding his scythe to slash out massive wind blades.

Though weakened by the time divine spell, he remained beyond the reach of ordinary transcendents.

Within mere minutes, two gold level enemies fell to Feng Lian's blades.

The Second Naval Fleet's high command comprised three gold level figures: Kong Pan, Yue Qian, and San Sha Man Tuo. Yet they were not the only ones. A handful of other gold level individuals remained as captains, commanding their respective warships.

Unable to stand idly by, Yue Qian requested permission from Kong Pan to engage.

“Feng Lian engaged Da Han head-on and still escaped unscathed...” Kong Pan was aware of this new intelligence.

Compared to his previous narrow escapes from a saint level enemy, Feng Lian's combat prowess had truly surged.

The battle unfolding before them was the starkest proof!

A flicker of conflict crossed Kong Pan's face, but he ultimately ordered, “Create an opening. Let Feng Lian's group depart.”

"Damn it!" Yue Qian clenched his fist and slammed it hard against the ship's wall.

But he was also well-versed in military strategy, fully aware that the Li Jian Clan was currently at a massive disadvantage on the battlefield. If the Divine Wind Pirates could withdraw, it might reduce the number of enemies they faced.

The military order was swiftly relayed.

The Li Jian Clan held full command over the Second Naval Fleet. This main force of the kingdom possessed high military discipline and acted in strict obedience.

Warships and drifting icebergs swiftly parted to either side.

The Divine Wind Pirates immediately felt the pressure ease.

“Oh?” Feng Lian sensed it, a sharp gleam flashing in his eyes.

He chuckled softly, his successful revenge lifting his spirits.

“But this isn't over yet. At most, we've only collected interest.” Feng Lian surveyed the surroundings and decisively ordered an accelerated retreat.

He acted with ruthless efficiency.

After breaking free from the iceberg cluster, he swiftly led the Divine Wind Pirates away from the battlefield.

Feng Lian had considered lingering near the combat zone, but out of caution for Da Han, he prudently abandoned that option.

“How long until the First Fleet arrives?” Kong Pan demanded.

“Based on our last communication, they'll need at least thirty minutes to join the fight,” the aide replied immediately.

Kong Pan's brow furrowed as she clenched her teeth.

She sensed the First Fleet was deliberately stalling!

Facing two enemies with two saint level combat power, 30 minutes was practically impossible.

“They're sacrificing us, using my Second Fleet as cannon fodder!” Kong Pan seethed with rage, yet felt utterly helpless.

“At least the Divine Wind Pirates have withdrawn, removing one formidable foe.”

“Clan Leader has already teleported here to reinforce us. If Feng Lian tries to seize this chance to attack the port or coastline, he'll face dire consequences.”

“As for the Icebound Pirates... we hold the intelligence advantage. Our new warships and naval tactics remain classified.”

Kong Pan steadied her resolve and issued a series of commands, directing the fleet to prepare for a frontal assault against the Icebound Pirates' charge.

The gold level pirate ship, Icebound, serving as the Icebound Pirates' flagship, did not lead the charge.

The saint level fighter Qian Xing remained concealed within the Icebound, keeping his presence undisclosed.

Through magic projections, Qian Xing surveyed the entire battlefield.

He witnessed the earlier scene: the Divine Wind Pirates had plunged headlong into the cluster of icebergs, only to be deliberately allowed to escape by the Second Naval Fleet.

“As captain, Kong Pan knows her limits,” Qian Xing remarked.

He scrutinized the icebergs and the new warships, silently shaking his head. "Deploying so many floating icebergs must serve some peculiar purpose. I wonder what the true capabilities of these new warships are."

“Only actual combat will reveal that.”

“If the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's saint level beings have arrived, would they hide among the icebergs?”

Qian Xing's greatest concern remained the three saint level beings of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Unlike the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Qian Xing, as an outsider, was unaware of the kingdom's intricate political dynamics.

In his view, the saint level beings of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were highly likely to join this battle.

“On the surface, the Icebound Pirates only possess two saint level combatants: Da Han and the Ice Crystal Jellyfish.”

"In truth, Ice Crystal Jellyfish is merely a saint level magic beast, not an intelligent species. Its methods are fixed and limited. When truly facing opponents of equal level, it can only be considered half a saint level being."

The Icebound Pirates had 1.5 saint level combat power, while the Ice Sculpture Kingdom possessed 3.

"We needn't deploy all forces, sending two saint level individuals will suffice to suppress them and secure battlefield dominance."

“This is precisely why I remain concealed.” Qian Xing understood his role well.

At that moment, Da Han gazed through the window at the distant sea mountains.

Within his right eye, scenes flashed continuously—he was employing the time divine spell to calculate future battle outcomes. The longer he calculated, the more he discerned, and the fewer hidden advantages the Second Naval Fleet possessed.

“Captain, should we bypass these naval forces?”

“The icebergs drift slowly. Most of our ships can outpace them,” the first mate advised.

“Unnecessary.” Da Han ordered the attack to commence.

His expression remained impassive as he treated his subordinates as pieces in his calculations.

As the pirate ships charged into the iceberg fields, the efficiency of his divine calculations surged dramatically, yielding significant results in no time.

“Quite interesting,” Da Han murmured with a faint smile upon glimpsing a fragment of the future.

The vanguard pirate ships charged headlong, while those behind unleashed frenzied cannon fire.

Yet countless cannonballs were deflected by the icebergs. The Second Fleet's warships anchored around the floating icebergs, launching their counterattack.

Each iceberg functioned as a colossal hub, forming magic links with the surrounding warships to generate pure white defensive light shields.

These shields offered excellent defense against cannonballs and spells, yet pirate ships charging directly through them could pierce them effortlessly.

Chapter 891: Sword of Time – Intertwined Sword Slash

Boom.

The pirate ship and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's warship collided violently.

Then spells and grappling hooks flew across, entangling enemy and friendly vessels alike.

Boards were immediately thrown across.

“Charge!”

“Follow me!”

“Slaughter these damned pirates!”

Pirates bellowed defiantly, while the snow elves roared in response.

The cries of battle intertwined as assault teams stormed opposing decks, casualties mounting instantly.

Blades clashed, flesh and blood spattered.

Battle qi and magic intertwined, and few fallen humans or snow elves rose again.

The iceberg emitted a soft white light, like a gauze shroud draping over the snow elves.

This radiance provided healing, stamina restoration, and defensive effects.

The Li Jian Clan commanded the Second Naval Fleet, with its lower-to-mid-level naval officers all members of the clan. United as one, they never abandoned comrades, often forming defensive formations of varying sizes to execute hit-and-run tactics.

In contrast, the pirates charged like raging waves, crashing violently against the flesh-and-blood barriers erected by the naval forces.

Compared to the Kingdom's Second Naval Fleet, the pirates' equipment and military discipline were inferior.

Thus, after a brief boarding engagement, pirate casualties far exceeded those of the navy.

The first mate hurried to Da Han's side and reported, "Commander! We've detected alchemy components of gold level within every iceberg."

"Currently, the drifting icebergs have demonstrated the ability to defend against cannon fire, protect vessels, and deploy soldiers. We suspect it may possess certain offensive capabilities."

"The Second Naval Fleet is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's regular army, its elite force. Now aided by the drifting icebergs and employing an uncommon naval tactic, they hold multiple advantages. Our vanguard is no match for them."

Da Han paused, still maintaining the time divine spell to project future visions.

After a long moment, he spoke slowly: “Have the Vegetable Pirates, Dread Firepower Pirates, and Ying Tou Pi charge forward.”

“Assemble the Ice Spike Squad, Ice Blast Squad, and Frost Archer Squad. Keep them on standby.”

“Order the magicians to prepare the spell, Light Snow. I want the entire battlefield covered.”

…

Da Han commanded with unwavering composure, issuing a series of orders.

The Vegetable Pirates, Dread Firepower Pirates, and others were all gold level pirate crews. Though smaller in scale than Da Han's Icebound Pirates, they couldn't handle the Ice Sculpture Kingdom alone. Yet they still sought to plunder it, so they temporarily aligned with Da Han.

These pirates formed the second wave of assault, and after their charge, the Li Jian Clan's maritime defenses visibly faltered. The once orderly defenses became jagged and disjointed under the pirates' onslaught.

Several icebergs collapsed in succession. Their alchemy components shattered within, causing the icebergs to slowly sink into the sea.

Without the icebergs for support, naval warships retreated in succession, regrouping around the icebergs further back.

Cong Mang wielded his long staff, crushing the skull of a silver level naval officer with a single blow.

He charged left and right across the warship's deck, reveling in the slaughter.

Behind him followed the signature squad of the Vegetable Pirates—the Hoodlum battle squad. Under Cong Mang's leadership, they inflicted staggering casualties.

The Dread Firepower Pirates fought differently.

Composed mainly of water pigmen, this pirate crew specialized in training elite naval combat units with formidable ship-boring capabilities.

Time and again, the hulls of naval ships were pierced by the water pigmen, but the holes quickly sealed shut with ice.

When their ship-boring attacks failed, the water pigmen didn't lose heart. They fully exploited another advantage—firepower!

A barrage of cannons, ballistae, and other weapons opened fire, weaving a dense, massive net of fire.

Under this torrent of fire, the snow elves moving on the deck quickly became corpses.

Ying Tou Pi roared with laughter: “Ram them!”

Though his flagship was only silver level, its ram was a gold level alchemy component.

Following his flagship were over a dozen magic ships, each equipped with superior armor.

Ying Tou Pi's pirate crew relished nothing more than steering their ships to crash into anything in sight.

Thud. Thud. Thud.

Ying Tou Pi's pirates sent the warships tumbling sideways, some capsizing instantly.

“These warships are too tough!”

“It's those floating icebergs causing trouble, what a pain.”

Ying Tou Pi gritted his teeth and cursed.

Every time his ship rammed into one, a layer of ice armor rapidly crystallized over the naval vessel's hull. The ice armor was controllable, capable of thickening to considerable depth in moments.

Ying Tou Pi's pirate ship had to shatter the ice layer first before damaging the warship itself. Much of the impact's destructive force was absorbed by the surface ice.

Some pirates slaughtered relentlessly, others were locked in stalemates, but overall they held the Second Naval Fleet at bay.

“The tactic of pairing new warships with floating icebergs gives us an advantage here.”

“But our mid-to-lower-ranking officers aren't a match for pirate captains, we've suffered heavy casualties.”

San Sha Man Tuo assessed the situation with tense concentration.

Pirates tempered themselves through years of combat. Those blessed with talent and luck rose through brutal elimination to become group leaders, ringleaders, and ultimately captains.

Such fierce competition naturally elevated only the elite of the elite to high positions.

In contrast, the navy's training intensity for mid-to-lower-ranking officers fell far short.

"The Li Jian Clan, to maintain control over the Second Fleet, practices nepotism, filling many key positions with their own kin. Combat prowess isn't the sole criterion; it's not even the primary one."

“Among pirates, however, might makes right...”

San Sha Man Tuo's thoughts diverged. Having joined the Li Jian Clan as an outsider, she had gained deep insight into its inner workings. Instantly analyzing the battle situation, she traced inward to uncover the true root cause.

Gripping her staff slightly tighter, San Sha Man Tuo realized: the time for her to act was approaching.

Sure enough, shortly after, Kong Pan issued the order for San Sha Man Tuo and her team to engage.

San Sha Man Tuo drew a deep breath, dispelled her spells, and deliberately revealed her aura.

As a magician, she had to protect herself. Especially in this chaotic battlefield, where the next moment was utterly unpredictable.

San Sha Man Tuo cast a debuff spell, instantly affecting numerous pirates. The nearby navy seized the opportunity to pounce and slay them.

Yue Qian quickly appeared in various spots across the battlefield.

He was a rising star of the Li Jian Clan, destined to become its future pillar. His Inner World bloodline ran thick within him, enabling him to cast racial magic that flashed him into short-range teleportation flashes.

This granted him exceptional support and evasion skills. The pirates cursed and raged, utterly unable to catch up to Yue Qian.

As a gold level, Yue Qian possessed formidable strength. He swiftly dispatched three silver level pirates and lightly wounded a gold level pirate.

With support from Yue Qian and San Sha Man Tuo, the naval forces barely stabilized their position.

But the respite was short-lived. At Da Han's command, the pirates' third wave of assault immediately surged forward.

“The defense line is about to collapse,” Kong Pan assessed the situation, sighing inwardly.

She drew a plate-shaped magic artifact, preparing to cast a spell herself to create a spatial barrier.

But in the next instant, an icy beam shot thousands of meters across the battlefield, piercing through it like a razor-sharp spear, heading straight for Kong Pan.

The alchemy artifact on her body flared with light, buying her crucial time.

Kong Pan dodged swiftly, evading the icy beam.

She immediately spat a mouthful of blood, forced to break her spell and suffer its backlash.

At the same moment, Da Han nonchalantly withdrew his index finger.

Just now, he had personally intervened!

Kong Pan was drenched in cold sweat, her face drained of color.

“How could this be?”

“I was just about to strike, yet Da Han reacted with such blinding speed!”

“No...”

"Da Han worships the Dusk God and wields the time divine spell. He must have used the time divine spell to foresee the next moment's events."

“Da Han... he's moved out...” Kong Pan stared at the magic projection, her expression grim.

After striking, Da Han didn't stop. He slowly ascended into the air, diving toward Kong Pan's position.

Kong Pan had no time to hesitate. She immediately activated her alchemy components to teleport to another ship.

She had long since left her flagship, precisely to prevent Da Han from executing a decapitation strike.

This was the world of the strong.

The pirate faction, possessing saint level combat power, held a clear advantage.

Da Han soared high into the sky, surveying the entire battlefield below. From his vantage point, the ships resembled wooden toy cars.

Pirates and navy forces clashed fiercely, while those not yet engaged in battle collectively looked up, watching Da Han's every move with intense focus.

To the naked eye, Da Han had shrunk into an indistinct black speck high in the sky.

No one dared approach Da Han, but Kong Pan resolutely ordered the bombardment.

Yet the gold level shells, upon striking Da Han's saint domain, fell silent one after another.

Da Han prayed with sincere devotion:

“My Lord, your faithful servant Da Han pays homage to you!”

“You are the Dusk, the God of Time, the inevitable end all things must face.”

“You govern the passage of time, and all phenomena fade with You.”

“Please unleash Your divine might, letting our enemies taste the world's mercilessness.”

Unlike prayers in peacetime, those on the battlefield were often brief.

As Da Han finished his prayer, infinite rays of light streamed down from the sky, forming translucent blades known as the Sword of Time.

The blades rained down like a torrential downpour.

Snow elves struck by the blades spat blood and retreated, their attempts to wield battle qi and spells nearly futile.

Where the blades struck the magic ships, explosions erupted. magic power circuits were damaged, and alchemy devices failed one after another.

Time divine spell - Sword Slash: Intertwined Time!

The time within each struck target's body became severely disjointed. Some parts accelerated, others slowed.

This severely disrupted the channeling of magic and battle qi, magic-powered ships were no exception. In truth, these vessels were more rigid than even the transcendents themselves, forced to endure damage to their magic circuits without the ability for fine-tuning adjustments.

“Such a divine spell!” Kong Pan gritted her teeth as Swords of Time rained down from the sky.

She wasn't without trump cards, but her powerful tactics, new warships and floating icebergs, had all been nullified by the Swords of Time.

“Da Han foresaw the future, so he struck early, specifically casting this divine spell to suppress us!” Kong Pan sighed deeply, head thrown back.

Her fighting spirit plummeted to its lowest.

“Withdraw... withdraw the forces!” she ordered decisively.

Having fought this far, the Li Jian Clan could now account for their actions.

If Snow Bird Harbor City Lord understood the kingdom's delicate political landscape, how could Kong Pan of the Li Jian Clan be unaware?

When Da Han struck without any interference from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, Kong Pan knew: she would not receive reinforcements from the kingdom's saints.

“We must preserve the clan's fighting strength!”

“Order all ships to enter the icebergs!”

With this command, the warships still capable of maneuvering crashed into the icebergs near them.

As they collided, a miraculous scene unfolded. The icebergs seemed like layers of light and shadow, merely rippling before swallowing the warships whole.

The icebergs' aura grew steadily stronger, each absorption of a warship adding a small surge to their power.

The Sword of Time's effect on the icebergs was greatly diminished.

"The drifting icebergs were constructed using vast quantities of time ice cubes as building materials. Its resistance to temporal magic and spells is truly remarkable."

Most warships, crew and all, vanished into the iceberg.

The drifting iceberg accelerated sharply, carrying the Second Fleet's main force as it made a decisive escape!

Da Han, however, made no further move.

He descended leisurely onto the flagship, the Icebound.

“Go chase them.”

“Go plunder them.”

“We are pirates!” he roared with laughter.

The pirates erupted in thunderous cheers.

Some pursued the drifting icebergs, smashing them apart and mercilessly slaughtering the naval forces. Others broke rank entirely, striking out alone toward the coastal towns of the Ice Sculpture Island.

Chapter 892: Withdrawing from the Capital

Dusk descended like a curtain, shrouding the sea that had been the battlefield. The former clamor had utterly dissipated, leaving only a tangled mass of corpses, ice floes, and shattered planks.

Crimson blood, illuminated by the setting sun, tinged the seawater with a magnificent purple hue.

Huge schools of sharks roamed the waters, devouring the corpses of both navy and pirates.

For them, this was a rare feast, one that only occurred when the pirates prevailed.

Had the navy won, they would have salvaged the bodies. But victorious pirates pressed their advantage, seldom retrieving comrades lost to the sea.

The First Fleet arrived late.

The gruesome scenes unfolded before the eyes of the naval officers and soldiers.

The Vice Admiral focused more on the drifting ice in the sea.

These were almost entirely remnants of shattered icebergs.

Soon, the observations yielded results—the casualties of the Second Fleet were not as severe as the Vice Admiral had anticipated.

“It seems the Li Jian Clan lost no more than a quarter of their forces; their main strength remains intact.”

“Hmph. The drifting icebergs did them a great favor.”

“Strange. Did Dao Hen not intervene?”

Subsequent reconnaissance reports began arriving.

Only then did the Vice Admiral learn: “Dao Hen did intervene, but not consistently...”

“The Li Jian Clan is utterly useless. They barely drew out any of Da Han's combat capabilities. The valuable combat intelligence gathered is minimal!”

The Vice Admiral's voice dripped with contempt and fury. Meanwhile, on another warship, Mei Lin leaned against the rail, her eyes flickering with reluctance.

The First Naval Fleet could have arrived in time, yet they deliberately delayed themselves.

Mei Lin disliked this course of action, but reason told her—it was the wisest choice.

Politically speaking: The royal family had long harbored resentment and apprehension toward the Li Jian Clan's control of the Second Naval Fleet. By delaying the First Fleet's support, they could weaken the Li Jian Clan while testing Da Han—a multi-purpose strategy.

Militarily speaking: With no saint level individuals from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom intervening throughout the battle, defeat was inevitable!

Mei Lin speculated privately: "Our nation's three saint level beings, the king and the Royal Grand Magician, represent the interests of the royal family and the Han Yu Clan respectively. They are both highly wary of the Li Jian Clan. They likely each harbor intentions to weaken the latter, hence none have entered the fray."

"Without opponents of equal standing, the Second Naval Fleet cannot prevail. Da Han alone was unmanageable, let alone with a saint level Ice Crystal Jellyfish."

That alone counted as two saint level combatants.

In a direct matchup, saint level beasts were relatively weak and easy to counter. The combination of Da Han and the Ice Crystal Jellyfish could be considered 1.5 saint level battle strength.

But when fighting below their level, against the Second Naval Fleet, the Da Han and Ice Crystal Jellyfish combo absolutely equaled two saint level forces. In fact, due to the beast's high-end offense and defense, coupled with its thick health pool, it became even more troublesome.

“This was a naval battle destined for defeat. It's highly likely the Vice Admiral knew of the higher-ups' plans and avoided it in advance.”

Later, Mei Lin also received battlefield investigation results.

Throughout the entire battle, Da Han had only intervened twice. On one occasion, he unleashed a saint level divine spell, decisively sealing the victory and fully demonstrating the might of the saint level.

As a higher realm within the transcendent levels, the saint level condensed immense power within a single individual, achieving a qualitative leap.

Their every move can effortlessly overturn an entire battlefield, stirring up regional upheavals.

This was a world of the strong. Without combat power of the same level, the Second Naval Fleet could only become targets for slaughter.

From this perspective, their escape and preservation of their main force demonstrated considerable skill.

What caught Mei Lin's attention more than the saint level divine spell was Da Han's earlier intervention.

Da Han hadn't merely executed a single move.

He had targeted Kong Pan.

Kong Pan wasn't instantly killed, but her spellcasting attempt was directly disrupted.

Mei Lin's gaze grew solemn: “Da Han wields the time divine spell, capable of foretelling the future.”

“Thus, he foresaw the impending scenario and struck preemptively, suppressing Kong Pan with pinpoint precision.”

"Moreover, his subsequent deployment of the divine spell, Intertwined Sword Slash, skillfully evaded the iceberg's ability to absorb ice elements. This was likely no coincidence, but rather the optimal attack method he had predicted."

“Such an enemy is truly terrifying...”

Simply being at the saint level was headache-inducing enough, exerting pressure like mountains that made breathing difficult. Now Mei Lin realized Da Han could also predict the future with his divine spell.

This made him at least ten times more troublesome!

Many tactics hadn't even been deployed before Da Han anticipated them.

Timely countermeasures against Da Han not only allow for more composed defense but even enable counterattacks.

Mei Lin knew that Kong Pan was formidable. Having commanded the Second Naval Fleet for years, she possessed adept and versatile naval tactics. Combined with her own spatial magic, she could dominate the seas as long as she didn't encounter a saint level opponent.

Yet in this battle, she hadn't even deployed proper naval tactics, likely because Da Han had preemptively neutralized them.

Reviewing the battle report, Mei Lin realized: The Li Jian Clan had been utterly powerless to retaliate throughout this offensive. Initially, their maritime defenses were breached by the pirate vanguard's assault, then crushed by the second wave, and finally annihilated by Da Han's third strike.

“If it were me, the First Fleet, facing Da Han, we wouldn't stand a chance either,” Mei Lin reflected, her mood heavy.

She sensed the vast disparity between enemy and ally through the battle reports.

The Vice Admiral summoned her for a meeting to discuss next steps.

With the Second Naval Fleet's retreat, the entire maritime defense line in this sector had been completely breached.

Through intelligence from various sources, the Vice Admiral had learned: the pirates had begun showing signs of dispersal even during the final stages of the naval battle. By the time the Second Naval Fleet was completely routed, the pirate coalition had already fallen apart.

“In the coming period, any coastal town on Ice Sculpture Island could face pirate raids and plunder.”

“The Second Naval Fleet is beyond hope. From now on, we must rely solely on ourselves.”

"Fortunately, these pirates lack discipline. A single naval victory has scattered them. This presents us with a golden opportunity to concentrate our forces and seek an opening to annihilate them!"

The Vice Admiral paused, then slammed his fist onto the nautical chart spread across the table, rallying the troops.

Mei Lin inquired, “Where is Da Han positioned?”

The key to the operation's success lay in evading Da Han.

The Vice Admiral fell silent. A Rear Admiral speculated, “Da Han is a formidable saint. If he strikes, his target won't be insignificant.”

“I believe any of our nation's eight major trading ports could be his target.”

“I propose we bypass these eight ports entirely and focus on eliminating pirate fleets elsewhere first.”

This suggestion was swiftly approved.

By the time Mei Lin left the flagship and returned to her command ship, the sun had set, and night had fallen over the sea.

The First Fleet cleared the battlefield before withdrawing.

Mei Lin gazed at the receding battlefield and sighed softly.

No matter how brilliant a commander, facing an overwhelmingly powerful individual, even the most exquisite tactics prove useless.

Against a saint level powerhouse like Da Han, the military tactics were like a joke.

A wave of helplessness washed over Mei Lin's heart.

At the same moment, Zi Di's heart was also filled with a crushing sense of powerlessness.

She was heading toward the teleportation formation with Zong Ge.
