Zong Ge remained unconscious, or rather, his soul had vanished, though his body still lived.

“I must leave the capital immediately!”

Zi Di was fleeing with Zong Ge.

Beyond his unconscious state, the most critical reason was the dwindling supply of pearl bubbles.

For this departure alone, Zi Di had used two more pearl bubbles, altering both her and Zong Ge's appearances.

This left her with only two pearl bubbles remaining.

She had to retreat.

Once the pearl bubbles were exhausted, her secret of being a necromancer would be exposed.

Then, all the forces of the royal capital would converge to hunt her down.

This retreat meant abandoning the duel and suspending her business plans. But Zi Di truly had no other choice—she could only retreat!

Of course, she had considered seeking help from the Li Jian Clan, but she quickly dismissed the idea.

The Li Jian Clan had already hinted at ill will. Asking for help now would signal weakness. It could swiftly turn this ally into an enemy!

Turning allies into foes? Zi Di would never make such a losing bet. Thus, she resolved to depart temporarily, vanishing mysteriously through the Deceptive Disguise spell, leaving the Li Jian Clan ample room for speculation. If she could instill fear in them, that would be ideal.

Fei She and others departed alongside Zi Di, though they split into separate groups.

Initially, Zi Di's retreat proceeded smoothly.

However, when she queued up with the wooden crate (containing Zong Ge) to enter the teleportation formation, it failed to activate.

“What's going on?” Others waiting to teleport questioned.

The official overseeing the formation stepped forward: “We've detected prohibited items. This transmission is suspended. We must inspect everyone.”

The crowd gasped, murmuring complaints, yet had no choice but to comply.

Zi Di sensed trouble brewing. She forced herself to stay calm, confident in her deception skills.

However, when she was led into the room alone, she encountered a familiar face—the steward of the Li Jian Clan.

"Magician Yao Ma, leaving the city so late at night—is there some urgent matter? Perhaps our Li Jian Clan could be of assistance." The steward smiled.

Zi Di's heart trembled violently.

She had clearly cast a fresh layer of Deceptive Disguise spell, yet it hadn't fooled the Li Jian Clan!

In an instant, she understood: the teleportation formation's shutdown was entirely the work of the Li Jian Clan.

The Li Jian Clan had come specifically for her and Zong Ge.

The steward, smiling, took a sip of tea. Seeing Zi Di's stunned expression, he continued, “Your corps and our clan have worked closely together. To depart without notice is rather impolite, don't you think?”

“Not only did you flee alone, but you took your leader with you.”

"So, what about the upcoming National Ceremony Grand Duel? What about the Warm Snow Cup tournament? What about our mount potion sales?"

Zi Di snorted coldly, cutting him off to ask the most crucial question: “How did you uncover our disguise?”

The steward flashed a smug smile. “Speaking of which, we've been quite curious ourselves.”

"Your corps' disguise technique is truly ingenious, distinct from most transformation abilities. The uninitiated would find it nearly impossible to detect, yet once someone knows you've altered your appearance, you appear no different from your original form to those in the know."

"What exactly is this method? May we hear the details?"

Zi Di instantly understood, narrowing her eyes as a cold glint flashed through the slits: “You've been monitoring us?”

The steward nodded, admitting it plainly: “Fortunately we did. Otherwise, with your corps' disguise techniques, wouldn't we have been completely fooled by you?”

Zi Di sneered coldly: "Even if we were discovered, what could you do? Don't forget, we've signed a magic contract."

This was a safeguard.

Neither party could kill the other.

The steward replied: “Magician Yao Ma, do not worry. My clan is honorable. We would never harm you.”

“But leaving without notice like this is utterly unacceptable.”

“Return to your duties.”

“Oh, and don't fret too much over Lord Long Fu.”

“His current state isn't necessarily a bad thing.”

Zi Di's expression darkened, her murderous intent unmasked. "So that's it! You tampered with the sorrow-dispelling red wine, plunging Lord Long Fu into this unconscious state."

The butler's smile only widened. “Don't even think about attacking me, Magician Yao Ma. Remember, we have a cooperation contract.”

Zi Di's personal strength was limited, especially against the prepared Li Jian Clan.

Escaping alone would be difficult enough, let alone with the burden of Zong Ge in tow.

The steward did not actually harm Zi Di, but forced her to turn back.

With no other choice, Zi Di was escorted back to her previous lodgings.

There, she saw Fei She and the others, also brought back under guard.

Zi Di's expression remained calm, but Fei She became visibly agitated upon seeing her.

He knew the pearl bubbles were running low.

Once they were completely depleted, the young lady's undead identity would be utterly exposed. At that point, even if the Li Jian Clan tried to protect Zi Di, it would be impossible.

Zi Di would be torn apart by the swarm of transcendents!

As for Zong Ge and Fei She, though their positions would also be exposed, their identities weren't as sensitive, allowing them to survive for a while longer.

Of course, this too would be temporary.

Once their true forms were revealed, their identities would soon be uncovered. And with that, the secrets of Mystifying Monster Island would be laid bare!

At that point, everything would be lost.

A dead end.

Zi Di, Zong Ge, Fei She, and the others had reached a dead end.

Chapter 893: Hua Tang Attacks Again

Ice Sculpture Capital.

In a room, Zong Ge was lying on a bed, his eyes already shut.

Zi Di stood in front of him, extending her hand to open his eyelids and saw two dilated white eyes.

Since Zong Ge had fallen into a coma, his eyes had turned back over to white.

Zi Di sighed.

She wanted Zong Ge to withdraw from the capital, but the Li Jian Clan saw through it and had him return.

“The situation is quite troublesome!”

“The Pearl Bubbles are almost gone; how long can they last?”

“Half a month? A week, or just a few days?”

This was not good.

Because Deceptive Disguise was depleted based on how detailed and attentive the investigation was.

It was very clear that the moment the Pearl Bubbles were depleted, it would be the beginning of Zi Di’s exposure and death.

“Calm down, keep calm.” Zi Di forced herself to maintain her rationality and subside her mind.

The commander had left earlier, and the auxiliary commander had collapsed.

The lady merchant could only rely on herself now.

“Fei She and the others are weaker, they need me too…”

“Then can I kill my way out?”

Zi Di assessed her combat strength.

“I am an iron level necromancer, not that strong on my own.”

“My undead methods are mostly composed of rat eye and snake shadow ghosts; they can be used for detection but are a huge letdown in battle.”

“My true combat strength is still the silver level gargoyles.”

“Alas…if only I could complete that damaged gold level gargoyle.”

The gargoyle was an alchemy puppet Zi Di and Zong Ge acquired after they beheaded Jia Bing.

Several gargoyles were given Deceptive Disguise and turned “invisible”, and they had saved Zi Di from danger.

Could it be done this time?

Zi Di shook her head.

She lacked faith in facing the Li Jian Clan.

Before, the gargoyle had a good showing at the Wisteria Store mainly because it had the advantage of surprise, catching the enemy unprepared. But despite that, Zi Di was still teleported to the research lab underneath the ice lake.

If it weren’t for Elder Yuan Ci saving her, Zi Di would have been in grave danger.

Now, the Li Jian Clan had acted, placing Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others under house arrest.

How much could the ice gargoyles do when the enemy was paying attention?

“Zong Ge, if you don’t wake up, then it’s too late.” Zi Di tried to heal Zong Ge with a scroll.

It didn’t do anything.

Zi Di had already tried doing this before.

Things were still the same.

The healing magic easily permeated Zong Ge’s body, but nothing happened.

“Zong Ge has a layer of mysterious divine power around him.”

“This divine power protects him, including suppressing all of the hidden dangers he was affected by.”

“Ther divine power defensive layer does not block healing magic, but so long as Zong Ge is harmed, it will stimulate the layer of divine power protection and strike back.”

Zi Di sighed to herself.

Compared to before, she now knew much more.

“This is a trick of the Li Jian Clan.”

“The root cause was those bottles of sorrow-dispelling red wine.”

“But Zong Ge doesn’t seem to be in a terrible situation…”

Zi Di guessed the reason was the magic contract.

She and the Li Jian Clan had signed a collaboration contract, and they could stab the other in the back, otherwise it would trigger the contract’s backlash.”

The contract was signed by the Li Jian Clan Leader Mian Li Zang.

Although Zi Di was a low level magician, she could still discern the power of the contract and how hard it was to violate.

“Unless Mian Li Zang has an unusual method that can unilaterally rescind the contract.”

“But even if that is done, so long as the agreement is unilaterally terminated and loses effectiveness, I can respond too.”

“Zong Ge’s current condition seems to benefit him. The Li Jian Clan’s steward held that opinion.”

“Therefore, this does not violate the collaboration contract, it just has massive immediate side effects.”

Zi Di sank into a hurried pondering.

The Li Jian Clan putting them under house arrest was a fatal move in her case.

After failing to rouse Zong Ge again, Zi Di had to look for other ways to escape confinement.

“Can I contact Elder Yuan Ci, could he find some way out?” Previously, Zi Di pretended to be Magician Gui Teng and contacted the Wisteria Merchant Alliance Elder Yuan Ci.

But soon after, Zi Di shook her head.

This method was not reliable.

Leaving aside that Elder Yuan Ci did not fully trust Zi Di, him coming to her rescue would expose many of Zi Di’s secrets. Not just the identity of Magician Gui Teng, but also the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ connection with Zi Di’s identity.

“Can Elder Yuan Ci vie with the Li Jian Clan?”

“He is just a silver level.”

“He could avoid the Imperial Secret Spy Agency’s manhunt, mainly because he relied on the Ice Sculpture Capital and hid in the Millennium Frozen Lake for years on end.”

Zi Di looked around, “No, the biggest obstruction is the Li Jian Clan’s surveillance!”

“This time, I used a new Deceptive Disguise spell to evacuate, yet I was still intercepted.”

“If I contact Elder Yuan Ci, will the Li Jian Clan notice?”

The answer was no.

Up till now, Zi Di never sensed any monitoring indicators.

She had to admit that the Li Jian Clan possessed formidable surveillance abilities!

“The Li Jian Clan’s alchemy technology is not outstanding, otherwise, they wouldn’t have energetically collaborated with us in the area of refining magic medicine.”

“Perhaps, their surveillance method is used through the Inner World bloodline?”

The Inner World bloodline was a well-known spatial bloodline, and it was the foundation of the Li Jian Clan.

Contacting Yuan Ci would be considerably stupid without eliminating or blocking this surveillance skill.

Zi Di had no idea where to start on resolving the targeted surveillance.

She had no confidence in this field.

But time waited for no one!

After a moment of silence, Zi Di thought of something, “Perhaps I should get help from Jiu Jin?”

The Alchemy Guild Elder Jiu Jin was once sponsored by the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. During prior negotiations, Jiu Jin was blackmailed by Zi Di and was forced to give in, he even divulged exam questions to Zi Di voluntarily.

Zi Di’s preparations allowed her to pass the first part of this year’s Warm Snow Cup.

But because of that outstanding performance, it triggered the Li Jian Clan’s vigilance, and the latter forced Zi Di to make concessions and sign an exclusive collaboration clause.

Zi Di could contact Elder Jiu Jin openly.

Even if the monitoring problem wasn’t solved, there was no harm in doing so.

“In fact, it is the time to exploit Jiu Jin into doing something for me.”

“The Li Jian Clan knows Zong Ge’s current state and also knows that Elder Jiu Jin is under my thumb.”

“They will be somewhat wary of this.”

“Moreover, in light of Elder Jiu Jin’s authority and status in the Alchemy Guild, how much of the guild’s power can he leverage for me?”

“When he learns of my current situation, will he really help me sincerely?”

Zi Di’s brows tightened, the more she analyzed, the more she despaired.

The execution noose was already around her neck, causing the boulder in her heart to grow ever heavier.

Despite thinking from different angles, Zi Di had no other ideas.

Finally, there was just one path.

“Surrendering…to the Li Jian Clan?”

“We have a collaborative relationship with the Li Jian Clan and are bound with deep benefits.”

“The reason the Li Jian Clan put us under house arrest, excluding the magic contract, is because of benefits, that is why they act and move this way.”

“So long as I renounce our interests completely, I should be able to exchange the Li Jian Clan to…”

Boom!

Suddenly, an explosion interrupted Zi Di’s train of thought.

Zi Di’s head looked up lightning fast, and she quickly ran to the second floor balcony.

A moment later, her pupils contracted fiercely when she saw a familiar enemy.

He had a slender figure, wore a silver mask on his face, and wore a white robe that had all kinds of exquisite and gaudy flowers on it. He was jumping around and attacking as flower petals sprinkled everywhere, exuding a beautiful aura of cold killing intent.

It was Hua Tang!

“He’s come to attack us again, and at this point in time, was information leaked?” Zi Di’s heart sank to rock bottom, thinking of a possibility.

For a long time, she and Zong Ge were barely able to keep the situation afloat and did their best to present an appearance of strength.

They had no choice but to do so.

The mysterious power was gigantic and gold level enemies seemed to breed like flies.

Only by showing strength could the enemy be restrained by fear, making them apprehensive and unwilling to act lightly.

But now, Hua Tang attacking once again seemed to forebode that this powerful and mysterious enemy had learned of their delicate situation.

“The Li Jian Clan monitored us and saw through my evacuation.”

“Could it be that there was a traitor planted here that quickly leaked information?”

“Hold on, perhaps this is a golden opportunity to escape the Li Jian Clan’s control?”

Zi Di’s mind moved, smelling an opportunity in the enormous dangers.

“Magician Yao Ma, please be at ease. Even if it is Hua Tang, it is impossible to break out!” The noble steward pushed open the door and strode in.

He was the person in charge here and was personally keeping watch over Zi Di and Zong Ge.

The Li Jian Clan’s steward also walked onto the balcony, stood by Zi Di, and shouted, “Hua Tang, do you think you can break in here a second time?”

“You really don’t care about the Li Jian Clan’s prestige? Then this time, you shall pay for your contempt in blood.”

Just as he spoke, a magic formation activated.

A half spherical light shield instantly appeared, covering Hua Hang and preventing him from escaping.

A moment later, a white ice wall condensed between Zi Di and Hua Tang.

Snow ball, Ice Arrow, and other ice type spells interweaved into a network of firepower that covered Hua Tang.

Hua Tang chuckled, and gold battle qi surged from his body into a defensive battle skill, then he charged on ahead.

He endured the densely packed magic attacks and made it to the ice wall.

He used a gold level battle skill in passing that shook the ice wall and caused cracks to quickly spread through its entire surface.

The noble steward’s expression changed, “Do you really think I’m the only one in the clan?!”

As he said that, parcels of land in the garden were inverted into a number of tunnels.

From the tunnels came out experts.

This was the Li Jian Clan’s elite battle squad – Space Armor Guards!

With the same equipment, Inner World bloodline, and matching battle skills, the Space Armor Guards had an unusual and outstanding defense.

Hua Tang used a gold level attack against these guards, only causing the surface of guards’ equipment to stir with a spatial vortex formation.

After the vortex banished the attack, it quickly subsided.

The Space Armor Guards was composed almost entirely of silver levels, and the twenty silver levels surrounded Hua Tang.

Hua Tang dashed left and right, keenly moving as gracefully as a butterfly.

The Space Armor Guards relied on their peculiar defenses and peerless coordination to maintain the encirclement.

Seeing this, the noble steward showed a complacent smile, saying to Zi Di, “You see Magician Yao Ma, even if Hua Tang attacks, so long as my clan is prepared enough, nothing will happen.”

Zi Di’s heart went ice cold.

The Space Armor Guard battle squad was quite strong. Just now, Zi Di thought she could rely on her personal strength to break out.

But now, she knew that even if she had ice gargoyles with Deceptive Disguise that were “invisible”, the ice gargoyles would be ineffective against the Space Armor Guards.

“Relying just on myself, I won’t be able to break out. The Li Jian Clan assigning this elite battle squad here seems to mean they value us greatly.”

Seeing Zi D silent, the noble steward looked at Hua Tang and yelled, “Hua Tang, last time I let you get away. But this time, you won’t do anything.”

“Run along now, the moment you attacked, the alarm was triggered. Many city guards are already rushing over!”

But Hua Tang didn’t withdraw, and he suddenly laughed, “So that’s how it is.”

He attacked again; this time his offensive had distinctly changed.

After a gold level battle skill was used, it formed a continuous attack.

The Space Armor Guards’ spatial armor vortex absorbed the first offensive, barely absorbed the second, but the third was difficult to deal with, and few were able to withstand it.

Pop, plop, pop…

The Space Armor Guards coughed up blood in succession, and the front was suddenly thrown into chaos.

Hua Tan g did not press the attack, instead he flew towards the ice wall and ruthlessly struck it.

Kaboom…

The cracks in the ice wall spread further, then with a bang, it collapsed completely.

But the noble steward did not panic, he maintained his poise because in the twinkle of an eye, a second ice wall formed to stop Hua Tang.

“It’s Hua Tang!”

“Is it really Hua Tang?”

“Catch him, this will be a great merit.”

The city guard had just hurried over to the scene.

The noble steward’s words were not bluster; the city guard had been alerted.

Hua Tang’s last attack caused the Li Jian Clan to lose face. So they learned from their mistakes and adjusted and made contingencies.

Unlike the silver level Space Armor Guards, the city guard commander was a gold level.

“Hua Tang! I’m afraid I won’t be able to catch this big fish by myself!” The gold level commander was self-aware, and after he arrived on the scene, he arranged to have a defensive line laid out.

At the same time, he also made an urgent call to a colleague.

This was the Ice Sculpture Capital, there were eight commanders, and more than eight gold levels in the city guard!

The city guard’s appearance allowed the noble steward to secretly relax.

He still felt psychological pressure.

After all, it was Hua Tang!

Seeing the casualties below, the noble steward sighed, “The Space Armor Guards are only silver levels, and in the end, are unable to stop Hua Tang for long.”

“But I don’t need to withstand it completely, I only needed to stall long enough for the city guard to save the situation.”

“Hua Tang is Hua Tang after all, no ordinary gold level.”

Boom.

Another loud sound.

It was actually Hua Tang throwing an alchemy bomb.

The bomb set off flames and clouds of dust as Hua Tang suddenly retreated.

“Be careful, he is escaping!”

“Capture him, don’t let him get away!”

The Li Jian Clan members and the city guard shouted together.

The noble steward immediately took control of the light shield and instantly increased its strength to the maximum in an attempt to slow down Hua Tang a little.

But a moment later, the defensive magic formation collapsed. The light shield violently shuddered, and after distorting, it rapidly dissipated.

Hua Tang vanished, only leaving a few petals behind.

“Damn it!”

“What just happened?”

“The magic formation’s underground core was actually attacked?”

“How did Hua Tang accomplish that? Don’t tell me he had an accomplice?”

Just as he thought of that, the noble steward’s expression radically changed, “Not good!”

Those were his last words.

A moment later, Hua Tang suddenly appeared behind him, dagger in hand.

Plop.

The noble steward turned as stiff as a puppet as he looked down at the dagger tip appearing from his stomach.

Gold battle qi rushed into his body and tore it apart recklessly.

The noble steward’s eyes glared tenaciously as his vitality was extinguished!

This scene shocked everyone, including Zi Di who was next to the old steward.

Danger!

As a magician, a fighter actually got up close to her.

Furthermore, Zi Di was only an iron level while Hua Tang was a gold level!

In that moment of life and death, Zi Di’s mind went blank, and she subconsciously wanted to use necromancy.

But Hua Tang’s dagger was already stabbing towards her face.

It was too late!

In a split second, Zi Di felt she had fallen into an icy cave, and her body and mind flooded with deathly stillness and despair.

Time seemed to slow at this moment.

Zi Di blankly stared at the dagger filled with gold battle qi that was about to stab into her eye.

Ding.

A hand suddenly appeared, precisely grabbing the dagger.

The dagger suddenly stopped, the tip only a centimeter from Zi Di’s eye.

But Zi Di’s attention wasn’t on the dagger.

She unwaveringly stared at the large hand that appeared.

The hand was covered in dragon scales, and it was tough and powerful. As it held the dagger, it seemed to spark with friction.

How familiar!

“Could it be, could it be…”

Zi Di quickly shifted her eyes, and from the hand, her eyes streaked across an arm covered with the same red dragon scales.

Then she saw that person of her dreams.

The one she yearned for day and night.

The dragonman youngster!!

Chapter 894: I Have Returned!

In that split second.

Zi Di felt the entire world disassociate and quickly leave her.

She couldn’t hear the cries and groans, nor see the bloody battlefield.

She couldn’t even feel herself.

Her eyes only had one person in it.

“Long Fu? It’s him!” When the city guards saw the dragoman youngster, they cried out in surprise, and many looked excited, as if they saw some celebrity in the flesh.

“Hold on, why are these two Long Fus?!” The Li Jian Clan members in the know were shocked.

They were aware that in the room, there was an unconscious “Long Fu” on the bed.

But Zi Di had zero doubts. At first sight, she knew this really was the dragonman youngster.

She also didn’t know why she could confirm it as so.

It came from the bottom of her heart!

Zi Di’s vision blurred, as she recalled past memories.

The many times they helped each other on Mystifying Monster Island.

“I am your knight.”

In Mystifying Monster Island’s underground magician tower, the young man shouted “I am a beast! But who isn’t?”

…

They made a promise for the future.

“We will have children.”

“Correct. In the spring, we will lead our children on a spring hike. We will lean against the castle window and look at the rainstorm outside. In the autumn, we will bring the children hunting. In the winter, we will sit around the furnace and tell them fairy tales.”

…

When Zi Di became a ghost, the dragonman youngster led the survivors to freedom.

Zi Di’s heart belonged to him, regardless of whether he was a dragonman or in another form.

“Our components are too complex; there are too many races and too many differing beliefs.”

“He is the only one who everyone can believe in.”

“Even you wouldn’t dare to believe this group contains a half beastman, a half giant, a goblin and even necromancers!”

“Even if Zong Ge becomes a gold level, he can never achieve this.”

It is extraordinary!”

…

On Snake Rat Island, when the survivors were trapped in self-blame, the dragonman youngster shouted: our purpose is to survive, this is righteousness!

Two Eyes Island, Ice Sculpture Island.

When the dragonman youngster wanted to save Lan Zao, Zi didn’t stop him, because she knew that was the dragonman youngster she recognized.

…

After leaving Snow Bird Harbor, Zi Di traveled through hardships and perils, until finally, she reached the capital.

When the situation wasn’t improving, she racked her brains, used all of her strength, and pitted enemies against each other, until allies also became enemies.

But she never gave up and never disappointed the dragonman youngster.

She believed in the young man but did not wholly depend on him.

In her journey to reach today, she went through innumerable scorn, set ups, misunderstandings, and underestimations. She even broke relations with her father for a time!

Like on Mystifying Monster Island, she was never a wastrel parasite on the young man.

The two of them helped each other!

“I must do my best to accomplish our common goal!” This was her conviction on Mystifying Monster Island, and it was this same motivation that drove her to hold fast to her business plan and depart Snow Bird Harbor for the capital.

Until the Li Jian Clan placed her and Zong Ge under house arrest, until the Pearl Bubbles in her hand were gone, until Hua Tang’s dagger aimed itself at her…

She worked hard and struggled to the best of her ability.

But she couldn’t hold on.

Her abilities were finite, her cultivation level was limited, and her talent was confined to business.

She even died!

She couldn’t hold on any longer.

She couldn’t support herself.

But at this crucial moment, he returned!

Just like before.

“I knew it, I always knew it.”

“My Lord, you have returned!”

Zi Di raised her head and looked sideways. She saw the unswerving determination that lined the dragonman youngster’s cheek, and a strong sense of security sprung up from deep within her heart.

“Now, you have returned to my side.”

“You are my knight.”

“The Beast Knight.”

Zi Di’s mouth twitched up, rippling with happiness.

The dragonman youngster also looked down at Zi Di with encouragement and comfort, and their eyes crossed,

Zi Di’s performance was better than he imagined, and after he appeared, Zi Di completely calmed down.

Trust, absolute trust.

The dragonman youngster sensed Zi Di’s strong confidence in him.

It was not baseless.

It came from Mystifying Monster Island, when the two stood by each other through untold dangers and difficulties, accumulating as they stepped over suspicions, misunderstandings, and past mistakes.

Zi Di’s performance saved the dragonman youngster a lot of trouble, so he raised his head to look at Hua Tang, the warmth in his dragon eyes vanishing, leaving only callous battle intent.

The dragonman youngster said, “Hua Tang, you’ve fallen into a trap.”

Hua Tang was unable to free his dagger from the dragonman youngster’s grip, forcing him to use a battle skill.

Hua Tang and the dragonman youngster were within arm’s reach, and all battle skills arrived in the blink of an eye.

Zi Di involuntarily cried out in alarm.

But a moment later, the dragonman youngster used a gold level battle skill to perfectly stop Hua Tang’s attack.

Zi Di’s cry came to a spontaneous stop, and she foolishly looked at the dragonman youngster.

“Lord Commander…you’re already gold level?!”

Originally, Zi Di exhausted herself coming up with a plan that used the Ice Cube Red Wine to raise the dragonman youngster’s transcendent level. This resulted in her falling into the Li Jian Clan’s trap, Zong Ge fell unconscious as a result, and Zi Di felt deep despair in her heart.

Disappointed that this method had hidden dangers and might not work on the dragonman youngster.

But she didn’t expect that when she saw the dragonman youngster again, he would be a gold level!

“What did he experience during this time?” Zi Di thought.

“Long Fu? I’m not seeing things; he’s a gold level?” When the bystanders saw this, they couldn’t help but rub their eyes.

Hua Tang abandoned his dagger and retreated, then after pulling some distance, he narrowed his eyes and cautiously sized up the dragonman youngster.

Long Fu once again spoke, as if he was explaining, “This is all thanks to you. Bing Yang’s Frost Aura’s power of time allowed me to break through the final layer and elevated me to the gold level.”

The young man stated as he moved towards Hua Tang.

Whoosh.

A moment later, he suddenly charged in a flash. His red dragon scales looked like a moving ball of fire to everyone’s eyes.

Ding!

Hua Tang caught the dragonman youngster’s attack trajectory.

A moment later, a dragon claw and a dagger collided head on, unexpectedly echoing like metal crashing together.

The dragonman youngster stopped in place, while Hua Tang was thrown back, this clash was enough to adequately display the enormous disparity between the two.

The dragonman youngster’s legs bent, suddenly stamping into the ground.

Battle skill – Surge.

Like a human artillery shell, he shot into Hua Tang.

Hua Tang put everything he had into a battle skill but could barely withstand it.

The dragonman youngster displayed his formidable wrestling abilities, and each fist and foot was incomparably heavy. Within a few rounds, the residences around were broken and in disorder.

Hua Tang was mainly relying on evasion now.

His attacks on the dragonman youngster yielded minimal results.

After reaching the gold level, the dragon scales’ defense seemed to increase sharply by several fold.

Plop.

Hua Tang’s abdomen was punched by the dragonman youngster, causing him to stoop and cough out a bit of blood.

The blood turned into flower petals in the air.

Away!

He did his utmost to fly away, and despite his injuries, he still moved freely and easily as the white hem of his flower gown danced in the wind.

In the blink of an eye, Hua Tang pulled distance from the dragonman youngster.

There had been a light shield before, but in the wake of the magic formation’s destruction, the light shield had vanished.

Without the light shield dome blocking Hua Tang, he easily retreated.

When the Li Jian Clan’s people saw this, they were very anxious. Someone among them shouted, “Commander Long Fu, chase after him!”

But the dragonman youngster didn’t pursue, rather he returned to Zi Di.

He brightly said, “What happens next is not my business.”

With that sentence of confirmation, the city guard commander shouted, “Chase him down!”

A group of people swarmed away in pursuit.

The battle ended.

The residence had been changed beyond recognition, and the vast majority of it had been violently demolished by the dragonman youngster.

Only a part of the balcony remained, and a wall that connected to the balcony was gone without a trace. A massive hole exposed the bedroom.

On the bed, Zong Ge, pretending to be Long Fu, was still lying on it motionless.

The dragonman youngster jumped up into the wrecked balcony, then walked to Zi Di.

He smiled, and opened up a hug, as if saying again – I have returned.

If Zi Di wasn’t dead, she would have been crying hot tears.

A moment later, she couldn’t help but throw herself into the dragonman youngster’s embrace.

What did it matter of Yao Ma’s status, what did it matter that she was exposing her private affection for the commander…those were tossed into the back of Zi Di’s mind.

The dragonman youngster hugged the girl, caressed Zi Di’s back, and didn’t say anything.

On the battlefield, the battle devastated the Li Jian Clansmen, some just stood there, some were lying down, some were soaked in blood, some had broken bones and snapped tendons, and some were even lying motionless.

Everyone was looking at the dragonman youngster and Zi Di, a number of them with complex expressions.

Many people knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been placed under house arrest by the Li Jian Clan.

A minority that knew the inside story was filled with suspicions.

“How can there be two Long Fus?”

“Neither have any flaws, but it seems the Long Fu that just appeared should be the real one.”

“What is going on? Could it be that it is just as Long Fu said, all of this was a scheme, and he was never injured?”

“If this was Long Fu’s scheme, then did my clan also fall into it too?”

“Commander Long Fu.” In accordance with their authority, a replacement noble steward from the Li Jian Clan came to the two people.

The dragonman youngster stopped hugging, looked at the person, and coldly snorted with annoyance.

“Li Jian Clan, well done…I only wanted to lure out enemies like Hua Tang, and I didn’t expect to see your clan’s true colors.”

The Li Jian clansman promptly waved their hand and stated: this surely must be a misunderstanding; the Li Jian Clan has always been a firm ally of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

“Enough.” The dragonman youngster gestured, stopping the Li Jian clansman from spouting more rubbish.

On the balcony, he took a look around.

The Space Armor Guards, the Li Jian Clan’s elite battle squad, had lost more than half their numbers to Hua Tang.

The dragonman youngster indifferently glanced at the noble steward, confirming he was deader than dead.

Soon after, he entered the bedroom and came to Zong Ge’s side.

Zi Di followed in behind.

The dragonman youngsters said to her, “Bring our people, we are leaving the city and shall search for another residence.”

“Ok.” Zi Di cutely answered.

The past pressure had vanished into near nothingness. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ true leader had returned, so she only needed to heed his orders.

Seeing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanting to leave, the replacement Li Jian Clan steward secretly cursed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ departure would lead to heavy blame he had to shoulder!

He summoned his courage and smiled, “Lord Long Fu, there is no need to leave this late at night, we are still very safe here...”

Whack.

The dragonman youngster tossed his arm and lightly punched the Li Jian clansman.

The latter was tossed like a sandbag, hitting a wall, then fell to the ground.

He covered his chest and stared at the dragonman youngster, the acute pain causing him to incessantly take in cold air.

The Space Armor Guards below sensed this, and they seemed to shift.

The dragonman youngster chuckled, and although the voice was soft, it was heard clearly in everyone’s ears, “Li Jian Clan, humph!”

“We must settle the accounts between us, one by one.”

“I will personally find Mian Li Zang for a chat.”

“If you aren’t qualified, then don’t speak. Isn’t it better to preserve your life?”

The Li Jian Clansmen were as quiet as cold cicadas.

Their leader, the noble steward, had been killed in the battle.

The Space Armor Guards were even more broken and disorderly. Many of them had been slain by Hua Tang, while Hua Tang had been directly repelled by Long Fu.

The most crucial thing was that Long Fu displayed gold level strength in this battle!

“Did he really rely on the Frost Aura to break through?”

“Or does he have something to hide?”

All kinds of suspicions and guesses rose and subsided in everyone’s hearts, but no one spoke.

It was just as the dragonman youngster said – none of them were qualified.

As a gold level, the dragonman youngster was qualified enough to speak with Mian Li Zang on equal footing.

This was the world of the strong!

Chapter 895: Introducing Two Familiar Friends

The dragonman youngster took Zi Di, Zong Ge, Fei She, and the others away without a second thought.

The Li Jian Clan’s people didn’t dare to stop them.

The capital didn’t have a night curfew.

The survivors were just passing through the city gate.

The gate guards were amazed to see a Long Fu standing before them and a “Long Fu” lying down.

Everyone stayed here for a while.

Because the city guard commander communicated that they hadn’t caught Hua Tang and he escaped. As witnesses, he requested the dragonman youngster’s help earnestly and hoped Long Fu would take a bit of time to work on a report.

They were worried the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wouldn’t be returning.

Long Fu stated on the spot, “Don’t worry, we are just finding a temporary dwelling outside the city. My corps’ Magician Yao Ma is participating in this year’s Warm Cup Grand Contest. As a newly promoted gold level, I want to participate in this year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel.”

When the dragonman youngster said this, it stirred the surrounding soldiers.

This was bombshell news!

After tonight, this information will be spread throughout the capital.

Long Fu’s reputation in the kingdom was extraordinary.

The dragonman youngster brought Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others officially past the city walls and traveled northwest.

After they gradually got far from the capital, they came to a snowy field.

The snowy field was empty and uninhabited, and this late into the night, it emitted a silent white light.

The dragonman youngster took the lead and came to a small hill.

To everyone’s amazement, he triggered some measure that caused the small hill to dissipate transcendent aura. Next, the dragonman youngster took a few steps forward and walked directly into the hill.

Everyone followed him inside in a line.

“Where is this?” Zi Di and the others were surprised.

Unlike what they envisioned, the interior of the hill was not a cave or a tunnel, rather it was a wide open space that looked like a mountain had been hollowed out.

The gigantic space inside had many tents.

“These are temporary shelters; you can freely pick which you want and stay here for the time being.”

“Tomorrow, we will return to the capital,” said the dragonman youngster.

Everyone picked a tent and found basic living goods inside them.

When Fei She saw this, his eyes leaked a complex look. All these signs indicated the dragonman youngster had come prepared.

“Follow me.” The dragonman youngster said to Zi Di alone.

After appearing, Zi Di had closely followed by his side, tenderly looking at him.”

“Ok.” Zi Di light nodded, acting quite cute.

The dragonman youngster left the tent community and came to a wide and empty area, then penetrated space and vanished again.

Zi Di followed the dragonman youngster into the second layer of space and noticed it was still vast here.

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish?” Zi Di exhaled.

In this internal layer of space, Zi Di noticed a most familiar magic ship.

“Come in, I have many things I want to tell you about.” Said the dragonman youngster.

“Me too.” Zi Di’s eyes were still glued to the dragonman youngster.

The two entered the ship through the landing cabin, where two others were waiting.

“Hua Tang?!” Zi Di’s pupils contracted as she gasped.

“Also…uh…” Zi Di saw the fishman Dao Hen but couldn’t place his name for a moment.

But Zi Di knew the latter, after all she had participated in the battle of Two Eyes Island and reviewed magic images of it afterwards many times. Then, Dao Hen displayed extraordinary silver level strength that left a deep impression on Zi Di.

Zi Di’s gaze quickly streaked past Dao Han, and once again focused on Hua Tang.

From the traces of battle on Hua Tang’s body, she was certain that the Hua Tang before her was the one who ran amok in the capital.

“So, you are a fake?” Zi Di was exceptionally intelligent and quickly realized the truth.

She suddenly turned around and looked at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “That’s right, it wasn’t the real Hua Tang who attacked the Li Jian Clan’s encampment.”

“I wanted to use it as a pretext to attack the Li Jian Clan’s encampment, giving me justifiable reasons to get you out, and get into contact with our auxiliary commander, I would reveal my gold level strength so I could easily join the National Ceremony Grand Duel!

“Sorry.” The dragonman youngster held Zi Di’s shoulder, “The Li Jian Clan’s encampment was not safe, and if I contacted you in advance, it was possible an enemy would detect it.”

Zi Di replied, “So, the Li Jian steward’s death…”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “That was a small lesson I gave the Li Jian Clan. They actually dared to place my people under house arrest; thus, they had to pay the price.”

“My people…” Zi Di took note of the dragonman youngster’s wording, her heart not having a hint of sadness in its abundant happiness.

“As for these two, they are our familiar friends.” As the dragonman youngster said that he raised his head and pointed to “Hua Tang”.

“Hua Tang” took the opportunity to take off his mask and reveal his real features.

“You, you are actually…Lord Hun Tong?!” Zi Di could have never expected that the one disguised as “Hua Tang” was actually a gold level and also Two Eyes Island’s Lord.

What was actually going on?

Hun Tong was a grand imperial noble and had his own territory. He had run Two Eyes Island well. Why wasn’t he being a perfectly normal ruling lord and instead secretly rushed to Ice Sculpture Island at the dragonman youngster’s orders?

When Hun Tong saw Zi Di’s expression change, he thought it was strange, “Have we met?”

Hun Tong wasn’t in the know, and to him, Magician Yao Ma was a stranger. The magician role Zi Di had played from before had already been assassinated by the imperial secret spy [Gold Hand].

“Alright, you two can rest for the time being.” The dragonman youngster waved his hand.

Hun Tong and Dao Hen silently withdrew at the same time.

Zi Di was amazed.

Dao Hen was a fishman and an enemy of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Even more, Hun Tong was an imperial noble, an actual ruling lord, so why was he acting more like a subordinate and not an ally?

“What happened to his Lordship while he was away?” Zi Di’s curiosity shot up.

The truth was actually very simple.

After the dragonman youngster finished stealing divinity, he contacted Dao Hen again with [Believer Perception].

Through Dao Hen’s, the big guy’s, and other memories, the fishman youngster immediately understood the whole story and knew the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s true identity.

With these secrets exposed, the fishman youngster was more worried about Zi Di’s and Zong Ge’s situation.

Unfortunately, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom capital had no Charming Blue believers.

Since there was none, one had to be dispatched.

Therefore, the fishman youngster transmitted a divine oracle to tell Dao Hen and Hun Tong to proceed towards the capital with all speed.

He gave them Deceptive Disguise so they could use the teleportation formation in the cities.

With the help of the teleportation formations, Dao Hen and Hun Tong arrived at the capital.

As the fishman youngster’s pawns, their first job was to scout out Zi Di’s and Zong Ge’s current situation.

It was very easy to inquire about a lot of information on Zong Ge and Zi Di.

Zong Ge was playing the role of Long Fu, a famous celebrity!

With the help of the fishman youngster using the [Mermaid Fairy Tale] for deception spells, Dao Hen and Hun Tong infiltrated the Li Jian Clan encampment, inquiring about many secrets and witnessed Zong Ge and Zi Di’s failed evacuation, along with the subsequent house arrest the Li Jian Clan placed them under.

They knew, which meant the fishman also knew.

After a bit of reflection, the fishman youngster decided to set a trap.

He first empowered his people with various deception divine spells, mainly to deceive the magic contract between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan.

Before, the fishman youngster used the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] to deceive the Main Earth Elemental God. He also used the divine artifact to dupe the [Truth Slate] right under Teacher Si Ling’s nose and smoothly stole a lot of divinity. Now, the fishman was just cheating a magic contract, which naturally was a cinch.

Then the fishman youngster had Hun Tong pretend to be Hua Tang and attack the Li Jian Clan.

The Li Jian steward was slain and the Space Armor Guards suffered heavy losses. The dragoman youngster then appeared to publicly claim this was a stratagem he deliberately put into place. Then in the following fight, he worked with Hun Tong to make it appear as powerful as possible!

Finally, he relied on these advantages to get Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others out without a hitch. Several goals were accomplished while simultaneously also maintaining their relationship with the Li Jian Clan as much as possible, fighting but not breaking up.

Before leaving, the dragonman youngster said he wanted to have a proper chat with Mian Li Zang, this was no empty promise, it was a part of his plan – he wanted to use this moment as a pretext to raise difficulties and take more benefits in the following collaboration agreement between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan!

The city guard was also deceived too.

It was just as Cang Xu lamented in Ocean Mother Nest, the dragonman youngster was becoming a more outstanding leader!

The dragonman youngster’s operation was founded on sufficient strategizing.

He was the biggest winner who deceived the entire capital.

Hua Tang was framed, and even if he wanted to assert the truth in the future, the masses wouldn’t believe him. In fact, in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, weren’t there a few people who pretended to be Hua Tang while committing crimes?

“The ship also has the fishman Magician Zhong Yong on board, he isn’t important, and there is no need to meet him.” The dragonman youngster brought Zi Di to the most important storage cabin.

The divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] was located here, along with the [Green Jade Gold Coffin] and some other priceless treasures.

“So, so many Pearl Bubbles?” Zi Di couldn’t help but shout.

Her eyes shined!

Reality gave her a most pleasant surprise when she saw the [Mermaid Fairy Tale], most of its large shell was filled with Pearl Bubbles!

After a twinkle of an eye, Zi Di’s gaze shifted back to the dragonman youngster, looking excited, “My Lord, this is amazing! This is truly amazing! You actually found a way to collect so many Pearl Bubbles.”

“Oh, that isn’t a lot.” The dragoman youngster smiled, not minding much.

Many times, the large shell couldn’t contain the full amount.

“Come here.” The dragonman youngster said, walking to the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale].

“I will first give you a detailed full body examination.” The dragonman youngster stretched out his hand towards Zi Di.

Chapter 896: Dueling Godhead

The dragonman youngster picked up the divine artifact and put it on Zi Di’s shoulder.

“I will first give you an examination.”

A moment later, the dragonman youngster used a divine spell.

Divine spell – Divine Artifact Mastery!

For a split second, the dragonman youngster took control of Charming Blue’s divine artifact and his strength soared innumerably.

“This is…a divine spell?” Zi Di’s eyes barely shifted from the dragonman youngster, she was astonished once again.

In her memory, her beloved was a battle qi expert, how could he use a divine spell?

Furthermore, he seemed quite proficient at it.

Immediately afterwards, a second, then a third divine spell was cast…

After several detection divine spells, the dragonman youngster ensured that there was no concealed enemy methods on Zi Di.

“My Lord, how are you so proficient at using divine spells?” Zi Di was stunned.

The dragonman youngster’s performance had broken the limits of Zi Di’s imagination.

“No, that isn’t the most crucial thing!” Zi Di shifted her gaze, tightly staring at the divine artifact in his dragon claw.

She was shocked!

“My Lord, you actually found a way of using the divine artifact?”

“I want to say everything is a chance coincidence, but actually, this is Charming Blue Goddess’ arrangement.” The dragonman youngster sighed.

“Charming Blue Goddess?” Zi Di’s eyes widened, “Hold on, what’s going on with the divine artifact?”

She noticed that the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] had changed color, with one quarter of it looking like it had been contaminated with a deathly black ink color.

“Oh, that is caused by the death divinity the divine artifact is storing.” The dragonman youngster replied.

“Death…Divinity?!” Zi Di grew more flabbergasted.

It wasn’t long before she felt she couldn’t make any sense of what she was hearing.

Divinity? How was it connected with his Lordship?

“Have we really only been apart for this long?” Zi Di had mixed feelings

The dragonman youngster continued, “It is with the help of the divine artifact [Mermaid Fairy Tale] that I was able to teleport both me and the ship from the seafloor depths to here.”

“Of course, the prerequisite is that I have a Charming Blue believer as a coordinate so I can use Divine Domain Decent.”

“It is very safe here, no need to worry about the Li Jian Clan monitoring methods.”

“I guessed they used some spatial skill that we aren’t able to detect.”

“But this space is special, it is a part of the Charming Blue Divine Domain!”

“I just examined your body and found nothing amiss.”

“I know you have a lot of questions, so let’s talk first.”

A very long conversation ensued.

“This period has truly been arduous for you.” The dragonman youngster sighed. He knew Zi Di and Zong Ge had overcome all kinds of untold dangers and difficulties. To deal with that mysterious and powerful enemy, Zong Ge made an all-out effort to show strength, leading to the enemy being restrained by fear and Zi Di doing her utmost to use benefits to rope in the Li Jian Clan, and with the help of their ally, she cleverly advanced her business plan and obtained a lot of gratifying achievements.

The capital’s Wisteria Store was a gigantic trap, but luckily Zi Di received Elder Yuan Ci’s unexpected help.

Hearing Zi Di’s account of this period, the dragonman youngster felt incomparably tense and worried, despite the fact that Zi Di was talking before him in good condition.

The dragonman youngster sighed again, “I didn’t think you would encounter such danger, it greatly exceeds my highest estimations.”

“Zong Ge…”

Zong Ge really had a bad time.

He was affected by Blood Vengeance, then Hua Tang’s battle skill (Willow Flower), then during a duel there was that strange poison, then after that was Bing Yang’s Frost Aura, and finally, the sorrow-dispelling red wine by the Li Jian Clan.

This led to Zong Ge falling into a coma and no one knew where his soul went.

“Zong Ge played the role of ‘Long Fu’ and slowly fell down till now.”

“Looking at this, he bore so much on my behalf.”

Zi Di sighed too, “He is a fighter, a man of steel!”

“Without him, we couldn’t have lasted until now.”

Zi Di had a negative impression of Zong Ge. But through working together for a long time, Zi Di’s recognition of Zong Ge deepened a lot.

Despite having many conflicts and disagreements with each other, that didn’t affect Zi Di’s admiration of Zong Ge and acknowledgement of the latter’s talents!

Conversely, Zong Ge’s feelings towards Zi Di were similar.

The dragonman youngster said, “Since this is the sorrow-dispelling red wine’s fault, and you have a collaboration contract, then I think he probably accepted a divinity trial.”

The dragonman youngster had gotten the secret knowledge of divinity trials from Teacher Si Ling.

Teacher Si Ling taught Cang Xu many things and helped him cast away his Charming Blue belief. Through magic images, the dragonman youngster studied this knowledge afterwards and also knew Cang Xu was taken away by Teacher Si Ling.

The young man wanted to go with Cang Xu, but Cang Xu was not free and was being looked after by Teacher Si Ling, so after thinking about it, the young man gave up on getting him back.

Teacher Si Ling was extraordinary. Not only was he one of the Twelve Saints, but he also controlled the divine artifact [Truth Slate] and could restrain Charming Blue divine spells to some degree.

Furthermore, Cang Xu’s original plan was to get in touch with the undead orthodoxy. This was a very good chance for Cang Xu and all of the survivors.

After careful deliberations, the young man approved of Cang Xu acting on his own again.

Cang Xu had left already, so he had no other reasons to keep staying in the Ocean Mother Nest.

The Ocean Mother Nest’s blood sacrifices would continue, but in the wake of the seafloor beast tide ending, it wouldn’t be held much more.

The young man made arrangements with Dao Hen, and through the coordinates this believer provided, he used Divine Descent to arrive near the capital.

On this desolate snow field, he made some temporary arrangements.

Afterwards, he went to Zi Di and Zong Ge, and after secretly observing them for a time, he showed himself officially.

After explaining what divinity trials were to Zi Di, Zi Di sighed out turbid air, “Are you saying that this isn’t a bad thing for Zong Ge, but rather a good thing?”

“Zong Ge’s beliefs might deviate, but it is absolutely better than suffering Blood Vengeance and the other things he’s sustained.” The dragonman youngster analyzed, “we cannot meddle in this matter, we can only believe in Zong Ge.”

“Sorry, I should have returned earlier.” The dragoman youngster’s eyes were full of tenderness.

Zi Di shook her head, affectionately gazing at the dragonman youngster, “No, my Lord, you made the right choice, the wise choice.”

“You don’t need to have any regrets, because you were in far more danger than I!”

“From a business point of view, the Ocean Mother Nest and blasphemous prayers were really profitable. Despite the immense risks, there were handsome profits in it!”

“I really didn’t think, I don’t think anyone in the world would think that your Lordship’s experience would be so bizarre, uninhibited, and legendary.”

The young man had been heavily injured by Feng Lian, and he even suspected he had died, then returned.

Soon after being teleported into Ocean Mother Nest, he gradually discovered that it was part of the Charming Blue Divine Domain.

The Ocean Mother Nest, the saint beast Snow Pear Octopus, the saint level Zhou Zhang, the teleportation blood sacrifice, unexpectedly rescuing Cang Xu, repeated blasphemous prayers that netted massive benefits, ingeniously taking Xiao Wang Zi, Qing Xin, Lady Sea Snake, Qi Ling, Ku Feng captive, gaining firsthand knowledge of how saint levels fight, deceiving the Main Earth Elemental God, saving Lan Zao, the big guy, Dao Hen, Hun Tong one after the other, deceiving one of the undead orthodoxy saints, the prominent figure Teacher Si Ling, stealing the death divinity, then finally, teleporting back to effortlessly save Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others…

Inside the ship hold, after the lovers had an understanding of what the other went though, they began to confide in each other.

This time of separation and many experiences had changed them both a lot, especially the dragonman youngster.

But their hearts hadn’t changed, on the contrary, they were more intimate.

“Although we escaped from Mystifying Monster Island, why are we now in even more danger?” Zi Di bitterly laughed, “In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, we encountered a formidable and mysterious power checking us. Although your Lordship benefited from Charming Blue Goddess; the consequences of blasphemous prayer are endless.”

The survivors were like a pitiful bunch on a small boat, the more they tried to survive, the more violent the ocean storm grew.

Zi Di revealed a strong worry, and she asked the most crucial question, “My Lord, what do you think Charming Blue Goddess’ condition is?”

The dragonman youngster was silent for a moment, then sighed, “I couldn’t confirm anything.”

“But I believe they are in a deep sleep.”

“Everything they bestowed to me, as well as how to use the divine artifact, was done by the Godhead, who automatically executed this result.

“Godhead…” Zi Di’s eyes flashed sharply, “So, your Lordship appeared with gold level strength to participate in the National Ceremony Grand Duel to ultimately compete for the Dueling Godhead hidden in Tranquil Hill?”

“That’s right, exactly as you said.” The dragonman youngster nodded, “I believe that this is an exceptionally good opportunity, gods are omnipotent, and although I only know the slightest amount of divine power from experience, it is enough to sense how boundless this grand power is.”

“If I possess the Dueling Godhead, then I will be elevated beyond imagination. Even if our future redemption plan fails, at least we will have the chance to stall for time.”

As he said that, the dragonman youngster’s eyes stared out into space, as if he was returning to the first time he did a blasphemous prayer.

Chapter 897: Dueling Sacrifices

Several days ago.

After the fishman youngster rested, he immediately used [Believer Perception] to gain first-hand information.

He knew Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s secret identity and his hidden conspiracy, this caused the fishman youngster’s desire to become a saint level to grow even more.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a gold level, and the fishman youngster saw that he was very strong in battle. Not only that, but he was also Snow Bird Harbor’s local boss with a high position.

To become his enemy, one not only had to consider his combat strength, but also his military might.

“Not just the forces behind him, he has contacts in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom that can leverage many more resources. More crucially, he is also an important member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency and can borrow that organization’s capabilities.”

“Only by becoming a saint level can I truly gain a qualitative change and be able to influence the entire situation sufficiently”

Holding this opinion, the fishman youngster began a blasphemous prayer.

He was very practiced in the process of blasphemous prayers.

In the prayer cabin, he knelt to the ground, tightened his fists, and hung his head.

“Oh, grand Charming Blue Goddess!”

“Your believer kneels in worship to You with a humble heart.”

“May You bequeath an ear to my pious prayer.”

“You are a grand existence, Your divine power is vast and boundless, vaster and more glorious than the star studded heavens. Your wisdom transcends time and space, You are omnipotent, Your power destroys all enemies, and none can compete with You.”

“You mercifully protect and vehemently bless Your flock.”

“I ask for your continued guidance and let me advance on the inexhaustible road ahead. Please sprinkle Your blessings on me, let me gain even more transcendent strength, let me be wreathed by the saint level.”

“I remember Your teachings, and under Your protection I have overcome all difficulties and challenges. I have glorified Your name with my actions and spread Your divine radiance.”

“Charming Blue Goddess, You are my eternal conviction, I will forever praise You. I hope Your divine power fills my spirit for eternity!”

The wording of this prayer had been meticulously designed, and it had only one request, that was to use a divine blessing to promote to the saint level!

But truth be told, the fishman youngster was uncertain about it.

The theory was possible.

But this time, he also knew that he was asking for too much, far more than the prior times.

Would Charming Blue Goddess consent?

Perhaps it was in accordance with the fishman youngster’s conjecture, and the Charming Blue Godhead would automatically carry out this request?

The fishman youngster was mainly relying on his god level fishman bloodline aptitude.

Very quickly, the fishman youngster noticed that this prayer was different from the others.

Previous prayers he almost instantly got a response, and the divine blessings were unable to hold themselves back. But this time, he waited for two full minutes.

Those two minutes seemed very long to the fishman youngster.

Then, he got a divine oracle.

The divine oracle was also different from prior ones.

The fishman youngster sank into a land of fantasy in a split second.

It was as if he was falling into the deep sea, sinking. Underneath on the seafloor depths, there was a gigantic shadow.

This was a familiar scene to the young man.

In the past, he had dreamed of this scene many times.

Then in that dream-like state, as he fell closer to the shadow, he discovered that it was Tranquil Hill!

The surrounding seawater suddenly vanished, and he helplessly fell through the air.

Very quickly, like a bird with broken wings, he was thrown down onto Tranquil Hill’s peak.

But he didn’t fall to his death, rather he was thrown further into the depths of the hill.

There hid a mysterious space.

The space was not wide, and apart from the fishman youngster, there was only one thing.

It floated in the gloomy air, emitting the color of the rainbow. It looked like a transparent crystal. Its shape was not fixed and constantly shifted, but no matter how it changed, it always looked like a geometric polyhedron.

“It is the Dueling Godhead.” Charming Blue Goddess’ oracle told the young man.

The young man’s heart suddenly quaked.

“Approach it.” Said the divine oracle.

The fishman youngster complied with the words and slowly stepped towards it.

The first few steps were easy. But very quickly, his expression changed and he noticed it was becoming more difficult.

The endless pressure around him forced him back. Stranger was that he obviously took many steps forward, but his distance from the Dueling Godhead remained the same.

“There is a way to approach the Godhead – obtain the Dueling Divinity’s favor,” said the divine oracle.

Immediately afterwards, the fishman youngster’s field of view changed abruptly.

He looked around and found himself in a dueling arena.

Two duelists were fighting, the arena was filled with spectators enthusiastically shouting, and the several thousand people present were soaked with a fanatical ambience.

None of them noticed the fishman youngster, as if he were completely transparent.

The fishman youngster’s field of view changed again.

It was another dueling arena, far larger than the last. The experts dueling here were both gold levels.

The fishman youngster’s field of view changed successively.

He experienced many different dueling arenas, some big and some small. The large arenas could hold tens of thousands of spectators while some small ones could only fit ten.

He witnessed many duels, mainly between two people, but there were also some against beasts and others composed of many people.

There were many duels, each with a clear focus to them.

Whenever these important scenes appeared, the vision would slow down and focus on the prominent entity in that duel.

The fishman youngster quickly concluded that all of the prominent figures that were emphasized were like Xiong Ju, people who complied with dueling rules voluntarily.

In other words, they were Dueling believers!

“No, to be more accurate, aren’t those fanatical spectators also believers too?”

“When it comes to dueling, the vast majority of them can barely be considered pan-believers.”

“This is an enormous foundation of belief!”

The fishman youngster sensed a type of shock, especially because he had used {Believer Perception] many times to sense Charming Blue believers.

There were few Charming Blue believers, and compared to the Dueling believers, they were like withered autumn leaves.

The fishman youngster’s field of view suddenly tilted upwards and he ascended to look down at the vast Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s territory from on high.

Within the gigantic landmass, there were dueling arenas big and small densely packed together, with all sorts of duels happening once or even several times a day in them.

Duels were not just entertainment, but rather an important part of life to the Ice Sculpture citizens. They relied on it to solve many daily disputes and conflicts.

This exceptionally good dueling ambience molded many dueling stars, and Long Fu was one of those famous celebrities.

This fertile dueling soil nourished countless Dueling believers. The power of this belief had been accumulating in this land for hundreds and even thousands of years!

They were concentrated on the Tranquil Hill divine domain, and in the depths of Tranquil Hill a new Godhead came into being.

The Dueling Godhead!

“Isn’t what is referred to as a duel actually a sacrifice to the gods?”

“Zhou Zhang caught ocean beasts and blood sacrificed their flesh and lifeforce. These duels are inherently sacrifices too.”

“They are sacrifices to the Dueling God.”

“Quantitative changes led to qualitative changes, ultimately molding a Godhead!”

The divine oracle gave the fishman youngster a sudden realization.

“There is another thing.”

“The Godhead hasn’t been completely…formed yet, it is almost there.”

The divine oracle continued to tell him things.

This year’s National Ceremony Grand Duel was pivotal!

The Grand Duel was a great national sacrifice and would make an enormous amount of belief that would fill in the last piece of the Dueling Godhead and truly complete it.

A complete Godhead could be bestowed to a transcendent lifeform.

Not all transcendent lifeforms were qualified to bear the Godhead. But when a transcendent individual was able to bear a Godhead, then they could use a portion of the Godhead’s power, thereby obtaining a portion of the gods’ divine power!

“Go and take part in the National Ceremony Grand Duel.”

“Go become the ultimate victor, the final winner.”

“By doing this, you can obtain a large amount of the Dueling Divinity’s favor, thereby being able to approach the Godhead.”

“If you can touch the Godhead, it is possible you can take custody of the Godhead.”

“Someday in the future, if you can constantly reinforce your control over it and ultimately absorb the Godhead, then as a transcendent, you will become historical, becoming an unprecedented…Dueling God!”

The divine oracle’s voice rumbled in the fishman youngster’s heart.

The young man was stunned.

The divine oracle then said, “Take note that before this, you cannot be promoted to the saint level.”

“The saint level is the first step on the road to godhood.”

“Any saint level whose foundation isn’t Dueling in nature will evoke the Dueling Divinity’s rejection and revulsion.”

“This will immensely raise the difficulty of approaching the Godhead.”

“The Surging Wave fishman bloodline being promoted to the saint level will be a water attribute saint domain. If you have it before going in, it is equal to erecting a mountain between you and the Dueling Godhead.”

The divine oracle ended there, and for a long time, the fishman youngster couldn’t calm down from his profound shock.

This divine oracle contained infomration that would leave anyone dumbfounded.

The fishman youngster learned a startling gargantuan secret from it!

“The Dueling Godhead!”

“I want to approach it; I want to obtain it.”

“With it, I can possess a portion of a god’s divine power! Compared to the help a saint level promotion would bring, this is absolutely much more!”

Chapter 898: “Pirates” Attacks the Harbor

The fishman youngster’s meticulously prepared blasphemous prayer failed, as ultimately he didn’t achieve his goal – promotion to the saint level.

But at the same time, he succeeded.

Because he learned of an extremely significant secret, and an even greater conspiracy.

The Dueling Godhead!

The Godhead was more important than the saint level.

How many saint levels were there, how many Godheads were there? It was obvious the latter was rarer.

Cultivating a saint level only took decades or a century, but it took the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s entire power and thousands of years of secret accumulation to form a Godhead.

The Dueling Godhead involved a portion of divine power, and that power could completely trounce a saint level.

“Why does Charming Blue Goddess know about this secret?” The fishman youngster pondered.

“Was this Charming Blue Goddess’ plot?”

“Did they make all kinds of plans earlier?”

“But why haven’t they acted? Was I molded for this?”

The fishman youngster thought back now, when Charming Blue Goddess was vigorously cultivating him. Each divine blessing was quite generous…it seemed fishman youngster slowly realized the implication that “Charming Blue Goddess was intentionally helping him”.

The National Ceremony Grand Duel was the moment the Dueling Godhead would be completed.

Charming Blue Goddess seemed to want to meddle in it and get a slice of the pie.

“Based on this, I am Charming Blue Goddess’ most competent pawn in their plan.”

“In the future, if my blasphemy is revealed, how much will it backfire on me?”

“If Charming Blue Goddess’ objective is the Dueling Godhead, and I come into conflict with them, what should I do?”

As for this, the fishman youngster schemed to have Cang Xu investigate it.

Since blaspheming Charming Blue Goddess produced such enormous profits, then they could completely blaspheme other gods.

They were too weak to vie with a raging Charming Blue Goddess who discovered the truth, but they could borrow the power of other gods to try to check and balance Charming Blue Goddess.

Of course, these are things to think about and do later.

From this, the fishman youngster had a new goal. Naturally, his main plan for redemption was still constant.

The Sheng Ming Empire was the most powerful force in the world; no other organization was a match for it.

To say nothing of a saint level, even with the Dueling Godhead, the fishman youngster was not a true god.

In fact, even if he was a true god, what could he do?

How many foreign gods have the imperial gods eliminated?

Wasn’t Charming Blue Goddess wounded into deep slumber by them?

“Having the Dueling Godhead will immensely benefit the entire redemption plan.”

“If nothing else, it will bring a fundamental change to the bargaining table!”

If the survivors were too weak, they wouldn’t be able to speak as equals and taking out divine artifacts as a bribe would court death! They might even be directly annexed by the imperial higher-ups!

Only with sufficient strength could they defend themselves and have the qualifications to negotiate with imperial leadership.

After this prayer was finished, the fishman youngster rapidly adjusted his plan. He had Dao Hen and Hun Tong leave Snow Bird Harbor and teleport to the Ice Sculpture Capital.

The fishman youngster used Divine Domain Descent to teleport over. Then after learning of Zi Di’s and Zong Ge’s situation, he acted.

He commanded Hun Tong, with the help of Deceptive Disguise, to play the role of Hua Tang and stage a show.

Through these moves, the fishman youngster successfully altered the situation, and created a façade that led to the Li Jian Clan’s wrong estimation.

Most importantly, the fishman youngster took over Zong Ge’s responsibilities as Commander Long Fu and would participate in the National Ceremony Grand Duel.

Bing Yang’s special Frost Aura, from a certain point of view, greatly lessened the problems for the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster claimed that because of the aura’s time power, he was able to break into the gold level!

“Next, I should show off my power in a duel.”

The dragonman youngster was completely confident in himself!

The many blasphemous prayers had reaped him many things. Then during the last prayer, although he wasn’t promoted to the saint level, Charming Blue Goddess still gave him a divine blessing.

After obtaining this divine blessing, the dragonman youngster became a gold level magician and was given a lot of cultivation knowledge and a huge volume of spells to use.

Of course, water attribute spells were the most numerous.

So far, the young man had three types of cultivation. Battle qi, magic, and divine spells were al;l promoted to the gold level!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

At dawn, Tan Mo rang at his study door.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed and opened the alchemy door.

The door opened and Tan Mo walked in.

“You got the news?” Tan Mo spoke bluntly, “The latest information from Ice Sculpture Capital, Long Fu has become a gold level.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded, “According to his public claim, it was because of Bing Yang’s Frost Aura that contained [Beautiful Future]. Its effect was a blessing in disguise and allowed him to be promoted earlier.”

“However, I am more inclined to believe that he was always a gold level, he was just concealing it.”

“This was a suitable excuse to openly reveal it and expand his influence. After all, gold level duelists are an entirely different thing from silver level ones.”

When Tan Mo knew Snow Bird Harbor City Lord knew this information, he nodded, “The Li Jian Clan’s second navy fleet has already been routed, and pirates might attack Snow Bird Harbor at any time. To prevent Long Fu’s influence from getting bigger, we should move! The earlier, the better.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s finger tapped his desk, he muttered to himself for a moment, then nodded, “We must act as soon as possible.”

“But this is immensely risky.”

“For example, we were never able to figure out how Long Fu killed three gold levels in a three on one battle.”

“If they built a private teleportation device in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, when we disguise ourselves as pirates to attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch, it is very likely that we will ‘enjoy’ this method.”

Tan Mo sank into silence.

But quickly, his hesitation was driven off by greed.

“Conservative estimates place Two Eyes Island’s property at 100,000 gold!” Tan Mo spoke his thoughts, “This amount of money is enough for me to gamble on.”

“Furthermore, during the process of this, I can hide behind the scenes.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Snow Bird Harbor branch only has a few silver levels in it, no experts in sight.”

As he thought that, Tan Mo had no hesitations.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord weighed up his words and observed his facial expression, then before Tan Mo could continue, he said, “If we act, then I believe that today is the best opportunity to do so.”

“The earlier the better!”

“First, we have to remove the magician tower.”

“I will secretly take control in advance, then when the time comes, I need your people to storm into the tower and slaughter everyone inside.”

After saying that, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord handed Tan Mo a list.

Tan Mo took a look at it and combined it with his own information. He laughed, “Many of these people are your loyal followers.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s face was cold, “But they are more loyal to the kingdom.”

“Perhaps, some of them are more loyal to me.”

“But I need them to be sacrificed so our operation doesn’t have any flaws.”

“After they are sacrificed, I will make arrangements to replace them with secret spies.”

“The magician tower is the highest priority, but apart from it, there are other strategic locations that I need you to do your best work on Tan Mo.”

Tan Mo shrugged, “You help me, I help you. Hey, I like this coordination.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s eyes seemed to penetrate the wall and looked into the ocean, “Thus, after the Thirteenth Prince returns, all of Snow Bird Harbor can be his to use.”

Night.

Snow Bird Harbor.

Inside the magician tower.

“I don’t know why, but I inexplicably feel a bit ill at ease.”

“What? Is it about your son’s college exams?”

“That’s not it…”

“By the way, have you considered my proposal from a few days ago? Do you want to ask for a holiday and go watch the National Ceremony Grand Duel at the capital? You have to know that duels of this scale are quite rare.”

“Alas, I wanted to go. But the kingdom’s recent naval battle loss means our vacation won’t be approved, right?”

A few magicians were chatting as they monitored Snow Bird Harbor.

Pop, pop, pop…

With a few soft sounds, the magicians were suddenly stabbed by assassins at the same time, and not a single death rasp was let out.

Ding.

An explosion suddenly occurred on the top floor of the magician tower, but it was a small one.

The assassins hurried to the top floor, but the assassin leader was already leaving with injuries, their hand holding a snow elf head.

It was the magician tower master.

The assassins let out a breath.

Very quickly, Snow Bird City Lord received a report of mission completion. It went very smoothly, apart from when the magician tower master, who managed to retaliate in a minor way before being assassinated.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord passed this news to Tan Mo.

Tan Mo ordered, “Move!”

Thus, a group of imperial navy disguised as pirates came from the water, dark corners of the port, and other places to gradually encircle the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch’s ships.

Chapter 899: Snow Bird Harbor Battle – Caught Off Guard

With the help of the dense night, along with their allocated alchemy equipment, the navies easily got near the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

They were structured into small teams, dispersed apart from each other, but they were silent and moved nimbly. Furthermore, they paid attention to their allies, constantly adjusting their position as they advanced together. All of this revealed the fine military attainments of the imperial navies.

The alchemy equipment they wore also had excellent camouflage capabilities. The navies almost blended perfectly into the night, and ordinary eyes could not detect them.

On the port, no alerts appeared.

The magician tower had been eliminated.

The alarms in Snow Bird Harbor’s port were disabled by the navies.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had their own defenses. But the defensive measures around them were silently demolished by the tiny frisbee devices the navies deployed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ greatest defense was their ships.

Each ship could be seen as a mobile fort.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had many ships berthed at the port. The flagship was silver level and was a retired warship from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s army. It had a slender model but actually had the greatest volume.

Next was the iron level Love Triangle. That ship was once the Applaud Pirates’ flagship, then it was seized by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

There was also the bronze level White Bandage and Iron Lump, they were once property of two mercenary corps on Two Eyes Island. After the battle of Two Eyes Island, the survivors bought them from the Island Lord Hun Tong, and although they were the lowest level ships, they still had alchemy installations that allowed the White Bandage to heal people and gave the Iron Lump excellent defense.

In addition to those ships, there were some ordinary vessels, and in fierce naval battles, they were cannon fodder and auxiliary ships, they were not worth mentioning.

Tan Mo’s side had exhaustive information on these ships.

The imperial secret spies worked in intelligence and added on is the fact that the survivors’ ships had been repaired by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s factories.

It was clear this information was very detailed and reliable.

In fact, Snow Bird Harbor Lord not only collected information, he also left a few tricks while repairing these ships.
